# Regia Engine v0.6.0

Builds on v0.5.0. v0.6.0 is the editor-complete release on the way to the v1.0 alpha, and it carries the one
content-format bump of the v0.6 line: every format that moved has a migrator, and `regia upgrade-project` rewrites a
v0.5.x project in place. The v0.5.1 line was never tagged, so this note also covers its fixes, including every bug the
v0.5.0 note lists under "Fixed after v0.5.0": a game pinned to v0.5.0 reads this one note.

Read **Upgrade: host changes since v0.5.0** first if you build a game or a shell against the engine crates. New
features are off, or leave existing projects unchanged, unless **Changed** says otherwise. **Known issues** lists what
is not yet accepted, starting with the gate and platform results of the v0.6.0 candidate; a feature is described as
working on a platform only where a cited run shows it (see **Platform evidence**).

## What v0.6 delivers

- **Editor workspace and level editing.**
  - Workspace: a new default look, Graphite, plus AMOLED Night, Paper and a high-contrast theme. Named layouts and nine
    built-in workspaces (Level, Animation, Materials, UI, World, Cinematics, Debug, Terrain, 2D tiles) switch without
    closing a document. Panels float in their own OS windows, the Outliner, Content Browser and Inspector open up to
    eight copies each, and the Content Browser and Output Log slide up as drawers over a console line. Universal search
    (Ctrl+T), scoped shortcuts with keymap presets, one Settings window, a project browser with level templates and
    samples, and editor feature profiles come with it.
  - Viewport: per-pane view modes, exposure and screen percentage; World, Local, Parent and View gizmo spaces with
    surface snap; lasso select, Select similar and a Measure tool; pilot and look-through; overlay groups; and blockout
    brushes with a CSG preview and a bake to a GLB mesh.
  - Outliner: a query language, folders, columns, configuration warnings, several open levels side by side and layer
    locks.
  - Inspector: property search and pins, expressions and units in number fields, a full color picker, inline editing of
    data, material and physics material assets, and a prefab overrides window with Edit prefab in place.
- **Content, undo and recovery.** The Content Browser gains multi-select with bulk actions, a keyboard folder tree,
  filter chips, collections and saved searches, drops from the OS, an import dialog with per-importer presets, and asset
  packages with their dependencies. Level edits and the asset editors share one undo history with an Undo History
  window. Graph and asset documents autosave on a worker thread and come back through crash recovery, File ▸ Version
  history keeps saved and autosaved versions, and two unclean exits in a row start the editor in recovery mode.
- **Graph and asset editors.** The material graph editor reaches Stage 2 (mesh previews, a typed node inspector, fuzzy
  node search, a Parameters panel, shader stats per render profile and a Code node view). The Logic Graph panel edits
  and debugs `.logic.ron` rules with breakpoints, watches and step. New editors cover biomes, camera rigs, motion
  databases, smart objects, data assets, bulk edits, vehicles (cars, bikes, hover hulls, trailers and aircraft),
  fracture, retargeting, the UI designer canvas and themes. The sequencer gains a dope-sheet timeline with ripple edits
  and Sequence ▸ Render movie….
- **Play, launch and profile.** Play in editor adds Simulate, split screen for up to eight players, a separate play
  window at a chosen resolution with phone and handheld device simulation, and Game tabs for separate-process servers
  and clients. **Launch standalone** and **Launch on Android device** run the game outside the editor, push live-cooked
  assets into it and mirror the device screen. The Profiler reads the edit world, play, a PIE process or a dev build,
  with History, Timeline, Memory, GPU resources, Loads and Lockstep tabs, and the Frame Debugger shows each pass's
  targets and draw records.
- **Teams, agents and extensions.** Revision control runs through git, with LFS file locks, side-by-side asset and graph
  diffs, review notes, LAN collaboration presence, and a remote Outliner and Inspector with a live link into a running
  game. Window ▸ Agent hosts runs configured external agent commands, and Propose mode stages an agent's edits for
  approval as one undo entry. Extensions add menus, commands, settings, viewport tools, overlays, workspaces and build
  hooks. A new editor control must be drawn through an agent action or classified (`agent_parity_scan`), `cargo xtask
  editor-smoke` drives every panel, menu item and palette command through the agent protocol, and screen readers can
  read and operate the editor window.
- **One format bump, with migrators.** v0.6.0 carries the one content-format bump of the v0.6 line, and every bumped
  format has a migrator: `regia upgrade-project` rewrites a v0.5.x project, and `--write --backup <dir>` copies its
  source files to a dated folder first. Bumped formats include `.animgraph.ron` v4, `.retarget.ron` v2, `.seq.ron` v6,
  `.ui.ron` v11, `.vfx.ron` v8, `.bake.ron` v3, `cooked-clip` v3, `motiondb-cooked` v3, the importer `.meta` v2 and
  theme v2. New formats include level variants, layer files, `logic-graph-ref`, `asset-ids`, `delivery-contract`,
  `key-track`, `release-chunks` and the world-geometry formats, and the editor's own files are now frozen formats.
  `cargo xtask check-formats --since-tag` compares the format table with v0.5.0's, and `release_notes::v060_note_truth`
  requires an upgrade row for every format whose version moved.
- **Tunnels and multi-level worlds.** The new `regia_world_geom` crate and terrain mesh layers bring tunnels, overhangs
  and multi-level maps: the cook names each failed check and its location, streams each layer with the terrain, writes
  indoor cells and portals so tunnel interiors cull with no hand-authored cells, and drives portal sound and baked
  zones. Navigation keeps several walkable layers per navmesh. Terrain adds raster descriptors and source tile sets (up
  to 4096 tiles), non-destructive edit layers, runtime height edits and a far backdrop ring.
- **Lockstep simulation.** The `regia_detsim` crate is a lockstep kit with no ECS, GPU or platform dependency: pinned
  math, seeded randomness, per-system desync hashes, snapshots, input logs and offline replay. On top of it come
  deterministic navigation, AI, hover, ground vehicles, projectiles, fog of war and ECS-free match rules. `cargo xtask
  det-golden` runs the determinism goldens on x86-64 Linux, qemu-aarch64, Android, the Mac, Windows and the Deck and
  compares them, and `cargo xtask det-scan` checks the lockstep crates and a game's own crates.
- **Networking.**
  - Lockstep: a lockstep mode over `NetServer`/`NetClient`, rollback lockstep for small action matches, a headless
    dedicated server as frame authority, and desyncs that name the subsystem and replay offline.
  - Transport v2: packet acknowledgments with RFC 9002 loss detection, up to 32 reliable streams per connection, rate
    control on every connection (64 clients on a 20 Mbit/s uplink: worst staleness p99 3 ticks, was 215), path MTU
    discovery and validated address changes.
  - Replication: a walking crowd costs 9.0 bytes per moving entity update (was 35.8).
  - Hosting: NAT traversal from the game socket, port mapping, relay protocol 2, a self-hosted lobby hub and automatic
    reconnect. `regia_net` builds without the ECS, and `cargo xtask net-soak` runs the real server and clients under 28
    named impairment profiles.
- **Renderer and loading.** `DrawList` brings 9,000 keyed draws to the frame with no entity spawned, and counted meshes
  and textures draw from their GPU upload with no CPU copy. Each split-screen view keeps its own occlusion, indoor cells
  and portals cull what no view reaches, and up to four directional lights shade the frame. Scene ready, an opt-in
  per-frame load budget, a paused scene hold, pipeline warm targets and a host memory budget pace loading. A `VeryLow`
  tier serves GPUs that miss 30 fps at Low, frame caps follow the app state, and telemetry names present waits and
  stalls.
- **Gameplay, animation, audio and UI.**
  - Gameplay: logic graphs run in prefabs and allocation-free, Lua mods run as host or presentation, and AI gains
    perception v2, formations, `MoveTo` and in-world debug draw, alongside fog of war, leashed photo mode and display
    settings that apply live.
  - Animation: Chain IK, Body Aim, Stride Warp and Orientation Warp graph nodes, foot placement with heel and toe, and
    clips compressed at cook.
  - Audio and UI: mixer effect chains, sends and snapshots, an EBU R128 loudness meter and portal sound; `.ui.ron` v11,
    theme v2 style rules and the `draw2d` HUD list.
- **Content pipeline and release tools.** Closure content ships only what the entry points reach, a cook diet caps
  textures and mesh LODs per profile, an asset id manifest keeps ids across fresh checkouts, and delivery contracts
  check models. glTF, material and texture lint report in SARIF, and package hygiene audits the finished artifact and
  writes a receipt. `regia play-gate`, `regia logs perf-gate`, `regia logs triage` and `regia engine chunk` cover play
  checks, performance gates, tester logs and release trees.

## Added

### Editor

#### Look, layout and windows

- New default editor look, Graphite: graphite panels, white reserved for the selection, links, focus and the active
  item, flat surfaces with a lit top edge, a 2 px focus ring and visible control outlines. Two more built-in looks,
  **AMOLED Night** and **Paper**; the editor ships JetBrains Mono, Space Mono and Geist (SIL OFL 1.1, editor only)
  (ADR-8585)
- Named layouts and workspaces (ADR-7753, ADR-8197): View ▸ Layouts saves, renames and deletes dock arrangements as
  `<user config>/regia/layouts/<name>.editor_layout.ron`. A switcher at the toolbar's right end (also View ▸ Workspace
  and palette `workspace.<id>`) offers Level, Animation, Materials, UI, World, Cinematics, Debug, Terrain and 2D tiles,
  plus saved layouts and workspaces that editor extensions register (`ToolExtensionContext::register_workspace`).
  Switching keeps every open document and restores each arrangement exactly; a workspace mode turns on the World,
  Terrain, 2D tiles, Animation or UI design tools with a toolbar strip for its panels. Agent: `editor.layout_list`,
  `editor.layout_save_named`, `editor.layout_apply_named`, `editor.layout_delete`, `editor.workspace_list`,
  `editor.workspace_set { mode? }`.
- Pane controls: Ctrl+Space (`view.maximize_panel`) shows the hovered or focused tab alone, F11
  (`view.fullscreen_viewport`) shows the viewport alone without menus and bars, and Escape stops play first, then leaves
  full screen. A keyboard-operable `+` button (Add panel) on every tab group lists its tabs and the panels to add; a
  tab's menu has Replace with, Maximize, Join (two neighboring areas into one stack) and Swap. Dragging an asset or
  entity over a tab for 30 frames brings it forward. Agent: `editor.maximize_panel`, `editor.panel_replace`,
  `editor.layout_join { from, into }`, `editor.layout_swap { from, with }`.
- Panels in their own OS windows (ADR-7821): a tab's menu has Float in own window and Dock back, tearing a tab out
  floats it, and the palette has `view.float_panel` and `view.dock_floated_panels`. Any panel can float, graph editors
  included; the windows share the GPU device, the egui context and the worlds, layouts save them relative to the main
  window, and drops between windows land on release. Preference `ui.floating_windows = os | in_window` (default `os` on
  Linux and Windows). Agent: `editor.panel_float`, `editor.panel_instances`.
- Panel instances (ADR-8145): Window ▸ New Outliner, New Content Browser and New Inspector (palette
  `window.new_outliner`, `window.new_content`, `window.new_inspector`) open up to eight copies of each, titled "Outliner
  2" and so on, each with its own view (search, folder, columns, lock). Layouts save and restore every copy. Agent:
  `editor.panel_new_instance { panel }`; `editor.layout_get` lists `layout.instances`.
- Every tab bar ends in an overflow button (▾) that lists the group's hidden tabs; a pick brings the tab forward and
  scrolls it into the bar. Agent method `editor.tab_overflow`. (ADR-7902)
- Status bar, drawers and console line (ADR-7754, ADR-7796): the status bar's indicators are buttons (Content, Log,
  Unsaved: N opens the Save all dialog, Jobs: N with progress and Cancel, Trace, Problems: N, and the branch with its
  changed-file count). The Content Browser and the Output Log slide up as drawers over the dock without changing the
  layout (Ctrl+Shift+Space, `view.content_drawer`; Dock keeps one as a tab). A console line under the Output Log runs
  command ids, agent methods with JSON parameters and project console variables, with Tab completion, parameter hints
  and per-project history (`.regia/console_history.txt`). Quick settings in the main toolbar cover snapping, realtime,
  show flags, preview sun and autosave. Agent: `editor.status_bar`, `editor.drawer`, `editor.console_run`,
  `editor.console_complete`.
- Main window state (`editor_window_state.ron` v1, user config): the window's monitor, size, position and full-screen
  state, the open level and the open material and animation graph documents come back on the next start of the project;
  a monitor that is gone falls back to the primary display.
- Editor view state per project (`<project>/.regia/editor_view.ron`): File ▸ Recent survives a restart, and per level
  the perspective camera, the camera bookmarks and the Outliner locks (by `EntityGuid`) come back when the level opens.
  Agents read it with `editor.view_state` (ADR-7275)
- Editor feature profiles (`.editor_profile.ron` v1): hide feature areas, panels or Create entity kinds; built-in Full
  and UI only; the active profile is per project (`.regia/editor_profile.ron`) and the palette leaves hidden commands
  out. Agent: `editor.feature_profile_get`, `editor.feature_profile_set`.

#### Viewport

- Viewport camera (ADR-7755): invert options for orbit, pan, fly look and the wheel (`camera.invert_*`); a speed slider
  of eight doubling steps (0.75 to 96 m/s) and "Speed scales with distance"; orbit around the selection; Bottom, Back
  and opposite Side orthographic views (Alt+2 to Alt+4); zoom toward the cursor (`camera.zoom_to_cursor`, on by
  default); navigation presets with three-button mouse and numpad emulation; Move camera to selection and Move selection
  to camera; per-pane field of view and clip planes (`camera.near_clip`, `camera.far_clip`); 2- and 3-pane layouts.
  Agent: new `editor.set_viewport` fields, `editor.view_preset` `bottom`, `back`, `opposite_side`,
  `editor.camera_to_selection`, `editor.selection_to_camera`, `editor.viewport_layout`.
- View presets on bare 1-4 (perspective, top, front, side), 0.25 s eased Focus, preset and bookmark moves, a pivot
  mode (median, individual origins, active) and a Snap toggle on the toolbar, Alt+drag gizmo duplicate and Ctrl+D.
  Agents: `editor.view_preset`, `editor.camera_ease`, `editor.set_pivot_mode`, `editor.set_snap`, `editor.gizmo_drag`.
  (ADR-7204)
- Viewport display (ADR-7770): per-pane view modes (Alt+1 to Alt+8: Lit, Unlit, Wireframe, Lit + wireframe, Lighting
  only, Light complexity, Overdraw, Base color) and every renderer buffer view; Show ▸ Icons by kind with size and
  opacity; per-pane exposure (Auto, Fixed EV -4 to 20, Level band) and screen percentage (25 to 200 %); pane badges for
  Realtime off and non-default settings with a reset; View ▸ Rendering Debug. H hides the selection, Alt+H shows
  everything and Shift+H isolates it, per user in `.regia/editor_view.ron`, never in the level or the undo history.
  Agent: `editor.viewport_badges`, `editor.hide`, `editor.isolate` and new `editor.set_viewport` and
  `editor.capture_viewport` fields.
- Gizmo spaces, pivots and snapping (ADR-7756): World, Local, Parent and View gizmo spaces (Ctrl+Backtick); a Select
  tool (Q); a bounds-center pivot and a temporary pivot offset (hold P); grid, angle and scale snap step menus saved per
  project in `.regia/viewport_snap.ron`; surface snap while dragging, with optional alignment to the surface normal and
  an offset; fixed-step rotate (U, Shift+U). Agent: `editor.set_tool`, `editor.rotate_step`; `editor.set_gizmo { space
  }`, `editor.set_pivot_mode { mode: "bounds", offset }`, `editor.placement_options { surface_snap, surface_align,
  surface_offset }`.
- Selection and placement tools (ADR-7769): lasso select (hold L), subtract (Ctrl+Shift) for box, lasso and click,
  Select similar (same kind, components, asset or layer), Alt+click to pick from overlapping objects, a Selection root
  saved with the level, a hover outline, a drop ghost while an asset is dragged over the viewport, and a Measure tool
  (M; hold V to snap to vertices). Agent: `editor.lasso_select`, `editor.select_similar`, `editor.pick_stack`,
  `editor.selection_root`, `editor.drop_preview`, `editor.measure`; `editor.box_select { mode: "subtract" }`.
- Pilot, look through, camera preview, screenshots and guides (ADR-7768): Pilot selection (Ctrl+Shift+L) locks the
  editor camera to a camera, light or any entity as one undo step; the Camera menu lists every scene camera to look
  through or pilot; a selected camera shows an inset preview (`viewport.camera_preview`, default `medium`); File ▸
  Screenshot… writes a PNG at 1x to 8x or up to 16384 px to `Saved/Screenshots`; rule of thirds, center cross, safe
  areas and letterbox guides. Agent: `editor.pilot`, `editor.cameras`, `editor.look_through`, `editor.camera_preview`,
  `editor.screenshot`, `editor.set_viewport { guides }`.
- Viewport overlay groups: the transform, snap, camera, view and show, tools and selection groups sit inside the active
  pane; each collapses to a named icon menu, docks to any edge or turns vertical from the keyboard-accessible Overlays
  menu, with per-user layouts per view kind and named presets. The Select group has Box and Lasso controls. Agent:
  `editor.viewport_overlays`.
- Viewport CPU picking against each drawn mesh's `regia_pick::MeshBvh` and the terrain when there is no id buffer (or
  `editor.pick { cpu: true }`), so a headless editor answers `editor.pick` (ADR-7204)
- Viewport **Resolution** preview frames the play window's picture at a chosen resolution with action-safe and
  title-safe guides (`editor.viewport_resolution_preview`). Import settings get a per-field **Reset** and edit several
  sources of one importer together (`editor.import_settings_open { paths }`, `editor.import_settings_edit { reset }`).
  The command palette has `prefab.create_variant`.
- Show menu: **Navigation** (`editor.set_viewport { navigation }`), **Build / Clear navigation preview**
  (`editor.nav_preview { action }`: an editor-only nav mesh baked from the level's static collision, never saved),
  **Post volumes** (`editor.set_viewport { post_volumes }`) and the **Colliders** and **Light radii** flags (collider
  outlines and light reach in the viewport and in `editor.capture_viewport`, off by default), which were agent-only
  (`editor.set_viewport { colliders, light_radii }`) (ADR-7217, ADR-7468, ADR-7593, ADR-7630)
- Create menu actors at the viewport's focus point: Player start, Trigger volume, Point, Spot and Directional light,
  Camera and Reflection capture (`editor.create_entity { kind, translation? }` makes every Create entry, shapes
  included). Player starts draw a flag and a floor arrow, trigger volumes a dashed outline. (ADR-7468, ADR-7592)
- Create ▸ Audio emitter, Rect light, Tube light, Decal, Post-process volume, Spline, Nav link and Smart object
  (`editor.create_entity` kinds of the same names). (ADR-7741)
- Reflection captures: a selected `LocalCapture` draws its influence box and blend margin under Show ▸ Bounds, and
  **Tools ▸ Bake reflection captures** (`editor.bake_captures { file? }`, `editor.bake_captures_status`) bakes every
  capture with the editor's renderer as a cancellable job, writes the level's `.fcap` with the bytes `regia
  bake-captures` writes, names it in `look.captures` and marks the level unsaved (ADR-7592)
- Reflection captures get box and blend-margin grips with the viewport's handle tool (Tools ▸ Collider handles (C)), one
  undo step per drag (`editor.capture_handles`, `editor.capture_handle_drag`), and the editor viewport draws baked
  reflection captures as a game does; a capture moved since its bake is not drawn until baked again. (ADR-7663)
- Blockout brushes (box, cylinder, wedge, stairs, sphere) with ordered add, subtract and intersect, per-face material
  slots, a background CSG preview, viewport grips, and a bake to a GLB mesh with optional static triangle-mesh
  collision. Agent: `editor.blockout_add`, `editor.blockout_edit`, `editor.blockout_bake`.

#### Outliner and Inspector

- Outliner tree (ADR-7757): the search box and `editor.hierarchy`'s `filter` take a query language (name words, `name~`,
  `name=`, `kind:`, `type:`/`has:`, `tag:`, `under:`, `parent:`, field comparisons such as `PointLight.intensity>1000`,
  `and`, `or`, `not`, parentheses); folders for top-level entities (saved `EditorFolder` component, Ctrl+Shift+N);
  rename in place (F2); Expand all and Collapse all; rubber-band selection; Paste as child and Paste at original
  position; a full keyboard tree. Agent: `editor.folder_create`, `editor.folder_rename`, `editor.folder_delete`,
  `editor.folder_move`, `editor.outliner_rename`, `editor.outliner_expand`, `editor.paste { into, at_original }`.
- Outliner rows (ADR-7772, ADR-7814, ADR-8126): a Columns menu (Type, Layer, Prefab source, Tags, Visible, Locked,
  Revision) with widths and sorting, saved per user in `.regia/outliner_view.ron`; ⚠ configuration warnings (a body
  without a collider, a zero-intensity light, a camera with near >= far, a missing prefab ...) with a *Level
  configuration* section in Problems and `ConfigChecks::register` for game checks; badges for Lua scripts, logic graphs,
  sequences and unique names that open the Logic Graph panel, the declaring file in the code editor or the Sequence
  Curves panel; tags on the selection; row colors; Change kind in place (light type, collider shape, camera projection);
  and unique names (`regia_scene::unique_name::UniqueName`, `find_unique`). Agent: `editor.outliner_columns`,
  `editor.outliner_view`, `editor.config_warnings`, `editor.tag_selection`, `editor.change_kind`,
  `editor.open_sequence`, `editor.open_badge`; `editor.outliner_set { color, unique }`.
- File ▸ Save all, Recent, Exit and Edit ▸ Cut, Copy, Paste, Duplicate, Delete, Select all with an entity clipboard
  (`editor.menu_invoke { path }`); Outliner eye, lock, prefab link icon, override marker and Focus
  (`editor.outliner_set`) (ADR-7204)
- **Select in level** in the Content Browser (`editor.select_by_asset { path, mode? }`) selects every level entity that
  references an asset; the Outliner filter (`editor.outliner_rows`) takes `type:<Component>` and `tag:<name>` terms
- Large projects: `editor.panel_timings` (per-panel draw times), `editor.outliner_rows` (the Outliner a window at a
  time), Outliner keyboard navigation and reveal of outside selections, an Inspector pager for containers over 200
  elements, and a 100 000-line Output Log history with a full-message view (ADR-7306)
- Inspector search, categories, pins and lock (ADR-7758): a fuzzy property search (Ctrl+F), `@Category` sub-headers,
  **Modified only**, fields pinned to the top of their component (per user in `inspector_pins.ron`), an Inspector lock
  and floating pinned Inspectors, and a searchable Add component picker with recent picks first. Search and Modified
  only filter the multi-selection too (ADR-8106). Agent: `editor.inspector_pin`, `editor.inspector_lock`,
  `editor.inspector_open_pinned`, `editor.component_types`; `editor.inspect_selection { search, modified_only, raw }`.
- The Inspector's pin tools refuse with `pins_file_unreadable` when `inspector_pins.ron` could not be read this session
  (malformed, or written by a newer editor), and leave the file untouched (ADR-7813)
- Shift+F10 opens a focused Inspector field's menu (Pin to top from the keyboard); Up/Down highlight a row in the
  add-component picker and Enter adds it (the first addable row when none is highlighted).
- Inspector field tools (ADR-7773): a field menu with Copy value, Paste value, Copy as RON, Copy agent path and Reset to
  default (Ctrl+C, Ctrl+Shift+C, Ctrl+V); component copy, paste, paste as new and reset; reset arrows on every field
  that differs from its default or prefab value (on a multi-selection, where any entity differs); tooltips with the
  field's doc, default and agent path; `@EditCondition` and `@EditConditionHides`; header checkboxes for `Option` fields
  and `@Toggle` structs; a Raw view; list item counts, Clear, drag handles, Alt+Up/Down and Duplicate. Agent:
  `editor.property_copy`, `editor.property_paste`, `editor.component_copy`, `editor.component_paste`,
  `editor.reset_property`, `editor.list_move`, `editor.list_duplicate`.
- Inspector values: number fields take expressions and units (`2*pi`, `+10`, `*2`, `5cm`, `90deg`, `10ms`), Ctrl snaps
  and Alt speeds up drags, and a label drag scrubs; a chain toggle links Vec2/Vec3 lanes (`inspector.linked_vectors`);
  `@Flags`, `@LayerMask`, `@FilePicker`, `@DirPicker` and `@Button` attributes; component actions (`ComponentActions`,
  `ToolExtensionContext::register_component_action`) run as one undo step on every selected entity. Agent:
  `editor.component_actions`, `editor.run_component_action`; `editor.set_property { expr }`.
- Inspector pickers (ADR-7786, ADR-8106): asset fields have Browse, Use selected and Clear, a shared-asset count with
  **Make unique**; entity fields have an eyedropper; key lists draw as curve and gradient fields with an editor; a full
  color picker (RGB floats, 0-255, HSV, OKLCH, hex, EV intensity for `@ColorHdr`, swatches and recent colors); a color
  eyedropper picks any pixel of the editor window, the rendered viewport included, into every selected entity. Agent:
  `editor.asset_field_set`, `editor.make_unique`, `editor.asset_referencer_count`, `editor.curve_field_edit`,
  `editor.color_swatches`, `editor.color_eyedropper`.
- Inspector asset fields: a field with `@AssetFilter` shows a thumbnail, takes a Content Browser drop and has a search
  picker, one undo step each (`editor.asset_field_choices`). The engine's asset fields are tagged: `MeshSource.mesh` and
  `material`, `AudioEmitter.sound_name`, `MaterialGraphRef.path`, the cloth, soft-body and destructible source assets,
  `ProcGenerator.graph` and `VideoPlayer.source`. (ADR-7472)
- Inline sub-asset editing (ADR-8107): an asset field naming a `.data.ron`, `.material.ron` or `.physmat.ron` expands to
  edit the asset in place, each edit one undo step checked by the asset's reader; File ▸ Save all lists it and its
  inline **Save** writes it alone. Agent: `editor.inspect_asset_inline`, `editor.asset_inline_set`; `editor.save_all {
  ids: [save_id] }`.
- Per-component **Remove** in the Inspector (`editor.remove_component`), undoable with the component's values (ADR-7472)

#### Content Browser and import

- Content Browser multi-select (Ctrl/Shift click, box drag, keyboard), bulk Validate, Re-import, Save, Move to…, Delete…
  and Add to collection, **Replace references with…** on delete, **Retarget… / Find similar** for missing references, a
  Save all dialog with checkboxes, and a rich tooltip. Agent: `editor.content_select`, `editor.bulk_asset_action`,
  `editor.delete_asset { replace_with }`, `editor.retarget_references`, `editor.save_all`, `editor.asset_details`.
  (ADR-7822)
- Content Browser navigation: a keyboard folder tree with drag-to-move, breadcrumbs, back/forward, favorites, Filter
  chips (kinds, tags, modified, unreferenced, has problems, review pending), search words `tag:`, `is:` and
  `meta:<key>=<value>`, and static or saved-search collections (personal or shared in `config/asset_collections.ron`).
  Agent: `editor.content_filter`, `editor.content_favorites`, `editor.content_favorites_get`, `editor.collection_list`,
  `_create`, `_add`, `_remove`, `_delete`. (ADR-7759, ADR-7798)
- Content Browser: Save all saves every document kind the dialog lists (behavior and state trees, tables, sound cues,
  effects, conversations, animation graphs, UI animations, sequences, procedural graphs, cloth, ragdolls, vehicles,
  abilities, localization, camera assets, smart objects, the Material panel and project settings; ADR-8147), unsaved
  files show a dot, Edit selection in grid, Export selection with dependencies… and a selection bar for bulk actions. A
  Library root copies an asset with its dependencies into the user config directory and back into any project as an
  independent copy. Agent: `editor.bulk_edit { assets? }`, `editor.export_package`, `editor.asset_library`.
- Drop files from the OS onto the Content Browser or a viewport pane (copied in, placed at the drop point as one undo
  step), custom and refreshed thumbnails, posed-skeleton animation previews for `.bvh` and skinned glTF, folder asset
  packages with their dependencies (`.regia_assetpkg.ron`), and View ▸ Asset Jobs. Agent: `editor.import_files`,
  `editor.thumbnail_refresh`, `editor.thumbnail_capture`, `editor.export_package`, `editor.import_package`,
  `editor.asset_jobs`. (ADR-7788)
- `editor.open_asset { path }` opens about 30 asset kinds in their panels as a Content Browser double click does, and
  `editor.asset_kinds` lists what opens and what creates: a `.cloth.ron` or `.soft.ron` opens in the Cloth panel (kinds
  `cloth` and `soft_body`) and a `.logic.ron` in the Logic Graph panel, and an Output Log path to one of these is a link
  that opens it. Content ▸ New adds Procedural graph, VFX system, Conversation, Curve table, Chooser, Data table, Biome,
  Camera rig, Montage, Cloth, Soft body, Logic graph (an empty `.logic.ron` v1), Motion database, Smart object, Tile map
  and Terrain. A folder row's menu runs Validate folder, Reference graph, Size map and Audit to CSV on that folder.
  (ADR-7725, ADR-7774)
- `editor.open_asset` and a Content Browser double click open a `.motiondb.ron` in the Motion database editor (kind
  `motion_database`) and a `.biome.ron` in the Biome editor (kind `biome`); Content ▸ New lists Motion database and
  Biome after Tile map.
- Content Browser New ▸ Anim graph, Sound cue, UI layout and Data asset create a file its loader reads under the first
  free `<kind>-<n>` name; `editor.create_asset` takes `anim_graph`, `sound_cue`, `ui_layout` and `data_asset` (with
  `type`) (ADR-7234)
- Import dialog and presets (ADR-7904, ADR-8376): Content ▸ Import… with several files or a glTF/GLB, FBX or OBJ, and
  **Re-import with options…**, open a dialog with a model preview, a scene tree and the importer form. Animations can be
  skipped, renamed or split by frame range; on glTF/GLB each mesh-bearing node has an Import mesh toggle and each
  material a Use choice (Embedded, Extract to `<model stem>_<material>.material.ron`, Replace with a project material).
  Named presets per importer live in `config/import_presets.ron`. Agent: `editor.import_presets`,
  `editor.import_dialog_open`, `editor.import_dialog_state`, `editor.import_with`.
- **Import settings…** in the Content Browser row menu of an image, glTF/GLB, OBJ, FBX or sound: typed fields over the
  importer's settings, **Apply** (writes the `.meta`, cooks once) and **Revert**. Agent: `editor.import_settings_open`,
  `_edit`, `_apply`, `_revert` and `_state`. (ADR-7469)
- Content ▸ Import settings ▸ Apply and Show ▸ Build navigation preview run as cancellable editor jobs with progress
  (`import_settings:<n>`, `nav_preview:<n>`), with Cancel, `editor.cancel_job` and an `async` parameter on
  `editor.import_settings_apply`, `editor.set_import_settings` and `editor.nav_preview`. (ADR-7664)
- The Asset Viewer's mesh view toggles **UV0**, **UV1**, **Normals** and **Collision** overlays (`editor.view_asset {
  overlay }`, `info.overlays`) (ADR-7469)
- A shared preview scene for the Material editor, mesh viewer and texture viewer (lighting preset, environment
  intensity, floor grid, turntable; textures on a lit sphere or plane), and mesh helpers **Add simple collision**,
  **Generate lightmap UV** and **Build nav preview here**. Agent: `editor.preview_scene`, `editor.mesh_helper`.
  (ADR-7771)
- Source lint runs as the editor job `content_lint`: parsing is off the editor thread, a result lands in Problems only
  when the source, settings and content hashes still match, threshold edits write `config/source_lint.ron`, and fix
  links are previews that never write the source. Saving or importing a `.gltf`/`.glb` queues one coalesced scan.
  (ADR-8391)
- `editor.project_icon_info`: the project's package icon, readable without the Assets permission; the Import settings
  state gains `invalid` and `can_apply`

#### Levels, prefabs and layers

- Several open levels, layer locks and level groups (ADR-7792, ADR-8128): open levels beside the host level, each with
  its own unsaved flag and Save; the active level receives new entities, pastes, drops and Create prefab; the Outliner
  lists each open level's roots under a heading row (` *` unsaved, `(active)`). A Lock toggle per layer keeps its
  members out of viewport picking. Agent: `editor.levels_open { op: list|open|close|save }`, `editor.active_level`,
  `editor.layer_lock`; `editor.outliner_rows { open_levels, close_levels }`.
- Prefab overrides window and Edit in place (ADR-7790, ADR-7835, ADR-8128): every override of the selected instance side
  by side with Apply and Revert, Apply all and Revert all, and Apply to the nested prefab, the outer prefab, the variant
  file or the variant chain's base (a batch is one undo step); Edit ▸ Edit prefab in place opens the prefab at the
  instance's transform with the rest of the level drawn dimmed and unpickable. Agent: `editor.prefab_overrides`,
  `editor.prefab_overrides_apply`, `editor.prefab_overrides_revert`, `editor.prefab_overrides_open`,
  `editor.prefab_edit_in_place`; `editor.prefab_apply_override { target }`.
- `editor.prefab_apply_override { entity, component, field? }`: the Inspector's Apply as a method, for prefabs and
  variants (ADR-7263)
- Outliner **Create prefab from selection…** (`editor.create_prefab { entities?, path }`): the selection becomes a new
  `.prefab.ron` and is replaced by one linked instance at the same world transforms, in one undo step (ADR-7468)
- Prefab break link (`editor.prefab_break_link { entity }`, one undo step) and layer delete (`editor.layer_delete {
  defs, layer }`, refused with `layer_in_use`, `layer_parent_invalid` or `layer_unknown`) (ADR-7203)
- Layers panel: *Layer definitions* with Bind/Unbind, *Create layer*, an *initial* state per layer and *Delete layer*
  (`editor.layer_create`, `editor.layer_bind`, `editor.layer_initial`); **Edit placed level** and an undoable **Break
  placed level** (`editor.break_placed { entity }`); prefab variants with *Create variant…*, Inspector override markers
  and *Clear override* (ADR-7614)
- `EditorOnly` (`regia_editor::editor_only`) keeps marked entities and `GltfEditorOnly` hierarchies out of saved levels,
  autosaves and glTF exports; a game panic's backtrace stays in the Output Log and in
  `editor.play_state.panic_backtrace` (ADR-7205)

#### Play in editor, builds and launch

- Play-in-editor modes: Simulate (`editor.play { mode: "simulate" }`: physics, AI and game systems with no possessed
  player), split-screen players (`editor.play { players: 2..8 }`, with `players` and `viewports` in `editor.play_state`)
  and Keep simulation changes (`editor.keep_simulation_changes { guids? }`, one undo step). With `editor.play { players:
  2 }` or more, the play window and a possessed pane composite one pane per local player in the player manager's layout;
  PIE adds a stand-in camera per player only while the game has no window camera of its own, and follows runtime joins
  and leaves, and `editor.play_state.play_window` reports `players` and `panes`. Clicking a possessed view captures the
  cursor; Shift+F1 releases it. Page `book/agents/pie-modes.md`. (ADR-7205, ADR-7395, ADR-7466)
- Play in a separate window (`editor.play { window: true }`, toolbar **⧉ Play in window**): the play world's game camera
  in a second OS window on the editor's shared device. Its input goes to the play world only, a click captures the mouse
  and **Shift+F1** or focus loss releases it, closing it stops play, and it reopens at the last size and (off Wayland)
  place. `editor.play_state.play_window` reports it (ADR-7272)
- Play resolution for the play window: the window's own size, `hd_720`, `hd_1080`, phone and tablet presets in portrait
  and landscape, or a custom size. The play world renders at that size, letterboxed and centered; the game's primary
  window reports the chosen size, cursor positions map back to play pixels, and the **⛶** toolbar menu stores the choice
  in the editor preferences. `editor.play { window: true, preset }` or `{ window: true, size: [w, h] }` picks it for one
  session (ADR-7283)
- Play-in-editor options (ADR-7829): start from a player start, the editor camera or a chosen start; a Speed slider
  (0.1x to 4x) and Mute while playing; a play-mode border and toolbar tint (theme tokens, preference `play.tint`); a
  Server / Client N view switch for PIE client worlds; device simulation in the play window (phone and handheld presets,
  safe-area insets, rotation, touch from drags, pinch, a virtual gamepad) with the device outline, cutout, an optional
  safe-area outline and labeled pad buttons drawn over the picture and operable from the keyboard. Agent: `editor.play {
  start, entity, time_scale, mute, device }`, `editor.play_controls`, `editor.pie_view`.
- Game tabs for separate-process play (ADR-7831): a dedicated server or client process can show in its own Game dock tab
  (about 15 pictures a second) with Pause, Resume, Step one frame, Slower, Faster, Mute, Pop out and Stop process; keys
  go to that process while the picture has focus (Shift+F1 gives them back). Agent: `editor.pie_process_view`.
- Several-client play-in-editor worlds keep the backtrace of a client's panic (`PieClientWorld::panic_backtrace`) and
  log it with the message.
- Cancellable editor jobs and builds: `editor.import { async: true }` answers `{ job: "import:<n>" }`, and
  `editor.cancel_job { id }` stops an import (copied files removed), an `agent.hot_reload` job or the game build; the
  game build and Content Browser imports have a Cancel button (`editor.cancel_task { id }`). A cancelled build kills
  cargo and keeps the loaded game library, and it kills its whole process tree: a process group on Unix and, new, a job
  object with `KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE` on Windows. A cancelled import stops before the next file. Play-in-editor panics log a
  backtrace with clickable `file:line` links in the Output Log. (ADR-7205, ADR-9770, ADR-7422)
- `agent_parity::PLAY_RESUME` (`play.resume` to `editor.resume`) for the play toolbar's Resume button, and
  `HotReloadHost::compile_task` (the running game build's cancel token and elapsed seconds) (ADR-7381)
- `regia_editor::play_input::frame_event`: the capture event one editor frame implies, used by the windowed editor's
  frame loop (ADR-7313)
- Launch the game outside play-in-editor from the Output Log's launch bar: **Launch standalone** cooks for the host,
  builds the launcher and starts it in a play copy of the open level (unsaved edits included); **Launch on Android
  device** packages a dev APK for the device's ABI, installs and starts it with `adb` and streams its logcat into the
  Output Log. Stop kills the whole process tree. Agent: `editor.launch { target, device?, wait?, timeout_ms? }`,
  `editor.launch_status`, `editor.launch_stop`, `editor.launch_devices` (need the `build` permission). (ADR-8980)
- Editor launch: an Android launch whose `[project] id` is not an Android package name is refused before any adb call
  (`launch_bad_app_id`), and `editor.launch` answers `pending` while the launch has neither run nor ended. New
  `regia_platform::child_bind::{ChildGroup::spawn, Spawned, kill_process_group}` and
  `regia_editor::launch::WAIT_BOUND_MS` (1000). (ADR-7548)
- Device iteration: while a **Launch standalone** game runs, every asset the live cooker rebuilds is pushed into it and
  swapped in place (`editor.launch_status` `pushed`, `push_failed`, `push_error`); with an Android device chosen, a
  **Device screen** row mirrors the screen through adb and forwards taps (`editor.device_mirror`, `editor.launch {
  mirror }`). (ADR-7836)
- `editor.launch_status.agent`: the forwarded host URL of an Android launch's agent server while its device transport is
  open (ADR-8594, ADR-8631)
- Launch Profiles (target, build profile, cook mode, package step, device, start level, arguments), Window ▸ Platforms
  (doctor checks as an editor job, with a launch-bar warning) and Window ▸ Size Report (bundle contents by kind and pak
  payload). Agent: `editor.launch_profiles`, `editor.platform_check`, `editor.package_size_report`, `editor.launch {
  profile }`. (ADR-7843)

#### Menus, search, shortcuts and settings

- Universal search (ADR-7766): Ctrl+T searches assets, level entities, palette commands and menu items, preferences,
  project settings keys and agent methods, grouped by provider (`a:` `e:` `c:` `s:` `m:` limit them). Up and Down
  preview without an undo entry, Enter opens, Escape restores the selection and camera. Query tokens Tab-complete here
  and in the Content Browser's search; saved searches are personal or shared in `config/saved_searches.ron`. Every
  palette row shows its menu path, every menu item is a palette entry, and F4 pins a row to the favorites popup
  (Ctrl+Shift+F). Alt+Left and Alt+Right walk the last 50 selections. Agent: `editor.search`,
  `editor.saved_search_list`, `editor.saved_search_save`, `editor.saved_search_delete`, `editor.selection_history`;
  `editor.command` takes `pin`.
- An asset result in the universal search previews its Content Browser thumbnail.
- Scoped shortcuts, keymap presets and contextual help (ADR-7767): a user shortcut has a scope (global, viewport,
  outliner, inspector, content, graph canvas, timeline or text field), and the focused panel's scope wins; Escape,
  Ctrl+S, Ctrl+Z, Ctrl+Shift+Z and Ctrl+Y stay global. The Keymap tab finds every command a pressed chord runs and draws
  a keyboard map of bound, unbound and conflicting keys. Named keymaps live in `<user
  config>/regia/keymaps/<name>.editor_keymap.ron` beside the built-in Default and Laptop. Rich tooltips (description,
  default, shortcut, agent route) cover menu items, palette entries, Keymap rows and preferences; F1 (`help.context`)
  opens the book page of the focused panel or the component section of a hovered or focused Inspector field. Agent:
  `editor.keymap_lookup`, `editor.keymap_preset_list`, `editor.keymap_preset_apply`, `editor.keymap_preset_save`,
  `editor.help_context`.
- Settings window (ADR-7767, ADR-8196): Preferences, Keymap and Project Settings tabs share one search box; changed
  values are marked; project settings show their documented defaults with a per-key Revert and a category Reset, each
  saved action one undo step; preferences export and import (merge or replace). Agent: `editor.prefs_export`,
  `editor.prefs_import`.
- The editor's own files are frozen formats checked by `cargo xtask check-formats` and listed by `regia
  upgrade-project`: `editor-prefs`, `editor-keymap`, `editor-layout`, `editor-window-state`, `inspector-pins`,
  `editor-profile`, `editor-view-state`, `viewport-snap`, `saved-searches` and `editor-theme`, with fuzz targets for
  each. (ADR-7902)
- Tools, Play and Help menus (Rebuild asset database, Clear caches…, Play mirrors the toolbar and adds Players ▸ 1–4,
  Documentation, Agent guide, About), **File ▸ Package for platform…** (Linux, Windows or Android, as a cancellable
  editor job) and **Project Settings ▸ Icon**. Agent: `editor.menu_list` (what the menus draw, pinned by a test),
  `editor.package_start`, `editor.package_status`, `editor.clear_caches`, `editor.rebuild_asset_db`, `editor.about`,
  `editor.help_open`, `editor.project_icon`; a package run is the job `package:<n>`. (ADR-7475, ADR-7496)
- **Tools ▸ Open code editor** (`editor.open_code_editor`) runs the configured code editor (the `code.editor_command`
  preference, `[editor] code_editor` in `.regia/agent.toml`, `$VISUAL` or `$EDITOR`) on the game crate's manifest
- File ▸ New script… wizard (`empty` or `component` template; `editor.create_script` takes `template`,
  `editor.script_wizard` shows it) and a project-browser template picker: Blank, First Person, Third Person, Top Down
  (ADR-7234)
- Project browser, level templates and samples (ADR-7856, ADR-8190): recent projects show a thumbnail (the manifest
  icon, `icon.png` or a viewport capture saved on close and on Save all), name, path, last-opened date, engine version
  and tags, with a `tag:` search. File ▸ New level offers Empty, Basic lit, Interior, Open world and every `.level.ron`
  under `templates/levels/`. Help ▸ Samples… (`help.samples`) copies a project template, an engine sample or an example
  extension into a chosen folder and opens it; extensions list their `samples/` folders in the Extensions panel. Agent:
  `editor.level_templates`, `editor.new_level { template?, discard? }`, `editor.recent_projects_edit`,
  `editor.recent_projects { query, tag }`, `editor.samples`.
- Project Settings pages for Localization, Packaging (`[platforms.android]` SDK levels, orientation, ABIs, permissions,
  signing references; `[platforms.linux] formats`) and Audio buses; `editor.project_localization_edit`,
  `editor.project_packaging_edit` and `pages` in `editor.project_settings_get` (ADR-7234)
- Project Settings authors closure content: **Content: all / closure** (`[cook.chunk_options] content`), an **Entry
  roots** list per chunk rule, and **Preview chunk assignment**, which computes on a worker what the next cook would
  ship from the unsaved settings (stale previews are discarded, quick requests batched). Saves with closure mode or
  entry roots resolve every root against the cooked registry first, and every content-chunk settings save is one undo
  entry that restores `Regia.toml` byte for byte. The Content Chunks panel pages and filters the unshipped list.
  (ADR-8543)
- Project Settings ▸ Packaging edits package-hygiene rules, allowances and scan flags with a preview, one undo entry per
  save and ADR-8247 definition digests. **Audit** runs `regia package --audit-dir` as job `hygiene_audit:<n>` (no
  package built), copies at most 128 scrubbed findings into Problems and emits one `hygiene_review` event per finished
  run. (ADR-8399)

#### Undo, autosave and history

- Undo History and recovery startup: Window ▸ Undo History lists the undo and redo entries with the documents each
  touched and jumps to any of them from the keyboard. Agent transactions group undoable script edits (a disconnect
  commits an abandoned group and logs it). Safe startup skips project code, restores and agent autostart; two unclean
  exits in a row start in recovery mode (`--safe-mode`, `--normal-mode`). Agent: `editor.history_jump`,
  `editor.transaction_begin`, `editor.transaction_commit`, `editor.transaction_abort`, `editor.safe_mode_state`,
  `editor.safe_mode_restart`; `agent.guide` topic `undo`.
- One undo history for the asset editors (ADR-8365): Biome, Ragdoll, Material, theme, UI Animation, UI designer, trailer
  and aircraft edits, table and vehicle documents and hosted edit-world `scene.*` calls (including `scene.season` and
  `scene.possess`) are entries of the editor's shared history, stepped by Ctrl+Z, `editor.undo` and
  `editor.history_jump` in order with level edits. A drag is one entry; the History panel names each edit's file and
  field. Autosave also covers a table or `.vehicle.ron` opened behind the one its panel shows
  (`DocAutosaveRegistry::register_retained`).
- Document autosave and recovery: material graphs, UI designer, UI Animation editor (kind `ui_anim`), procedural graph,
  behavior and state trees, Tables, sound cue, VFX, conversation and anim graph documents with unsaved work are
  snapshotted to `.regia/autosave/docs/` on a worker thread, and the Crash recovery window offers each back with Restore
  and Discard (`editor.autosave_state`, `editor.autosave_now`, `editor.recovery_list`, `editor.recovery_restore`,
  `editor.recovery_discard`). VFX autosave keeps the disabled-module stash, so a recovered module comes back disabled
  with its values. (ADR-9770, ADR-7340)
- Document autosave for sequences (kind `sequence`), prefab edit mode (kind `prefab`) and shot lists (kind `shots`):
  Restore reopens each at its original path, dirty, byte-identical. `editor.seq_curve_state` reports `path` and `dirty`,
  and `editor.prefab_mode` reports `dirty`. (ADR-7382, ADR-8730)
- Montage panel: a warp form for every spec 34 M2 field it can send, and an undo journal covering slot-table edits
  (`editor.montage_panel_slot_edit`). The Abilities asset editor has Undo and Redo (`editor.abilities_panel_asset`
  `undo`/`redo`). Document autosave and crash recovery for the cloth panel, Ragdoll editor, Vehicle Tuning, the
  Abilities asset editor and the Localization edit box. (ADR-7731)
- Incremental level autosave: a tick reflects only the entities whose saved components changed (one entity after one
  edit on a 20 000-entity level) and writes bytes identical to a full snapshot; `editor.autosave_state` reports
  `level_snapshot { reflected, reused, full }`. (ADR-7748)
- `doc_autosave::is_valid_doc_id` and `AutosaveWorker::ticks_in_flight` (ADR-7345)
- File ▸ Version history… keeps autosaved and saved versions in `.regia/history/` with Preview and Restore (an undoable
  change), capped by `history.keep_autosaves`, `history.keep_saves` and `history.max_mb`; File ▸ Duplicate project…
  copies the source files. Agent: `editor.versions`, `editor.version_preview`, `editor.version_restore`,
  `editor.duplicate_project`. (ADR-7928)

#### Material graphs

- Material graph editor, Stage 2: the graph preview and the Material panel draw on a sphere, cube, plane or a project
  `.glb`/`.gltf` (first part, at most 250 000 vertices) with drag-to-orbit and a light slider; a typed node inspector
  (drag values, color pickers, dropdowns, a texture picker with thumbnails, a function picker for `Call`, RON as an
  Advanced row); fuzzy node search (Space or Tab), dropping a wire into empty space opens the search filtered to kinds
  that take it; copy, cut and paste within and across tabs and through the system clipboard; Shift+drag box select,
  align and distribute; comment frames and reroute points; per-node preview swatches cached by pipeline key; **Apply to
  selection** as one undo step; Content ▸ New makes material graphs and functions. Agent: `editor.matgraph_node_fields`,
  `editor.matgraph_node_set`, `editor.matgraph_search`, `editor.matgraph_clipboard`, `editor.matgraph_arrange`,
  `editor.matgraph_annotate`, `editor.matgraph_node_preview`, `editor.matgraph_apply_to_selection`,
  `editor.material_preview`; `editor.matgraph_preview` takes `shape`, `mesh`, `yaw`, `pitch`, `light_yaw`. (ADR-9700)
- Material graph editor (ADR-7851, ADR-8187, ADR-9970): a Parameters panel of typed rows grouped by category with ranges
  (sliders), tooltips and reordering; instance reparenting and a Hierarchy section; shader stats per render profile
  (instructions, samples, bindings, samplers); a Tier picker on the preview; Find usages, Preview this node and
  Disconnect all; the shared preview scene controls; rerouted wires drawn through their points and comment frames moved
  and resized on the canvas. Agent: `editor.matgraph_params { op: list | set | reorder | remove | meta }`,
  `editor.matgraph_instance_reparent`, `editor.matgraph_hierarchy`, `editor.matgraph_cost`,
  `editor.matgraph_find_usages`, `editor.preview_scene { editor: "matgraph" }`; `editor.matgraph_preview { profile, node
  }`, `editor.matgraph_edit { op: "disconnect_all" }`.
- Material graph function tabs preview on their own (the calling graph drawn with the tab's definition, else the first
  output unlit), a click on a wire adds a reroute point and a right-click on a rerouted wire disconnects it, a read-only
  **WGSL** view with Copy, and a **Function users** list of the graphs that call the open function. (ADR-7730)
- Material graph Code node view (ADR-7852): selecting a Code node shows its inputs, output and texture rows and a
  monospace code view with line numbers, keyword colors, bracket matching, Find, an error list, Apply (Ctrl+Enter) and
  completion from the inputs, include files and WGSL built-ins. Agent: `editor.matgraph_code_set`,
  `editor.matgraph_code_errors`.
- Content Browser **New ▸ Material instance** (`editor.create_asset { kind: "material_instance", parent }`), and
  `editor.matgraph_find`, the read-only twin of `editor.matgraph_search` for agents without `edit_scene`
- A shared graph toolkit, `regia_editor::graph_kit` (ADR-8567): the material, procedural and logic graph panels share
  node search, comment frames, reroutes, error badges, the node preview slot and canvas framing.

#### Logic graphs and AI

- Logic Graph panel (`Panel::LogicGraph`): an event → condition → action node canvas for `.logic.ron` with a palette,
  typed wires, an inspector, the issues list, one undo step per gesture, and while the game plays the watched graph's
  rules colored by what they did with pause, resume and step. `editor.logic_edit` takes `control: "pause" | "resume" |
  "step"` and drives the watched play-world logic graph as the panel's live view buttons do (refusal `bad_control`).
  Node positions live in an editor-only `x.logic.layout.ron` sidecar; saving during play reloads the play world's
  graphs. Agent methods `editor.logic_open`, `editor.logic_state`, `editor.logic_edit`, `editor.logic_save`,
  `editor.logic_validate`. (ADR-7224, ADR-7774)
- Logic Graph panel debugger (ADR-8021, ADR-8565): rule and event breakpoints (F9; per user in
  `.regia/logic_breakpoints.ron`, development builds only) filtered to the picked instance, Step to the next fired rule,
  a Watch list with Live values and an evaluate box, pulsing wires of fired rules, fuzzy palette and dropped-wire
  search, a Variables list with renames that rewrite every reference, and a Usages tab that finds every graph, animation
  graph, logic action and code line using an event, key or animation parameter. Agent: `editor.logic_palette_search`,
  `editor.logic_variables`, `editor.logic_variable_set`, `editor.logic_variable_remove`, `editor.logic_breakpoint_set`,
  `editor.logic_breakpoint_list`, `editor.logic_watch`, `editor.logic_evaluate`, `editor.logic_goto_node`,
  `editor.logic_find_usages`; `editor.logic_edit { control: "step_to_fire" }`.
- Logic Graph keyboard route: a **Nodes** list beside the canvas (Tab, Up/Down, Enter frames, Ctrl+Shift+C wires,
  Ctrl+Shift+A adds, Alt+arrow moves, Delete removes), each key issuing the matching `editor.logic_edit` op (ADR-8628)
- Viewport Show rows for seven AI debug layers (behavior tree, state tree, GOAP, squad, formation, MoveTo, blackboard);
  turning one on enables the `ai_debug` gizmo group (ADR-8463)
- The `regia_ai::MoveTo` leaf appears in the behavior-tree, state-tree and logic-graph palettes (ADR-8293)
- Smart Object Definition panel for `.smartobject.ron` (tags, rules, queries, slots, entrances; validator messages;
  undo; autosave) and viewport slot handles. Agent: `editor.smart_object_def_state`, `editor.smart_object_def_edit`,
  `editor.smart_object_slot_handles`, `editor.smart_object_slot_handle_drag`.
- Show ▸ **Fog of war**: watcher sight circles, the grid outline, the fog texture and a viewer team picker
  (`regia_editor::fog_debug`, ADR-8523)

#### Procedural, terrain and world

- Procedural viewport tool mode for all four kinds: Paint strokes stamped in the background under a 2048-ray budget with
  a seeded random yaw per point, Box footprints that write a box `Shape`, Path and Area (Area on a biome preset writes a
  `BiomeArea`). Each tool's hit filter persists in the editor preferences. The graph panel's Path and Area buttons arm
  the road and forest presets; click adds a point, drag moves one, Backspace drops the last, C closes a path, Enter
  commits one undo entry, Escape cancels. Spline control points and Free / Aligned tangent handles drag, one undo entry
  per release. The graph panel's node menu collapses a selection to a subgraph, expands or isolates a node, and the
  preview bar pins, rerolls or copies the seed and sets the preview tier. (ADR-7215, ADR-3480)
- Procedural preview bar (follow selection, tier, Isolate, Around view, Tiles, Seed, Pin) and a Plant preview with a
  six-seed variants strip; agents `editor.procgen_tool`, `editor.plant_preview`, `editor.procgen_preview { follow }`.
  Four new starter presets ship (six in all, with `starter/tool_fence.pgraph.ron`). (ADR-7215, ADR-7239)
- Lit plant thumbnails: with a render device, the plant preview and its variants strip draw each mesh variant with the
  batch thumbnail renderer instead of skeleton strokes (`editor.plant_preview { lit: true }`) (ADR-7275)
- Procedural graph panel: a Parameters tab (declare, rename with every `Bind` following, reorder, retype, default,
  range, doc; variant overrides), typed setting widgets (drag fields, curve editor, asset picker, name lists),
  data-table pin tabs, filter, pager and sortable headers, theme pin colors (`data_kinds`) and node badges, Promote to
  parameter with a name, and a Collapse boundary check. Agents `editor.procgen_widgets`, `editor.procgen_badges` and
  `editor.procgen_promote`; `editor.procgen_param` takes `declare`, `rename`, `index`, `ty`, `min`, `max`, `doc`;
  `editor.procgen_table` reads the panel's view and takes `toggle_sort`; new reason `procgen_param_range` (ADR-7239)
- Procgen Inspect: a `(tier, i, j)` cell picker (`editor.procgen_table { cell }`), row hover that highlights the element
  in the viewport, and a biome filter tooltip. `editor.procgen_param { name, remove: true }` removes a parameter and
  resets its bound settings in one undo entry. (ADR-7452)
- Biome overlay views (claim tint, blend bands, entry footprints, filtered candidates) through `editor.procgen_view_set`
  and `editor.procgen_biome_overlay { at?, radius_m? }` (ADR-7238)
- Biome editor (Window ▸ Biome): typed forms for `.biome.ron` layers, entries, filters, footprints and grass
  multipliers; a refused edit changes nothing and shows its code; renames follow references; 128 undo steps; it refuses
  to open over unsaved edits until **Discard edits and open** is clicked. Agent: `editor.biome_panel_open` (`discard`),
  `editor.biome_panel_get`, `editor.biome_panel_edit`, `editor.biome_panel_save`. The procedural **Area** tool picks its
  biome from a drop-down, and tier-neutral nodes show `~` before their tier's cell size. (ADR-7743, ADR-7793)
- Terrain ▸ New terrain… (and Content ▸ New ▸ Terrain…) creates, cooks in the background and loads a flat terrain or one
  imported from a 16-bit PNG or RAW heightmap (`editor.terrain_create`, cancellable as `terrain_create:<n>`). (ADR-7727)
- Terrain panel ▸ **Import source**: pick a schema-2 JSON or 16-bit PNG count source (optional `heightfield.json`
  mapping), preview it in the background with progress and **Cancel**, read the mapping verdict and changed samples,
  then **Apply** or **Reimport** the recorded source later. An import over the undo limit must be acknowledged and first
  writes the original heights as a 16-bit PNG backup. Tile sets show `reading source tile N of M` and cancel between
  tiles. (ADR-8541, ADR-8595)
- Terrain panel ▸ **Overview** runs a registered overview bake as job `terrain_overview:<n>` (ADR-8596)
- World Map panel (ADR-7802): a top-down map of a streamed level's cells from its cooked world index (content height,
  loaded cells, damaged cells, the camera's view cone) with click, box and keyboard selection, Load selected, Unload
  selected (asking about unsaved edits) and Load around camera as a cancelable job. Agent: `editor.world_open`,
  `editor.world_cells`, `editor.world_load`, `editor.world_unload`.
- 2D authoring: Content ▸ New ▸ Tile map, tileset and layer binding edits, an auto-tile rule editor with a preview
  painted by the runtime's auto-tiler, and keyboard palette and map-cursor painting. Agent: `editor.tilemap_new`,
  `editor.tileset_candidates`, `editor.tilemap_tilesets`, `editor.autotile_get`, `_open`, `_new`, `_edit`, `_save`,
  `_preview`, `_select`. (ADR-7742)

#### Animation and sequencer

- Animation graph editor node kinds **Chain IK**, **Body Aim**, **Stride Warp** and **Orientation Warp** with joint
  pickers, inspectors and undo (ADR-8475, ADR-8540)
- Animation tools: live parameter writes in play, a record-and-scrub animation debugger, clip preview speed, loop modes,
  a preview range, onion skins and a root-motion floor, clip search and sort, and multi-curve views. Agent:
  `editor.anim_param { live_value }`, `editor.anim_record`, `editor.anim_scrub`, `editor.anim_clip { op: "preview" |
  "list" }`, `editor.curve_view`. (ADR-7800)
- Retarget panel (ADR-7801): pick source and target rigs (`.glb`/`.gltf`, `.bvh`, the demo biped), auto-map by body
  chain then name, edit the two-column map and per-chain rest offsets, align to a T-pose or A-pose, preview a clip on
  both rigs, save a `.retarget.ron` and bake clips to `.cooked` files in a cancelable job. Agent:
  `editor.retarget_open`, `editor.retarget_auto_map`, `editor.retarget_map_set`, `editor.retarget_preview`,
  `editor.retarget_save`, `editor.retarget_batch`.
- Retarget panel: an IK goals list (role pickers, mode, weight, feet and hand presets, per-goal residual and slide) and
  a **Preview with goals** toggle; the panel saves `.retarget.ron` v2 (ADR-8476)
- Motion database window for `.motiondb.ron`: schema, tags, clips, a span timeline per clip with keyboard edits, a pose
  preview with the frame's feature vector, a live background rebuild and a cancellable **Rebuild** job, undo, autosave.
  Agent: `editor.mm_db_open`, `editor.mm_db_state`, `editor.mm_db_edit`, `editor.mm_db_save`, `editor.mm_db_rebuild`,
  `editor.mm_db_scrub`. (ADR-7740, ADR-7848)
- Montage panel: warp and blend forms, section and follow links, a slot field for a second slot track and a slot table
  editor; State Tree Editor: tree fields, ambient tasks, includes and utility scores. Each control sends the agent's own
  edit as one undo step.
- Sequences and shot lists are documents: Content Browser **New ▸ Sequence** and **New ▸ Shot list**
  (`editor.create_asset` kinds `sequence` and `shot_list`), a Shots panel path row with **Open**, **Save** (Ctrl+S /
  Cmd+S over the panel) and an unsaved mark, and **Edit player's shots**, which adopts a `SequencePlayer`'s own list as
  an unsaved document. Agent: `editor.shot_panel_open { path, discard? }`, `editor.shot_panel_save { path? }`,
  `editor.shot_panel_adopt { player?, discard? }`. (ADR-8730, ADR-8940)
- Event tracks (`.seq.ron` v6) are dope-sheet rows in the sequencer timeline: their keys are drawn, box-selected, moved,
  scaled, deleted, copied, pasted and rippled with the other keys (ADR-8628)
- Sequencer timeline **Ripple** edits: with `ripple` on (`CutEdit::{Add, Move, Trim}`, `editor.seq_cut_edit { ripple?
  }`, a toolbar checkbox), a cut move shifts every later cut and the sequence's later keys and markers by the same delta
  in one undo entry. Clip, audio, sub-sequence and video sections are dope-sheet rows whose start and end marks
  box-select and drag with key rows (`editor.seq_keys` takes `{ track, index, end }` refs). (ADR-8640)
- `editor.seq_section_edit { op: "sub_binding", track, index, from, to }` and a **Bindings** row set one sub-sequence
  section's target renames, one undo entry per call
- The sequencer's auto key runs once per frame from the shell, so a gizmo move or Inspector edit on a bound entity is
  keyed with the Sequence Curves panel closed (`seq_tracks::auto_key_frame`) (ADR-8730)
- The edit-world sequence preview (**Preview in level**, `editor.seq_preview`) plays sound sections on the editor's
  audio output (spatial sections at their emitter), shows video sections on a bound entity's video player with a
  frame-exact seek, and poses the rigs of clip sections, also rigs with an `Animator` (suspended, never written). Stop
  puts every joint and player back bit for bit. Results add `animators`, `sounds`, `videos` and `voices { started,
  stopped }`. (ADR-8730, ADR-8940)
- `ShotPlayer::load_count` (counts `load` and `clear`, not `replace_sequence`) and
  `regia_agent::seeing_overlays::sensor_segments` (the outlines of sensor colliders only) (ADR-7495)

#### Physics, vehicles and destruction

- Physics layer matrix editor (Edit ▸ Project settings… ▸ Physics layers): collision and query matrices, add layer,
  remove last layer; `editor.physics_layers_get` and `editor.physics_layers_edit` (all or nothing, undoable). Page
  `book/agents/physics-layers.md`. (ADR-7233)
- Vehicle Tuning panel for `.vehicle.ron` v4: typed sections, a draggable torque curve, plots from the runtime's own
  math, live **Apply** to a play car without a respawn, recorded proving-ground runs side by side, comment-keeping
  atomic saves; trailers (`.trailer.ron`, towed on the proving ground) and aircraft (`.aircraft.ron`, **Fly** trims and
  flies them); proving tracks (flat, ramp, slope, bump strip). Agent: `editor.vehicle_tuning_open`, `_state`, `_set`,
  `_undo`, `_redo`, `_apply`, `_save`, `_run`, `_fly`. (ADR-9760, ADR-7745)
- Vehicle Tuning opens bikes (`.bike.ron`) and hover hulls (`.hoverhull.ron`) beside the car, with Open, New, Save, Undo
  and Redo, and **Ride 1 s** / **Respawn** ride the open document on the flat proving ground, reading edits every
  step (`editor.vehicle_tuning_ride`) (ADR-8630)
- Fracture panel: support chunks (a `support_below_y` plane stored in the `.meta`, viewport picks or a box), a test
  break against the saved cook's break model with the same `structure_hash` a game would get, a bond view colored by
  break impulse, grid and explicit sites, and **Place**, which spawns a `DestructibleSource` into the level
  (`editor.fracture_test_hit`, `editor.fracture_test_reset`, `editor.fracture_bonds`, `editor.fracture_place`)
  (ADR-9750)
- Fracture panel **Run debris** replays test hits with the destruction bridge and draws where each chunk comes to rest
  (`editor.fracture_test_debris`). (ADR-7745)
- Fracture panel *Impact sites* and *Clustered sites* rows (`editor.fracture_panel_set { impact, clustered }`)
  (ADR-9870)
- `FracturePanel::clamp_clusters` keeps the clustered clump count within the chunk count (ADR-7550)

#### Asset editors

- Data Asset panel: a `.data.ron` value drawn with the inspector's reflection widgets, one undo step per edit, atomic
  Save, opened by double-click. Agent methods `editor.data_asset_types`, `editor.data_asset_open`,
  `editor.data_asset_set` and `editor.data_asset_save` (ADR-7234)
- `editor.data_asset_open { path, discard: true }`: the Data Asset panel's **Revert** through the agent (ADR-7452)
- Bulk Edit panel: a spreadsheet of selected entities or a folder's data assets by one type's fields, with sort, filter,
  pinned columns, TSV copy and paste, fill-down, one undo step per range commit (checked cell by cell first), and
  10 000-row virtualization. Agent: `editor.bulk_edit_open`, `_set`, `_state`, `_view`, `_save`. (ADR-7787)
- Camera rig editor: typed forms for every `.camera.ron` node kind and transition, add, remove, duplicate, reorder and
  rename of nodes, rigs and transitions (a rig still in use is not removed: `camera_panel_rig_in_use`), one undo history
  per asset, Save/Ctrl+S, document autosave, and `editor.camera_panel_edit` with 22 ops. (ADR-7734)
- Tables panel: the Chooser tab adds and removes rows and columns, edits row results and sets table options
  (`editor.tables_panel_chooser_edit` ops `remove_row`, `remove_column`, `set_options`); the Curves tab has **Add
  curve**, **Rename** and **Delete curve**; the Cue Editor edits the caption and low-pass filter (`editor.cue_edit { op:
  "set", caption, filter_cutoff_hz, filter_offset_octaves }`) (ADR-7605)
- Form parity: the Montage panel's slot table gets a **Set slot** row (`anim.montage_slot_edit` `set`), the state tree's
  **List under** takes an index, the Tables panel adds `FloatDistance` columns and catch-all outputs, the ability form
  adds list entries and enum variant pickers, and a banner warns before the first typed edit drops an ability asset's
  RON comments (`asset.comments_warning`, `comments_dropped`).
- Abilities inspector: typed forms for `.attrs.ron`, `.effect.ron` and `.ability.ron` with the raw RON behind
  **Advanced**, **Add attributes** and **Apply effect** on the inspected entity (`editor.abilities_panel_call` ops
  `add_attributes`, `apply_effect`), and Content Browser **New ▸ Attribute set, Effect, Ability** (ADR-7472, ADR-7604)
- UI designer (ADR-7827, ADR-7858, ADR-8252): split, foldable and tabs presets, a Markup box with a preview, a
  Navigation section with Pick and a Show navigation overlay, a Variant row (Base, each variant, Add portrait, Add
  landscape) with an Overrides list and Reset, an anchor preset grid with keep-position, preview scale controls and
  margin and padding bands, Disable subtree, Pivot and Screen back rows, and a Project Settings UI-scale curve and scale
  rule. Play mode runs the samples through a private runtime UI with input and focus; the Style section shows winning
  and losing style sources. Agent: `editor.ui_designer_variant`, `editor.ui_designer_style_sources`,
  `editor.ui_scale_settings`; `editor.ui_designer_preview { interactive, input }`.
- UI designer: typed rows for a text input's `max_chars`, `secret`, `read_only` and `filter` and an image *fit* combo,
  and group drag: dragging one of several selected nodes moves them all with group snapping in one undo step
  (`editor.ui_designer_edit { op: "transform", nodes?, delta, snap? }`) (ADR-9980)
- Theme panel: a runtime-rendered widget and state gallery with click-to-style, type and state property editing,
  variation chains, highlighted overrides with reset, pinned-state copies and selective imports from built-in themes,
  files or editor palette roles, one undo step each. Agent: `editor.theme_panel_import`; new `editor.theme_panel_set`
  and `editor.theme_panel_state` fields.
- Localization and tables (ADR-7855): **Compile** in the Localization panel writes the same shipping bundles as `regia
  loc compile`, with findings linked to their file and line; a translator-context column edits `# note:` comments,
  carried into PO and XLIFF; a Preview language picker in the main toolbar switches the edit viewport and play
  (**Source** resets both); the Tables panel imports and exports CSV and JSON for data and curve tables. Agent:
  `editor.loc_compile`, `editor.loc_set_comment`, `editor.preview_locale`, `editor.tables_panel_import`,
  `editor.tables_panel_export`.
- VFX editor and debug drawing: effect overview cards with preview enable and solo, spawn summaries and costs, an
  emitter timeline with keyboard scrubbing, project debug colors, and navigation path gizmos. Agent:
  `editor.debug_colors`; `editor.vfx_edit` ops `preview_emitter` and `scrub`; `editor.vfx_state { bounds_time }`;
  `editor.set_viewport { paths }`.
- Utility panels (ADR-8189): an editor dock tab built from a `.ui.ron` document and a logic graph or Lua script named by
  a `.editorpanel.ron` sidecar, listed under Window ▸ Utility Panels; its commands run `read` and `edit_scene`
  `editor.*` methods and registered actions as one undo step. A starter "Rename selection with prefix" panel ships.
  Agent: `editor.utility_panels`.

#### Profiler and debugging

- Profiler: a Source picker (edit world, in-process play, a separate-process PIE `server` or `client <k>`, or a dev
  build at `ws://127.0.0.1:<port>` with its agent token, read on a worker thread and retried on disconnect); a
  **History** tab (300 frames, hitches over twice the median and at least 4 ms with their five costliest spans,
  click to freeze); an **Entities** tab with the 20 most-held components; a **Memory** tab from `memory.report`; a
  **Compare** tab for two `.rprof.json` captures. Process sources get every retained frame and the ranked component
  counts, not one sample per poll. Agent: `editor.profiler_state`, `editor.profiler_source { kind, address?, token? }`,
  `editor.profiler_capture_slot { slot, path? }`, `editor.profiler_freeze { frame? }` (the History tab's freeze click,
  hitch row and Unfreeze: an absent `frame` unfreezes, and an index out of range answers `profiler_bad_frame`).
  (ADR-9740, ADR-7849, ADR-7409)
- Profiler **Timeline** tab: one frame per thread, one row per depth, zoom about the pointer, drag pan, hover with name
  and duration, and click to hold a frame, for the edit world, play, a PIE process and a dev build by address. A
  **Frames** control (1 to 8, default 1) lays consecutive retained frames on one time axis with a boundary line and
  frame number at each frame start. Agent: `editor.profiler_timeline { frame?, frames?, zoom?, pan?, width?, max_bars?
  }`. (ADR-7325, ADR-7409)
- Profiler (ADR-8141, ADR-8142): Memory ▸ Categories and snapshots (heap and GPU current and peak per subsystem, named
  snapshots, diffs of categories, archetypes, components and asset kinds, JSON export to `captures/memory/`); Memory ▸
  GPU resources (every registered texture and allocation, largest first, with a live mip and channel preview); a % of
  frame toggle; Audio, Physics, UI and Rendering module tabs; a Monitors tab for any named counter; a Loads tab of
  per-asset read, decode and upload times; marks, level loads and hitches on the History graph and the timeline. Agent:
  `editor.memory_snapshot`, `editor.gpu_resources`, `editor.profiler_units`, `editor.profiler_modules`,
  `editor.profiler_monitors`, `editor.profiler_loads`, `editor.profiler_marks`.
- Profiler ▸ **Lockstep** tab: per-system tick-profile rows (p99 bar against the budget, p50/p99/max), the whole-tick
  row, over-budget ticks newest first and a toggle per deterministic debug-draw part; Viewport Show ▸ **Lockstep** draws
  play's frame (ADR-8562)
- Frame Debugger: capture the next frame, freeze the main viewport, list the render graph in order with per-pass
  metadata, and preview read-only color and depth copies of every built-in pass's `color`, `depth` and `output` targets.
  Agent: `editor.frame_capture { action, passes?, budget_bytes? }`, `editor.frame_pass { index }`; command
  `view.toggle_frame_debugger`. (ADR-7860)
- Frame Debugger draw records (ADR-8224): a captured frame lists its depth-prepass and forward draws under their pass
  (pipeline, mesh, material, object, entity, counts, indirect, target); Previous draw and Next draw select the entity;
  with a play window open the debugger captures its next frame. Agent: `editor.frame_draw`; `editor.frame_capture {
  source: "play_window" }`.
- Selected Object Debug draws the selection's perception, state path, claims, navigation, abilities and effects in the
  viewport, and a Visual Log records bounded per-entity line and text entries with a keyboard timeline
  (`editor.debug_overlay`, `editor.visual_log`).
- Problems, Output Log and Project Health (ADR-7789, ADR-8187): Group by source, Collapse duplicates, Copy all and Copy
  selected, entity GUID links that select and frame; the open material graphs' compile errors and the open logic graph's
  issues as rows that open the node. Tools ▸ Project Health ranks validator findings, budget overruns, missing
  references, large unreferenced assets, cook warnings, `regia doctor` findings and autosave leftovers with an action
  each. Agent: `editor.project_health { action: run | state | open }`, `editor.precommit_hook`.

#### Collaboration, revision control and agents

- Revision control through git's command-line program (ADR-7837, ADR-8126): status letters on Content Browser rows and
  tiles, the open level in the Outliner's Revision column, the branch and changed-file count in the status bar, and a
  Revision Control window (Staged and Changes, Stage, Unstage, Discard, a text or structural side-by-side diff of RON
  assets, Commit after validating the staged assets, History, branch picker). No git process runs per frame. Agent:
  `editor.vcs_status`, `editor.vcs_stage`, `editor.vcs_unstage`, `editor.vcs_discard`, `editor.vcs_commit`,
  `editor.vcs_diff`, `editor.vcs_log`, `editor.vcs_branch`.
- git LFS file locks (ADR-7864): the first edit of a `lockable` file asks to lock it (`vcs.lock_on_edit`), Content
  Browser rows show lock badges (and read-only badges with `vcs.lockable_read_only`), saves over another user's lock are
  refused with `locked_by_other` (Save anyway as a copy), and a File Locks window offers Refresh, Unlock and Unlock all
  mine. Agent: `editor.vcs_locks`, `editor.vcs_lock`, `editor.vcs_unlock`.
- Asset and graph diff against a git revision: **Diff against HEAD…** and **Diff against revision…** for `.ron` assets,
  side-by-side graphs (material graphs and functions, animation graphs, logic graphs) with a keyboard change list, and a
  field tree for other assets. Read-only. Agent: `editor.asset_diff`, `editor.asset_revisions`. (ADR-7799)
- Review notes and Activity (ADR-7841): notes saved with levels, placed at a surface or attached to content, with
  replies, resolve and reopen, filters, framing and viewport badges (N opens a draft); the Activity panel joins agent
  calls and undo commands in session order. Agent: `editor.note_add`, `editor.note_reply`, `editor.note_resolve`,
  `editor.note_list`, `editor.activity`.
- LAN collaboration presence (ADR-7842): opt-in editor sessions with explicit acceptance of each joiner, a Collaboration
  panel, participant cameras and selection indicators, Outliner participant dots and jump to a participant's view.
  Agent: `editor.collab_host`, `editor.collab_join`, `editor.collab_leave`, `editor.collab_participants`,
  `editor.collab_jump`.
- Remote Outliner and Inspector with a live link (ADR-7833, ADR-8374): a source switch shows the edit world, the play
  world, a standalone launch, separate-process play children, attached runtimes or an Android device game. The remote
  Outliner is a tree read 200 rows at a time; the remote Inspector edits numbers, text and enum fields of every selected
  entity as one transaction per gesture. The Live link (off by default) mirrors edit-world property sets, transform
  drags, spawns (under their parents) and deletes into the session by entity guid. Remote calls run on a thread per
  connection. Agent: `editor.remote_sources`, `editor.remote_select { collapse, expand }`, `editor.remote_set`,
  `editor.live_link`.
- Window ▸ Agent hosts explicitly configured external agent commands (owner-only preference `agents.commands`) with a
  keyboard-operable transcript, per-session modes and plan approval; each child gets a private token that Stop revokes.
  The editor contacts no service by itself. Agent: `editor.agent_sessions`. (ADR-7844)
- Agent panel Propose mode (ADR-8375): a hosted agent's live `editor.*` edits are staged with a preview of what they
