  touch; Approve all, Approve checked, Reject checked and Reject all run approved calls in order as one undo entry, all
  or none.

#### Extensions

- Editor extensions: `register_editor_action` adds entries to a new **Extensions** menu (and toolbar), and
  `register_asset_tool` adds tools for project assets by suffix; the editor also discovers `<engine>/extensions/`, and a
  project extension with the same id overrides the engine's. Example extension `extensions/hello_panel`; agent methods
  `editor.extension_ui`, `editor.extension_action`, `editor.extension_asset_tool`, `editor.extension_panel` (ADR-9850)
- Editor extensions can add commands with palette categories and default shortcuts, selection-aware menu entries
  (`SelectionPredicate`), typed user and project settings (`register_setting`), viewport tools
  (`register_viewport_tool`), viewport overlays (`register_overlay`) and build and launch hooks (`register_build_hook`).
  Agent: `editor.extension_shortcut`, `editor.extension_settings`, `editor.extension_tools`, `editor.extension_action {
  assets }`. (ADR-7791)

#### Agent parity and accessibility

- Agent parity: `editor.actions { panel?, class? }` lists every UI action and how an agent performs it; new palette
  commands for the shell menus, the crash-recovery window and the Material panel; `editor.prefab_override`,
  `editor.video_boot` and `editor.scatter_class` cover the Inspector's prefab Revert/Apply, the boot movie list and the
  Terrain panel's scatter classes; `agent.guide` topic `editor-methods` (ADR-7307)
- Agent routes for values only the panels could set: `editor.level_reload_resolve { choice? }` (the changed-on-disk
  prompt), `editor.seq_key { entity }`, `editor.edit_material { emissive, alpha_mode }`, `editor.xr_settings { set }`,
  `editor.lookdev_trace { out }`, `editor.project_settings_set { content: { base_url } }`, `editor.anim_skeleton { op:
  "add_socket", rotation }`, `editor.anim_preview { speed }`. (ADR-7747)
- `editor.a11y_contrast` (the WCAG AA contrast table of a theme) and palette commands `window.open_<panel>` for every
  main (built-in) panel (ADR-9810)
- `editor.save_all { cancel: true }` and `editor.screenshot { close: true }` close the dialogs an agent opened
  (ADR-8583)
- The agent-parity scan also finds `ui.radio(` and `egui::Checkbox::new(` controls, and the editor's radio pickers and
  checkboxes are classified against their panels' agent methods.
- Keyboard routes for six painted canvases (`regia_editor::canvas_keys`): **Nodes** columns for the material and
  procedural graphs (Ctrl+Shift+A or **Add node…**, **Connect…** by input name, Delete, Alt+arrow moves, Enter selects
  and frames), **Keys of <track>** and **Sections of <track>** lists in the sequencer (Left/Right move one frame), and
  **Spans** / **Items in view** lists for the profiler and trace timeline. Each canvas is an AccessKit `Canvas` node
  named with its count, and every list action runs its agent method's edit path. (ADR-7383)
- Editor accessibility: Inspector checkboxes and list buttons announce their field ("Add element", "Remove element <i>",
  "Move element <i> up"), "⚠" warnings in graph panels are read as text with their message, the fracture panel's half
  extents are named per axis, and each viewport pane is a focusable widget ("Viewport pane 1" to "4"): Tab into a pane
  makes it active, so its hotkeys, 2D mode keys and terrain tool keys act there. New helpers `InspectorUi::field_name`
  and `a11y_audit::{warning_glyph, named_drag_value, spoken_only, label_names}`. (ADR-7396, ADR-8870)
- `regia_editor::virtual_list::variable_rows`: a scroll list for rows of different heights that lays out only the rows
  in view (ADR-7326).

### Rendering

#### Draw lists and counted meshes

- `regia_render::DrawList`: keyed draws without one entity per draw. A host that mirrors its own scene keys each draw
  with a `DrawKey` (`DrawKey::pair(owner, part)`) and stores a `KeyedDraw` (mesh and material, model matrix, layer,
  visibility, shadow policy, skin palette, decal layer, GI proxy, static flag, second UV set). `extract_frame` appends
  visible items after the mesh entities in key order, so 9,000 keyed draws reach the frame with no entity spawned and no
  per-frame write for a draw that did not move. Feed it retained (`insert`, `set_transform`, `set_visible`, `remove`) or
  per frame (`begin_sync`, `submit`, `end_sync`, `sync`). Agent method `render.draw_list { limit? }`, guide topic
  `draw-list`. (ADR-8087)
- LOD chains and cross-fades for keyed draws: `KeyedDraw::with_lod_chain(Arc<MeshLodChain>)` selects like an entity with
  a `MeshLodChain` (bias, tier scales, hysteresis, dither cross-fade, coarser shadow LOD, `with_shadow_lod_hint`), with
  hysteresis memory keyed by the `DrawKey`. `DrawList::lod_stats()` (`DrawLodStats`) reports the last selection, and
  `render.draw_list` adds `chained`, `local_casters`, `shadow_off`, a `lod` object and per-draw `lod_levels`. (ADR-8457)
- Counted (GPU-only) meshes and textures through the App path (ADR-7330): `regia_render::counted_mesh` and
  `MeshRenderer::counted(handle, material)` draw from the GPU upload with no CPU vertices; `CountedMeshPlugin` wires the
  uploaders and releases unloaded meshes even when no frame draws; `AssetTextureUploads` uploads counted KTX2 textures
  and RGBA8 images once; `MaterialImage::Asset` binds one; `cooked_model::load_cooked_models_counted_textured` loads
  meshes plus their base maps as counted assets. A counted draw with `MeshUv1` draws lightmapped and through graphs
  reading `TexCoord1`. `render.stats` `mesh_cache` reports `adopted_meshes` and `adopted_mesh_bytes`.
- The frame renderer draws counted meshes straight from their GPU upload (`FrameRenderer::set_asset_meshes`,
  `load_cooked_models_counted`, `CountedCookedModel::push_frame_meshes`), byte-identical to the CPU path, with no CPU
  vertex copy kept (ADR-7256, ADR-7284)
- Counted meshes (`MeshRenderer::counted`) deform with morph targets on the GPU (`AssetMeshUploads::morph_targets`),
  skin on the compute path from their adopted vertex buffer, and are GI probe proxies with a brick baked from a GPU
  readback (`AssetMeshUploads::gi_readbacks`). `MorphStats::counted`, `SkinningStats::counted`, `anim.morph_weights`
  `renderer.counted` and `render.skinning` `last_frame.counted` report them. (ADR-7721)
- Counted meshes draw more from their GPU upload: skinned meshes up to `MAX_JOINTS_PER_SKIN` joints (vertex-shader
  skinning), every `MeshLodChain` level (`MeshLodChain::from_cooked_asset`), and visibility-buffer clusters (one compute
  repack per mesh into the cluster arena, none on steady frames). New `FrameRenderer::{adopted_skinned_asset_mesh_count,
  counted_material_rebinds, counted_lod_index_uploads, counted_vis_stats, asset_mesh_counts}` and
  `visbuffer::CountedVisStats`. (ADR-8610, ADR-7428, ADR-7462)
- A counted texture (`AssetTextureUploads`) is one GPU texture with an sRGB and a linear view, so each material slot
  samples in its own color space; `GraphTexture::from_asset` binds a counted texture in a material-graph slot, and
  `FoliageSway::adopt` keeps it in the sway twin (ADR-8610)
- `FrameRenderer::asset_mesh_upload_serial(key)` and `render.stats` `counted.vis.kept_meshlet_bytes`. (ADR-7542)
- `regia_render::cooked_model::load_cooked_models_all_lods` loads every authored part with its stable node and mesh
  identity, its full per-LOD material table and selectable generated index LODs, batching texture decodes and sharing
  image handles (`CookedModelLods`, `CookedModelLodPart::{lod_indices, lab_mesh}`, `CookedModelLodsError`). It loads
  GPU-ready and compact meshes; `REGIA_PAK_GPU_MESHES=1` desktop paks are its fast path. Guide topic
  `cooked-model-all-lods`. (ADR-7962, ADR-8025)
- Vertex-cluster far LOD for models split into many pieces: `regia_render::cluster_pieces` clusters every piece on one
  shared grid to a triangle budget, keeps seams closed and reports `max_error`; helpers `cluster_cpu_meshes`,
  `cluster_lab_mesh`, `clustered_cpu_mesh`, `clustered_lab_vertices`. Agent `render.cluster_lod { entities,
  target_triangles?, screen_size?, dry_run? }` appends the result to each piece's `MeshLodChain`. (ADR-8464)

#### Visibility, occlusion and indoor cells

- `ViewVisibility` is written each frame by culling: true when one of the entity's draws passes some view's layer mask
  and frustum (`regia_render::update_view_visibility`, change-checked and allocation-free on a steady frame;
  `ViewVisibilityStats` holds the last run's counters). New `regia_render::visibility` (`is_drawn`, `DrawVisibility`,
  `visible_entities`, `visible_to_camera`), `FrameRenderer::take_shared_draw` and `regia_terrain::parked_draw`
  (`WithheldTerrainDraw`, `withhold_parked_terrain_draw`, `terrain_drawn`). Agent method `render.visible_entities
  {camera?, limit?}` returns the on-screen count, up to `limit` ids (default 64, at most 4096) and the frame's culling
  counters (errors `F0002`, `F0003`; guide topic `visibility`). (ADR-7200, ADR-7588)
- `ViewVisibility` folds in the GPU two-phase occlusion result, read back without a stall (one frame late), so a mesh
  behind a wall reads false; with several views (split screen, render-to-texture cameras) each window view has its own
  readback and an entity is visible when any view sees it, and visibility-buffer clusters fold in per instance
  (`OcclusionFeedback`, `ViewVisibilityPerView`) (ADR-7255, ADR-7286)
- Split screen: each window view keeps its own Hi-Z pyramid, cluster visibility bits and cluster LOD hysteresis history,
  so an occluder only one view sees hides what is behind it in that view, and each view holds its LOD across view
  changes. A view not drawn for `OCCLUSION_VIEW_RETIRE_ROUNDS` (16) rounds frees its history with no host call. New
  `FrameRenderer::{retain_occlusion_views, occlusion_pyramids_into, retired_occlusion_views}` and
  `OcclusionViewPyramid`. (ADR-7329, ADR-8620)
- Indoor cells and portals: `regia_scene::components::VisCell(pub u16)` names the cell an entity belongs to (0 =
  outdoors), and a `<map>.viscells.ron` v1 file describes cells as oriented boxes joined by convex portal quads
  (`regia_render::viscells`: `VisCells::from_ron`, `load_viscells_ron`, `viscells_ron_path`, `viscells_file_version`,
  `VISCELLS_FORMAT_VERSION`, typed `viscells_*` validation codes). A per-view portal walk (`traverse`, after Luebke and
  Georges 1995) and `cull_unreachable_cells` drop draws of cells no view reaches from the color pass; `VisCellSettings {
  cull }`, `VisCellStats`, a `viscells` gizmo group (`VisCellGizmos`, off by default) and agent methods
  `render.viscells` / `render.set_viscells`. Nothing is culled until a host inserts a `VisCells` resource. (ADR-7586)
- Indoor cells stage 2: an entity without `VisCell` inherits its nearest tagged ancestor's (`InheritedCells`,
  `VisCellMembers`), local lights whose range touches no reached cell leave the frame with their shadow update
  (`light_reaches`, `VisCellStats::{cell_lights, culled_lights}`), glass panes of culled draws add no glass instance
  (`VisCellCulled`), `audio_zone_mouths` derives zone audio mouths from the portals (`AudioZonePose`, `AudioZoneMouth`),
  and `ExtractedFrame::local_light_entities` maps extracted lights to entities. `render.viscells` `frame` reports
  `cell_lights`, `culled_lights` and `culled_glass`. (ADR-7673)
- Indoor cells doorways: `VisCells::locate_view_cells`, `VisPortal::{doorway_depth, tolerance, in_doorway}`,
  `CellBox::distance`, `Traversal::{doorway, depth_capped}`, `TraversalScratch::start_cells` and
  `VisCellViewStats::{eye_cells, depth_capped}`; `render.viscells` reports `portals[].doorway_depth`,
  `views[].eye_cells` and `views[].depth_capped`. (ADR-7639, ADR-7646)
- Terrain and scatter culling inside a cell: an eye inside a sealed cell (a tunnel tube) draws only the terrain nodes
  and scatter instances its portals show (at least half fewer from inside the reference tube); an eye outdoors draws
  exactly what it drew before. `regia_render::viscells::OutdoorPortals` and `outdoor_portals(..)` for hosts that drive
  `FrameRenderer` themselves, `FrameRenderer::{set_outdoor_portals, outdoor_cull_counts}`,
  `ScatterField::extract_meshes_through`, `PlaneSet::{intersects_box, intersects_sphere, shifted}`,
  `TerrainDrawStats::portal_culled_nodes`, and `render.stats` `interior { restricted, frusta, mouths, terrain, scatter
  }`. (ADR-8413)
- Sun-sealed cells: a covered cell the sun cannot reach through any portal stops drawing its rigid draws into the sun's
  cascades (`regia_render::viscells::sun`, `SunCover`, `ShadowCast::casts_sun`) (ADR-8414)

#### Lights, debug views and overlays

- Up to four directional lights: the sun keeps the cascaded shadow map and three more shade unshadowed in the forward
  pass, the visibility-buffer resolve and the Mobile forward; a fifth is refused and counted (ADR-7211)
- Up to three extra directional lights shade the terrain and its grass (Lambert plus GGX, unshadowed), as the forward
  already did (ADR-7254)
- `AmbientVolume`, a reflected axis-aligned fill box adding `color × intensity` lux to diffuse ambient with a
  `blend_distance` fade; up to four per frame (ADR-7211)
- `PostSettings::band_bounds_final_exposure` (off by default): auto exposure subtracts compensation and the meter's
  bright-subject bias first and clamps to the auto-exposure band last, so the band is the range the frame prints at;
  `PostSettings::auto_exposure_band()` returns the band. `render.get_exposure_settings` and
  `render.set_exposure_settings` report and set the switch. (ADR-7969)
- `regia_render::aerial_interior_slots(volumes, eye)` says which `InteriorVolume`s fog and aerial perspective read;
  `render.aerial_interiors` reports each bound slot's `index`. (ADR-7661)
- Twelve debug view modes (`unlit`, `normals`, `base_color`, `roughness`, `metallic`, `ambient_occlusion`, `overdraw`,
  `light_complexity`, `shadow_cascades`, `lod_color`, `motion_vectors`, `mip_level`) through `DebugView` and
  `render.set_debug_view`, also in the editor viewport (ADR-7211)
- The Mobile Low and High forward draws all ten shader debug view modes instead of a lit image (ADR-7254)
- `DebugDraw::line_for` keeps a line for N frames and `DebugDraw::text` draws a camera-facing world label; agent
  `render.lights` reports the sun, extra lights, ambient volumes, refusals and the debug view (ADR-7211)
- With `UiPlugin` in the app, `DebugDraw::text` labels draw UI font atlas glyphs (`install_debug_label_glyphs`); without
  it the stroke font draws as before (ADR-7254)
- New gizmo groups, all off by default: `ai_debug` (`AiDebugGizmos`), `ai_formation` (`FormationGizmos`), `lockstep`
  (`DetGizmos`) and `det_hover` (`DetHoverGizmos`) (ADR-8463, ADR-8358, ADR-8562, ADR-8550)
- Fog-of-war world overlay `regia_render::fog_overlay` (`FogOverlayPass`, registered pass `fog_of_war_overlay` at
  `before_post`; `FogOverlayHandle`, `FogLook`, `FogOverlayStats`, `FrameRenderer::{enable_fog_overlay,
  disable_fog_overlay}`): soft edges, explored and unexplored darkness and tint, only changed rows upload. Off by
  default. (ADR-8523)

#### Loading, warm-up, memory and pacing

- Scene ready: `regia_render::SceneReadiness::prepare(map_id)` holds the scene (the UI keeps presenting) while the
  engine warms the whole extracted map on a warm worker, re-warming as more of the map spawns, up to
  `SCENE_PREPARE_MAX_ROUNDS` (8). The `SceneReady` message is sent once after the present of the first frame that drew
  the scene (or late with `timed_out`). Also `SceneReadyState`, `SceneReadyStatus`, the systems `take_scene_prepare`,
  `publish_scene_ready` and `advance_headless_scene_ready`, `GameFrameWarm::{holds_scene, prepare_status, host_tick,
  host_presented}` with `HostTick`, agent method `render.scene_ready` (`F0011` without the render plugin) and a
  `scene_ready` telemetry phase. (ADR-7883)
- Per-frame load budget, opt-in: `FrameRenderer::set_load_budget(FrameLoadBudget)`, `load_budget()` and
  `load_budget_report()`; `FrameLoadBudget { upload_bytes_per_frame, new_pipelines_per_frame, late }`,
  `LateDraw::{Block, Defer}`, `FrameLoadBudget::RECOMMENDED_60FPS` (64 MiB, 8 pipelines, `Defer`) and
  `LoadBudgetReport`. Under `Defer`, late meshes past the byte cap upload on the next frames nearest first, and
  material-graph sets past the pipeline cap build on the graph workers. Agent methods `render.load_budget` and
  `render.set_load_budget`; the hitch record's `created` block gains the deferral counts. (ADR-7884)
- Paused scene hold: `regia_render::SceneHold` (off by default) renders one scene frame, keeps it, and then draws only
  the UI over the kept image until `refresh()`, a resize, an HDR toggle or a recreated surface;
  `regia_render::scene_hold` (`RetainedScene`, `HoldKey`, `HoldStep`, `SceneHoldFrame`, `SceneHoldCounts`,
  `gpu_pass_report`, `last_window_frame`), `WindowSurfaces::scene_holds`, `GameFlowSettings::hold_scene_when_paused`
  (default false) and agent methods `render.scene_hold` / `render.set_scene_hold { on, refresh }`. Held frames are
  counted apart in telemetry (`held_frames`, summary `held`, `LiveFrame::held`). (ADR-7890)
- Pipeline warm targets: `pipeline_warm::register_warm_target(format, samples)` (and `PipelineWarmQueue::warm_target`,
  `registered_warm_targets`, `registered_warm_formats`, `clear_warm_targets`, `WarmTargetKey`) makes every later frame
  warm also render into a render-to-texture camera's, second window's or capture's format and sample count. Every
  pipeline creation record carries `CreateDetail` (formats, samples, warm thread, call site, new `KeyAxes`); the
  `pipeline_after_warmup` event and `created_after_warmup` summary list them, and `render.pipeline_warm` answers
  `targets` and `recent_pipelines`. (ADR-7885)
- `FrameRenderer::warm_texture_views(device, frame)`: the game-frame warm of a frame with no window view (a headless
  host's render-to-texture cameras). (ADR-8122)
- `regia_render::pipeline_cache::pipelines_created_on_this_thread()` and the opt-in `log_pipeline_creates` /
  `take_pipeline_creates` log (ADR-8584, ADR-8453)
- Host memory budget for the engine's CPU mesh copies: resident draws (a `FrameMesh` with empty slices under a resident
  host key draws from its GPU buffers; `FrameRenderer::{mesh_buffers_resident, gpu_only_draw_supported,
  host_mesh_bytes_needed}`) and `regia_render::host_memory` (`HostMeshStore`, `HostMemoryBudget` with `for_preset` and
  `UNLIMITED`, `HostMemoryCounters`, overrides). Over budget, CPU copies of resident pak-backed meshes are dropped least
  recently drawn first and re-read from the mapped pak on demand. Agent method `render.set_host_budget`, a `host` block
  in `render.memory_budget` and telemetry `memory.host`. (ADR-7891)
- `VeryLow` quality tier: Desktop Low at render scale 1/3 upscaled with SGSR 1, for GPUs that miss 30 fps at Low
  (`QualityTier::VeryLow`, `GraphicsPreset::VeryLow`, `QualityTier::{EVERY, content_tier, very_low_columns,
  VERY_LOW_RENDER_SCALE, VERY_LOW_TRIGGER_FPS}`). Content budgets treat it as Low. The measured Intel N150 (8086:46d4)
  picks it; its unmeasured siblings stay on Low. `quality.get_detected` gains `step_down`. (ADR-7882, ADR-7979)
- Frame caps per app state: `regia_app::PacingCaps { play, paused, menu, loading }` and `PacingState`; the tighter of
  the state cap and the player's cap paces. `GameFlowPlugin` sets the state on every flow transition
  (`GameFlow::pacing_state`). Also `FramePacing::{effective_fps, state_fps, set_state_fps, wait_for_with}`,
  `tighter_cap`, `PacingClock`/`SystemPacingClock`, `App::pace_frame_with`, `DynResController::{hold, resume, set_held,
  is_held}` and `regia_render::DynResGovernor` (holds outside play by default). Agent methods `app.pacing`,
  `app.set_pacing_state` and `render.dynres`. (ADR-7889)

#### Frame graph, capture and diagnostics

- `RenderGraph::execute` opens one GPU breadcrumb scope per live node in compiled order, and game frame-graph passes get
  one each; they show in `render.breadcrumbs` and a `.regiatrace`'s `passes` stream (ADR-9070)
- `render.graph` reports owners, readers, writers, bytes and generations for light and cluster storage, the CPU draw
  list and ReSTIR reservoir buffers; a declared persistent buffer read before its writer is a compile error
  (`RenderGraph::declare_buffer`) (ADR-8065)
- Transient pool slot keys: `TransientPool::{acquire_keyed, keep_latest, latest, entry_info}` and `TransientEntryInfo`;
  `render.graph` and `FrameGraphInfo` gain `keyed`, `transient_writes` and `pooled`. (ADR-7877)
- `regia_render::frame_capture` (`CaptureSession`, `CapturedFrame`, `CapturedTarget`) and pass-boundary copies requested
  with `FrameRenderer::request_pass_capture`; frames without a capture request do no work for it. (ADR-7860)
- `regia_render::DrawRecord`, `DRAW_RECORD_PASSES` and `draw_log_counters`: a pass capture request records the copied
  frame's draws; frames without one record nothing (ADR-8224).
- `regia_render::read_texels` (`Texels`) reads one mip and layer of a registered target back as floats; `resource_usage`
  and `usage_names` report a target's usage flags (ADR-8142).
- Still tiles for high-resolution capture (ADR-8200): `ExtractedCamera::window(image, rect)` projects a pixel window of
  a camera and `FrameRenderer::render_camera_window(device, scene, rect)` renders it offscreen with full-image culling
  and sun-shadow placement; temporal and neighbor-dependent effects are off for stills.
- Telemetry says what happened: present waits (a frame whose excess is mostly in acquire + present is counted in
  `present_waits`, not as a hitch), a pacing floor (no frame under 1.5 pacing periods is a hitch under a cap or vsync),
  hitch-record `cause`, `background_spans`, `unaccounted_ms`, `event_loop_ms`, `interval_ms`, `window_events` and
  `bound_reason` (`regia_telemetry::UnknownBound`), `regia_core::profiling::{mark_render_thread, SpanTimes}`,
  `FrameProfiler::last_frame_split`, and `telemetry.live` thresholds. (ADR-7898)
- Telemetry stall attribution: renderer timings carry their source frame and a publication id, so summaries consume each
  sample once and null sample values say why they are absent (`hidden`, `no_scene`, `renderer_idle`, `no_renderer`,
  `no_gpu_timing`, `pending`); an `event_wait` frame phase and runner focus, occlusion, redraw-stall, surface-refusal
  and throttle diagnostics (`RunnerState`, `App::record_event_wait`, `EVENT_WAIT_SHARE`). (ADR-7965)
- Texture memory: `TextureMemoryReport::{tiny_uncompressed, uncompressed_limit}`, `over_uncompressed_limit()` and
  `regia_render::texture_budget::{TINY_TEXTURE_TEXELS, is_tiny, counts_as_uncompressed}`; `assets.texture_memory`
  returns the new fields. (ADR-7898)
- Shadow cache and glass counters: `ShadowCacheReport::{atlas_caster_draws, atlas_faces_kept, sun_caster_draws}` and
  `GpuTimings::{refraction_copy_ms, glass_front_layer_ms}` (timed apart from `forward_glass_ms`). (ADR-7986)
- `DrawIntegrity::{cull_missed_draws, cull_missed_draws_total}`: draws the GPU cull missed that the frame drew per
  object; `render.draw_integrity` returns both and `healthy` requires the total to be 0. (ADR-8028)
- `regia_render::instance_creation_count()` (diagnostic) (ADR-7557)
- `render.get_upscalers` rows gain `resolve`: on `fsr3`, the SDK version the resolve ports and one `{ id, adr, summary
  }` per output-changing difference; the same list for hosts is `regia_render::{FSR3_REFERENCE,
  FSR3_REFERENCE_DIFFERENCES, Fsr3ReferenceDifference}`. (ADR-7933)
- `regia_render::uv_sphere_deterministic(radius, segments, rings)`: the `uv_sphere` mesh with bit-identical positions
  and normals on every host. (ADR-7878)

### Lightmaps, GI and lighting

- `.bake.ron` v3 lists up to four `suns`; `regia bake-gi` and `regia bake-lightmaps` integrate every sun in the probe
  and lightmap bakes (CPU and GPU), and a two-sun bake equals the one-sun bakes summed. `plan.lights.extra_suns` lists
  them in the dry run (ADR-7818)
- Extra directional lights light probe GI ray hits (unshadowed) and, shadowed, the path tracer, the GPU and CPU probe
  bakes and both lightmap bakes (`PtScene::extra_suns`) (ADR-7254)
- `DdgiSettings::young_budget`: the most young probes the dynamic GI update traces per frame (0, the default and every
  tier's value, traces all), picked deterministically on the GPU nearest first, then in view, then longest waiting;
  `DdgiSettings::young_settle_frames()`, `DynamicGiBudget::{young_budget, young_settle_frames}` in
  `render.get_dynamic_gi`, and `render.set_dynamic_gi { young_budget }` (new error `dynamic_gi_no_renderer`). (ADR-7870)
- Probe pyramid regional rebuild: `regia_render::HeightEdits` (`HEIGHT_EDIT_RECTS` 16) hands a heights generation's
  changed rectangles to the probes through `HeightfieldInfo::edits`, and `DdgiPyramidStats` (`DynamicGiStatus::pyramid`,
  `render.get_dynamic_gi` `renderer.pyramid`) counts full and regional rebuilds and texels reduced. (ADR-7659)
- Far band for large-mesh occluder proxies: past the 32 nearest proxies, up to `MAX_FAR_MESH_OCCLUDERS` (128) more that
  opt in (`MeshOccluder::with_far_band`, `OcclusionProxy::far_band`) each darken the sky and sky-body fill under their
  footprint as one soft ground-contact blob, with a 5 m fade (`FAR_BAND_FADE`) so a hull crossing the boundary does
  not pop. New `OccluderBands`, `MeshOccluderBlock::{bands, far_count, near_fade_of}`,
  `FrameRenderer::mesh_occluder_bands()`, `MeshOccluder::contact_blob()` and the CPU mirror
  `regia_render::far_band_shade`. Off by default. (ADR-7970)
- `regia_terrain::FillCanyonScope { Off, MapWide, NearOccluders }` with `TerrainSharedDraw::set_fill_canyon_scope` (also
  on `TerrainGpu` and `GroundLookHandle`): on airless maps `NearOccluders` takes the reduced canyon-scale occlusion on
  the planet-shine fill only in the sun shade of the frame's mesh occluders, keeping the full stack elsewhere. CPU
  mirrors `fill_visibility` and `near_occluder_shade`; `terrain.get_ground_look` and `terrain.set_ground_look` carry
  `fill_canyon_scope`. `Off` is the default. (ADR-8412)
- `regia_render::aerial_interior_pick`: the interior volumes the aerial composite and the froxel fog bind (all of them
  up to four, else the four nearest the eye) (ADR-7975)

### World

#### Tunnels, overhangs and multi-level maps

- New crate `regia_world_geom` (no ECS, GPU or platform dependency) for tunnels, overhangs and multi-level maps. Stage
  A, `regia_world_geom::compat`, carries a lockstep game's tunnel code with its arithmetic unchanged so the game keeps
  its checksums: spline tunnel layouts and the mesher, `TunnelCollisionMesh`, the column query of free spans
  (`compat::surface::column`), the lockstep tunnel state `TunnelsState`, hover-hull ground rules, sensing helpers, the
  0.75 m on-foot walker grid (`compat::onfoot`, host seams `OnFootParams` and `OnFootSolids`, a glTF grid loader with
  no glTF crate) and the baked tunnel file loader. Cross-arch bit tables (`tests/fixtures/stage_a_bits.ron`) freeze the
  Stage A answers. (ADR-8050, ADR-8166, ADR-8167, ADR-8306, ADR-8432)
- `regia_world_geom::QUERY_BITS_VERSION` (2): the version of the mesher, hole and query bits a lockstep handshake sends
  beside the map's content hash (ADR-8166, ADR-8167)
- World geometry formats: `<map>.meshlayers.ron` v1 (`terrain-mesh-layers`: layers with `Passages`, `Gltf`, `Collision`
  or `Volume` sources, `GeomSettings`, the sim ground mode), `<map>.passages.ron`, and the cooked `<map>.meshlayers` v1
  (`terrain-mesh-layers-cooked`, a canonical section whose BLAKE3 is `WorldGeom::content_hash`).
  `regia_world_geom::cook::cook` meshes `Passages` layers and runs the weld and trim steps, and `WorldGeom::from_cooked`
  loads the result. (ADR-8112)
- `regia terrain passages import <tunnels.json> [-o OUT] [--json]` converts a baked tunnel export into
  `<map>.passages.ron`; `regia terrain passages export` writes it back byte for byte (ADR-8112)
- `regia_world_geom::tunnel_bake` bakes `do27-tunnels-v2/1` tunnel descriptions in Rust (validate, sample, meshes,
  terrain cut, nav, five consistency rules); `regia terrain passages import` takes such a description (`--map-id`) and
  `regia terrain cook <map.meshlayers.ron>` cooks on flat ground and prints the report (ADR-8175)
- The mesh layers cook draws `Passages` layers: each canonical face is split `look.subdivision` times per edge (default
  4) in integer millimeters on the sim's own section planes, each render triangle names the face it lies on, and ramp
  openings get mouth quads. `regia_world_geom::render` holds the types. (ADR-8175)
- The `Passages` cook cuts sections (cover changes, junctions, blocker runs, a 4 m chord limit), cooks portals with a
  nav node, one zone per section (exposure band, `tube`/`gallery`/`open` reverb preset) and blocker plugs; `regia
  terrain cook --json` lists sections and portals (ADR-8210)
- One ground for render, holes and the sim: `regia_world_geom::ground` (`GroundLattice` with integer-millimeter
  vertices, `locate`, `ground_at`, `height_in_tile`, `plane_height`), `regia_world_geom::cut` (mouths as named holes,
  ring loops and pins, per-tile cut bitmaps, the collar) and `regia_world_geom::pitch` (the `Nested` ground mode, the
  sim-geometry budget and the `ground_pitch_seam` check). `regia_terrain::HoleShape::lattice_rings` gives the render
  hole that matches the lattice cut cell for cell. (ADR-8214)
- `regia terrain passages import ... --json --meshlayers <doc> --heightfield <png> --world-width-m <m>` adds a `ground`
  summary (mode, tile levels, hanging vertices, mouths, sim bytes and budget, largest pitch-border step,
  `content_hash`); `--height-scale-m` and `--height-offset-m` map PNG counts to meters. New cook codes `mouth_unknown`,
  `mouth_not_simple`, `mouth_off_map`, `ground_over_budget`, `ground_pitch_seam`. (ADR-8214)
- An over-budget ground fails `ground_over_budget` with the smallest `Nested { max_level }` that fits (`fits`), and a
  `Nested` map lists up to 32 tiles held finer than its level (`tiles`) (ADR-8436)
- Mesh-layers cooks fail each spec 121 § 10 check by name and location, and `terrain.bake_report` lists every check,
  passed or failed, with a code, a location and a measured value (`regia terrain cook --json` includes it). An authored
  `waive` list in `<map>.meshlayers.ron` waives a check only for the cooked triangles whose hash it names, and optional
  `audit` probes add stated checks. `regia terrain check-layers <map> [--ground-y <m>] [--width-m <m>] [--json]` cooks a
  mesh-layers map in memory, runs the same checks and writes nothing. (ADR-8359)
- Terrain mesh layers stream and change LOD with the terrain: `cook_mesh_layers` cuts each layer's canonical triangles
  on the CDLOD node grid and simplifies LODs 1-3 with borders and mouth loops locked; `TerrainLayerStream` and
  `TerrainLayerChunk` draw chunks within `visible_radius` of a `StreamViews` source under `budget_bytes`, keeping the
  coarsest LOD resident. Agent method `terrain.mesh_layers`. (ADR-8398)
- `regia cook` cooks the mesh layers of every level whose map has a `<map stem>.meshlayers.ron` (no flag) and resolves
  each layer's `material` to its cooked `CookedStandardMaterial`, drawn with its texture slots; a missing material fails
  the cook (`meshlayers_material_missing`, `meshlayers_material_type`). New `regia_asset_pipeline::level_mesh_layers`,
  `regia_terrain::{LevelGround, load_level_ground}`; `terrain.mesh_layers` reports each layer's `material_id`.
  (ADR-8451)
- The cooked `<map>.meshlayers` gains a chunk section after the presentation section, stored only when not empty; `regia
  cook` writes both the render meshes and the chunks, and the level load reads the chunks from it. (ADR-8576)
- Mesh-layer seam: the cooked mouth zips onto the lattice ring and a ground collar replaces the temporary band
  (`regia_terrain::mesh_layer::{zipper, collar_triangles, attach_ring, seam_report}`); read-only `terrain.seam_report {
  hole?, pass?, eye?, texel_m? }` measures the render seam; `TunnelMeshOptions { pin_boundary }` for collision-sourced
  layers (ADR-8291). Approach strips join a ramp top that sits off the ring pins (`CLEAR_OF_RING_MM`) (ADR-8396).
- Lattice hole windows: a mouth a cooked mesh layer fills cuts the drawn terrain on the cooked ground lattice cell for
  cell, at every LOD and morph (`LatticeHoleCut`, `TerrainMeshLayers::lattice_hole_cut`,
  `HolesMask::gpu_words_with_lattice`, the `sync_mesh_layer_lattice` system). `TerrainDrawPasses` records the passes the
  shared draw ran, so `terrain.seam_report` can measure the drawn selection (`source`, `passes`). (ADR-8470)
- The mesh-layer cook writes `<map>.viscells.ron` (version 1) from the sections it cuts, so tunnel interiors cull with
  no hand-authored cells: cell 0 is the outdoors, each section is a cell with an oriented box, and mouths, junctions,
  cover changes and chord cuts are portals. `regia terrain cook --json` adds `viscells`, `vis_cells` and `vis_portals`.
  (ADR-8371)
- Agent methods for cooked world geometry: `terrain.surface_at` (support spans at the caller's height),
  `terrain.mesh_layers` (layers, sections, generation, `content_hash`; optional `map` and `section` in, and `map`,
  `geom_generation`, `layer_reports`, `mouth_reports`, `section_rows`, `portal_rows` and `blocker_rows` out),
  `terrain.bake_report` (every bake check with location, weld and trim counts, floor facets; `passed`, `waived`,
  `failure`, `ground_mode`, `memory_bytes` and `report_content_hash` with the cook's real check rows) and
  `terrain.blockers` (plugs with active flags; flips one in a dev or editor session), with the `TerrainMeshLayers` and
  `TerrainMeshLayersLoading` resources. `terrain.surface_at`, `terrain.bake_report` and `terrain.blockers` are on the
  v0.6 line. (ADR-8386, ADR-8627, ADR-8398)
- Native world-geometry query core behind `regia_world_geom`'s `stage_b` feature: fixed-buffer columns and support,
  surfaces, placement, ceiling confinement, disc wall push, segments, rock and line of sight over canonical geometry,
  with optional work counters and a native/compat column benchmark. `WorldGeom::tri` returns one canonical triangle from
  a packed query id; divergences from the reference transcripts that the spec forbids are classified in a committed
  table. (ADR-8246)
- Section blocker plugs behind `regia_world_geom`'s `stage_b` feature: `GeomState::set_blocker` flips one blocker word
  and one state-hash term at one generation, so queries, nav cells and sound occlusion change together;
  `apply_blocker_flips` applies a tick's flips in a fixed order. `WorldGeom::{section_open, portal_open,
  closed_sections, cell_route}` give a deterministic route over the cooked section graph. (ADR-8346)
- Sim-owned ground edits behind `regia_world_geom`'s `stage_b` feature: `GeomState::apply_ground_edit` with
  `GroundEdit::{Crater, Berm, Rect}` in integer millimeters (never under a layer roof plus 2 m cover, never moving a
  mouth-ring vertex or a layer floor, a smooth rim profile), tile promotion on `Nested` maps, `DirtyRect` and
  `GeomState::height_rects`, and an O(1) `GeomState::state_hash` with a byte-stable `encode`/`decode` snapshot
  (ADR-8313)
- Zones from the bake: `regia_world_geom::zones::section_volumes` gives one exposure band, reverb zone and interior
  volume per cooked section (not capped at four); `ExposureEase` eases by the frame clock (half-life 0.45 s) with an
  explicit `cut`, and the depth blend is on by default. `render.get_exposure` lists `zones` once the host inserts
  `regia_agent::BakedZoneList`. (ADR-8384)
- Interior chase camera `regia_world_geom::interior`: a swept-sphere eye under a lid with junction hysteresis, a
  0.5 s mouth blend and a ceiling clamp, defaults in `CameraInteriorParams` (4.5 m exterior boom, 2.5 m
  interior boom, 0.5 m sphere, 12 m floor reach). `InteriorProfile::place` is behind the `stage_b` feature; agent
  method `camera.interior_state` steps the zone machine only. (ADR-8336)

#### Terrain

- Terrain grid origin: `TerrainMap::with_grid_origin([x, z])` places a map exactly on a cell-spaced simulation grid and
  moves the drawn terrain, collider (`CollisionHeightfield::center`), holes, height patches (`PatchTarget::grid_origin`,
  `samples_within`) and picks together; `regia_terrain::resample_cell_grid_mm` maps a coarse sim grid onto the render
  grid. `terrain.get` reports `map.grid_origin`. Maps without one are unchanged bit for bit. (ADR-8179)
- Rectangular terrain maps: `TerrainMap::with_world_depth` gives a map a Z extent of its own, so a 4100 × 1540 grid
  draws and collides without padding to a square (ADR-8369)
- `TerrainMap::quadtree_samples()`: the sample count along the side `quadtree_width()` measures, the `samples` input of
  `quadtree_span`. (ADR-8421)
- Cooked photoreal terrain: `regia_asset_pipeline::write_photoreal_terrain` cooks a host's final exact-f32 heights,
  holes and photoreal material into the map under stable `terrain/*` ids; `TerrainSharedDraw::from_cooked` builds the
  map from them before the first frame, `set_material_cooked` takes pak-backed blobs without a copy, and
  `assets.terrain_tiles` reports `final_heights`, `holes` and `material`. New format `terrain-final-heights` v1.
  (ADR-8024)
- Far backdrop ring: `regia_terrain::backdrop::build_visual_map` continues a visual-only ring past the playable map
  (plateau, widening border average, ridge noise, optional `terraces` or `chaos_blocks` landform), shaded with the inner
  map's materials and seamless at the edge; collider, navigation and simulation keep the inner map. `BackdropRing`
  settings (skipped on `MobileLow` by default), `RingProfile` overlays, agent method `terrain.backdrop_status`.
  (ADR-8459)
- World-anchored scatter placement (`PlacementOrigin::World(WorldAnchor)`): rocks stay put when a map grows by a
  backdrop ring or a tier drops it; `bake_scenery_colliders` and `SphereBlockers` bake gameplay sphere colliders from
  the same placement (`terrain.scatter_colliders`); forest queries and the HLOD stream cook take the same origin
  (`ScatterQuery::with_placement_origin`, `StreamScatter::placement_origin`) (ADR-8029, ADR-8496)
- Non-destructive terrain edit layers (ADR-7825, ADR-8202, ADR-8381): named add or set height layers over the base, with
  show, hide, lock, opacity, reorder, delete, rename and Flatten into base, each change one bit-exact undo step; layers
  also carry signed material-weight deltas (folded, clamped and normalized per sample) and an optional procgen target
  whose generator publishes and restores only that layer. The cook bakes the flattened result. Stored in a
  `<map>.terrain.editlayers` sidecar. Agent: `terrain.edit_layers`, `terrain.edit_layer_create`,
  `terrain.edit_layer_set`, `terrain.edit_layer_delete`, `terrain.edit_layer_reorder`, `terrain.edit_layer_flatten`,
  `terrain.edit_layer_weights`, `terrain.edit_layer_paint_weights`, `terrain.edit_layer_procgen`; `terrain.stroke {
  edit_layer }`.
- Terrain and kit tools: brush size, strength, hardness and Smooth, Linear, Sphere or Tip falloff sliders
  (`BrushFalloff`; `terrain.stroke { falloff }`); region copy and paste of exact heights and weights with quarter turns
  and height offset; regional PNG/RAW heightmap exchange; a modular kit palette with floor-grid painting, line fill,
  erase and quarter turns, one undo step per stroke. Agent: `editor.terrain_region`, `editor.kit_paint`.
- Terrain operators in `regia_terrain::ops` (terraces, craters, flattened pads, domain warp, fBm or ridged noise), the
  bake-only procgen nodes `terrain.terrace`, `terrain.crater`, `terrain.flatten_pad`, `terrain.domain_warp`,
  `terrain.noise`, and `terrain.apply_operator` for the live terrain (ADR-8465)
- Runtime terrain height edits: `TerrainHeightEdits::new(draw.height_patches_handle())` and `push(HeightEdit { col, row,
  width, height, heights_mm })` take absolute millimeters, with refusal codes `edit_too_large` (`MAX_EDIT_SIDE` 128),
  `edit_frame_full` (`MAX_EDITS_PER_FRAME` 16) and `edit_out_of_range`. Scatter re-sits only the instances on changed
  ground (`ScatterField::heights_changed`, `ScatterResitStats`, `resit_runtime_scatter` in `ScatterRuntimePlugin`), and
  `TerrainDrawStats` gains edit counters (`VtEditCounters`, `TerrainGpu::vt_edit_counters`). `terrain.patch_heights`
  takes `heights_mm` and replies `edits_this_frame`. (ADR-7585)
- `ScatterField::prepare_height_edits()` takes the field's own copy of the heights at load so the first edit in play
  copies nothing; `ScatterResitStats::heights_copied_bytes` reports any copy. (ADR-7638)
- `regia_terrain::prepare_terrain(queue, color_format, build)` and `TerrainPreparation` (`is_finished`, `is_ready`,
  `take`) build a configured terrain, with its grass, impostors and virtual texture, on the pipeline warm queue's map
  tier; installing the result creates no pipelines. (ADR-7964)
- `regia_terrain::gpu::{without_aniso_footprint, without_cloud_map}` strip the dormant footprint and cloud-map paths
  from the shared color pass source. (ADR-7886)

#### Terrain sources and overviews

- Terrain source import: schema-2 JSON and grayscale PNG heightfield readers keep raw counts, rectangular dimensions,
  spacing and origin; CPU overview baking writes north-up shaded PNGs (`terrain.inspect_source`,
  `terrain.bake_overview`) (ADR-8063)
- Terrain JSON and PNG sources have per-request budgets, deterministic counters and cooperative cancellation, and
  `terrain.inspect_source`/`terrain.bake_overview` report effective limits (ADR-8362)
- Terrain count sources keep their mapping through cook, index and level load: a schema-2 `heightfield.json` count
  mapping is stored in the terrain index and builds the level's map exactly (`regia_terrain::source::CountMapping`)
  (ADR-8471)
- Count-raster descriptors (`"raster_descriptor": 1`): independent X/Z spacing, row and column order, required
  registration, origin, `nodata_counts` and a valid mask. `regia cook` reads `heightfield.raster.json` and permutes
  north-first or east-first counts exactly; `TerrainSource::terrain_map(MissingDataPolicy::Holes)` cuts holes over
  missing samples, and overview pixels with no valid sample are magenta. (ADR-8542)
- Source tile sets (`heightfield.tiles.json`, `"terrain_source_tiles": 1`): up to 4096 schema-2 JSON or 16-bit PNG tiles
  with exact seam checks. `regia cook` cooks them without assembling the grid and rebuilds only the cooked tiles a
  changed source tile covers. (ADR-8595)
- Terrain source raster descriptors (`heightfield.raster.json`) and source tile sets (`heightfield.tiles.json`) are
  frozen formats at v1 with schema fingerprints, corpus files and fuzz targets. (ADR-8639)
- Registered terrain overviews: `OverviewFit::PreserveAspect`, `bake_overview_registered` (pixel-to-world mapping,
  elevation legend, 1-2-5 scale bar, cancellable progress), plus-sign markers and a cached `<name>.overview.json`
  descriptor (ADR-8596)
- `regia terrain source-inspect <path>`, `regia terrain source-import <path> --dest <heightfield.png> [--adopt-source]
  [--dry-run]` and `regia terrain source-overview <source> --output <png> ...` (ADR-8541, ADR-8596)
- `regia terrain overview-bake <map>` bakes north-up overview and minimap PNGs from `heightfield.json`, `layout.json`
  and optional `tunnels.json`. `regia_world_geom::overview` (`bake_directory`, `bake_pixels`, `hide_covered_ground`,
  `UnitView`, `BakeOptions`, ...) hides covered ground through a host's `covered_at` predicate and redraws tunnel
  mouths; per-unit view flags (`ROCK_HIDDEN`, `LIT`, `HEADLIGHT_ON`, `PING_FLASH`) and the read method
  `terrain.unit_view`. (ADR-8333)

#### Deterministic navigation

- Deterministic navigation grid `regia_nav::det::grid` for lockstep sims: movement and size classes, `NavStatic::build`
  over any `GridGround`, obstacle stamps (`NavGrid::sync_stamps`), queries (`passable`, `can_step`, `los`,
  `nearest_passable`, ...) and `bits_hash`/`state_hash` for desync checks (ADR-8049)
- Deterministic grid areas and cost overlays: `GridSettings::areas`, `NavGrid::paint_area`, `CostOverlay` per profile
  mask, `NavGrid::generation()`, stamp shapes (`Disc`, `Square`, `OrientedBox`, `ConvexPolygon`) rasterized in integer
  centimeters, allocation-free `sync_stamps_from`, and neutral class and profile names (ADR-8379)
- Deterministic grid at scale: configurable `GridSettings::cluster_cells` (2 to 128, two-byte labels from 16),
  `regia_nav::det::region_graph::RegionGraph` and the budgeted `regia_nav::det::requests::PathRequests` queue served in
  `(submit tick, unit id)` order (ADR-8295)
- Deterministic flow fields `regia_nav::det::flow`: `FlowField` (integer integration field plus a direction byte per
  cell), `ClusterFlow` for large maps, `FlowCache`, optional line-of-sight and bilinear blend; determinism golden
  `flow_fields.detgold` (ADR-8294)
- Deterministic route search `regia_nav::det::search` (two-level A* with `(f, h, index)` ties, string pulling and a
  per-tick expansion budget) and the tunnel nav layer `regia_nav::det::layer` (`TunnelLayer::build`, portals at ramp
  tops, closable nodes) (ADR-8195)
- Deterministic navigation mesh `regia_nav::det::navmesh` (`NavMesh::bake` from a walkable raster, tunnel corridors,
  rooms, stairs and doors; `find_path`, `locate`, `straight_walkable`) with the cooked format `nav-det-navmesh`
  (`<map>.detnav`, v2) (ADR-8174)
- Tunnel navigation on the deterministic navmesh: `regia_nav::det::tunnel_bake::TunnelNavMeshBake` arms
  `TunnelsState<NavMesh>` with one corridor per passage, linked to the ground at each mouth, with the source game's bake
  bytes and `bake_id`; `TerrainSampler` implements `GridGround` (ADR-8215)
- Deterministic layered navigation `regia_nav::det::layers`: `LayeredBake::bake` turns a layered navmesh into integer
  millimeters and `LayeredBake::plan` plans bit-identically on every architecture, keeping to the right-hand lane in a
  passage; `LayerState` applies blockers and ground edits per generation. Agent method `nav.plan`. (ADR-8486)
- Deterministic agents `regia_nav::det::agent`: per-mover path following with stuck recovery, re-plans, kill-volume
  refusal and integer local avoidance, behind `DetAgentWorld` and `DetMover` seams; agent method `nav.det_agents`
  (ADR-8405). Crowd lanes `regia_nav::det::crowd` add formation slots, three choke files through a registered narrow and
  slots that slide back along a blocked leg (`DetAgents::crowd_refresh`, `crowd_slot`, `register_choke`, ...;
  `CrowdSettings`) (ADR-8448).

#### Navigation

- Navmesh queries: `NavQuery::raycast` along the mesh, `random_point` and `random_point_in_radius` (seeded, the same
  point on every platform), `find_path_partial` (path to the reachable polygon nearest an unreachable goal) and typed
  `NavQueryError` codes (ADR-8380)
- Stuck detection for `NavCrowd` agents and `NavAgent`s, off by default: re-path, nudge toward the polygon center, then
  report `Stuck` with a message (ADR-8380)
- Multi-level navmesh: `NavMeshBuilder::build_layered(triangles, areas, volumes, n)` keeps up to `n` walkable layers (a
  tunnel floor under the ground, the ground under a bridge, every floor of a building), linked where an agent can step
  across; `NavMesh::layer_count()` and `nav.get_mesh` `layers` report them. (ADR-8076)
- One polymesh per walkable layer: `NavMeshBuilder::build_with_nav_level_cap` keeps every level up to the setting and
  fails past it; per-hull agents `NavAgentParams::{pilot, hover, for_radius}` (a hover bake erodes an extra meter per
  side); pilot ladder and deck-stair climbs (`pilot_layer_links`, `NavLinkFlags::PILOT`, `NavQueryFilter::{pilot,
  hull}`); `NavAreaCosts::passages` (tunnel 0.75, flank 0.20); `NavQuery::locate_body` retries below the body point.
- Navigation: `NavTileCache::try_set_off_mesh_links` (refuses the whole list with `NavLinkError`),
  `MAX_STEPS_PER_ADVANCE` (3600), `NAV_LINK_GONE` and `NavObstacles::try_add_box` (`nav_invalid_obstacle`). (ADR-7558,
  ADR-7679)

#### Procedural generation

- `regia_gen` crate: bit-identical procedural meshes on every platform (`extrude` with bevels, `rounded_rect`, `lathe`,
  `clip`, `intersect_convex`, `merge`, UV projection, `mesh_stats`, `mesh_hash`) and a serde `Recipe`; read-only agent
  method `gen.mesh { recipe, mesh? }`, page `book/agents/gen-mesh.md` (ADR-7240)
- Rounded primitives and sweeps in `regia_gen`: `rounded_box`, `cylinder` (chamfer, knurl, optional caps), `annulus`,
  `tube`, `sweep_rounded_rect`, `sweep_section`, with recipe `Shape` variants `rounded_box`, `cylinder`, `annulus` and
  `sweep`. (ADR-8044)
- Marching-squares contours (`contours`, `ScalarGrid`, `Contour`, `ContourOptions`: asymptotic saddle decider, Chaikin
  smoothing, minimum length) and the read-only agent method `gen.contour { grid, levels, smooth?, min_length? }`.
  (ADR-8044)
- Texture synthesis in `regia_gen`: seeded value, gradient and cellular noise, masks, blur, curvature and wear masks,
  serde `TextureRecipe`; heightfield tools (noise, terrace, pads, thermal erosion, slope and normal maps, terrain and
  mesh export, `HeightfieldRecipe`), bit-identical on Linux, Windows and Android. Agent methods `gen.texture` and
  `gen.heightfield` (ADR-7280)
- Generator output follows data layers: a parked generator's outputs and instance sets are parked and hidden, an absent
  one is despawned with its output, and it reloads or reruns when live; `procgen.list` and `procgen.get` report `layers`
  and `presence` (ADR-7214)
- `spawn.mesh` `target_layer` puts its instance sets on a data layer; biome grass re-bakes onto the GPU grass when
  `GrassMultipliers` change (`GrassRebake`, `TerrainGrassHandle::replace_mask`) (ADR-7214)
- Grass re-bakes with density maps (`GrassRebake::density_maps`), and any change to a `GrassRebake` re-bakes (ADR-7262)
- Procgen bakes ship inside the pak and load through the asset server when no loose file exists, so Android games load
  baked generators (ADR-7214)
- Procedural bakes of placed levels, streaming cells and sublevels ship in a build profile's package and load from the
  sublevel's directory or the pak, anchored generators included (`ProcLevelBakeDirs`, `ProcBakeOrigin`); `procgen.list`
  and `procgen.get` report each bake's `path`, `level`, `source`, `dir` and `kind` (ADR-7262, ADR-7287)
- `biome.layer` output pin `filtered` lists the candidates each entry filter removed in preview runs (ADR-7238)
- `.pgout` bakes keep `spawn.prefab` overrides and a loaded bake applies them as a live run does. (ADR-7750)
- `regia procgen bake --prune --dry-run` lists what prune would delete and keep; `bake` and `verify` load the prefab
  templates a level's instances need, so generators inside prefab templates bake and verify. (ADR-7743)
- `procgen.verify` returns `anchored_generator_missing` findings (an entity with a cell bake but no `ProcGenerator`) and
  `regia procgen verify` prints them; new `regia_procgen::world::{bake_target, BakeTarget, lost_anchored_generators,
  LostAnchoredGenerator}`, and `regia_scene::prefab::derived_guid` is public (ADR-7331)
- Procedural generation: a `spline.modules` pattern size check (`nodes::{check_pattern_size, expanded_terms,
  MAX_EXPANDED_TERMS}`, new code `pattern_too_large`, counted without building), `world::refresh_instance_colliders`
  (instance colliders follow `ProcMeshLibrary` changes without a re-run), `biome::def::MAX_CHILDREN_PER_PARENT` and the
  warning `output_name_replaced`.

#### Levels, layers and streaming

- Level variants (`.levelvariant.ron`, new format `level-variant` v1, ADR-7823): a level defined as a base level (or
  another variant) plus its differences (added and removed entities, field overrides, component and resource changes).
  Base changes flow into every variant that does not override the field; chains up to 16 steps, loops refused
  (`variant_loop`, `chain_too_long`). `load_level_source` resolves variants at runtime, `regia package` ships each
  flattened, and the editor has File ▸ New level variant…, Outliner and Inspector marks and Revert to base. Agent:
  `editor.new_level_variant`, `editor.level_variant_state`, `editor.level_variant_revert`.
- Layer files (new format `layer-file` v1, ADR-7792): "Store in its own file" moves a layer's members to
  `<level>.<layer>.layer.ron`; every level reader (runtime, stream cook, bake tools, editor) folds them back in and
  `regia package` ships them folded; "Merge back into the level" restores the original bytes. Agent: `editor.layer_file
  { layer, separate }`.
- 2D tile tools (ADR-7853): Line, Rectangle, Bucket and Pick tools, right-click erase, a weighted random brush, a
  terrain path mode, a layer tree with editable object layers, rulers and guides saved per level with smart guides, and
  saved tile patterns in a `.tilepatterns.ron` v1 sidecar. Agent: `editor.tile_layers`, `editor.mode2d_guides`,
  `editor.tile_pattern_list`, `editor.tile_pattern_save`, `editor.tile_pattern_use`, `editor.tile_pattern_delete`; new
  `editor.tile_stroke` and `editor.mode2d_set` fields.
- Loading screens: `Regia.toml` `[project] loading_screen` names a prefab kept alive while a `Replace` open waits for
  its data, with progress that never goes down (`LoadingScreenPlugin`, in `regia::DefaultPlugins`; `level.status`
  `loading_screen`) (ADR-7263)
- Level switch with cancel: `regia_core::load_switch::LoadSwitch<T>` loads one value at a time on a worker, cancels the
  load in flight and frees the old value on a retire worker before the next load starts (`CancelToken`, `Cancelled`,
  `SwitchEvent`, `LoadSwitchStats`); `regia_framework::level_switch::{LevelSwitch, LevelSwitchPlugin}` (in the `regia`
  default plugins) reads and parses a level on a worker and opens it with `Replace`;
  `level_assets::load_level_source_parsed`. Agent methods `level.switch { path | cancel }` and `level.switch_status`
  (error `level_switch_bad_params`). (ADR-8027)
- `WorldStreaming::queued_move_level(pin)`, and `StreamOpenError::NotCooked { chunk }` with `unmounted_chunk()` for a
  streamed level with no cooked world index (ADR-8812, ADR-8813, ADR-8862)
- `regia_scene::placed::check_placement_with_prefabs(levels, prefabs, level, chain)` counts what each prefab-instance
  record instantiates, for the placement cap. (ADR-7601)

#### Effects

- Mesh particles draw registered mesh assets (`.vfx.ron` v8 `render: (mode: Mesh, mesh: Some("name"))`), lit with `lit:
  true` on flat face normals, each registered mesh drawing only its own instances; missing or refused meshes draw
  nothing and are reported (`vfx.describe` `render.mesh_status`, `mesh_draws`) (ADR-7210, ADR-7261)
- Effect bounds (ADR-8355): `.vfx.ron` `system.bounds` is an authored box that replaces the computed bound for culling
  and significance (it never kills particles); `regia_vfx::bounds` measures bounds that hold every drawn particle, and
  `VfxGpu::read_particle_bounds` reduces the live GPU pool on request. The VFX editor has Fixed bounds fields, Set fixed
  bounds from measured, a box gizmo and a Read GPU bounds readout. Agent: `editor.vfx_edit` ops `set_fixed_bounds`,
  `clear_fixed_bounds`, `fixed_bounds_from_measured`; `editor.vfx_preview { bounds: true }`.
- `ProbeSettings::particle_capacity`: the CPU VFX probe can model the GPU's main ring, so a whole-pool frame keeps the
  GPU's events when the event budget overflows (ADR-7431)
- VFX: `regia_vfx::events::MAX_PACKED_EVENT_COUNT` (16,777,215) and `sim::MAX_INSTANCE_INTENSITY` (4.0) (ADR-7560);
  `HiddenStaticDecals`, `DecalDrawScratch`, `DecalPool::visible_in_spawn_order` and `DecalId::sort_key` (ADR-7670).

### Animation and characters

- `ChainIK` graph node (FABRIK or CCD over a chain of any length, pole, per-joint cone and hinge limits) and `BodyAim`
  graph node (a stack of joints turned by share, with a pass count that removes offset-joint parallax) in
  `.animgraph.ron` v4, allocation-free once warm (ADR-8475)
- `StrideWarp` (`Rate` or `Pose` mode) and `OrientationWarp` graph nodes match clip speed and heading to the character's
  real ground speed and heading; on the demo walk at 1.3x clip speed planted ankles slide at most 1 cm per stance
  (0.2 m without the node). `anim.get_state` and `anim.list` rows gain `warps`. (ADR-8540)
- `regia_animation::foot_ik::FootPlanter`: a stateful foot planter that pins a planted foot in world space until the
  clip lifts it (planted ankles stay within 1 cm while a retimed clip skates about 10 cm per stance), tilts feet
  toward the ground normal (up to 25 degrees), smooths ground, pelvis and tilt, and fades in the air.
  `apply_foot_plant`, `FootPlant::ik_plan` for `TwoBoneIK` graphs, `FootPlantSettings`, and the reflected
  `FootPlantReport` agent anchor. (ADR-8088)
- `FootPlacement` component: the engine probes, plans and applies foot IK for a rig (pose output or graph parameters),
  fades on `weight_param` or `grounded`, resets on teleport or `FootPlacementRespawn`, and inserts `FootPlantReport`
  each frame. `GroundProbe`/`GroundProbes` is the physics-free probe seam; the character plugin installs a physics
  raycast. (ADR-8388)
- Heel and toe placement (`FootPlacement::foot_shape`): feet pitch to the ground line, rest on stair treads, never sink
  a planted heel, ball or toe, and bend the toes; `FootPlantReport` gains per-leg `heel_gap` and `toe_gap` (ADR-8505)
- Cooked foot-contact curves: `.meta` `contact_curves` cooks one `contact.<foot joint>` curve per foot onto every
  skinned clip, and one `PlantLock` state machine drives the foot planter, `FootLocker` and the crowd foot lock with
  bit-identical results. `anim.clip_report` lists each clip's `contact_curves`. (ADR-8308)
- IK goals at retarget bake: `RetargetOptions::ik_goals` (`IkGoalMode::Contacts`, `ScaledWorld`, humanoid presets) holds
  planted feet and two-handed holds across rigs of different proportions; `Retargeter::check` and `anim.retarget_check`
  report per-goal residual and slide (ADR-8476)
- `regia_animation::retarget_chain`: body chains (`RetargetChain`), reference poses (`ReferencePose`),
  `rest_offset_rotation`, `rest_offset_degrees`, `bind_bone_direction` and `chain_alignment_offset`;
  `HumanoidBone::from_name` and `HumanoidBone::chain` (ADR-7801).
- Six-leg tripod gait `regia_animation::gait` (`step_gait`, `GaitParams`, `GaitState`, `FootTarget`): alternating
  tripods, floor sampling, swing arcs, speed controls, slope refusal and power-loss freeze, with reflected state and
  guide topic `tripod-gait`
- Driven gait: `regia_animation::gait::step_gait_driven` copies a simulation body's position, yaw and velocity bit for
  bit and only steps the feet; a body jump longer than `step_length` replants at neutral (ADR-8474)
- Gait idle settling and turning in place by stepping, one tripod at a time; reflected `GaitState::settled` (ADR-8539)
- Rigid node drivers (`regia_animation::node_driver`): hinge, spin and slew drives applied after the skeletal pose,
  reported by `anim.node_drivers` (ADR-8410)
- Animation clips compress at cook: `cooked-clip` v3 key-reduces and quantizes joint tracks within 5 mm and 5 mrad
  (chain-propagated); a dense clip shrinks below a quarter of lossless. `AnimationClip::to_compressed_cooked_payload`
  (ADR-7227)
- `lossless_clips` import setting (`.meta` v2): a glTF, FBX, USD or BVH source cooks every clip as a bit-exact `Raw`
  `cooked-clip` v3 body; off by default, settable by folder rule or `editor.set_import_settings` (ADR-7819)
- glTF plain-node translation, rotation and scale tracks import and cook to `nodeclip/` sub-assets with exact STEP,
  LINEAR and CUBICSPLINE sampling and parent composition; `anim.clip_report` exposes the node hierarchy and
  start/middle/end matrix samples (ADR-8157)
- Cooked motion databases (`motiondb-cooked` v3) store per-frame clip speeds and arc lengths of distance-matched clips,
  so the player no longer samples them on first use; results are bit-identical (ADR-7817)
- Motion matching components and settings are reflected, so inspectors and `scene.set_property` reach their tuning
  fields (ADR-7216)
- Montage commands: every `MontagePlayer` change is a serializable `MontageCommand`; with recording on,
  `TimedMontageCommand`s stamped by the player's clock go out as `MontageCommandRecorded` and come back through
  `ApplyMontageCommand`, so a stream replays on another app. Agent `anim.montage_commands { entity, record? }`.
  (ADR-7216)
- Montage commands over the network (`MontageNetPlugin`, feature `net`): the server sends recorded montage commands as
  reliable tick-stamped messages and clients apply them on the same tick (`anim.montage_commands` `net`) (ADR-9370)
- Montage checks: `montage_unknown_slot` load warnings (`MontagePlayer::load_issues`, `anim.montage_state`
  `load_issues`), a `regia cook` error for a slot no `.slots.ron` names, and `montage_past_end` warnings for a short
  `rescale: false` variant (ADR-7216)
- `anim_lab` moving-target acceptance: two-bone IK and look-at reach targets moving every frame within 1 mm over
  200 frames, on a code-built rig and the checked-in graphs (ADR-7216)
- `Skeleton::parent_of(joint)`: the parent of a joint, or `None` for a root (a malformed parent entry counts as a root)
- `SkeletonProfile::check_skeleton` reports an empty arm measurement (the rest-pose check fails) for a skeleton whose
  inverse bind matrices do not cover an arm joint, instead of panicking.

### Gameplay systems

#### Lockstep simulation kit

- New crate `regia_detsim`, the lockstep kit with no ECS, GPU or platform dependency: `math` (`sin`, `cos`, `atan2`,
  `sqrt`, `powf`, `exp`, `sin_f64`, `cos_f64` through `libm`), `tick` (`TICK_RATE_HZ` 60, `TickRate`) and `fpu`
  (`ensure_default_fp_env` resets FTZ/DAZ, rounding mode and traps on the sim thread) (ADR-8012, ADR-8013)
- Lockstep math, randomness and fixed point: more `regia_detsim::math` transcendentals and `_f64` forms through the
  pinned `libm`, `regia_detsim::rng::DetRng` (xoshiro256** with per-system streams and unbiased ranges) and
  `regia_detsim::fixed::{Mm, Mm2}` (ADR-8483)
- `regia_detsim::float`: `det_min`, `det_max`, `det_clamp` (and `_f64` forms) give one answer on every host and
  optimization level (a zero result is `+0`, a NaN result the canonical quiet NaN), plus `canonical`/`canonical_f64`
  (ADR-8085)
- `regia_detsim::float::det_cmp`, a host-independent total order on `f32`, and `WingmanCommand::EscortCommander`.
  (ADR-8655)
- `regia_detsim::ids::DetIdAllocator<u16 | u32>` (FIFO reuse, sparse `reserve`, generation handles) and
  `regia_detsim::broadphase::DetBroadphase`, which visits the same contacts in the same order with the same bits as the
  all-pairs loop (900 bodies: about 0.036 ms instead of 0.262 ms), with height bands and `FloorRule` (ADR-8048)
- `DetSpatial` range, nearest and first-k queries, segment, sector and box queries, `build_from` for rewind indexes, and
  `any_within` (ADR-8478, ADR-8473)
- Lockstep state snapshots `regia_detsim::state` (`DetState`, canonical codec, `SnapshotRing`, `SimHarness::save` and
  `resume`) (ADR-8479), and input logs and offline replay `regia_detsim::replay` (`InputLog`, `replay`, `ReplayReport`
  naming the first diverging tick and system, `catch_up`) (ADR-8525)
- `DetRng` is a `DetState`: a snapshot restores the generator and every stream exactly (ADR-8634)
- `regia_detsim::hash`: the per-system lockstep desync hash. `Mix` (a streaming word hash that is also a hashing
  `serde::Serializer`), the canonical float rule, `Systems<N>`/`SystemFold<N>`, a 240-tick `SystemRing`,
  `diverged_systems` and `DivergenceReport`, and the `DesyncHash` trait (ADR-8047, ADR-8078)
- `regia_framework::desync_hash::DesyncHashPlugin<N>` records one word per system after every step for ECS games,
  folding components in entity-id order (or a game's own key with `order_by`), and the agent method `sim.desync_rows {
  to?, count?, against?, split?, left?, right? }` names the split systems, their first steps and the last agreed step
  (ADR-8080)
- `sim.desync_rows` `report.verdict` (`same`, `canonical`, `float_bits`, `partial`, `no_overlap`), and sim hosts that
  restore and record desync rows (`start_with_snapshots_and_desync`, `start_lockstep_with_desync`) (ADR-8621)
- Desync diagnosis: a raw diagnose ring names zero-sign and NaN-payload splits at their tick, serializer failures mark
  rows partial, and external sims behind the sim host feed `sim.desync_rows` (ADR-8547)
- `regia_detsim::golden` (re-exported as `regia_test_utils::det_golden`): step a `DetScenario`, hash state every tick,
  compare with a per-target `.detgold` golden and name the first diverging tick; `DetRegistry`, `rate_gate` (60 Hz vs
  120 Hz agreement) and four engine scenarios (`steer_slalom`, `slope_follow`, `disc_collide`, `hover_libm_1000`)
  (ADR-8046)
- `regia_detsim::{harness, soak, profile, arena}`: seeded step/snapshot orchestration, constant-memory paired soak runs
  that catch bootstrap and transient divergence, p50/p99/max profiling with a timing-independent fingerprint, and the
  headless `ai_arena` sample with JSON reports (ADR-8151)
- Per-system tick profiler `regia_detsim::profile::TickProfiler` (fixed ring, p50/p90/p99/max per system and tick,
  over-budget ticks, thread CPU clock) (ADR-8498)
- `regia_detsim::scale_soak`: a configurable 40-seat, 900-entity load scenario with a release example reporting per-pass
  and whole-step p50/p99/max and every tick over 0.1 ms
- AI-on scale soak `regia_detsim::scale_soak::ai` (900 units, 40 seats, 4 teams by default) with ten profiled systems
  and an oracle mode (ADR-8561)
- The threaded sim host runs lockstep sims: `HostPacing::Lockstep`, the `LockstepSim` trait, ordered frame feed,
  snapshots and restore on the sim thread, catch-up under a tick budget, a guarded floating-point environment and a hold
  while the app is suspended (ADR-8526)
- Render-free debug draw of the deterministic indexes (`regia_detsim::debug`, `regia_framework::det_debug`), off and
  free by default (ADR-8562)
- A session records extension hot reloads as nondeterministic (`regia_framework::session_determinism`);
  `sim.state_hash`, `sim.state_diff` and `demo.inspect` report it (ADR-9850)

#### Lockstep movement, vehicles and combat

- `regia_detsim::avoid`: integer disc avoidance in millimeters with fixed neighbor order and configurable `OrcaSettings`
  (ADR-8150)
- Integer ORCA avoidance: a stated input domain with typed errors, `OrcaScratch` (no allocation once warm), index-fed
  neighbors (ADR-8377), and an opt-in integer linear-program solver `OrcaSolver::Lp` with static wall segments from the
  det grid (ADR-8555)
- Lockstep contact response `regia_detsim::contact` (mass-weighted push-apart, restitution, Coulomb friction, `Ram`
  reports) (ADR-8563); lockstep tracked and wheeled ground vehicles `regia_detsim::ground_vehicle` (ADR-8551); lockstep
  projectiles `regia_detsim::projectile` with a `SegmentHits` seam and an aim solver `regia_detsim::aim` (ADR-8509)
- The lockstep hover step `regia_detsim::hover` (`step`, `step_world`, `step_tuned`, hops, lean) with the
  engine-authored `HoverParams::DEFAULT_HULL` (a generic 30 t hull), `FlightMode`, `ClimbTuning`, plus
  `regia_detsim::{ballistic, site_physics, lowg}` and an optional `serde` feature (ADR-8079, ADR-8164)
- Lockstep hover: a host kit (`hover::host`: floor samplers, `integrate`, `ground_attitude`, `HoverFleet`) (ADR-8508),
  an ECS plugin `regia_framework::det_hover::DetHoverPlugin` (ADR-8550), `.hoverhull.ron` hull files (ADR-8512),
  `HoverLimits` and `WorldPhysics` as values with `step_with` (ADR-8349), and bad-input guards with
  `validate`/`sanitized` on hull parameters (ADR-8309)
- Hover hull handshake: `hull_handshake_bytes` covers params, flight mode and every limits field (273 bytes), and
  `hull_handshake_mismatch` names the first field two peers disagree on (`detsim.hover_hull_check` `handshake_hex`)
  (ADR-8632)
- Hover feel-target rig `regia_detsim::handling` (`fly`, `measure`, `format_report`) and the `handling_telemetry`
  example; the caller passes its own `HoverParams` (ADR-8393)
- Deterministic character mover over the native world geometry (`stage_b`): `WorldGeom::push_capsule` and
  `clamp_capsule`, `regia_world_geom::mover::{move_disc, move_capsule, body_probe, dressing_grid}`, a cooked
  `skylight_props` prop layer so walkers stand on drawn rubble, and the agent method `geometry.body_probe` over a cooked
  `.meshlayers` file (ADR-8323)

#### AI

- Deterministic AI in `regia_detsim::ai` (also `regia_ai::det`), with the source game's exact bits under ADR-8012:
  - Core: seeded `AiRng` streams, time-sliced `AiSchedule`/`AiCadence`, the Infinite-Axis Utility scorer
    (`ResponseCurve`, `Consideration`, `score_options`) and `AiCore<P: DecisionPolicy>` with serde snapshots and `rows`
    for host agent methods (ADR-8152)
  - Perception and influence: the `LineOfSight` seam (`OpenField`, `HeightfieldLos`, `LosFn`; implemented by
    `regia_world_geom`'s `TerrainSampler` and `TunnelCollisionMesh`), the team `SightingsBoard` and an 8 m
    `InfluenceGrid` (ADR-8395)
  - Tactics, difficulty and aim: `Hysteresis` decisions, the twelve-option `catalogue`, `DifficultyProfile` as decision
    quality only, lead targeting, ballistic aim and `aim_error` (ADR-8462)
  - Motor and combat: `arrive`, `pursue`, `orbit`, `kite`, `evade`, energy throttle, target priority, the fire seam with
    mortar arcs, the Re-up rule and the escort V (ADR-8516)
  - Squads and mode bots: wingman formations, orders to plans, low-hull and low-ammo reports, and king-of-the-hill,
    capture-the-flag and free-for-all bot orders hooked to the match rules through `regia_framework::rules::bots`
    (ADR-8554)
- Deterministic AI core: `PerceptionIndex` sighting refresh on the spatial index with `AiTickWorld` per-unit views
  (ADR-8518); versioned target selection `TargetSelector` with `TargetRule::{Exhaustive, Ringed}` (ADR-8517);
  `AiCadence` channels, importance tiers and per-tick caps (ADR-8560); `TeamRosters` and `SlotIndex` (ADR-8484); and
  `UtilityCatalogue` options and inputs as data with `.detutility.ron` v1 (ADR-8609). Defaults keep every existing pin.
- Perception v2: `Sense::Touch` and `Sense::Custom(u16)` senses, per-stimulus confidence and last known velocity with
  per-sense half-lives, `TeamKnowledge` sharing per team and squad, `Visibility` and multi-ray `SightSamples`, and a
  detection meter (ADR-8357)
- `regia_ai::path_provider`: the `PathProvider` trait with `navmesh`, `det_grid` and `det_navmesh` providers, and the
  `MoveTo` behavior-tree leaf and state-tree task that plans, follows, re-plans and fails with codes; `ai.bt_live`
  answers `move_to` progress (ADR-8293)
- `.formation.ron` formation assets, minimum-cost slot assignment (`regia_ai::assign_slots`: Hungarian method up to 64
  members, greedy above) and formation steering in `NavCrowd` through `CrowdGroup`, with a column through narrow
  corridors (ADR-8358)
- In-world AI debug draw (`regia_ai` feature `debug_draw`, `AiDebugDraw`): behavior-tree leaf names, state-tree state,
  GOAP step and goal, squads, formation slots, MoveTo targets and paths, and watched blackboard keys, with a filter and
  a label cap (ADR-8463)
- `regia_ai::MAX_BT_DEPTH` (256) and the validator code `too_deep` (`ai.bt_validate`) (ADR-7559)

#### Fog of war

- Fog of war: `regia_detsim::fog` answers team visibility as a pure function of sim state (`FogWorld`, `SightTable`,
  `TeamVision`, `FogGrid`), `regia_detsim::los::LineOfSight` is the line-of-sight hook, and
  `regia_framework::fog_view::FogViewSim` filters the sim bridge per viewer so a hidden unit never spawns;
  `FogViewSim::set_reveal_all` lifts the per-viewer filter. Agent methods `fog.status` and `fog.set_viewer`. (ADR-8154)
- Fog of war v2: explored, visible and remembered (ghost) cells, stealth and detectors, terrain and elevation line of
  sight (`GridLineOfSight`, `ElevationRule`) and a spatial index from 16 watchers (ADR-8473); a per-viewer fog texture
  `regia_framework::fog_texture` and `FogPresentationPlugin` (ADR-8523)

#### Match rules

- Match modes and match flow: an ECS-free, lockstep-safe match core with `MatchMode` (`tdm`, `ffa`, `ctf`, `koth`,
  `custom`), objective entities on an `ObjectiveBoard`, capture-the-flag and king-of-the-hill skeletons, and `MatchFlow`
  (host and friendly pause on the sim clock, ready-up that starts on an agreed lockstep step, side-limited observers,
  host-approved join requests), stepped with `Playlist` by `MatchSession`; teams, seats, caps, limits and the countdown
  are `SessionSettings`. Agent methods `rules.match_get`, `rules.match_setup`, `rules.match_input`,
  `rules.match_objective` and `rules.match_step`, guide topic `match-rules`. (ADR-8102)
- Match rules: the ECS-free `regia_rules` crate (ADR-8239); seat-checked match-flow issuers with ordered join queues,
  pause budgets and `FlowInput::Leave`; match phases, time limits, overtime, rounds and forfeits (`regia_rules::phases`,
  ADR-8485); capture the flag on multi-level maps (`touch_height`, `flag_return_ticks`, drop checks, ADR-8506); teams,
  per-seat ledgers, named counters, assists and friendly-fire rules
- Best-of series, map vote, win rule and match-end stats: `rules.series`, `rules.vote_cast`, `rules.vote_status` and
  `rules.end_stats` (team totals and MVP; the host may `note_kill` and `note_structure`), refusals `vote_closed`,
  `vote_bad_option`, `series_length` (not 1, 3, 5, 7 or 9) and `lives_range` (outside 1..=10).
- Match ledgers get stable row ids (`PlayerLedger::id`, `LedgerBook::generations`, `LedgerBook::reopen`), seat moves
  (`MatchSession::seat_moves`, `regia_framework::rules::retag_moved_seats`), and `MatchRules`, `FriendlyFire` and
  `SelfDamage` are reflected, so hot reload keeps them. (ADR-8657)
- Match-rules proof: golden scenario `match_rules_ctf_koth_3600`, `objective::host_unit_order` and a bounded event queue
  (`max_pending_events`, default 4096)

#### Sim bridge

- Threaded fixed-step sim host (`regia_framework::sim_host`): `ThreadedSimHost` runs a `FixedStepSim` on its own thread,
  realtime or manually paced, and publishes a consistent frame pair (`sim.host_status`, `sim.host_hold`,
  `sim.host_step`) (ADR-8083)
- Sim bridge handedness and blend policy: `SimHandedness::LeftHandedReflectX` reflects X at the boundary, mesh winding
  flips for sim-authored meshes, and per-kind `Lerp` / `Snap` / `Hold` blending with teleport distance and presentation
  delay (`sim.bridge_settings`, `sim.set_bridge_settings`) (ADR-8456)
- Sim bridge reactions: the event router applies `Vfx`, `Decal`, `SoundCue` and `Announcer` reactions on client builds
  where `ExternalSim::event_place` places the event (`sim.counters` `reactions`) (ADR-7210)
- Presentation physics beside an authoritative sim moves into the engine:
  `regia_framework::sim_bridge::presentation::PresentationPhysicsPlugin` (ADR-8356)
- `sim_bridge --presentation-physics` sample: bridged sim entities get kinematic hulls driven from snapshot poses, the
  terrain collider and drawn ground share one `TerrainMap` and `HolesMask`, and debris collides with both

#### Logic graphs and scripting

- Logic graphs (working name): `.logic.ron` v1 event → condition → action rules compiled against the task registry and
  ticked in `FixedUpdate` after the state trees, with `Start`, `Timer`, `BlackboardChanged` and `EntityEvent` arms,
  built-in actions, any `BtTask`, priorities, cooldowns, `LogicInbox`, `LogicGraphStatus`, hot reload and
  `LogicAuthority`. Agent methods `logic.compile`, `logic.control`, `logic.emit`, `logic.reload`, `logic.status`.
  (ADR-7201)
- Logic graph breakpoints in development builds (`LogicGraphDebug`: pause on rule or event, disabled rules),
  `LogicGraph::step` and `logic.control { op: "step" }`, and agent `logic.debug` (ADR-7224)
- Logic graphs, phase 3: world blackboards (`world/<board>/<key>`, `logic.world`, `logic.world_set`), event arms
  `OnSequenceMarker`, `InputAction` and `OnNetMessage`, the `regia::logic_bridge` (feature `logic`: sequences, cues, Lua
  calls, input actions, net messages, `NetRole` authority) and presentation-only actions on peers without authority
  (ADR-7268)
- Logic graphs run allocation-free in steady state, including rule firing, scope writes, inbox events and tracing
  (`LogicGraph::write_trace_line`, `Blackboard::assign`) (ADR-7282)
- Logic in prefabs (ADR-8566): the reflected `regia_ai::logic::LogicGraphRef` component names a project `.logic.ron`
  (with `enabled` and optional starting values for locals), and `AiPlugin` compiles it into the entity's `LogicGraph`,
  so every prefab instance runs its graph; failures land in `LogicGraphRefLoads` with a reason code. Prefab edit mode
  has Open graph and New graph for this prefab. New format `logic-graph-ref` v1. Agent: `editor.prefab_logic`.
- `regia_ai::logic::LogicScopeView` and `LogicGraph::scope_view` give read-only access to a graph's variables
  (ADR-8021).
- Host and presentation Lua runtimes: `ScriptAuthority::{Host, Presentation}` (`LuaMods::with_authority`,
  `LuaModsPlugin::with_authority`, `regia.authority`), a bounded host command queue (`regia.command(name, value?)`,
  `HostCommandRules`, `LuaMods::drain_host_commands`; a refusal never disables the mod), a snapshot hook
  (`LuaMods::publish_snapshot`, `on_snapshot`, `regia.snapshot()`) and agent method `lua.authority`. (ADR-8466)
- Lua: `regia.emit(name, value, target)` takes an optional entity target, and `LuaMods::call_function` calls a mod
  function with a return value (ADR-7268)
- Lua mods are bound to their mount: `ModFiles::mount()` (`mods:<mount id>`, `dir:<canonical-path BLAKE3 hash>`, or a
  game-bound `ModFiles::with_mount`, `MAX_MOUNT_BYTES` 256), `LuaMod::{mount, save_key}` and `LuaMods::{contested_ids,
  held_state}`, reported by `lua.list`. (ADR-7881, ADR-7951, ADR-7992)
- `SaveSections::register_required(key, write, read)`: a save section whose write error aborts the save; Lua's state
  section uses it. (ADR-7992)
- `ModSaveLine::skipped_entities` counts the owned entities a Lua mod's save left out (each warned once), shown in
  `lua.list` `last_save`.
- Hot reload rebuilds game script slots from the new library with their fields, flags and position (`start` does not
  rerun); a type the new library drops loses its slots with one warning. The `agent.hot_reload` result reports
  `scripts_rebuilt` and `scripts_dropped`. (ADR-7206)
- Extension settings declared with `declare_settings::<T>()` save to the user settings file and win over `Regia.toml` at
  the next start (ADR-7237)

#### Physics, vehicles and destruction

- `regia_physics::teleport` (`TeleportVelocity::Keep | Clear`), `TeleportCommands::teleport`, `ScriptCtx::teleport` and
  agent `physics.teleport`; `ScriptEvent::ContactForce`; `ClosestPointResult` `normal` and signed `distance`, also in
  `physics.closest_point` (ADR-7233)
- Agent `physics.parented_bodies`: dynamic bodies under a non-physics parent, logged once each in the play world
- Soft-body plasticity (`Plasticity { yield_threshold, creep, max }`, `SoftSolver::set_plasticity`);
  `physics.cloth_set_params` patches `gravity_override`, `stretch_compliance`, `bend_compliance`, `lod_distances` and
  `plasticity`, and `physics.cloth_inspect` reports them (ADR-9070)
- `Vehicle::set_config` retunes a vehicle in place (pose, velocity, rpm, gear and wheel state kept or clamped; refused
  configurations change nothing) and returns a `ConfigChange`; `Vehicle::config_revision`, `VehicleConfig::digest`.
  `regia_physics::vehicle_proving` runs scripted `accel` and `skidpad` runs at 60 Hz on flat, ramp, slope and bump
  tracks, with an optional towed trailer. (ADR-9760, ADR-7745)
