- `.bike.ron` v1: a two-wheel bike as a named file (`BikeAsset`) (ADR-8630)
- `DestructibleSource { asset, seed }`: a level-placed destructible is defined at its entity the first frame its asset
  can be read (ADR-9750)
- Fracture site sampling: `FractureSites::Impact { count, seed, at, bias }` (fine debris at a hit, coarser with
  distance) and `FractureSites::Clusters { count, seed, clusters, spread }`, with the same bytes on every platform
  (ADR-9870)
- `regia_asset_pipeline::fracture::MIN_CLUSTER_SPREAD` (0.001), the smallest `FractureSites::Clusters` spread (ADR-7550)
- Limits `regia_physics::vehicle::MAX_VEHICLE_SETTING`, `destruction::MAX_IMPACT_COOLDOWNS` and
  `vehicle_track::MAX_TRACK_STEPS`, and `regia_vfx::ambient::unmatched_profile_tier` (ADR-7431)

#### Cameras, players and display

- Camera blends with one `CameraDirector` per local player (`blend_to`, `camera.blend_to`, `blend` in `camera.get`),
  level load progress (`level.status`) and the runtime `[project] startup_level` (ADR-7203)
- Per-player camera shakes: `camera.shake { player }`, `camera.get` `players`, and a `PlayerCamera(n)` feels only player
  `n`'s director shakes (ADR-7263)
- Split-screen cameras: a camera tagged `SplitScreenCamera { player }` gets that player's `SplitScreenLayout` rect as
  its viewport, written on join and leave; `players.list` entries add `viewport` and `cameras`. (ADR-7880)
- Spectator Orbit, POV and Overhead modes, including in paused replay playback; in the same frame Orbit and POV follow a
  target whose parent moved (ADR-8043)
- Leashed photo mode: 30 m roam offline, training, dead or spectating, 12 m in a live online match, refused in
  competitive matches; never pauses or drives the sim. Captures `<game>-<map>-t<tick>-<n>.png` to the pictures directory
  at up to 7680 px. Agent `photo.enter`, `photo.set`, `photo.capture`, `photo.status`. (ADR-8415)
- Display settings apply live: `display.resolution`, the new `display.fullscreen` and `display.vsync` reach the primary
  window on the frame they change, without a restart or a new device (vsync off asks for `Immediate`, then `Mailbox`,
  then FIFO; Android keeps vsync and records `android_display_paced`). Window size requests are clamped to the monitor,
  rotated panels are oriented first (`orient_monitor`), and refusals carry a code in the new `WindowStatus` component.
  `settings.get` answers `applied.display`. (ADR-7880)

#### Abilities, data and debugging

- Abilities end a despawned target's effects with `EffectEnded { reason: target_gone }`, counted in `abilities.stats`
  `ended`; cooked format `abilities-cooked` v1 (`AbilityAssets::cook_text`, `load_cooked`) (ADR-7216)
- Ability assets cook: `regia cook` writes `.attrs.ron`, `.effect.ron` and `.ability.ron` to `abilities-cooked`, and
  `app.load_ability_assets_at_startup` loads the cooked form in a packaged game (ADR-9370)
- `regia_framework::abilities::chance_replay_steps()` (ADR-7573)
- `CurveRef` is reflected and `regia_framework::curve_table::validate_curve_refs` reports `curve_ref_unresolved` per
  data-table row
- `regia_framework` feature `visual-log`: `visual_log::{VisualLog, VisualLogPlugin, ...}` and `debug_log!` (the macro
  discards its arguments when the feature is off, as in shipping builds).

### Networking and Steam

#### Lockstep

- Lockstep mode (`regia_net::lockstep`) for games whose deterministic simulation is too large to replicate: a sans-IO
  `LockstepHost`/`LockstepSeat` carried over `NetServer`/`NetClient` (`lockstep::net`), frames that repeat
  unacknowledged frames so a lost packet does not stall the match (`FrameRedundancy::Interleaved` by default), one-bit
  frames for unchanged inputs, an input delay that follows the link, equal delay for a player host, and hash authority
  by quorum with resync, tie policy and self-check. Agent method `net.lockstep`. (ADR-8146, ADR-8209)
- Lockstep sessions: silent seats step with the game's `MissingInputPolicy`, late inputs merge into the next frame
  (`InputRules::merge_late`), agreed leave and match end, silence by the session's `TimeoutPolicy`, late join and
  reconnect with catch-up (`LockstepHost::catch_up`, `resume_token`/`resume`, 120 s resume window), and late-stamp
  detection (`HostEvent::LateStamp`, `LockstepHost::note_transport_rtt`). (ADR-8212, ADR-8209)
- Rollback lockstep for small action matches: `LockstepMode::Rollback`, `LockstepConfig::rollback(hz)`, `RollbackRunner`
  and `RollbackGame`, with rollback counters in `net.lockstep`; four seats on bad Wi-Fi for 10 minutes had no desync.
  The game owns its rollback through `regia_net::rollback::RollbackLog` (snapshots, inputs, `reconcile`, `amend_inputs`)
  and `VisualOffset`; agent method `net.rollback`. (ADR-8361, ADR-7953)
- A dedicated server can be the frame authority of a lockstep match, headless and without the ECS:
  `regia_net::lockstep::server` (`LockstepServer`, `LockstepObserver` stepped on its own thread and voting in the
  quorum, `ObserverThrottle`, `LockstepServerSettings`), `server_host::{LockstepHostGame, LockstepSeatLink}`, the
  `server.toml` `[lockstep]` section, a `lockstep` object on `/health` and `server.status`, console command `lockstep`
  and agent method `server.lockstep_status`. (ADR-8558)
- `regia_net::lockstep_server` holds the lockstep server game type (`regia_net::lockstep::server` re-exports it), and a
  40-seat soak runs it on the `bad-wifi`, `mobile-4g`, `lockstep-mix` and `hostile` profiles. (ADR-8656)
- Desync detection that names the subsystem: `regia_net::desync` (`DesyncHash` with up to 64 named subsystem hashes,
  `SubsystemRing`, `DesyncReport`), `LockstepSeat`/`LockstepHost::record_state`, and `HostEvent::DesyncProbed` naming
  the first diverged subsystem, tick and last agreed tick. (ADR-8211)
- Every desync reproducible offline: the `*.desync.ron` bundle (`DesyncBundle::from_host`/`from_seat`, `write_to_dir`),
  the `DesyncReplay` trait and `ReplayRegistry`, `regia net desync-replay <bundle> [--json]`, the engine's reference
  desync simulation (`desync::sample`), and agent methods `net.desync` and `net.desync_inject` (development and test
  builds). (ADR-8455)

#### Transport

- Transport v2 acknowledgments: never-reused 62-bit packet numbers, ack frames with up to 32 ranges and the ack delay,
  delayed acks (every second data packet or 10 ms), RFC 9002 loss detection, opt-in receive timestamps and a second
  copy of every small (≤ 256 B) reliable fragment and repair. Small reliable messages at 5 % loss reach p95 at about
  1.1x the one-way delay. (ADR-8184, ADR-8400)
- Transport rate control (`regia_net::cc`): a delay- and loss-aware rate controller and pacer on every connection (loss
  cuts only with congestion evidence), an essential-class floor of 16 KiB/s up and 32 KiB/s down, a circuit
  breaker that marks a starved peer interrupted, a shared-uplink controller for listen servers
  (`ServerConfig::listen_server`, `shared_uplink`, `uplink_bytes_per_second`) and 97 % bottleneck headroom; replication
  sizes its frames to `NetServer::send_allowance`. `config/net.ron` `session.rate_control` configures it and
  `LinkConfig::rate.enabled = false` turns it off. 64 clients on a 20 Mbit/s uplink: worst staleness p99 3 ticks (was
  215). (ADR-8301, ADR-8400, ADR-8490)
- Reliable streams: up to 32 per connection, `Channel::Stream(StreamId)`, the engine stream table (`ENGINE_STREAMS`,
  eight engine streams, ids 0-7) plus up to 24 game streams in `LinkConfig::streams` (ids 8-31), ordered or unordered,
  with per-stream reassembly, ordering, flow-control credit and stall clock; a loss on one stream never delays another,
  and the background bulk stream fills only the path's bandwidth-delay product (ADR-8302)
- A stream scheduler with strict priority by class, weighted round robin, per-class reliable message caps (240/s, chat
  30/s, `StreamSpec::msgs_per_s`) and background anti-starvation; `SocketReport`/`DatagramSocket::socket_report` and
  `socket_drops` counters. (ADR-8302, ADR-8407)
- UDP socket buffers and DSCP EF: UDP buffers of 2 MiB receive / 512 KiB send with a DSCP EF mark where allowed;
  `SocketBufferSettings`, `SocketBufferReport`, `UdpTransport::{bind_with, from_socket_with, buffer_report}`, constants
  `DEFAULT_RECV_BUFFER_BYTES` (2 MiB), `DEFAULT_SEND_BUFFER_BYTES` (512 KiB), `MAX_SOCKET_BUFFER_BYTES`
  (64 MiB) and `DSCP_EF_TOS`, and the read-only agent method `net.socket_buffers`. (ADR-7998, ADR-8407)
- Opt-in path MTU discovery (RFC 8899) with black-hole fall back to 1,200 bytes: `server.toml` `[net] mtu_probing`,
  `LinkOptions::mtu_probing`, `LinkConfig::mtu_probing` (ADR-8300)
- Validated address changes (RFC 9000 §§ 8-9): a new address is probed before the endpoint moves, traffic to it is
  capped at 3x what it sent, and clients follow a moving server; NAT rebinds and Wi-Fi to cellular moves follow in about
  one round trip (ADR-8299)
- Side-specific version refusals (`ClientEvent::VersionMismatch`, `LinkEvent::VersionMismatch`, `NetGame::GAME_VERSION`)
  and a frozen plaintext version probe (`version-probe` v1) answered by servers and the relay; `NetLink` checks the
  replicated schema and content hash (`content_differs`) (ADR-7985)
- `TimeoutPolicy`: one interrupted/restored event pair per outage, a 30 s startup grace, paced outage recovery, and
  `NetInterrupted` replicated only after `NetPlugin::replicate_interrupted_marker` (ADR-7950, ADR-7995)
- Bounded reliable reassembly: per-connection (`LinkConfig::connection_buffer_bytes`, 2 MiB) and server-wide
  (`ServerConfig::server_buffer_bytes`) caps, a fatal `fragment_too_long`, and a per-stream stall deadline stretched up
  to 4x by the peer's measured loss (ADR-7981, ADR-8521, ADR-8302)
- Reliable input lane: `NetClient::send_input` sends an input too large for one datagram on the reliable channel, up to
  `MAX_RELIABLE_INPUT_BYTES` (16 KiB), and it reaches the server's `TickDriver` intact on its tick. Also
  `NetClient::input_bundle_budget`, `InputHistory::{bundle_within, bundles_within, len, is_empty, newest_tick}`,
  `InputBundle::last_tick`, `BUNDLE_HEADER_BYTES`, `MAX_INPUT_BUNDLES_PER_SEND` and
  `EndpointTotals::{input_bundles_trimmed, reliable_inputs}` (also in `net.status` `totals`). (ADR-7896)
- `ServerEvent::Rejected { peer, kind, error }` and `RejectKind` (`input_too_long`, `input_malformed`,
  `message_malformed`, `unknown_lane`) report every undecodable message a peer sends, before any `PeerLeft` it causes;
  `TickDriver::{has_input, ready_for}` answer input readiness for one peer or a chosen set without taking anything.
  (ADR-7892)
- `inputs_too_far_ahead` in endpoint totals and peer rows counts input samples rejected beyond the server's future input
  horizon. (ADR-7949)
- Inbound policing on the server (`ServerConfig::inbound`, `InboundRateConfig`): packet and byte buckets per peer
  charged before decryption, a message bucket, a fair per-receive slice, and removal with `DisconnectReason::Flood`
  after two seconds over rate; `NetServer::{peer_inbound, inbound_rates}`. (ADR-8181)
- `regia_net` builds without the ECS: a default-on `ecs` feature carries replication, prediction, interpolation, lag
  compensation, the recorder, `NetSession`, `NetLink`, the lobby plugin and the dedicated-server host;
  `--no-default-features` builds the core (wire, connection, crypto, handshake, `NetServer`/`NetClient`, tick,
  congestion, relay, join codes, port mapping, discovery, snapshot delta, sessions and lobbies) with no ECS, app, scene,
  asset or platform crate. Net messages and `#[derive(NetMessage)]` build without it too (`NetMessageRegistry::{decode,
  validate}`, `ValidateContext::core`), with the headless example `net_core_headless`. (ADR-8032, ADR-8501)

#### Replication and prediction

- Replication: a 16 KiB per-entity record cap with withdraw-and-return, an RTT-based in-flight timeout, compact
  quantized transform records (replication protocol 4) with deltas against the acknowledged baseline, velocity-predicted
  position residuals, and an optional extrapolation gate (`NetProfile::extrapolation_gate_m`, off by default) with
  steady records that clients keep extrapolating. A walking crowd costs 9.0 bytes per moving entity update (was 35.8)
  and the default budget carries 142 walkers per send tick (was 45). `net.state_channel` reports `transform_record`,
  round trips and oversize state (`rtt_ticks`, `in_flight_timeout_ticks`, `oversize_pending`, `too_large_entities`);
  `ServerReplication::too_large_entities`. (ADR-8419, ADR-8467, ADR-8522)
- Distance send-rate tiers per profile (`NetProfile.rate_tiers`) and combat heat (`net_note_combat`); `net.bandwidth`
  reports `tier_held`, `tier_sent` and `interest`, `net.profiles` reports `rate_tiers`. The 128-connection,
  20,000-entity bench (`net_relevance_bench`) gathers in 4.11 ms mean (was 5.21). (ADR-8031)
- Client entity cap: a client keeps state for at most `MAX_CLIENT_ENTITIES` (65536) entities or a lower
  `ClientStateChannel::entity_cap`, and a server that spawns past it ends the session with `protocol_error`. The server
  warns once per connection at 90 % of the cap (`ReplicationCounters::relevant_near_cap`), and `net.state_channel`
  reports `entity_cap`, `tombstones`, `ownership_held`, `spawns_over_cap` and `cap_evicted`. (ADR-8760, ADR-8850)
- Replication for a simulation outside the ECS: `regia_net::external_replication` (`ExternalReplication` with per-client
  relevance, priority, rate tiers, a byte budget and an acknowledged-baseline stream; `ExternalInterpolator` on the
  client), the shared `regia_net::interest` module, and `SnapshotEncoder::encode_selecting` with `RecordSelection {
  defer, force }`. (ADR-8186)
- `SnapshotDecoder::reset` and `SnapshotDecoder::is_rebasing` (ADR-7983)
- Prediction and timing: a server-to-client `LinkFeedback` with a steered input lead (`NetClient::input_lead_ticks`,
  `due_input_ticks`, `ClientEvent::InputsLate`), a client tick estimate that never runs backward, interpolation clock
  spike filtering, unacknowledged-only and loss-aware input redundancy, time dilation as a time scale with exact input
  capture, late inputs merged into the next tick (`NetInput::merge_late`, `NetCodec::merge_late`), and RTT-scaled
  rollback depth: client prediction stays on at high round trips, the rollback depth scales with RTT
  (`ClientPrediction::resim_depth`, up to `MAX_HISTORY_TICKS` 128) and the clock waits for the first transport RTT
  sample; `net.prediction` reports `resim_depth`, `snaps` and `last_correction_m`. (ADR-8077, ADR-8312, ADR-8217,
  ADR-8219)
- The interpolation delay gains a burst-loss term, and proxies the bandwidth budget defers extrapolate by their own
  update cadence instead of stopping and going (`net.interpolation` `loss_gap_ms`, `net.proxy_timeline`
  `update_interval_ticks`). (ADR-8418)
- Per-shooter lag-compensation windows (`regia_net::lagcomp::window`): a carried view tick is checked against `min(RTT +
  input lead + interpolation delay + 1 tick + allowance, max_rewind_ms)` and clamped and counted per shooter;
  `lagcomp.stats` returns `windows`. (ADR-8315)
- Refusals the replication runtime decides are named with their sender and charged: `NetNode::reject`,
  `NetNode::take_rejections`, `NetSender::reject_peer`, `VoiceRelay::take_rejected`, and `net.status` `rejections` on a
  server (ADR-7984, ADR-8438)
- Fog relevance filter (`regia_framework::fog_relevance`, feature `net`): a server never replicates an enemy to a client
  whose team cannot see it, with linger and lead ticks and an event policy (ADR-8524)
- Session lifecycle: join-critical entities ride the replication join frame outside the spawn budget,
  `NetPersistAcrossTravel` keeps entities and their `NetId` across a travel, `regia run` hands a dev
  `REGIA_NET_MANIFEST` to the game, mounted mods join the manifest, and dev `protocol_mismatch` names the differing
  types (ADR-7212)
- Remote players become `Player { kind: Remote }` with their own `PlayerController`, and possession sets the pawn's
  `NetOwner` (`regia_framework` feature `net`); `session.state` reports `join_snapshot` and `travel` (ADR-7212)
- Clients drop the old level at `TravelBegin` (a keep list of persistent `NetId`s and a floor below which ids are old
  level), and the reliable tick stream drops in-flight old-level records instead of showing the copies again
  (`session.state` `travel { despawned, persistent, tombstoned }`) (ADR-7259, ADR-7295)
- Net profiles load through the asset server: built-ins at `engine://net/profiles/<name>.netprofile.ron`, project
  `.netprofile.ron` files cook and ship in the pak (ADR-7232)
- Mod version text: `ModsVfs` reads each mod's `luamod.ron` `version` into the session manifest, and a mismatch reads
  `mod:<id>: different (client <v>, server <v>)` (ADR-7259)
- An engine prefab spawner for Prefab spawn records, `CameraRig::follow` (`Smoothed` default or `Simulated`, also in
  `camera.get`/`camera.set_mode`), and `NetSessionPeers` (ADR-7213)

#### Sessions, reconnect and hosting

- Dormant resume for `NetLink` games: a timed-out player stays dormant for `LinkOptions::resume_grace_ms` (60 s) with
  its `PeerId`, entities and ownership, its entities carry `NetInterrupted`, and `NetLink::resume()` brings it back as
  the same peer; `NetGraceHook` decides at grace end. Agent method `net.resume`. (ADR-8298)
- Automatic client reconnect after a timeout: fresh handshakes with the resume ticket (`NetLink`) or reconnect token
  (`NetSession`), 1 s doubling to 8 s apart with jitter for the 60 s grace, over the transport the client
  connected with (`ReconnectPolicy`, `LinkEvent::Reconnecting`, `NetLink::connect_via` with a socket opener).
  `net.status` shows `state: "reconnecting"`, and the net-game sample and template show a `link_banner` HUD overlay.
  (ADR-8352)
- Session resume: a live reconnect token takes its own slot over (same `PeerId`), `resume()` reconnects over the
  original transport (`SocketOpener`, `NetSession::connect_via`), and backpressured reliable sends are held instead of
  ending the link (`backpressured` in `session.state`) (ADR-8180)
- `GameRulesSessionRules` (feature `net`): a dedicated host ends the match from `GameRules::is_match_over` or
  `MatchRules` and rotates through `.playlist.ron` rows of map, mode and options (ADR-8345)
- `regia package --server` also writes `deploy/regia-server.service`, `deploy/regia-relay.service`,
  `deploy/regia-hub.service` and `tier.ron` (the `low` install tier)
- NAT traversal from the game socket: on a protocol 3 relay a join by code is ICE-shaped (candidates, paced connectivity
  checks from both game sockets, the relay winning at 1.5 s if no direct pair completes); `regia_net::nat`
  (`Candidate`, `CheckAgent`, `CheckPacer`, `NatReport`) and `relay::SimNat` with every RFC 4787 behavior. In the
  simulated NAT matrix every pair joins, directly wherever the NATs allow it. (ADR-8340)
- Hosting flow for a dedicated server with `--relay HOST:PORT`: port mapping by PCP, NAT-PMP and UPnP IGD raced off the
  host loop and renewed at half-life, a port-forward self-check through the relay, and an uplink capacity estimate
  (`regia_net::port_map`, `regia_net::host_check`, agent method `net.host_check`). (ADR-8370)
- Relay protocol 2: one relay allocation per client with permissions, refresh by key, per-host (40), per-IP (8),
  port-pool and bandwidth caps; `RelayServer::bind`, `RelayStatus`, `--relay local` with 64 allocation ports and the
  dedicated-server `relay` console command (ADR-7982, ADR-8183)
- Join by code with relay fallback: `NetSession::connect(ConnectTarget::JoinCode(..))` and `connect_by_code_on` try the
  host and switch to an allocation at 1.5 s on one socket; the host registers from its game socket
  (`server_host::relay_split`) and confirms each relayed client, so a host behind a NAT is joinable by code. Agent
  `net.relay_status`. (ADR-8183)
- LAN discovery and store-neutral join admission: `DiscoveryConfig` (`magic` and `port`, default `RGNA` and UDP 7900), a
  real `LanSocket`, address join codes (`regia_net::address_join`, 12-hour default expiry), a join ticket hook
  (`JoinTicketCheck`, `TicketVerdict::{Admit, Refuse, Pending}`, `NetServer::set_ticket_check`,
  `NetServer::finish_ticket`) and `regia_steam::SteamJoinTicketCheck` with `LiveSteamTickets` under the `steamworks`
  feature. (ADR-7954, ADR-7989)

#### Lobbies, stores and Steam

- Self-hosted lobby hub (`regia_net::lobby::hub`, core build): `HubServer` (presence, a lobby directory with deltas,
  invites with a TTL, visibility, rate-limited chat), `HubHost` sealed under the hub's identity, and `HubLobbyBackend`
  pinned to the hub's `ServerKey`. Agent method `lobby.hub_status`; fuzz target `net.lobby_hub_msg`. (ADR-8559)
- Player identity and join brokering on the lobby hub: `regia_net::crypto::{PlayerIdentity, PlayerKey,
  verify_player_proof}` bound to the sealed link's handshake hash, `ReachOffer`/`AskReach`/owner approval and a
  `JoinPlan` (mapped, punch with relay fallback at 1.5 s, relay), `HubReach` on the member's game socket and
  `LobbyEvent::Reached`. Agent method `lobby.hub_join_status`. (ADR-8607)
- Lobby hub rate settings `HubSettings::data_per_sec`, `lobby_data_per_sec`, `global_chat_per_sec`, `list_rewind_ms` and
  `reliable_msgs_per_s`; error `hub_data_rate_limited`; `HubServer::resync(member)` sends a session's room, invites,
  open asks and a directory snapshot after a gap; `HubHost` holds a dormant peer's session within
  `ServerConfig::resume_grace_ms` and resyncs it on resume. (ADR-8633)
- `CryptoError::WeakKey` (`weak_key`) for a player proof with a low-order X25519 key. (ADR-8665)
- `LobbyPlugin` takes the lobby identity from the app's `NetSession` manifest (`NetSession::manifest()`), the values the
  content handshake checks, and keeps it in step every frame: `Lobbies::{adopt_session_identity, identity,
  identity_values, set_game, allow_unidentified, unidentified_allowed}` and `LobbyIdentity::{Unset, Session, Explicit}`;
  `lobby.status` gains `identity`. (ADR-7213, ADR-7881)
- Store-neutral platform (`regia_steam::store`): None, Steam and GOG stores behind one trait with fallback to None,
  store-prefixed account ids and `store.status`
- `regia_steam::SteamAuthSessions` (with `SteamAuthApi`, `BeginAuthError`) holds the Steam auth bookkeeping, built and
  testable without the `steamworks` feature (ADR-8502)
- `regia_steam::MockSteamNet` impairs each user's link with the soak harness's seeded profiles (`bad-wifi`, `mobile-4g`,
  `flappy` and others), with virtual time and `link_stats()`. (ADR-8351)
- Fuzz targets `net.snapshot_delta`, `net.ssdp_answer`, `net.upnp_description` and `net.state_frame_apply` (ADR-8761,
  ADR-8850)

### Platforms

- Android pack size caps (`[content] android_pack_max_size`, default 512 MiB; `android_total_max_size`, 4 GiB):
  `regia package --platform android` fails with `pack_limit` before building. The defaults are placeholders to check
  against the store's published limits. (ADR-7236)
- Android metered-network detection (`regia_platform::network::active_network_metered`) feeds the content manager, so a
  content request on a metered phone waits for `content.approve_metered`; agent `content.network` (ADR-7236)
- Optional base references: the asset tag `optional_references` turns a base asset's reference into another chunk into a
  cook warning (ADR-7236)
- Android device creation refuses to start below 8 storage buffers per stage or without fragment storage buffers, and
  logs why (`regia_render::storage_floor`) (ADR-7211)
- Android dev launch transport (ADR-8472): an Android launch profile may set `start_level` and `extra_arguments`,
  carried in one intent extra (`regia.dev_launch`) and read with `regia_framework::dev_launch::launch_arguments(world)`.
  A dev build binds a one-time agent token on a device port that the editor forwards with `adb forward`, so live-cooked
  assets push to the running device game and the device game shows in the remote Outliner and Inspector (source
  `device`). `editor.launch_status` answers `transport: { host_port }`.
- XR frame budget diagnostic `xr_over_budget` (ten frames over 0.9 of the display period) and a runtime-only
  `auto_scale` option that lowers `render_scale` by 0.1 per raise down to 0.5 (ADR-7237)
- Windows: a bare Alt tap no longer opens the system menu of the runner's window; Alt input, Alt+Space and Alt+F4 behave
  as before (`regia_platform::alt_menu`)
- `regia_platform::rename_no_replace(from, to)` (Windows): moves a file only when the name is free (ADR-8504)
- `regia_platform::local_utc_offset_seconds` gives the host's UTC offset on Linux, macOS, Android and Windows (ADR-8586)
- XeSS temporary heaps are zeroed on GPUs without Resizable BAR: CPU write of host-visible memory, driver zeroing
  through `VK_EXT_zero_initialize_device_memory`, or a GPU fill at the first execute (`regia_xess::HeapZeroing`,
  `Context::temp_heap_zeroing()`, diagnostic `REGIA_XESS_HEAP_ZEROING`). The XeSS row of `render.get_upscalers` and
  `render.get_capabilities` adds `temp_heaps`. (ADR-7820)
- XeSS packaging opt-in: `REGIA_PACKAGE_XESS=1` with an absolute `XESS_SDK` makes `regia package --platform windows`
  stage the unmodified `libxess.dll` and its two license files (`xess_package_invalid`), and `cargo xtask demo-package
  --xess-sdk <SDK>` does the same for the demo with a notices section `--verify` checks (`demo_package_xess`).
  (ADR-7820)

### Tools, pipeline and agent protocol

#### Cook and content

- Closure content for chunk cooks: `[cook.chunk_options] content = "closure"` ships only the dependency closure of the
  `entry` globs in `[cook.chunks.*]` rules plus the startup level; `include` and `tags` then place shipped assets but
  never ship one. `regia cook` prints how many assets the entry points left out, `regia assets chunks --json` adds
  `content` and `unshipped`, `regia assets why` answers `reason: "unshipped"`, and the editor's Content Chunks panel
  gains an **Unshipped** list. `content = "all"` stays the default. (ADR-8092)
- One closure root resolver (`regia_asset_pipeline::closure_roots::resolve_roots`) for `regia cook`, `regia assets
  chunks`, the Content Chunks panel and `regia package --profile`: typed extraction of UI layouts and levels, root
  reports with coverage, and `regia assets why` roots (ADR-8477)
- Required-edge content integrity: chunk and profile shipping reject incomplete required dependencies with root paths
  and counters (`chunks::check_dependency_integrity`, `check_assignment_integrity`)
- Cook diet: `[content.diet]` (and `[profiles.<name>.diet]`) in `Regia.toml` caps texture edges per glob
  (`texture_caps`, `default_max_edge` 4096), drops finest mesh LODs until a profile fits `max_triangles`
  (1 500 000), picks one member of variant groups and reads old path prefixes as new ones. `regia package
  --profile` writes `diet-report.json` and a summary line; agent method `assets.diet_report`. (ADR-8461)
- Asset id manifest `.assetids.ron` (`asset-ids` v1): `regia assets ids export` records the ids of sources kept out of
  version control, `regia assets ids import` writes missing `.meta` sidecars back without replacing a conflicting one,
  and `regia cook --ids FILE` imports before cooking so a fresh checkout keeps every id. Agent methods
  `assets.ids_export` and `assets.ids_import`. (ADR-8460)
- Overlay asset roots: `[cook.overlay_roots] paths` (or `REGIA_COOK_OVERLAY_ROOTS`) fill a glTF's missing relative
  buffers and images from the earliest overlay root that has them, then cook one tree; `CookReport.overlay_fill`,
  `overlay_fill.json` and the read-only agent method `cook.overlay_roots`. Off by default. (ADR-8155)
- Delivery contracts: `.contract.ron` (`delivery-contract` v1) per asset class sets unit scale, axes, ground origin, the
  LOD chain with triangle budgets, and kit checks (joint influences, required nodes, sockets and clips, material counts,
  texture edges, footprint, socket clearance). `assets.check_contract` judges an observation,
  `regia_asset_pipeline::contract_observe::observe_gltf` measures a `.gltf`/`.glb`, and `regia assets contract-check
  --contract FILE --report DIR --asset FILE` writes `report.md` and `report.json`. (ADR-8401, ADR-8450)
- Custom data assets: `#[derive(Asset)]` and `App::register_data_asset::<T>()` make a reflected type a `.data.ron` asset
  (format `data-asset` v1) that cooks and loads with `DataAssets::load_path` (ADR-7232)
- Counted built-in assets: `CpuMesh`, `CookedTexture`, `CookedImage` and `CookedStandardMaterial` load as counted
  `StrongHandle`s, unload after the grace frames, drop their CPU copy after upload unless `keep_cpu_data`, count toward
  budgets, take a view of a mapped pak's bytes and record load forms; `assets.handles` lists them with pinned counts and
  reference cycles (ADR-7256, ADR-7284)
- `Vfs::mount_pak` mounts a `.fpak` read-only, an `engine://` source serves text assets shipped in code, and agent
  methods `window.get`, `window.set`, `vfs.mounts`, `vfs.exists`, `vfs.list`, `vfs.read` and `vfs.mount_pak` arrive
  (ADR-7232)
- `[cook] texture_quality = "draft"` cooks textures without their own `.meta` quality at the low ladder (ADR-7217)
- `regia cook --texture-tier NAME=MAX_EDGE_PX` (repeatable) writes alternative download sets that keep the capped tail
  of each material texture's mip chain with identical payload bytes and asset ids (ADR-8059, ADR-8144)
- Texture import rules and image `.meta` take `heightfield: Some(true)` to keep a standalone L16 heightfield beside a
  glTF material texture (ADR-8143)
- glTF importer settings take `clips: [(animation, skip, name, ranges)]` (applied to glTF/GLB and the GLB that FBX and
  OBJ convert to), `meshes` and `materials` in the `.meta`; `gltf_part_keys` lists a model's mesh and material keys
  without cooking (ADR-7904, ADR-8376).
- `GltfImporterSettings::from_meta_string_converted`: the lenient settings parse the converted FBX/OBJ path uses.
- A mesh source (glTF and the FBX, OBJ, PLY and USD sources that cook through it) selects a `lod_classes.toml` class by
  its filename stem when no explicit `lod_class` extra or `[[assign]]` rule does. (ADR-8054)
- `regia_asset_pipeline` Cargo features `http_cache`, `fbx_import`, `usd_import`, `svg_import` and `audio_transcode`
  (all on by default): a player build with `default-features = false` cooks glTF at run time without the TLS stack and
  the FBX, USD, SVG and Vorbis importers. Example `runtime_gltf_cook`. (ADR-7972)
- `PakWriteOptions::min_ratio`: with compression, a blob lz4 shrinks by less than this fraction is stored raw and read
  as a view of the mapping; `[cook.chunks.<name>] compress = false` writes one chunk's pak raw (ADR-7971)
- `regia_asset_pipeline::pack_cooked_directory_report` and `CookedPack { paks, skipped }` list the paks written and the
  cooked assets in no pak (`UNMATCHED_CHUNK` names the placeholder). (ADR-7612)
- Chunk length check at mount: a packaged chunk pak whose length is not its manifest `ChunkEntry.size`, or that does not
  open, is not mounted (`AssetServer::refused_chunks()` codes `chunk_size_mismatch` and `chunk_unreadable`;
  `content.chunks` reads `Failed(size_mismatch)`). An unchanged install costs one stat per pak
  (`ContentManager::scan_counts`). (ADR-8810, ADR-8860)
- `regia engine chunk plan|cut|pack|verify` and the `release-chunks` v1 format: release trees cut into deterministic
  `.tar.zst` chunks under a byte cap (default 1.9 GiB) (ADR-8404)
- `regia prepare --scene <level>` (with `--project` and `--target`) warms a game's maps before play, and `regia package
  --prepare-scene` records them in `build_info.json`; hosts run `prepare::run_on` behind a "Preparing shaders" page
  (`render.pipeline_warm` `prepare`) (ADR-8453)
- Feedback bundles can carry the renderer's last saved `.fpc` pipeline cache, and `regia package` collects caches from
  feedback ZIPs, loose files and directories, keeping the largest valid blob per GPU/driver/shader/wgpu key;
  `--pipeline-cache-reference` selects caches of the current game build
- `regia upgrade-project --write --backup <dir>` copies the project's source files to a dated folder before migrating;
  `--json` reports `backup`. (ADR-7928)
- `regia_asset_pipeline::loc_exchange` is the PO/XLIFF export and import shared by `regia loc` and the editor.
  (ADR-7677)
- `regia table to-json` and `regia table from-json`; `regia_asset_pipeline::table_json` converts data and curve tables
  between CSV, JSON and RON (ADR-7855).
- `regia_asset_pipeline::cook_lock_holder(project_root)`. (ADR-8366)

#### Lint, validation and package hygiene

- Source-lint policy `config/source_lint.ron` and one evidence report (compact JSON and SARIF 2.1.0) for geometry,
  material and texture lint, with agent methods `asset.content_lint_rules` and `asset.content_lint_policy` (ADR-8248).
  `regia assets lint-geometry` and `regia assets validate --target --report --cancel-file` share the cook and package
  scan (ADR-8390).
- glTF geometry and rig lint: read-only `asset.gltf_lint` and `regia_asset_pipeline::gltf_lint` report duplicate mesh
  geometry, UV-aware near-coincident triangles and rig-channel warnings, with configurable tolerances and work limits
  (ADR-8068)
- Robust glTF coincidence mode (`settings.mode: "robust"`) with explicit work budgets and `mesh_evidence` rows
  (ADR-8347); shared glTF lint and content-tool file-input budgets (`input_limits`, `input_usage`) with cooperative
  cancellation (ADR-8226)
- glTF material lint: `regia assets lint-materials` (with `--fix`, `--json`, `--settings`, `--skip-staging`,
  `--profile`) and agent methods `asset.gltf_material_lint` and `asset.gltf_material_fix` check factors, ORM, volume
  transmission, emission and extension declarations; profiles `standard` and `compatibility`; fixes keep the authored
  color-times-strength product (ADR-8061)
- Material lint checks texture indices, UV channels, slot color space and volume and IOR domains, and reports
  `compatible` and per-extension support (ADR-8392); `MaterialLintProfile::{Standard, Compatibility}` with each finding
  labeled `preserves_appearance` or `changes_appearance` (ADR-8250)
- Texture lint evidence: `texture_wrong_compression`, `texture_missing_mip`, `texture_impossible_dimensions`,
  `texture_usage_mismatch` and `texture_budget_unknown` (ADR-8536)
- The built-in `naming_rules` validator reports `validate_name_case_collision` when two asset paths differ only by ASCII
  case. (ADR-7961)
- `regia assets validate --staged` validates what is staged in git (text assets parse from the index; with a cooked
  registry the validators run over the staged paths), and `regia hooks install|uninstall|status` manages a git
  pre-commit hook that runs it (`--force` keeps and later restores a foreign hook) (ADR-7789).
- Package hygiene for shipping desktop, server and Android packages: forbidden strings, deny terms and secret signatures
  (decoded pak payloads included), absolute build paths scrubbed from text assets, internal notes dropped, `[package]
  stage_allow`, and dev-flag or cheat-probe binaries refused. `regia package --audit-dir DIR` and `agent.package`
  `audit_dir` audit an extracted package without building.
- Package hygiene rules with stable ids and BLAKE3 definition digests, so an allowance never follows another rule
  (ADR-8062, ADR-8247); an aggregate audit memory budget with streamed scan windows (ADR-8244); scan-local location ids
  so findings never show raw sensitive paths (ADR-8229)
- Final-artifact package hygiene: shipping packages audit the finished archive in place (tar.gz, dir, AppImage, zip,
  APK, AAB, nested `.fpak`) and write `<artifact>.hygiene-receipt.json` with a BLAKE3 artifact hash; `regia package
  --audit-artifact` and `--verify-receipt` (ADR-8348)
- `regia package parity` diffs a dev access trace and an extracted-package trace along one scripted path (`--classify`
  reads strace text); read-only agent `package.parity`

#### Agent protocol

- `agent.cook` and `agent.package` run `regia cook` / `regia package` as jobs with progress lines (desktop only;
  `confirm: true` for package); `regia package --json`, `regia run --json` and a plain `regia cook --json` print one
  result object (ADR-7217)
- `seeing.screenshot` `target: "camera"` (one camera entity offscreen) and `target: "ui"` (the runtime UI alone), plus
  `colliders`, `light_radii`, `bounds` and `grid` overlays on every runtime target (ADR-7217)
- `play.frame` returns the newest picture of a running game as a scaled PNG with its frame number (headless apps fill
  `regia_agent::play_view::FrameFeed`), and `play.mute` mutes its root audio bus (ADR-7831).
- `play.wait_until` and `play.expect` read the `Content` predicate resource (`requests.N.state`)
- `assets.push { asset | source | shader, data }` (Assets permission, at most 16 MiB) writes a cooked asset into a
  running game's cooked cache and hot-reloads it, stages a validated shader, or raises `SourceAssetChanged` for a source
  under `assets/`. (ADR-7836)
- Opt-in runtime asset access trace: `REGIA_ASSET_TRACE` logs cooked-cache reads, pak opens and payload reads and
  manifest lookups, and `assets.access_trace` reports recent events and sink health
- Agent methods `content.check_placement` (authored points against a flat height) and `content.install_tier` (built-in
  residency caps `minimal`, `low`, `high`, `ultra`)
- `profiler.compare { a, b, top? }` and `regia_telemetry::profile_compare::compare` (byte-identical output);
  `memory.report` `assets_by_type` (`AssetServer::memory_by_type`); `regia_core::profiling` adds
  `FrameProfiler::frame_times`, `frame_spans` and `flame_context`. `profiler.frame` takes `since_frame` and `entities:
  N`. (ADR-9740, ADR-7849)
- `regia_agent::call_local` and `LocalReply` dispatch an agent call in-process with a client name;
  `regia_agent::AuditObserver` lets hosts observe dispatch (`ready`, `begin`, `recorded`), with `AuditEntry`,
  `AuditLog`, `ChangeLog` and `unix_ms_now` re-exported (ADR-7754, ADR-7841).
- `regia_agent` exposes `SceneEditHook`, `SceneEditHandler`, `server::ConnectionLease`, `server::RequestLifecycle` and
  `server::start_at` for hosts that bind their own history or port.
- `agent.changes { limit }` pages the change log (default 200, at most 1000), and `regia_agent::params_hash` gives the
  audit log's hash of a request's params (ADR-7454)
- `errors.explain` answers `unit_view_bad` and `server_no_rotation`.
- `TracePlugin` (in `regia::DefaultPlugins`) records a trace from launch with `--record-trace`, `REGIA_RECORD_TRACE`, or
  an Android `record-trace` marker file; Android traces go where `adb pull` reaches them (ADR-9070)
- `regia_framework::report_sources` lists configured log, crash, feedback and pipeline-cache artifacts (including
  preserved native crash dumps) with parsed cache keys, scan issues and explicit truncation (`ReportSourceDirs` with
  `from_world` and `list`, `ReportSourceOptions`, `ReportSourceKind`, `ReportArtifact`, `ReportSourceIssue`,
  `ReportSources`); agent method `diagnostics.report_sources` (read permission). (ADR-8058)
- `regia_framework::packaged_config::PackagedConfig` exposes a package's startup metadata and diagnostics, and
  `boot_movies::BootMoviesOrigin` the boot list's source; `render.get_profile`, `quality.get_detected` and
  `media.boot_state` return a `configuration` object. (ADR-7893)

#### Logs, gates and release tools

- `regia logs triage` stitches tester upload pieces in source-offset order and reports per-session exit state, frame and
  network summaries, event rates, silence gaps and storms from JSONL, folders, ZIPs and numbered ZIP parts; `--out`
  writes scrubbed artifacts. The read-only agent method `logs.triage` (MCP tool `triage_logs`) runs the same code on
  supplied text bundles. (ADR-8442)
- `regia logs perf-gate --thresholds <toml>` judges session summaries (frame-time p50/p95/p99, hitches per minute, peak
  RSS and VRAM) per map, resolution class and tier, with `--baseline` regression checks and `--run` for traces;
  read-only agent `logs.perf_gate`
- `regia play-gate`: key tracks (`.track.ron`, `key-track` v1) replayed against log lines on a virtual clock (`check`,
  `replay`), and `run` installs a package into a fresh home and drives physical keys through `play.inject_input`
  scancodes. Page `book/agents/play-gate.md`. (ADR-8403, ADR-8452)
- `regia contact-sheet`: a labeled JPEG grid and a luma CSV from `--frames`, `--dirs` or `--pairs` (ADR-8036)
- `regia test --timeout-s SECONDS` gives the status poll its own budget (default 60 s), and
  `regia_cli::session::Poll::with_budget` computes a poll's bound for a budget
- `cargo xtask check-formats --since-tag <tag-or-file>` compares the format table with the last release's
  (`docs/formats/format-hashes-v0.5.0.ron`) and fails on a released format that changed shape without a bump, moved two
  versions in one release, went back or left the registry. (ADR-7724)
- `cargo xtask check-net-core` asserts that `regia_net` without default features reaches no ECS, app, scene, asset,
  platform or audio crate, then builds and runs the core and its headless example; preflight and CI run it. (ADR-8501)
- `cargo xtask api-snapshot` lists a derive inside `#[cfg_attr(<predicate>, derive(..))]` with its condition instead of
  skipping it.
- `regia_scene::level_dir::is_upgrade_stage_dir_name` (ADR-8861)

### Audio, media, UI and accessibility

#### Sequencer

- Sequence authoring in the editor: New sequence, Add track (every kind), reorder, rename and remove tracks, markers at
  the playhead, Bind selected and Remove binding, **Key**, **Auto-key** and **Preview in level** (bit-exact restore;
  level saves never hold previewed values); Inspector ▸ **Add to sequence** keys a field. Edits live in
  `regia_framework::sequencer::edit::tracks`. Agent: `editor.seq_new`, `editor.seq_track_edit`,
  `editor.seq_binding_edit`, `editor.seq_key`, `editor.seq_preview`. (ADR-9710)
- Sequencer **Timeline**: a dope sheet of every keyed track and section span (select, move with frame snap, Alt-scale,
  copy and paste), markers (with CSV import and export), cuts with fades and ripple edits, and a transport (J/K/L,
  in/out range, timecode); one undo entry per gesture, 200 tracks draw only the rows in view. Batch edits live in
  `regia_framework::sequencer::edit::timeline`. Agent: `editor.seq_keys`, `editor.seq_marker_edit`,
  `editor.seq_cut_edit`, `editor.seq_transport`, `editor.seq_timeline_state`. (ADR-7322, ADR-8640, ADR-7847)
- Sequencer event tracks, track lock, mute and solo (ADR-8188): `Event` keys fire `SequenceEventFired` when forward play
  crosses them (once per loop pass, also on `skip_to`, never on a seek or reverse play); `locked: true` blocks every
  edit of a track (`seq_track_locked`); solo is editor session state. Track rows have a menu (Rename, Duplicate, Delete,
  Lock, Mute, Solo, Move, Select all keys; Shift+F10). The UI animation editor gets a dope sheet with multi-track key
  moves (`editor.ui_anim_edit` op `move_keys`).
- The sequence preview plays pitch-preserving sound sections stretched, applies Doppler and occlusion with the runtime's
  math, and shows fullscreen and mesh video sections, so it matches the runtime and render-out; `editor.seq_preview`
  reports `pitch`, `doppler`, `occlusion`, `stretched`, `video_opens` and `media`. `regia_media::publish_frames_once`.
  (ADR-8591, ADR-7847)
- Sequence ▸ Render movie… renders a saved `.seq.ron` as a cancellable editor job with the same frames and hashes as
  `render.out_job` (`editor.render_movie`, `editor.render_movie_status`); a **Restore on stop** row
  (`editor.seq_restore_on_stop`); shot camera position and rotation keys in the Shots panel.

#### UI documents, themes and widgets

- `.ui.ron` v11 gains theme-token bindings (`(theme: "color.accent")`), animation bindings on the game clock (reduced
  motion shows the end value), a virtualized list row height taken from the template, and the image fit `tile`, with
  designer pickers, lint codes and an `editor.ui_designer_edit` `bind_source` op. (ADR-7751)
- `.ui.ron` v11 text input options (`max_chars`, a `secret` mask that refuses copy and cut, `read_only`, `filter`
  `any`/`integer`/`numeric`) and an image `fit` (`stretch`, `contain`, `cover`) (ADR-9980)
- UI document containers, markup and focus neighbors (ADR-7827): `.ui.ron` `split`, `foldable` (accordion by `group`)
  and `tabs` containers (runtime `TabPages` / `TabPage`; `ui.set_value` selects a tab), opt-in rich-text markup (`<b>`,
  `<i>`, `<u>`, `<s>`, `<color=#rrggbb>`, `<size=px>`, `<icon=name>`) and declared `nav: (up, down, left, right)`
  neighbors that arrow keys and the D-pad follow. `ui.nav_graph` nodes report `name` and `declared`.
- Layout variants (ADR-7858): `.ui.ron` `variants` pick override sets by window orientation, width and aspect
  (`regia_ui::layout_variants`); the first match applies over the base and the root re-lays out once, keeping focus and
  scroll. `ui.inspect` reports `layout_variant`.
- Disabled subtrees and screen back handling: `DisabledSubtree` / `Disabled::recursive` block pointer, focus,
  accessibility and agent input below a node (`ui.set_disabled`), and `ScreenBack` with `ScreenStack::{set_back_pop,
  back_pop}` pops a screen on back (`ui.screens` `back`, `set_back`). Documents set them with `back`, `disabled_subtree`
  and node `pivot`.
- `.uisettings.ron` stores an optional project scale rule, `scale_mode`, used by roots still on the engine default and
  by the designer (ADR-8252).
- Theme v2 style rules (ADR-8303): structured selectors over an ancestor widget type (`inside`), the node's widget type,
  variation, classes and state, applied by specificity then file order, and a keyboard-only `focus_visible` layer on any
  type or variation (`StyleClass::focus_visible`). `ui.inspect` names the winning rule, `ui.theme_get` returns `rules`
  and `ui.theme_set` edits a type's `focus_visible` layer.
- Theme `padding`, `min_height`, `max_width`, `max_height` reach layout, scrollbar color slots, caption `subtitle`
  tokens with a high-contrast caption theme, glyph atlas pages per size bucket, and `slots` in `ui.theme_list`
  (ADR-7208)
- Theme `height`, `padding_x` and `gap` reach layout, and caption tokens `subtitle.font`, `subtitle.size`,
  `subtitle.outline`, `subtitle.outline-width` style subtitles (ADR-9340)
- UI inspection: `ui.inspect_events` returns recent frame-stamped events (non-draining, at most 1,024),
  `ui.set_live_style` applies inline layout and text edits to the running game, and `ui.inspect` adds
  `properties[].sources`, `events`, `document` and `live_edits`; `editor.ui_inspector_copy_to_document` saves live edits
  as one undoable batch.
- Runtime UI events bubble to ancestors (`UiBubbledEvent`, `UiStopBubble`) and reach scripts (`ScriptEvent::Ui`); a
  `UiPointerGate` keeps clicks on UI from firing gameplay mouse bindings (ADR-7070)
- Text fields draw IME composition inline with its cursor and hand the caret rect to the OS candidate window on desktop
  (`ImeCursorArea`); Left/Right move visually in mixed-direction text; `ui.text_state` reports `preedit_cursor`,
  `ime_rect`, `visual_caret` (ADR-7208)
- Text fields: Ctrl+Left / Ctrl+Right step word edges in screen order and Home / End draw the caret at the line's visual
  edges in right-to-left and mixed text (`CaretMap::visual_word_step`, `CaretLine::rtl`) (ADR-9340)
- `regia_quick::draw2d`: an instanced, allocation-free 2D draw list for HUDs and menus (rounded, soft and gradient
  boxes, outlines and rings, anti-aliased segments, shaped text from `regia_ui`'s glyph atlas, images from four slots,
  clip rectangles) drawn with one pipeline and one draw call; `Draw2dPlugin`, a CPU `reference::rasterize` oracle and
  agent method `quick.draw2d_stats` (ADR-8458)
- Draw2d dirty regions and `HudLayer`: per-tile hashing redraws only changed tiles into a retained UI target (a steady
  HUD uploads nothing; `Draw2dSettings`, `Draw2dTiles`), and `HudLayer` adds nine screen anchors, aligned text and the
  common HUD elements. Sample `samples/hud_draw2d` and bench `hud_draw2d_bench`. (ADR-8514)
- Performance overlays anchor at any screen corner with offsets (`PerformanceOverlayPlacement`), hosts can switch the
  default UI font (`TextSystem::set_default_family`, `ui.set_default_font`), and pipeline loading widgets and a delayed
  shader notice show warm-queue progress (`regia_framework::loading_widget`)
- Agent method `ui.glyph_atlas` (pages, glyphs, wipes, `thrash`, `thrash_frames`), `GlyphAtlases::use_page` and
  `QuickRenderer::glyph_atlas_thrash()` (ADR-8832, ADR-8930)
- `UiRenderer::trim_backdrop()` releases the backdrop scratch pool at once; `UiRenderer::BACKDROP_IDLE_FRAMES` (120) is
  the automatic idle limit. (ADR-7869)
- `regia_ui::text::count_font_scan_warnings` folds fontdb's per-file warnings into one `debug` line per process.
  (ADR-7898)
- `regia_ui::ron_merge::merge_ron_text`: a comment-keeping three-way merge for any RON text.
- Translator context: `regia_ui::loc_po::FtlBlock::context` holds the comment above a message, exported to PO (`#.`) and
  XLIFF.
- `Regia.toml` `[runtime]` with `pause_on_focus_loss` and `keep_screen_on` (off by default), reported by `settings.get`
  (ADR-7070)

#### Editor UI design and accessibility

- UI designer canvas: move and resize nodes in the preview with snapping guides, typed rows for every widget kind,
  multi-select with align, distribute, group, duplicate and delete, subtree copy, cut and paste across documents, zoom,
  pan, rulers and device frames. `editor.ui_designer_edit` gains `select_many`, `transform`, `align`, `group`, `copy`,
  `cut`, `paste`. (ADR-9730)
- UI designer custom preview sizes, safe-area insets and a theme picker (kept across sessions), instance slot rows
  (`editor.ui_designer_set_instance_slot`); a Localization panel Exchange row for PO/XLIFF export and import with a
  dry-run report (`editor.loc_export`, `editor.loc_import`). (ADR-7677)
- Screen readers (Orca, NVDA, Narrator, VoiceOver) can read and operate the editor window: toolbars, dock panes named
  after their tabs, buttons, checkboxes, sections, rows, number fields and sliders; the tree is built only while a
  reader is connected (`REGIA_ACCESSKIT=0` opts out). Agent: `editor.a11y_tree`, `editor.a11y_keep_tree`,
  `editor.a11y_action`. (ADR-7746, ADR-7795)
- Keyboard lists for eleven more editor canvases (animation graph, state machine, state tree, VFX curves, conversation
  graph, UI animation keys, camera rig graph, blend spaces, animation timeline, curve editor, clip notifies) with a
  focus ring and announced role and count; Content grid arrow navigation; interface zoom commands `view.zoom_in`,
  `view.zoom_out`, `view.reset_zoom` (Ctrl+Alt+0) saved as the UI scale. (ADR-7733)
- Preferences ▸ Theme edits every theme token with a live preview and saves, imports and exports custom themes
  (`themes/<name>.editor_theme.ron`); a high-contrast theme (WCAG AAA), `appearance.text_scale` (0.8-2.0), diagnostic
  colors, and View ▸ Color Vision on the chrome and viewport. Agent: `editor.theme_save`, `editor.theme_import`,
  `editor.theme_export`, `editor.theme_list`, `editor.color_vision`. (ADR-7838)

#### Input

- Pointer claims: a mouse press that starts on a hit-testable `regia_ui` node belongs to the UI until release, so
  gameplay actions bound to that button see no press, hold or release (a press that started on the world stays the
  game's). `regia_input::PointerClaims`, the `claim_pointer_buttons` system and agent method `input.pointer_claims {
  enabled?, watch?, expect_untriggered? }`. (ADR-7976)
- UI navigation reads the `Menu` input context when declared (`InputConfig::with_menu_context`), script-field UI
  bindings (`BindSource::ScriptField`), `#[derive(InputAction)]`, and an `InputGlyph` table (`project.glyphs.ron`) with
  agent `input.glyph` (ADR-7070)
- Keyboard layout presets: `regia_input::KeyboardLayout::{Qwerty, Colemak, Azerty}` map labels to physical keys, with
  inverse labels and positional movement sources; the read-only agent query `input.keyboard_layout` needs no input
  plugin.

#### Audio and media

- Mixer effect chains, sends and snapshots (ADR-8354): the Audio Mixer dock edits each bus's effect chain (bypass,
  reorder, add, remove), post-fader sends and named snapshots with blend times, and saves `config/audio.ron`; every
  control works from the keyboard. `BusEffect::Bypassed`, `Audio::set_bus_layout`, `Audio::apply_snapshot` and snapshot
  bus mutes and send levels come with it. Agent: `editor.audio_mixer_chain`, `editor.audio_mixer_send`,
  `editor.audio_mixer_snapshot`, `editor.audio_mixer_save`.
- Pre-fader bus sends (`SendTap::PreFader`, `config/audio.ron` `tap: PreFader`): a reverb or monitor feed keeps its
  level when the fader is pulled down or the bus muted; the mixer dock has a **Pre** toggle per send (ADR-8590)
- Live EBU R128 program-loudness meter after the master limiter (momentary, short-term, integrated, range, true peak),
  allocation-free and off by default; agent `audio.program_loudness` and a Program row in the editor mixer
  (`editor.audio_mixer_get` `program`). `LoudnessMeter` measures 5.1 with BS.1770-4 weights. (ADR-7227)
- Audio patch layers: one-shot (threshold, step or `Audio::patch_trigger`), granular (Hann-windowed grains), and
  parameters bound to reflected component fields (`AudioPatchSource`); agents `audio.patch_state` and
  `audio.patch_trigger` (ADR-7226)
- Persistent audio: `Audio::start_persistent_loop` loops and spawned patches survive output suspension with their ids
  and state; `regia_audio::{polyline, layer_outputs, LayerOutputs}` evaluate patch curves (ADR-8052)
- Portal sound over cooked world geometry (`GeomSoundField`): sources in a passage are heard from the mouth along the
  portal path, rock occludes straight lines, and the listener's section arms its baked reverb; `audio.list_zones` and
  `audio.probe_zones` report `geom_field`, `portal_path` and `armed_reverb` (ADR-8416)
- Binaural (HRTF) rendering at 88.2, 96, 176.4 and 192 kHz convolves with HRIRs prepared at the mixer's rate
  (`SphereSpectra::with_rate`, at most `HRTF_MAX_RATE_TABLES` tables), so the interaural delay keeps its length;
  `HrtfControl::{mixer_rate, table_rate, stale_rate}`, `Audio::hrtf_table_rate`, and `audio.list_emitters` `hrtf_rate`
  (ADR-7461)
- Routed sound cues take `max_distance` (meters) in `config/events.ron` (`Reaction::SoundCue`); left out, the default
  50 m emitter range applies (ADR-7549)
- Audio output stall watchdog (`regia_audio::stall`, resource `AudioStallWatchdog`): a live output whose clock stands
  still for `stall_frames` (240) frames and for the `AudioStallWatchdog::min_stall` time floor (default 3 s) is
  suspended and resumed once, with doubling back-off up to `max_backoff` (16); `audio.mix` reports `stall_watchdog`
  (ADR-8830, ADR-7543)
- Audio resume retry: `AudioResumeRetry` (`RESUME_RETRY_FRAMES` 30, `RESUME_RETRY_ATTEMPTS` 20) retries a lifecycle
  resume that could not re-open the output; `audio.mix` reports `output { suspended, resume, resume_failures,
  resume_retry_in_frames }`. `regia_audio::{MAX_SOUND_SAMPLE_RATE, probe_sound_header, SoundHeader}` read a file's
  declared rate and length without decoding. (ADR-7602)
- The loopback `VoiceStream` takes a click-free 3D `VoicePlacement`, from a `VoiceStreamAnchor` entity or agent
  `audio.voice_place`; `audio.voice` reports `loopback.placement`
- Voice chat `VoiceChatEvent::DecodedFrame` (opt-in), `speakers[].entity` and `errors` in `voice_chat.status`, and a
  `ServerMuted` error event for a server-muted client (ADR-7237)
- Caption speaker labels placed by `Speaker.side` and `CaptionPolicy::honor_newlines` for translated captions (ADR-7237)
- `play.hitch_report` reports `streaming_waits` (texture streaming held or backing off, render residency waits)
- `regia_media::player::MAX_LOOPED_EVENTS_PER_TICK` (4), `container::MIN_DECLARED_DURATION_NS` (1 ms) and `impl
  Default for container::AudioParams` (the Matroska defaults). (ADR-7563)
- `regia_audio::media_feed::PcmConsumer::capacity_frames()` and, with feature `opus`,
  `regia_media::fuzzing::{opus_packet_run, OpusPacketRun}`. (ADR-7635)

### Core, licence and security

- Generic asset API: `AssetLoader` with `LoadContext` dependencies, async `load` returning reference-counted
  `StrongHandle<T>` with a grace-period unload (`unload_grace_frames`, default 120), load groups on `LoadSets`, sent
  `AssetEvent`s, `PrepareAsset`/`keep_cpu_data`, a per-type memory budget and agent `assets.handles` (ADR-7202)
- Per-thread profiler timeline: `FrameProfiler::set_timeline_enabled` (off by default) records each span's thread, name,
  start, end and depth, up to `MAX_TIMELINE_SPANS_PER_FRAME` (8,192) a frame, over a 300-frame history (`TimelineFrame`,
  `TimelineThread`, `TimelineSpan`). Agent: `profiler.timeline { frame?, frames?, threads?, max_spans? }`,
  `profiler.set_capture { timeline }`, `profiler.capture { timeline? }`;
  `regia_telemetry::profile_capture::timeline_strip` lays several frames on one axis. (ADR-7325, ADR-7409)
- `FrameProfiler` interns every ECS system name past `MAX_SYSTEMS` (2,048), so each keeps its own name in the timeline
  and flame; `profiler.frame` reports `systems` and `systems_over_cap` (ADR-7409)
- `regia::telemetry::counters::counter(name, value)` reports a named game counter to the Profiler's Monitors;
  `regia_asset::AssetServer::load_times()` reports per-asset read, decode and upload times (ADR-8141).
- Debug drawing palette: `DebugColors` and client-only `path_debug::{PathGizmos, path_segments}`, initialized by
  `DebugGizmosPlugin`; `debug.set_gizmos` takes the `paths` group.
- `TelemetryLevel::Ship` (`REGIA_TELEMETRY=ship`) makes the tester-log upload safe from a player build: an allowlisted
  session header (`SHIP_HEADER_KEYS`) with RAM and VRAM tiers instead of exact values, no hitch events, problem codes
  without message text, and a reduced `summary.json`; it also filters the `REGIA_JSON_LOG` file.
  `TelemetryLevel::from_env` and `memory_tier_gb`. (ADR-8417)
- Opt-in per-session, per-peer logs: `regia_core::log::session::PeerLog` with diffable `hash_trace` checkpoint rows,
  explicit clean completion, and bounded evidence directories for unclean previous sessions (`check_previous`).
- Opt-in ordered async peer-log writer (`SessionOptions::with_async_writer`, `AsyncWriterBounds`) with queue caps and
  counters on `regia_core::log::peer_async`; the default writer stays synchronous
- Published unclean-session reports include `privacy.txt` (policy `regia.recovery.privacy` v1), and
  `regia_core::log::session` exposes `RecoveryManifest` and readers that tell a sanitized report from a raw log
  (ADR-8494)
- Report outbox (`regia_crash::outbox`): an incremental, scrubbed session-log uploader that sends only each log's new
  tail and resends a crashed session's log once; opt in with `CrashReporter::outbox` (`report.outbox_status`) (ADR-8402)
- Opt-in Linux GNU all-thread stack snapshots and saved hang reports (`regia_crash::stack_dump`,
  `regia_platform::stack_sampling`, `write_hang_report`), listed in `diagnostics.crash_reports`
- `regia_core::log::devenv` gates developer environment reads behind the opt-in `devtools` feature, off by default and
  in release builds.
- `regia_core::brand::{KEY_PREFIX, LEGACY_KEY_PREFIX, legacy_key, key_matches, find_key}` read engine-owned keys in
  either spelling (ADR-8670)
- `cargo xtask notices`: a copied data file that cannot carry a comment holds its copy header in a `<file>.NOTICE`
  sidecar and needs a `do27-owner-code` provenance row naming the source path and commit. (ADR-8497)

### Tests, docs and tooling

#### Determinism

- `cargo xtask det-scan [--json]` scans every crate on `docs/ci/lockstep-closure.toml` for std transcendentals, direct
  `libm` calls, fast-math, `f64` parses, hash containers, rayon, SIMD types, float `min`/`max`/`clamp`, std float
  functions used as values and glam trig paths (`glam_trig`), and checks each closure crate's lockstep `clippy.toml`;
  `[[module]]` rows scan one module of a non-lockstep crate (a directory or file of a crate outside the lockstep closure
  whose code runs on the tick), and the JSON report gains `modules`. `cargo xtask preflight` runs it. (ADR-8013,
  ADR-8085, ADR-8049)
- `cargo xtask det-scan --external ROOT --crate NAME` scans a game's own crates with the same rules (`--math-module`,
  `--with-closure`, `--json`); `--list-rules` prints the rule table (ADR-8192)
- Determinism scan rules `wall_clock`, `os_random`, `env_read`, `address_order`, `thread_spawn`, `arch_cfg` and
  `float_sort`, multiline matching and reasoned waiver tests (ADR-8367)
- `cargo xtask det-golden` (`stage`, `run`, `gate`, `verify`, `--adb`) runs the determinism goldens on x86-64 Linux,
  qemu-aarch64, Android, the Mac, Windows and the Deck and compares them; a planted SIMD divergence proves the runners
  see one. Jobs `det-golden-nightly` and `det-golden-rc` and the new `docs/release/rc-checklist.md`. (ADR-8081)
- Determinism golden policy for planted negative controls and the registered `regia-det-golden-1` fixture format
  (ADR-8231)
- New engine determinism scenarios: `math_rng_fixed_vectors`, `input_log_arena`, `hover_bad_input`,
  `hover_fleet_terrain`, `hover_mixed`, `lowg_ballistic_mixed`, `projectile_mix`, `contact_pile` and
  `ground_vehicle_mix`; `det_golden` also checks the AI soak's 600-tick fingerprint, and the clean scenario list
  includes `match_rules_ctf_koth_3600`
- Cost examples in `regia_detsim`: `perception_cost`, `ai_targets_cost`, `roster_cost`, `hash_cost`, `fog_cost`,
  `contact_cost`, `hover_fleet_cost`, `replay`, `broadphase_cost --queries`/`--shots` and `scale_soak --ai`; and
  `match_rules_cost`
- `regia_world_geom` feature `stage_b` (empty today) and test `stage_a_bits_are_the_same_with_and_without_stage_b`
  (ADR-8432)

#### Networking

- `cargo xtask net-soak` runs the real server and clients on a seeded in-process impairment network (latency, jitter,
  Gilbert-Elliott loss, reordering, bandwidth caps, outages, NAT rebinds) under 28 named profiles with spec 120 bars,
  writing `result.json` and `summary.md`; dev-only crate `regia_net_soak`, page `book/agents/net-soak.md` (ADR-7899,
  ADR-8030)
- Net soak: lockstep profiles `lockstep-mix`, `lockstep-mix+action` and `lockstep-mix+bots` (a seeded wander-fire mix,
  40 seats for 600 s in the gate plan) judged by `desync_zero` and `lockstep_upload`; scale plans (`--plan
  scale|scale-gate`: 40, 64 and 128 clients, listen servers at 16 and 32), a `+hostile` variant of any profile, the real
  UDP wire (`--wire udp`), scale metrics in `result.json` per run and `net_scale_tick_p99_*` budget rows, and `cargo
  xtask net-soak --two-host HOST_A HOST_B` printing a two-machine recipe. (ADR-8209, ADR-8409, ADR-8454)
- Net time harness (`regia_net_soak::timed::TimedHub`): replication and prediction behind real `NetLink`s on per-process
  clocks in virtual microseconds, with a `TimeReport` per client (ADR-8185)
- `regia_net_soak::vectors` steps a game's per-system hash vectors through `LockstepHost`/`LockstepSeat` and names the
  first diverging row and system; the lead game's 1,200-tick steering golden is in the repository byte for byte and
  replays through the driver on 8 seats. (ADR-8307)
- `regia_net/tests/malformed_attribution.rs` covers malformed-session removal, an honest lossy client kept for
  30 minutes, an off-path spoofer and forged records; `regia_steam/tests/net_impaired_steam.rs` covers Steam sessions
  and resume on impaired links. (ADR-8606, ADR-8351)
- Stateful net fuzz targets checked against spec models: `net.connection_sequence`, `net.sealed_endpoint_sequence` (each
  with a `.hostile` variant), `net.shared_port_sequence`, `net.voice_relay_route`, `net.snapshot_sequence` and
  `net.input_bundle_budget`, listed by `fuzz.targets` and run by `fuzz.run` or `cargo xtask fuzz run --target`.
  (ADR-8468)
- Fuzz targets `net.payload_sequence`, `net.link_pair_impaired`, `net.relay.allocate`, `net.relay.refresh`,
  `net.relay_reply.*`, `net.version_probe`, `agent.cook_package_output`, `abilities.cooked`, `logic` and
  `gpu-breadcrumbs`

#### Editor

- `cargo xtask editor-smoke` launches the real editor on a throwaway sample copy and drives every panel, menu item,
  palette command and UI action through the agent protocol (200 % UI scale and light-theme passes, a keyboard pass on
  Xvfb), checking panel body crops, undo depth, `play.expect` replies and real focus changes. (ADR-7865, ADR-7955)
- `cargo xtask editor-monkey [--seeds N] [--steps M] [--seed S] [--plant panic@K|nan@K]`, planted-fault checks in the
  monkey gate run, and fuzz targets `editor.view_state`, `editor.autosave_index` and `editor.tool_manifest`. (ADR-7748)
- `agent_parity_scan` with `agent-parity-allow.ron`: a new editor control must be drawn through an action helper or
  classified; `cargo xtask editor-agent-coverage [--update]` checks every editor method's book row, page, guide topic
  and test call (ADR-7307)
- The editor's agent-parity scan covers value editors (689 sites: drag values, combo boxes, sliders, text edits, color
  buttons); an unclassified one fails `agent_parity_scan`. (ADR-7747)
- `ui_text_scan` with `ui-text-allow.txt` keeps agent method names, codes and internal names out of panel text
  (ADR-8586)
- `large_project` soak: 100 000 entities, 100 000 log lines, a 100 000-element list and 50 000 assets with
  per-panel bounds (ADR-7306)
- Tutorials as doc tests: `book_tutorials` runs the editor and packaging tutorials' steps against a headless editor and
  the CLI (`regia_test_utils::tutorial`) (ADR-9810)
- `regia_test_utils::tutorial::refuse_mutating_until`: a book tutorial page whose `# until` names a mutating method
  fails its test (ADR-7451)
- `cargo xtask book-check` checks that every `enum Panel` variant has an editor-guide page row or a reasoned no-page
  row.
- New tests: `regia_editor` `pane_tool_press` (ADR-7674), `logic_graph_dogfood`, `extension_points`, a fresh-clone
  first-import fixture, `regia_net` `playlist_fixture_copy`, `regia_net_soak` `lockstep_server_soak`, fuzz targets
  `terrain.raster_descriptor`, `terrain.source_tiles` and `ui-designer`, and a canvas scan that fails on a new painted
  canvas with no keyboard list or `CANVAS_ALLOW` row.
- A test compiles the localization bundles and compares every key in every shipped locale with the runtime that reads
  loose `.ftl` files; `master_mute_silences_output` checks the mixer's Master mute; Outliner tests cover the prefab link
  icon and override dot.

#### Rendering and GPU

- New GPU and acceptance tests: `regia_render --test load_budget`, `regia_render/tests/decal_scene_copy.rs`,
  `fsr3_067_ghosting_meets_spec27`, `fsr31_ghost_residual_holds_at_067_and_050`, `regia_pt` `stacked_floors` (a

## Changed

### Editor

#### Viewport and gizmo

- Bookmark recall moved from the bare digit to Shift+digit (bare 1-4 are view presets); Snap is a toggle that Ctrl
  inverts; Alt+LMB on the gizmo starts a duplicate drag; `editor.pick` reports `method` and never returns a locked
  entity (ADR-7204)
- The viewport drives the transform gizmo through `Gizmo::update` and `Gizmo::draw`: each press has one owner decided
  at press time, so a drag that starts beside the gizmo stays a box select or camera drag. (ADR-7674)
- The toolbar's "4 views" toggle is a Layout picker (1 to 4 panes); the wheel zooms toward the pointer by default; in a
  quad layout's orthographic pane a view key turns that pane; the live gizmo sits at the selection's world pivot.
- `editor.capture_viewport` without `debug_view` draws the pane's own view mode, and viewport agent frames take up to
  16384 px a side (was 2048); past the device limit they answer `screenshot_needs_tiles`. File ▸ Screenshot always
  captures Lit.
- A prefab dropped in the viewport lands at the drop point (it spawned at the origin), and Ctrl+Shift+click subtracts
  the clicked entity (it toggled).
- `editor.viewport_overlays` needs `edit_scene`, even for `get`; while the secondary or middle mouse button is down,
  overlay chrome leaves the hit test so orbit and pan reach the pane.
- Viewport controls draw over the pane without taking layout space, so an overlay change never resizes the rendered
  view; possessed viewport overlays show explicit collider, navigation and path flags.
- Ctrl+click in the 2D pane picks a tile as the brush.
- 2D mode and terrain tool keys follow the pane's own keyboard focus like the viewport hotkeys; a focused text field
  still keeps them (ADR-8870)
- Ctrl+F no longer frames the viewport selection; the viewport's F frames it only without Ctrl/Cmd.
- Ctrl+0 is only camera bookmark 0 in the viewport; egui's own keyboard zoom is off. (ADR-7733)

#### Outliner, Inspector and prefabs

- Bounds pivot counts an entity's cached model box, not each vertex; Inspector ▸ Modified only shows a component with no
  baseline with every field (ADR-7813)
- An entity that gets or leaves a parent loses its Outliner folder (undo restores it), and Create Prefab no longer
  copies the folder into the `.prefab.ron`. Outliner `type:`, `has:` and `tag:` terms are computed on demand once per
  tree generation, anywhere in the query. (ADR-8575)
- The add-component picker and `editor.component_types` list by recent pick, then category, then match score.
- An `Option` field's None/Some selector is a checkbox; every component header has a context menu; the flat Add
  component menu is replaced by the picker; `regia_ecs::attrs::Category` groups Inspector fields; a color swatch opens
  the new picker.
- `Camera.layer_mask` draws as a render-layer grid (drag to paint, Space flips a focused cell); the value is unchanged.
- Number fields apply typed text on commit (Enter or leaving the field), not per keystroke.
- Duplicates are numbered ("Lamp 2", "Lamp 3"); a copy of a unique-name entity gets its next free name in the same undo
  step. While searching, Select all, Shift ranges and the rubber band select only matching rows.
- `editor.outliner_columns` is a mutating method needing `edit_scene` (it writes `.regia/outliner_view.ron`); read-only
  agents use `editor.outliner_view`.
- The Inspector renders model and material pictures for asset fields itself; `DestructibleSource.asset`'s filter takes a
  model's fracture manifest sub-asset; the `material` filter takes `.matgraph.ron` and `.matinst.ron`, and the `mesh`
  filter `builtin:cube`, `builtin:plane` and `builtin:sphere` (ADR-7472)
- Palette commands `file.new_level`, `file.new_level_discard` and `recovery.restore` refuse while a prefab is being
  edited, as the File menu and `editor.recovery_restore` do
- Apply override writes the prefab's `.prefab.ron` when the project has the file (one undo restores it), and
  directory-form level saves write only dirty entity files; `level.convert` refuses unsaved edits (`level_dirty`)
  (ADR-7203)
- Apply on a prefab variant instance writes the variant's `.variant.ron` (it was refused), atomically, with one undo
  step (ADR-7263)
- Create prefab keeps entity references working: fields that named a selected entity name the instance's entity
  afterwards, through undo and redo (`editor.create_prefab` `retargeted`) (ADR-8593)
- Instancing, duplicating or pasting a prefab that holds a reflection capture gives each instance its own capture id,
  so a level with two instances bakes (ADR-7716)
- The Inspector marks a prefab instance's overridden field beside its label (right-click: Revert override, Apply to
  prefab). The Create prefab variant dialog shows with the Content Browser and Outliner closed.
- While a prefab is open in place or on its own, File ▸ Save level, Save level as, Save all, the close dialog's save and
  `editor.save_level` are refused (`prefab_in_place`, `prefab_open`) until it is saved or closed. The Outliner's
  override dot opens the Overrides window.
- Prefab edit mode: `editor.save_prefab` clears the prefab's dirty flag, the level autosave writes the stashed level
  while a prefab is open, and a level save or autosave adopts user entities of no level into the open level (ADR-7382)
- `editor.create_prefab_variant` records one file-only undo step: undo deletes the new `.variant.ron` and its `.meta`
  (ADR-7614)

#### Play in editor and launch

- Play in editor follows the game: the game's `register` runs before the level spawns, then `on_load`, and the play
  world has physics only when the game adds `PhysicsPlugin`; without a game crate the play world adds physics before the
  level spawns, so authored bodies fall. A plugin the play world already has is skipped with a warning. (ADR-7205,
  ADR-7251, ADR-9180)
- The play toolbar's Play, Simulate, Pause or Resume, Stop and Step are registered editor actions; while a session is
  paused the toolbar shows **▶ Resume** in place of Pause, so a paused session can be resumed from the toolbar again;
  glyph buttons and separately labeled fields carry accessible names (ADR-7381, ADR-9810)
- While a session plays in the separate play window, a click on the editor viewport no longer captures the editor
  window's cursor (ADR-7313)
- While the play window is open the viewport shows a placeholder, so play renders once per editor frame, and a
  resolution picked during play applies at once with a `WindowResized` (ADR-7283)
- Several-client PIE worlds run game code through the play world's panic boundary: a client panic logs its backtrace
  with `file:line` links and files a `pie` crash report.
- A one-player game with a second window camera composites both views in a possessed pane and the play window; PIE
  stands its own joined controllers apart once, in the first frame's `PostUpdate`, and never moves a game's controllers
  (ADR-7395, ADR-7466)
- `editor.play_state`'s `entities` count the PIE stand-in cameras that exist (ADR-7466)
- While play runs the Outliner and Inspector still show the play world by default, and focusing a PIE client shows its
  world.
- `editor.launch { wait: true }` holds the editor at most 1 s and a launch still building answers `pending: true`;
  `launch_timeout` is retired. An Android Stop sends `am force-stop` only after the launch started the app, Windows
  launch children start suspended inside the kill job, and Unix Stop uses `killpg`. (ADR-7548)
- The game-build Cancel, `editor.cancel_task { id: "game_build" }` and `editor.cancel_job { id: "build" }` set one
  token; `editor.cancel_task { id: "import" }` stops the Content Browser's import and removes copied files (ADR-7381)
- `editor.launch_status` reports the device agent transport's forwarded host URL in `agent`
  (`ws://127.0.0.1:<host port>`). (ADR-8631, ADR-8472)
- Canceling an `agent.hot_reload` job kills its cargo build (`build_stopped` in the result) (ADR-7205)
- Separate-process PIE children advertise their agent endpoint under `build/pie/<session>/agent/<role>`, the dedicated
  server process starts with `--agent`, and a standalone or Android launch gives the game its own agent directory,
  `<project>/.regia/launch/agent-<id>` (`REGIA_AGENT_DIR`), so none of them replaces the editor's own endpoint.
  (ADR-7836, ADR-9740)
- A saved Android launch uses its chosen build profile, and packaged desktop launches run the published binary from its
  bundle directory. (ADR-7843)

#### Content Browser, import and packaging

- Content ▸ New lists, after the earlier creators, Montage, Cloth, Soft body, Camera rig, Smart object and Tile map;
  `editor.create_asset` takes every kind, including `sequence`, `shot_list`, `attribute_set`, `effect` and `ability`
  (ADR-7555, ADR-7774, ADR-7813)
- The Content Browser's folder tree keeps the folder menu (Validate, Reference graph, Size map, Audit) on every row.
  Personal collections and favorites live in `<project>/.regia/asset_collections.ron`; an existing user-config copy
  moves there on first open. (ADR-7902)
- A Content Browser double click and an Output Log asset link open every kind `editor.open_asset` opens. (ADR-7725)
- `editor.move_asset` rewrites path mentions in referencing assets instead of refusing, as one undo step
  (`fixed_referencers`, `undoable`). The close prompt, File ▸ New level, Open level… and Recent use the Save all
  dialog. File ▸ Save all's agent route is `editor.save_all { dialog: true }`; `editor.menu_invoke { path: "File/Save
  all" }` saves every document the dialog can save and reports `saved`, `failed` and `skipped`. (ADR-7822)
- Content Browser: the folder pane is resizable; collections and favorites changes re-read their file and apply to what
  is on disk; navigation scrolls the tree without taking focus; Alt+Left/Right only step history while the browser has
  focus; a rescan keeps unchanged folders' facts. (ADR-7798)
- Content ▸ Import… with several files or a model opens the import dialog (one image or sound still imports at once).
- With the content drawer open, a docked Content tab shows a one-line note instead of a second browser (ADR-8702)
- The Content Chunks panel passes the startup level's assets to the assignment, so they show pinned in `base` as the
  next cook writes them (ADR-8092)
- `editor.content_chunks_set_rule` refuses a save whose `entry` root matches nothing (it used to save and fail the next
  cook); the Content Chunks panel and Project Settings reload after an undo rewrites `Regia.toml` (ADR-8543)
- The data-asset list (New ▸ Data asset, `editor.data_asset_types`, `editor.create_asset { kind: "data_asset" }`) offers
  only types registered as data assets; any other type is `data_asset_unknown_type` (ADR-7490)
- Import settings ▸ Apply is disabled, and `editor.import_settings_apply` answers `tools_busy`, while a package, cook,
  clean or import job runs or another holder has the project cook lock; an agent edit clears that field's refusal
  mark
- A failed cook after an import-settings write puts the old `.meta` back; `editor.set_import_settings` is refused with
  `tools_busy` while another holder has the cook lock (pass `async: true` to wait in a job). (ADR-7664)
- `editor.set_import_settings` checks settings against the source's importer: a mistyped or out-of-range value is
  `asset_settings_invalid` and nothing is written or cooked (ADR-7469)
- `asset.gltf_lint` and `asset.gltf_material_lint` refuse a file over 1 MiB with `content_lint_limit` and point at
  `asset.content_lint_start` (ADR-8391)

#### Levels, saving and project settings

- File ▸ New level opens the template dialog, also when the level has unsaved edits (tick its Discard box to replace
  them); palette `file.new_level` still makes an empty level. (ADR-8039)
- File ▸ Save all with nothing unsaved shows "Every document is saved." with one **Close** button (ADR-8583)
- The editor-close prompt lists a dirty Shots-panel shot list and Save and exit writes it; the Import settings panel
  keeps Apply disabled while a field shows a refused value (`asset_settings_invalid`); `editor.procgen_param` refuses a
  non-boolean `remove` or `remove` with `generator` (ADR-7495)
- The project browser's "Create blank project" is "Create project" with the picked template (ADR-7234)
- `ProjectSettingsState::save` refuses an invalid `[package_hygiene]` table and does not rewrite the file (ADR-8399)
- One physics-layers editor: Project Settings shows only **Physics layers (config/physics.ron)** and agents use
  `editor.physics_layers_get`/`editor.physics_layers_edit`; `editor.project_settings_get` `pages.physics` is the
  `editor.physics_layers_get` result. Removing a layer that is not the last is refused (`layer_not_last`). (ADR-7267)
- `editor.layer_delete`, `layer_create` and `layer_bind` refresh the Layers panel at once; `editor.layer_initial` checks
