  the open level's binding only (ADR-7614)
- The status bar's Unsaved: N opens the Save all dialog. A console line a person types is audited as client
  `editor:console` and the trace toggle as `editor:status_bar`; Ctrl+Shift+Space does nothing during play or in a text
  field; Shift+Tab in the console line moves focus back.

#### Material, procedural and logic graphs

- The material graph layout sidecar is version 2 (`comments`, `reroutes`); the canvas's Add node menu is the searchable
  list; plain wires are cubic curves so the hit test matches the drawing; a graph calling a function open in a tab
  compiles the tab's unsaved definition. (ADR-9700, ADR-7730)
- The Material panel renders its preview only when the material, shape, orbit, light or size changes;
  `editor.matgraph_preview` and `editor.material_preview` draw their editor's preview scene (images unchanged with the
  default scene).
- Moving a material graph parameter changes the uniform slot order, so its generated code and pipeline key change once;
  a Code node that fails its check previews magenta.
- Material graph node previews keep at most `NODE_PREVIEW_SWATCH_CAP` (256) swatches; the fracture panel's bond view
  cooks once per run, settings change or support edit (ADR-7451)
- The material graph, procedural graph and sequence curve panels draw a list column beside the canvas; in the procedural
  graph, Delete with a Nodes row focused removes that node only (ADR-7383)
- Logic Graph panel: bottom tabs Issues, Breakpoints, Variables, Watch and Usages; accessible names on the canvas and
  fields; node search menus take focus only when they open, so Tab reaches the entries.
- The procedural graph canvas draws only on-screen nodes and reuses its layout while nothing changes; generate around
  view gates cells by a 16:9 frustum (ADR-3480)
- Procedural Box binds generators with `ProcBounds::FromShape`; the panel applies one UI pass's edits per egui frame,
  and an explicit preview choice is kept (ADR-7215)
- `editor.procgen_collapse` answers `procgen_collapse_invalid` for a disconnected or re-entering selection;
  `editor.get_theme` and `editor.set_theme` have a `data_kinds` section (ADR-7239)

#### Panels and tools

- The Biome, Ragdoll, Material, theme, UI Animation, UI designer, trailer and aircraft panels' own Undo and Redo step
  the shared history: they undo the editor's latest edit when it belongs to the panel's document, and their
  `*_nothing_to_undo` codes mean the shared history has nothing for it. Undo routes are refused with
  `transaction_active` while an agent transaction is open.
- The UI designer hierarchy draws only the rows in view and keeps Ctrl/Shift group selection; `regia procgen verify`
  names an extra `.pgout` with `orphan_bake` per bake directory (ADR-7331)
- A UI designer edit inside a layout variant is one shared-history step. (ADR-8365, ADR-7858)
- The Fracture panel's clump count is bounded by the chunk count, and `editor.fracture_panel_set` takes one site choice
  per call (two answer `F0002`) and changes nothing when refused (ADR-7550)
- A reflection capture moved, resized, deleted or added during Tools ▸ Bake reflection captures ends the run
  `cancelled` with `bake_capture_changed` and writes nothing. (ADR-7663)
- Bake reflection captures ends `cancelled` with `bake_level_changed` when the open level or project changes while it
  runs, and takes its captures off the editor's renderer however it ends (ADR-7592)
- The Localization panel's Exchange row has separate export and import paths (ADR-7716)
- The tier badge names every tier a shared node runs in, the biome tint drapes on the terrain, and File ▸ Recent in
  the headless editor follows the open level (ADR-7275)
- Workspace switching activates the workspace's terrain or tile tool and finishes a stroke before deactivating it.
- The mixer dock names its faders and send sliders for screen readers, with a separate named value box per slider; a
  send or blend-time drag is one undo step.
- The VFX bounds readout and `editor.vfx_state` `bounds` measure with every motion module (curl, vortex and attractor
  motion) and each particle's drawn size (`kind` is `particle_extents`, was `particle_centers`) (ADR-8355)
- `editor.shot_panel_state` is a pure read; a Save with no path after a detach answers `shot_panel_detached`.
  (ADR-7662)
- `editor.shot_panel_save` always writes the current `.shots.ron` version, and the Shots panel edits the adopted
  player's list while one is adopted (ADR-8940)
- The `.regiatrace` timeline merges a row's sub-pixel bars into one counted bar and keeps its layout while trace and
  view are unchanged (it rebuilt its layout from every event on every repaint; `editor.trace_timeline` items carry
  `count`) (ADR-7325)
- `editor.profiler_timeline` is mutating (`edit_scene`): its `frame` parameter holds the tab on a frame (ADR-7409)
- Profiler: a click on the already selected source restarts its worker while its link is `disconnected`, and a process
  source whose worker panicked reads `disconnected` with a reason instead of `connecting` (ADR-7427, ADR-7493)
- `editor.abilities_panel_asset` takes a typed `field` + `value` edit and answers `asset.form` and
  `diagnostics[].field`; `format_version` is read-only (`abilities_panel_read_only`; a typed edit could change it), and
  a non-finite number shows as its spelling (it blocked every typed edit) (ADR-7472)

#### Menus, docking and shortcuts

- The View menu has Workspace, Layouts, Maximize panel, Full-screen viewport, Panels and Feature profile submenus.
- View ▸ Maximize panel, its palette command and `editor.maximize_panel` take the focused tab; only Ctrl+Space takes
  the tab group under the pointer. While a tab is maximized, an extension panel that becomes active joins the dock the
  maximize replaced.
- Tools menu order: Rebuild asset database, Clear caches…, Open code editor, Bake reflection captures; opening another
  level clears the navigation preview and cancels a running capture bake
- The shell's File, Create, Edit and View menus, Play toolbar, prefab banner and unsaved-changes prompt draw through the
  action registry (ADR-7307)
- `editor.keymap_bind` takes `scope` (default global); binding a chord takes it only from commands in the same scope;
  the Settings tab is titled **Preferences**.

#### Look, text and accessibility

- Panel text is written for people: error lines show the sentence with the code in a tooltip, activity rows use local
  `HH:MM:SS`, unnamed entities read `<Kind> (unnamed)`, empty panels show one sentence, long paths cut in the middle
  (ADR-8586)
- The Problems panel and Output Log color rows with the theme's diagnostic colors (errors and DEBUG/TRACE text); the
  dock tab bar grows with the text scale. (ADR-7838)
- High contrast: a hovered control is black with yellow text and outline, and overlay groups, the play toolbar and the
  Content Browser search row wrap at large text scales (ADR-8679)
- `editor.a11y_tree` names `Label` nodes by their text and `labelled_by` nodes by their labels, as the OS adapters do
  (ADR-8587)

#### Performance

- Outliner, Output Log, Inspector and other long lists lay out only the rows on screen: an Outliner of 100 000 entities
  draws in 7-12 ms instead of 3 s, an Inspector with a 100 000-element list in about 23 ms instead of 4.2 s (headless
  debug build) (ADR-7306)
- Large projects (ADR-7326): the Inspector no longer clones an unchanged component on every repaint, the Layers panel
  validates once per change, the editor frame no longer walks every entity, and the Smart Object panel and UI designer
  hierarchy lay out only rows in view. On the headless large-project bench an inspector frame drops from 26.6 ms to 14.2
  ms median and from 148 832 to 5 481 allocations.
- The Layers panel's Open levels section no longer scans every member of every open level on each repaint (ADR-8680)
- The **Version History** window reads the history folder only when it changed, not every frame (ADR-8435)

#### Agent methods

- In Propose mode, `editor.*` EditScene and Assets calls answer `{staged, staged_id, preview, pending}` instead of
  `session_propose_live_unsupported` (history control, world replacement, `editor.command` and `editor.menu_invoke` are
  still refused).
- `editor.guide`'s editor-menus, editor-localization and ui-components topics name the capture handle, select-by-asset,
  material graph find, localization exchange and component slot methods
- `editor.lookdev_trace { out }` refuses a path outside the project, temp, working directory and `output_roots` with
  `path_outside_project`.
- `editor.open_code_editor` is mutating and needs the `build` permission; `editor.view_asset`'s `overlay` is
  `string|array`.
- `editor.ui_designer_edit` refuses conflicting parameters (`rect` with `delta`, `rect` with `nodes`, `nodes` with
  `node`) with `F0002`; `editor.recovery_restore` refuses a `level:` row while a prefab is open (`prefab_open`);
  `editor.seq_new` and marker add/rename refuse blank names (`seq_new_name_empty`, `seq_marker_name_empty`)
- `editor.nav_preview` is declared mutating and spends the mutating-call budget; its preview belongs to its level and is
  dropped on a level replace; a level too big for one nav build is refused before the bake reads it
  (`nav_preview_too_large`) (ADR-7630)
- The agent-parity scan has no gap rows: `editor.fracture_panel_set { supports: { below_y: null } }` clears the support
  plane like the checkbox

### Rendering

#### Lights and shadows

- Several directional lights no longer draw only the sun; the frame uniform (`LabFrame`) grew from 768 to 1072 bytes
  (ADR-7211)
- Only the sun carries the specular disc; the up to three extra directional lights are points (ADR-7277)
- A frame with more than one directional light looks different on terrain and grass and in probe GI; a one-sun scene
  traces, hashes and bakes the same bytes, and no shipped sample spawns a second sun (ADR-7254)
- DDGI probes see both the image light's ground plane and the extra directional lights (ADR-7310)
- `AmbientVolume` turns with its entity in the forward, visibility-buffer and Mobile shaders (ADR-7275)
- The traced sun scene skips `ShadowCast::Local { max_cascades: 0, .. }` casters, as documented (ADR-8414)
- A local light's shadow atlas face draws only casters that reach the light's sphere and the face, and a dirty light
  redraws only the faces a moved caster reaches. The frame's timestamp query set has 49 queries. (ADR-7986)
- Emissive area light distance-probe shadows fade over at most 0.25 m on Desktop High and Ultra. (ADR-7661)

#### Visibility, culling and LOD

- Hidden entities draw nothing: `Visibility::Hidden` on an entity or any ancestor keeps it out of every extraction
  path (meshes, skinned meshes, shadow casters, visibility-buffer clusters, mask decals, glass, occlusion proxies,
  procgen outputs and `InstancedMeshSet`s). `MeshRenderer` requires `InheritedVisibility` and `ViewVisibility`, so a
  mesh child of a hidden parent is hidden. (ADR-7200, ADR-7588)
- Hidden lights light nothing: a hidden `PointLight`, `SpotLight`, `RectLight`, `TubeLight` or `DirectionalLight` (or
  one under a hidden ancestor) is not extracted, and the sun is the lowest-entity directional light that is drawn. A
  hidden `StaticDecal` stops drawing but stays in the pool. (ADR-7255, ADR-7670)
- A parked or hidden terrain stops drawing in a game and its draw comes back without a rebuild; a host-placed
  `AmbientBirds` flock with a hidden anchor flies no birds. (ADR-7200, ADR-7588)
- Skinned meshes are culled at their posed bound and morphed draws' bounds grow by the largest displacement (ADR-7255)
- The terrain draw leaves out of its camera passes the nodes outside every outdoor portal frustum on matching
  main-view frames, and runtime scatter leaves out instance blocks outside them; sun cascades and casters within
  shadow reach of a mouth are unchanged. (ADR-8413)
- `cull_unreachable_cells` reads cells through `VisCellMembers` and writes `VisCellCulled`, which `extract_glass` reads;
  `update_view_visibility` skips shadow-only draws. (ADR-7673, ADR-7586)
- `select_mesh_lods` reads the `DrawList`, and a cross-fade's outgoing draw and a shadow-LOD draw xor their tag into the
  object key instead of replacing it (identical for entities) (ADR-8457)
- `retain_occlusion_views` also frees the removed views' LOD histories, and a camera cut drops only the drawn view's LOD
  history (ADR-8620)
- `regia_render::Camera::layer_mask` carries `@LayerMask("render")`.

#### Upscalers, sky and transparency

- FSR 3.1: a restarting pixel takes its nearest render sample, and `Fsr3Settings::default()` uses
  `min_disocclusion_accumulation` -1 and `accumulation_added_per_frame` 1/4. The port matches the vendor SDK v1.1.4
  FSR 3.1 upscaler apart from these two documented differences. (ADR-7678, ADR-7933)
- TSR resolve follows a history lock above the 0.1 ownership floor (was 0.3): render scale 0.5 orbit thin geometry
  0.905 to 0.952 on RTX 5090 and lavapipe, 0.889 to 0.921 on Metal. TSR is opt-in. (ADR-7876)
- Physical-sky backgrounds draw before transparent materials on High and Epic: blended draws, OIT and glass over clear
  sky are visible, the glass refraction copy holds the sky, and `GraphSlot::AfterOpaque` passes receive an HDR image
  that already has the sky. The sky's cost moves from `atmosphere_ms` to `forward_ms`. (ADR-7963)
- VFX on profiles without the alpha sort rank emitters by the slot-ordered gather's alpha key before the stable radix
  pass (ADR-7277)

#### Meshes and memory

- `regia cook --ship` (or `--gpu-meshes`) stores vertex-colored meshes GPU-ready, skipping the decode and the
  MikkTSpace pass at load; `GpuMeshView::to_cpu_mesh` returns `COLOR_0`, while `load_cooked_model` keeps drawing white
  vertex colors. Skinned and morphing meshes stay compact. (ADR-7919)
- `AssetMeshUploads` uploads the frame renderer's vertex layout (64-byte `LabVertex`), and unloading drops its buffer
  handles instead of destroying them (ADR-7284)
- Counted mesh vertex and index buffers are created with `COPY_SRC`. (ADR-7721)
- The mesh cache never evicts a counted mesh it adopted; `CacheBudgets::mesh_bytes` and `CacheStats::resident_bytes`
  count its own entries only (ADR-7330).
- The cluster arena's geometry stream holds GPU-owned ranges and grows by a GPU copy, a counted mesh's vertex buffer
  gains `STORAGE` on devices with the visibility-buffer path, and the meshlet bridge checks a counted stand-in's `.fmlt`
  against the upload's counts (ADR-7462)

#### Warm-up and scene prepare

- The game-frame warm builds every deferred blended and transmission forward variant and the GPU-driven cull
  pipelines; warm time goes up, play compiles less. `VfxGpu::warm_formats` and `terrain_warm_formats` include
  registered targets. (ADR-7885)
- Headless apps: a scene prepare with a render device runs the window path's warm rounds and becomes `presented` only
  after a texture-view frame of the resident scene was submitted; texture views are held during a prepare, and
  `take_scene_prepare` runs before `render_texture_views`. (ADR-8122)
- A window with both an open scene prepare and the paused-scene hold shows the UI over a clear while the prepare
  holds, then renders and keeps one frame and resumes the hold.
- `scene_load_end` gains `map` and `complete` and is sent once `complete` is known. (ADR-7883)
- A `--profile` prepare applies the preset's post stack to every window view (ADR-8453)
- The terrain shared color pass compiles the anisotropic footprint loop and cloud reads out where they are dormant,
  and the terrain warm builds both cloud states; `TerrainGpu::set_anisotropy_footprint` after the first frame rebuilds
  the shared pipelines. (ADR-7886)

#### Quality presets and telemetry

- `RenderSettings::preset()` masks the upscaler (`temporal_upscaler`), the render scale and the host memory budget
  together and compares a profile using each row's `host_memory`, so a profile that differs from a preset row only there
  (including a host-set budget) still reports that preset and keeps its quality tier; `MemoryReport::table` prints a
  host-copies line. (ADR-7891, ADR-8577)
- The confirmation probe (still off by default) also runs on a Low pick and steps Low to Very Low only when Low misses
  the lower of the target and 30 fps; VRAM floors never step below Low. (ADR-7882)
- Telemetry: a hitch's `top_cpu_spans` lists only main and render thread spans; `hitches.top_causes` never names a
  worker span; held frames never feed frame-time histograms or bound classification; `frame_bound` classifies only
  visible ticks with fresh CPU and GPU samples; summary `cpu_ms`/`gpu_ms` add `samples`. `DEFAULT_FILTER` and
  `RECOMMENDED_FILTER` add `fontdb=error`. (ADR-7898, ADR-7890, ADR-7965)
- Feedback bundles: `hitch_report.txt` percentiles read only ticks with a fresh renderer sample and count them;
  `crash.txt` prints "no fresh sample" for an absent renderer time.
- The decal scene-color copy warning names its reason. (ADR-7898)
- `TerrainGpu` creates through a tracked device, so terrain bind groups, buffers and textures show in the frame-path
  counters; terrain pipelines carry tracked labels, sites and warm-worker attribution. (ADR-7964)

#### Shaders, devices and frame graph

- `regia_matgraph`: calls of the same function with the same arguments share one expansion; graphs without repeated
  identical calls compile to the same code and keys. `CompiledGraph::function_calls` counts expansions. (ADR-7730)
- `regia_quick`'s `draw2d.wgsl` is part of the engine shader-source hash.
- One headless wgpu instance per process: `RenderDevice::headless*`, `list_adapters` and `hwrt::probe_adapters` share an
  instance per descriptor, created with the engine's instance flags; a window's instance stays its own (ADR-7557)
- The two HDR scratch images are keyed transients of the view's keyed pool and appear as graph transients of the
  `atmosphere`, `clouds`, `fog`, `translucent_hdr`, `taau` and `post_stack` nodes. (ADR-7877)
- The path tracer's and GPU lightmap baker's BVH stacks live in workgroup memory (28 KiB per 64 invocations, 12 KiB
  per 32), and `baked_gi.wgsl` reads its volume through `bgi_u()`; outputs are unchanged. (ADR-7879, ADR-7587)

### Lightmaps, GI and lighting

- Inside an `InteriorVolume` that holds the eye, the forward shade reads a covering baked GI volume (irradiance and
  probe light) instead of the neutral floor and legacy probe bounce, on every desktop tier and the Deck; uncovered
  surfaces are unchanged bit for bit (ADR-7975)
- With more than four `InteriorVolume`s, the aerial composite and froxel fog (fog and aerial perspective) bind the four
  nearest the eye, so the passage the eye stands in always takes its air out of the sky in-scatter;
  `render.aerial_interiors` `bound[]` gains `index` (ADR-7661, ADR-7975)
- `set_fill_canyon_visibility(bool)` now sets `FillCanyonScope::MapWide`/`Off` (same frames); `render.mesh_occluders`
  adds `bands` and per-occluder `far_band` (ADR-8412, ADR-7970)
- `.bake.ron` at v3: a v0-v2 `sun` reads as `suns: [s]`, a one-sun file bakes byte-identical outputs, and
  `sky_atmosphere` follows the first sun (ADR-7818)
- `DdgiSettings::clamped` caps `sdf_pool_bytes` at 128 MiB (one storage binding); every tier's pool is unchanged
  (ADR-7456)
- The baked-GI split refuses a split grid past 2^20 columns per axis with `BakedGiError::Layout` instead of aborting on
  allocation (ADR-7456)
- The probe update re-reduces only the edited rectangles of its heightfield pyramid when it holds the previous
  generation (bit-identical to a full rebuild; a 13 x 13 crater re-reduces under 1 % of the pyramid, was all of it), and
  a new heights generation wakes settled probes, so runtime height edits under settled probes are traced;
  `ddgi_maxmip.wgsl` reads a per-level rectangle list. (ADR-7659)

### World

#### Terrain and ground

- The ground budget is spec 121 § 11's 34 000 000 / 64 000 000 bytes everywhere, and `TerrainBakeReport::memory_bytes`
  is the authored mode's sim-ground bytes (ADR-8436)
- The sim-geometry budget for maps up to 2.56 km is 35,000,000 bytes (was 34,000,000), and an authored floor at least
  1 m above every ring pin it covers is kept over the ring (ADR-8396)
- The small-map sim ground budget is 35 MB everywhere (`regia_world_geom::checks::GROUND_BUDGET_SMALL`). (ADR-8529)
- The runtime keeps the cooked millimeter triangles and rebuilds planes from them: a full-pitch reference map fits in
  34,000,000 resident bytes. With no ground supplied, weld and trim receive no ground. (ADR-8246)
- The cook builds `GroundMode::Nested` (it was refused with `cook_not_built`); a layer cuts only the holes it lists in
  `fills`. A `Full` map over the sim-geometry budget fails with `ground_over_budget` naming the smallest
  `Nested(max_level)` that fits. (ADR-8214)
- A level whose terrain has a count mapping builds its map from it (`height_scale_m`/`height_offset_m` do not apply),
  and a cooked terrain index this build cannot read fails the level load with `level_terrain_decode` (ADR-8471)
- A monolithic terrain source over its limits answers with `data.hint` naming the tiled form (ADR-8595); the source
  inputs digest includes a named raster descriptor (ADR-8542)
- The terrain index is version 2; the standard cook writes v2 without heights, holes or material and keeps its cook key
  (ADR-8024)
- Cooked terrain index v2 is one layout (the v1 fields, then optional exact `heights`, `holes`, photoreal `material`
  and `source_mapping`), and `load_level_terrain` builds a count-source level from `LevelGround::source_mapping`.
  (ADR-8619)
- CDLOD level 0 on a full-detail root whose cell count exceeds the sample intervals uses the lattice pitch, so a
  4096-sample map draws a slightly larger root (ADR-8291)
- The CDLOD span of the full-detail round-up is taken over the quadtree side of a rectangular map (rows on a map deeper
  than wide); square and wider maps draw bit for bit as before. (ADR-8421, ADR-8369)
- `CollisionHeightfield::scale` and the terrain uniform follow a map's world depth (ADR-8369)
- The terrain draw, `regia_pick`'s terrain ray, camera clearance and footstep surfaces follow a map's grid origin
  (same bits on the world origin) (ADR-8179)
- `terrain.wgsl` tests lattice windows at the pre-morph grid position and zeroes cliff relief within two lattice cells
  of a filled mouth's cut; the DDGI trace reads a coverage-only copy of the hole words (ADR-8470)
- Height strokes on a terrain with edit layers write the active layer; height changes made outside a named layer
  (patches, craters, imports, procgen without a target) belong to the base and survive recompute and save. Flatten bakes
  weight deltas into the base weights along with the heights.
- Terrain brush hotkeys and sliders share one state; the default smooth brush gives the same output as before.
- Sun cascades re-key only those an applied height band lands in, grass reselects only for edits in its range, and
  the virtual texture keeps up to `VT_EDIT_RECTS_MAX` (256) edit rectangles before merging. (ADR-7585)

#### Tunnels, mesh layers and world queries

- A displacement whose absolute sum is above 0.05 m fails `render_off_collision`; a column over `max_floors` or the
  span capacity (16) fails `column_over_max_floors`; a sweep with no patches and no on-foot grid fails `input_missing`
  (ADR-8359)
- While a blocker plug is active its top is a floor and its faces are solids and walls for every geometry query. A
  state with every plug clear answers bit for bit as before. (ADR-8346)
- Every native world query reads the state's ground edits; a fresh state answers as before (ADR-8313)
- `QUERY_BITS_VERSION` is 8: near-equal floors and equal-height lids tie by the canonical integer-millimeter triangle
  key, wall queries use a fixed world-space neighborhood, and the character mover's `push_capsule` joins the query table
  without moving an earlier answer. Stage A arithmetic and hashes are unchanged. (ADR-8246, ADR-8323, ADR-8531)
- A cook whose terrain has a `*_skylight` patch over a layer floor writes an extra `skylight_props` layer, so
  `CookReport::layers` has one more entry than the document (ADR-8323)
- Mesh-layer chunk normals are shared across chunk borders and LODs (no lighting seam); the chunk importer is 1.1.0 and
  older chunks recook (ADR-8451)
- The mesh-layers cook runs the mouth seal, the viscell emit, the bake checks and the prop layer together: section and
  plug triangle ranges stay true through the seal, and `CookOutput` carries `viscells_ron` and `bake_report`.
  (ADR-8421, ADR-8529, ADR-8571)
- Skylight and shaft openings cook as open cuts, and sections, portals, zones and plugs join the canonical section:
  every map's meshlayers `content_hash` moves once, so re-cook `<map>.meshlayers` (ADR-8210)
- `regia terrain cook` of a `Passages` layer writes `<map>.viscells.ron`; a section with a 0.5 m wall gap fails
  `viscell_leak`. (ADR-8371)
- One `terrain.mesh_layers`: without geometry it answers `loaded: false`, never `no_world_geom`. (ADR-8627)
- Section zones store a canonical yaw (the lockstep `atan2`, signed zero as `+0`; a `-0`/`-π` yaw would have frozen a
  sign bit into the lockstep hash), half extents round through `m_to_mm` (up to 0.5 mm smaller), and `reverb_size_m` is
  never `-0`, so cooked section bytes and `content_hash` change for maps with sections. (ADR-8579)
- The mesh-layer cook gives a filled mouth whose ramp climb opened past its cut an approach strip on the ring pins,
  marks the threshold sill `TRI_FLAG_NO_SUPPORT`, and keeps a collar triangle on every ring edge no band holds; cooked
  bytes of every filled mouth change (ADR-8533)
- Tunnel maps with a layered bake route on `det::layers`; the centerline graph and navmesh corridor path stay for Stage
  A parity (ADR-8486)
- A chase on the plain uses the caller's floor reach; open cuts are not heightfield floors. (ADR-8336)
- `regia_world_geom::compat`: the mouth-hole trig uses `regia_detsim::math` (libm 0.2.16) and every float
  `min`/`max`/`clamp`/`sqrt` is the deterministic form, so x86-64 and arm64 peers get the same hole yaws (ADR-8166)
- `compat::ground::query_ground` is the column's `span_at(y)` with no step band, `STEP_BOUND_MM` is 50 (was 200), and
  `sample_floor` no longer reads `sticky` (ADR-8167)
- `PilotGrid` gets `wall_span_cap` (default `MAX_WALL_SPANS`, 4) and `add_wall_capped` / `stamp_wall_capped`;
  `PilotGrid::span_cap_hits` reports the four-span cap (ADR-8306, ADR-8432)

#### Procedural generation and scatter

- Generated output follows layers: output roots copy the generator's `LayerMembership` (procedural output roots follow
  their generator's layer presence), get a derived `EntityGuid` (from the generator's entity when it has no guid) and
  park and unpark with it, both ways; a generator that moves layer takes its applied output, and a generator that goes
  away takes its output roots and tile cells. (ADR-1585, ADR-7556, ADR-7595, ADR-7621, ADR-7715)
- `regia_procgen::draw::extract_proc_instances` skips parked (`LayerParked`) and hidden outputs (ADR-7634)
- A procgen generator both parked by a data layer and hidden draws nothing; the `Query` dependency hash frames each tag
  list (ADR-7277)
- `procgen.bake`, `regia procgen bake`, streamed bakes and cell re-bakes accept graphs with prefab overrides (baking a
  generator whose `spawn.prefab` uses overrides failed; `procgen_bake_overrides` is retired). (ADR-7750)
- Procgen bake and verify write and compare a placed or additive member's bake in its source level's directory under its
  source guid; `regia procgen bake` and `verify` load the levels a level places, and verify re-bakes into scratch copies
  only. An unpinned `Baked` inline-placed member runs with its source guid's seed. (ADR-7331)
- Procgen named outputs: the last write wins across called graphs and the replacement warns; `spline.modules` phase B is
  no longer quadratic.
- The biome bake runs `biome.layer`, `biome.areas` and `biome.grass_mask` on the procgen worker pool with unchanged
  output bytes on every schedule, so the 2 km three-biome bake no longer runs its three heaviest nodes on one thread
  (ADR-7252)
- `SplineCurve::closest` skips segments that cannot hold the answer, `spawn.spline_mesh` samples once per axis
  coordinate, and run and world hashes stream through a 64 KiB buffer, all bit-identical (ADR-7238)
- Painted and clump scatter placement use the deterministic math kernels, so painted stores and clump assets are the
  same bytes on every target
- `Parallelism::from_env` builds one `REGIA_PROCGEN_THREADS` pool per process; the `input_hash` of runs with
  `in.entities` dependencies changes once (ADR-7214)
- `attr.lookup nearest_within` checks the cancel token every 4096 rows; packages built with a build profile grow by
  the bakes of placed levels and sublevels (ADR-7262)
- Tiled procgen counts `terrain.flatten_pad` and `terrain.domain_warp` as reading past the halo (ADR-8465)
- World-anchored scatter placement runs on the per-tile height windows, bit for bit as the whole map (ADR-8496)
- Scatter tile placement copies only the height window its tile reads, so a height edit while tiles stream writes in
  place instead of copying the whole map on the main thread; `TerrainMap` keeps its height range current through
  single-sample writes; `ScatterField` records which samples runtime edits wrote (one bit per sample). (ADR-7638,
  ADR-7646)
- `gen.mesh` recipe shapes include `rounded_box`, `cylinder`, `annulus` and `sweep`. (ADR-8044)

#### Navigation

- `GridSettings::default()` is `three_class()`, and `NavGrid::cost` includes the area scale and overlay (same values
  without them) (ADR-8379)
- Nav grid areas and the cost overlay work at every `GridSettings::cluster_cells` and both label widths; region costs
  are mean cell costs, equal to the old values at default settings. (ADR-8379)
- On a multi-layer navmesh, `region_count` counts groups of linked polygons and `NavQuery::find_nearest` prefers the
  polygon nearest in height among equally near ones; single-layer meshes are unchanged. (ADR-8076)
- `nav.det_agents` adds a `crowd` object (`groups`, `chokes`, `lane_groups`, `flows`) (ADR-8448)
- `NavObstacles::add_box` refuses a box with a non-finite corner. (ADR-7679)

#### Streaming, layers and saves

- A missing world index opens as `stream_not_cooked`; in a packaged game whose chunk holding the index is not mounted,
  the message names the chunk to request (ADR-8812, ADR-8862)
- A `LayerMembership` on an entity without an `EntityGuid` is switched with its layer (`Absent` parks it), and a parked
  `CrashSequence` stops its effects and holds its clock. (ADR-7200)
- A placed-level fill with a `PrefabLibrary` checks the 100,000-entity cap with `check_placement_with_prefabs`, and
  `level.place`'s preview counts prefab expansion too, refusing with `placed_limit` before anything spawns. (ADR-7601)
- A save load despawns `Saveable` entities with the stack-safe `hierarchy::despawn_tree`. (ADR-7601)

#### VFX

- VFX significance: a new instance that outranks a dwell-held instance takes its slot on its first ranking; the
  ambient-life tier for an unknown render profile follows `RenderProfilePreset::platform_default` (ADR-7431)
- Mobile alpha emitters draw back to front per emitter for the current view (ADR-7210)
- The VFX pipeline warm builds the mesh-asset pipeline (7 per color format), and a compute pass buckets mesh particles
  per registered mesh (ADR-7261)
- The air hover wash draws soft rounder puffs instead of long streaks; rates, lifetimes, colors and intensity are
  unchanged. (ADR-8026)
- `events::scaled_count` clamps to `MAX_PACKED_EVENT_COUNT`. (ADR-7560)

### Animation and characters

- `.animgraph.ron` is version 4: `ik_retarget::retarget_graph` maps the new nodes' joint names, `ChainIK`, `BodyAim` and
  `PerJoint` limit entries refuse unknown fields, and a `BodyAim` turning joint with an empty name is refused at load
  (ADR-8475, ADR-8540, ADR-8732)
- Below `idle_speed` gait feet no longer replant at neutral every step, and an idle step longer than one
  `settle_period` is refused with `gait_step_too_large` (ADR-8539)
- The third-person sample drives foot IK through `FootPlacement` graph parameters (ADR-8388)
- `Retargeter::check` measures a clip as the bake plays it when the retargeter has IK goals (ADR-8476)
- Both cooked clip writers keep node channels losslessly, and the glTF cache revision advances so sources that lost
  their plain-node channels recook (ADR-8157)
- Pose-search trajectory offsets are meters on scaled roots (a centimeter rig stored 100 per meter) (ADR-7241)
- Despawning an entity with a `MontagePlayer` ends its montages as interrupted, and `evaluate_animators` runs between
  `apply_montage_commands` and `publish_montage_commands` (ADR-7216)
- `AnimationClip::to_cooked_payload` writes a lossless v3 payload (ADR-7227)
- Cooking writes `motiondb-cooked` v3 and every cooked database's bytes change; `regia upgrade-project` migrates v1
  and v2 (ADR-7817)
- `InstanceBindPose::new` and `from_joint_positions` return `BindPoseError::WrongLength` for a `parents` list that does
  not match the joint names
- The montage slot check keys on the graph's `Slot` names and the rig's slot list, not the graph's address. (ADR-7596)

### Gameplay systems

#### Match rules and templates

- The match core lives in its own `regia_rules` crate (no framework ECS or physics needed); `regia_framework::rules`
  re-exports it. (ADR-8102)
- `MatchSession::step_with` credits bound seats for captures and hill points on the phase path too, and a launch's phase
  events go through the flow's pending-event cap. A match that ends books the series round and opens the vote when
  the map-vote flag is on. (ADR-8659)
- A flag capture credits the bound carrier and a hill point each bound occupant
- The arena sample's warm-up, round and match-over flow runs on the core phase machine (ADR-8485)
- `match_clock = PerMatch` starts `match_tick` at 0 when a match launches
- `match_rules_step` traces under `regia_rules::session` (ADR-8239)
- First Person, Third Person and Top Down templates spawn one shadowed sun (90,000 lux), and `SpawnRequest.prefab`
  spawns the authored prefab root for local and remote joins. (ADR-7868)

#### AI, behavior trees and abilities

- `AiCore::decide` runs through the cadence due list; with default settings slices, order and decisions are unchanged
  (ADR-8560)
- `SightingsBoard::refresh` runs the indexed path (same boards and line-of-sight calls, bit for bit); its span
  `det_ai.perception_refresh` covers the indexed refresh (ADR-8518)
- `TeamVision` compiles the sight table only when it changes and queries the spatial index from 16 watchers (same
  answers); `FogViewSim::set_viewer` clears the view's grid when the team changes (ADR-8473, ADR-8523)
- With `detection_threshold` above 0 sight of a new target waits on the meter; the default (0) acquires at once
  (ADR-8357)
- Perception copies `TeamId` onto `Perceivable.team` when both components are present
- A formation with a `custom` asset uses the cost assignment, and a crowd keeps the leader's last facing when it stops
  (ADR-8358)
- `Blackboard` keeps both the change set (`drain_changes`) and the logic-graph write counters, and `assign`/`append_str`
  record a real change, so a behavior tree sharing a blackboard sees the graph's writes (ADR-7277, ADR-7762)
- A behavior-tree decorator with `abort` set is a gate on its key, checked each time it is entered from rest in every
  abort mode; a shut gate fails without ticking its child (ADR-7559)
- `RouteAll` without `scan_limit` runs a budgeted pre-pass (one work unit per alive item) instead of resolving every
  anchor in one tick (ADR-7559)
- The behavior-tree depth cap spans state-tree bridge chains (the heights in a BT to ST to BT chain must sum to at most
  `MAX_BT_DEPTH`, 256), a `condition` node with an empty key fails `BehaviorTree::compile`, and a spatial query whose
  generator or test number is non-finite ends `failed` with `sq_bad_param`. A `Pick::All` run with zero survivors sets
  `<key>_error` to `no item survived the filters`. (ADR-7596)
- The AI tick budget schedules logic graphs as a third tree kind, and `AiTickStats::trees` counts them (ADR-7201)
- A state tree reloads when a file it embeds changes (ADR-8593)
- `WingmanCommand::from_team_directive(TeamDirective::Follow)` returns `EscortCommander`. (ADR-8655)
- `regia_detsim::ai::AiTickWorld` (in `ai::world_view`) is the per-tick borrowed world; `ai::AiWorld` is the ported
  combat read view. The engine golden registry ends with `hover_fleet_terrain`, `projectile_mix`, `input_log_arena` and
  `contact_pile`. (ADR-8573)
- Ability chance streams advance in place (O(1) per roll, same values), and the request kind order (apply, start,
  cancel, revoke) is documented as the contract (ADR-7573)

#### Physics, vehicles and hover

- `regia_physics::vehicle_track::step` runs the lockstep tracked model: track speeds and yaw are unchanged, but X and
  Z can differ from older builds in the last bits (the same bits on every target now) (ADR-8551)
- Hover defaults are engine-authored: `ClimbTuning::AIR_TIME` and `FlightRules::AIR_TIME` carry Regia's own fan-grip
  and lift numbers, the libm-1000 vector and golden are on the default hull, and the hover, ballistic, low-g and golden
  tick paths use `det_min`/`det_max`/`det_clamp` (ADR-8164, ADR-8085)
- The `.hoverhull.ron` v1 code is a submodule of the ECS hover module, re-exported from `regia_framework::det_hover`.
  (ADR-8637)
- `physics.vehicles` rows gain `config_digest` and `config_revision`. (ADR-9760)
- `FloorRule::Bands` (the default) no longer pushes apart two outdoor bodies on stacked floors;
  `FloorRule::UndergroundOnly` keeps the old behavior (ADR-8048)
- Impact fracture sampling uses density `1/r^bias`, flat within 1 % of the box diagonal; bias 0 and 1 draw in the
  bounding box, so a thin pane hit at a corner fills like a cube (it failed `fracture_site_sampling`); sites for a seed
  differ from the preview (ADR-7550)
- The parented-body warning covers `Dynamic` bodies only and prunes despawned ones (ADR-7320)
- `physics.cloth_set_params` refuses `shear_compliance` and `friction` with a message naming ADR-2250 (ADR-9070)

#### Determinism and sim hosts

- A realtime `ThreadedSimHost` behind `SimBridgePlugin` draws a blend one tick behind its newest frame; pick `Snap`
  for kinds that must show the newest pose (ADR-8456)
- A lockstep sim host drops bridge input (its frames carry the input) and counts it in `edges_dropped` (ADR-8526)
- Lockstep clippy policy also denies std clock reads, environment reads, worker creation, `RandomState` and
  `thread::Builder` (ADR-8367)
- `canonical_f32_bits`/`canonical_f64_bits` are the lead game's bodies, returning the same bits for every input
  (ADR-8078)
- Grid patch minima, height clamps, grid hashes and patch signatures use deterministic float order with `+0` and the
  canonical NaN; `DetBroadphase::new` bounds `max_cells` to `1..=2^24`
- Capture-the-flag base positions use the lockstep `det_max`/`det_clamp`: `ctf_base_position_for_starts` clamps teams 3
  and 4 through them (ADR-8085)
- `regia_detsim::ai` clamps utility scores with `det_clamp`/`det_max`: a zero result is always `+0.0` and a NaN the
  canonical NaN on every host. (ADR-8085)
- Scale-soak pass timings use the tick profiler, and the `regia-scale-soak` JSON gains a top-level `clock` field
  (ADR-8498)

#### Scripting, mods, cameras and settings

- A routed `regia.emit(name, value, target?)` event pushes only `name` into a logic graph inbox; the value is dropped
  (documented as intended; ADR-7268). `route_input_actions`, `route_lua_events` and `route_net_messages` have spans.
- `LuaMods::restore_state` enforces component-name uniqueness like load and reload (`lua_bad_value`); scratch
  `lua.run` spawns are despawned with the sandbox; a refused run on a disabled mod reports zero instructions and
  memory.
- A Lua mod whose id another mount holds is refused with `lua_id_taken`, local claimants load before Workshop items,
  and mod save entries stay keyed by manifest id: a Workshop mount does not take a legacy entry (held and written back),
  local `mods:` folders, `.fpak` archives and `dir:` mounts restore it, an entry whose id contains `:` is quarantined,
  and held bytes are reserved before live mods. (ADR-7881, ADR-7951, ADR-7992)
- Player directors own their shakes and their FOV kick decays every frame (ADR-7263)
- A `Window::logical_size` written by a game is clamped to the monitor, and one below 320 x 180 is refused. (ADR-7880)

### Networking and Steam

#### Transport and congestion control

- `REGIA_NET_WIRE_VERSION` is 5, the v0.6 line's one bump: transport v2 and the resume ticket together (4 added input
  feedback). (ADR-8387, ADR-8184, ADR-8298, ADR-8077)
- Every connection is rate-controlled and paced by default: a large reliable transfer takes about its size over the
  path's rate, a joining client starts at `min(project rate, 256 KiB/s)`, and a link that gets faster is found up to
  15 s later (ADR-8301, ADR-8490)
- The rate controller holds a saturating sender at 97 % of the measured bottleneck for 15 s after a queued cut, never
  sets a target under the essential class's rate plus 4 KiB/s, and recovers to 97 % of the delivered rate after a
  congestion cut. Its startup doubles and exits only on rounds the sender filled. (ADR-8490, ADR-8534, ADR-8592)
- The rate controller's delay gradient reads its floor over 64 acknowledged packets on a lossy path, bulk data never
  puts the pacer in debt, and before the first round-trip sample the reassembly stall deadline grows with the oldest
  unanswered packet's wait, up to `LinkConfig::resend_max_ms`. (ADR-8658)
- Path MTU and path validation probes are not samples of the rate controller's rounds. (ADR-8487)
- Loss is declared from acknowledgment evidence only, and one RFC 9002 probe timeout replaces per-fragment resend
  timers; keepalives are acknowledged pings that probe the path during an outage (ADR-7995, ADR-8184)
- The per-packet header is 3 bytes plus the ack frame (about 6 on a clean path) instead of 9; `PACKET_HEADER_BYTES`
  stays 9 as the reserve (ADR-8184)
- Acknowledgment frames name only what the peer has not confirmed hearing about (6 bytes per datagram instead of about
  70 after loss). (ADR-8423)
- The replication bridge rides stream 3, reliable input stream 1, `Channel::Reliable` stream 2;
  `max_reliable_in_flight` is an admission cap per stream (ADR-8302)
- The reassembly stall clock runs per stream with a deadline stretched up to 4x by measured loss. Session control rides
  stream 0. (ADR-8521, ADR-8572)
- V1 transport sends at most 32 data packets ahead of its oldest unresolved packet; unreliable queues are capped at
  64 KiB and inputs, tick sync and state acknowledgments go ahead of reliable backlog. (ADR-7949, ADR-7988)
- Default silence timeout 20 s, interruption from 1500 ms, reliable resend backoff up to 8 s (ADR-7950)
- `NetServer` reports reliable-stream failures as fatal `ServerEvent::Rejected` and drops the peer with
  `protocol_error`; the shared game port drops datagrams over 2,048 bytes uncopied (ADR-7981)
- QUIC starts at a 1,200-byte path MTU with discovery on, and endpoint framing fits QUIC's minimum path. (ADR-7946)
- `UdpTransport::bind` and `from_socket` apply default buffer requests and the DSCP EF mark; a refused option does not
  fail the bind, and a grant under half the request logs one warning. (ADR-7998)
- `regia_net::congestion::CongestionNotches` is deprecated (ADR-8301)
- `regia_net` and `regia_framework` depend on `regia_detsim`.

#### Security and abuse handling

- The malformed score counts only authenticated traffic and weights each refusal: malformed packets, inputs, messages,
  unknown lanes and voice score 16 points (spectator input 1), so a malformed stream is removed in about a second while
  one bad message every 4 s never accumulates. Forged records charge no one. (ADR-8181, ADR-8606)
- Trial decryptions from a new address are budgeted per peer and address, `max_peers_per_ip` limits joins only, and
  peer lookups by address and token are hash-map lookups (ADR-8299)
- A resume ticket or session reconnect token is spent only when the client proves it holds the newer one, so a lost
  accept no longer turns a reconnect into a new join (ADR-8535)

#### Input, prediction and lag compensation

- Input is latched per fixed step and delivered on exactly one tick; the input packet limit is a token bucket holding up
  to 16 ticks' worth, so a stalled Wi-Fi link's burst is kept. (ADR-8217)
- `ClientConfig::input_redundancy` is the most unacknowledged inputs repeated, default 32 (was 8); an acknowledged input
  is never repeated, and a measured loss raises a lower value (3 copies at 5 %). (ADR-8312)
- Size-aware input bundles: each send cuts the history into bundles that fit the datagram budget, at most 4 per send;
  `REGIA_NET_WIRE_VERSION` is 3. (ADR-7896)
- `NetClient::estimated_server_tick` uses a filter over the last 8 tick syncs: it never moves backward and slews up to
  5 % toward a new offset. (ADR-8077)
- A rollback deeper than 32 ticks re-runs to the client tick and smooths through `NetVisualOffset`; the first jitter
  sample is 0 (ADR-8219)
- `lag_compensation.max_rewind_ms` defaults to 1,000 ms (was 250) and caps every shooter's window; a short-link
  shooter's window stays short. (ADR-8315)

#### Lockstep

- The 40-seat lockstep upload bar is a per-second ceiling over 5 minutes: 4.0 Mbit/s in the 95th-percentile second and
  4.4 Mbit/s in the busiest. (ADR-8209)
- Late inputs at the lockstep host reach a later frame instead of being dropped, and a lockstep seat silent past 20 s is
  removed. (ADR-8212)
- Lockstep hosts mirror a seat's datagrams to its candidate address while path validation runs; a port-only move keeps
  the round-trip estimate, and any probe answer validates the candidate. (ADR-8422)

#### Replication and travel

- `sync_possession_owners` runs in `FixedUpdate` before `NetSet::Assign`, so a possession change made by a fixed-tick
  system becomes `NetOwner` in the same tick's gather
- The 16 KiB entity-state cap applies to updates, every record obeys the byte budget, and the in-flight timeout follows
  the measured state-frame round trip (ADR-8419)
- The interest grid keeps one band per relevance radius, so relevance cost no longer grows with the largest profile
  radius, and spawns, despawns and owner changes no longer force full passes (spawn-heavy servers rescanned every held
  set) (ADR-8031)
- Snapshot baselines are shared copy-on-write, so 64 baselines cost less than two world copies (ADR-7983)
- A level travel despawns replicated entities without `NetPersistAcrossTravel`, and `LinkShaper` is compiled out of
  release and shipping builds (ADR-7212)
- The protocol hash starts with `SESSION_WIRE_REVISION` (2), and a reliable-stream update of a tombstoned id is no
  longer a resync ask (ADR-7259, ADR-7295)
- Join snapshots belong on `StreamId::REPLICATION` (stream 3), not `StreamId::BULK`. (ADR-8624)
- The content-manifest check refuses a mod mismatch in both directions; in dev builds only,
  `dev_allow_content_mismatch: true` in `config/net.ron` downgrades it to a warning
- The join snapshot feed re-captures a loading peer only when replication state changed; `JoinSnapshotCounts::captures`
  counts the passes
- Predicted components are fingerprinted by codec layout in the dev `protocol_mismatch` type list
- A second `NetReplayPlayer::open` while another player of the same world is open is refused
  (`NetDemoError::ReplayOpen`, `replay_already_open`); `open_replay` and `net.replay_open` still replace a running
  replay (ADR-8762)
- Server vehicles free a seat whose occupant no longer exists (`net.vehicle_seat_freed`) (ADR-8790)
- A peer whose streaming source has no `Transform` or a non-finite position holds an empty loaded-cell set (fail closed)
  (ADR-8791)

#### Servers, lobbies and relays

- `server.toml` is version 4: `levels.playlist` is optional and exclusive with `levels.rotation`. A failed next row
  is logged as `server_next_level_failed` and skipped. (ADR-8345)
- `server.toml` `[net]` rates override `config/net.ron` only when named (a dedicated server naming only some no longer
  resets the others); `sent_to_all` waits for `InGame`; the client's declared rate lowers its budget; a client camera
  follows the smoothed pose by default (ADR-7213)
- The dedicated server host's crash exit, signal and console handlers and admin token check live behind the platform
  seam, with no behavior change
- Lobbies: `LobbyPlugin` takes the protocol and content hashes from the `NetSession` manifest, and until the game calls
  `Lobbies::set_identity` the lobby keys carry them; a `Lobbies` with no identity refuses `create`, `list` and
  `start_matchmaking` with `lobby_no_identity` unless the game calls `Lobbies::allow_unidentified(true)`; the net-game
  template names its game. (ADR-7310, ADR-7881, ADR-8385)
- Lobby hub: when a lobby owner leaves, the session address it confirmed is withdrawn (`ServerSet` of `None`); only the
  new owner confirms the next, held to the lobby-data budget, and an owner hand-off drops the old owner's reach offer
  and every open ask or plan. An outbox overflow resynchronizes a session instead of dropping it, and `HubHost` sends at
  most `reliable_msgs_per_s` per peer. `Hello`'s player proof is optional; the hub keeps friends as player keys only, a
  freed friend handle is pinned to the player key that held it, and a friends-only lobby refuses a session with no
  player key; a mute holds on the muted player's key across rejoins (leaving and rejoining cleared it).
  `HUB_WIRE_VERSION` is 1. (ADR-8607, ADR-8633)
- `RELAY_PROTOCOL_VERSION` is 3; a protocol 3 relay answers protocol 1 and 2 requests as before. (ADR-8340, ADR-8370)
- A protocol 1 relay request with an allocation tag gets `VersionTooOld`, a newer one `VersionTooNew` (both were
  dropped); the registrar renews its lobby every 15 s (ADR-7982, ADR-8183)
- `DiscoveryHub` gives each listener its own queue. (ADR-7989)
- `LanSocket::poll` returns each announce with its sender and reads at most 256 datagrams; a dormant player keeps its
  store session for the resume (ADR-8502)

### Platforms

- Every Android build declares `android.permission.ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE`, and `regia assets chunks` prints reference
  problems with a severity (ADR-7236)
- An Android launch's `am start` carries `--es regia.dev_launch <value>` (shipping builds ignore it); Android launch
  profiles with a start level or extra arguments launch instead of being refused, and `launch_profile_unsupported` now
  means only a package-off Android profile. (ADR-8472)
- On Windows a canceled game build ends cargo and every `rustc` it started (a job object with `KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE`), as
  the Unix process-group kill does (ADR-9770).
- Auto quality: only the measured Intel N150 (PCI 8086:46d4) starts on Very Low; its siblings start on Low until
  measured
- GPU selection precedence is PCI, UUID, index, name. Linux resolves `REGIA_GPU_UUID` from
  `/proc/driver/nvidia/gpus/*/information` and runs the timed `nvidia-smi` query only when that tree is absent; a PCI
  pin never launches it and cross-checks only its own card; UUID prefixes must match exactly one GPU. (ADR-7958,
  ADR-7991)
- In a DLSS or XeSS build, `REGIA_GPU_INDEX` / `REGIA_GPU_NAME` selecting a DX12 adapter opens DX12, and
  `list_adapters` lists every adapter with the numbering `REGIA_GPU_INDEX` uses. (ADR-7820)
- `regia_terrain::gpu::headless_bcn_device` returns one process-wide device. (ADR-7613)

### Tools, pipeline and agent protocol

#### Cook, paks and packaging

- Cook and package run source diagnostics before publishing and never repair sources. `cook: Fail`, and package when
  `config/validation.ron` does not name `cook`, stop on an unsuppressed geometry or `texture_*` warning or an
  incomplete report. (ADR-8390, ADR-8536)
- The chunk cook's startup pin includes the startup UI layouts' pictures and the startup level's bakes; with
  `content = "closure"` travel-target levels ship with their content; a malformed level or UI layout, or a missing
  startup level, fails the cook and the package (ADR-8477)
- A shipping package whose final artifact is rejected fails after the archive is written and keeps no receipt; ZIP and
  tar headers are scanned as content (ADR-8348)
- `regia cook --ship` stores blobs that lz4 shrinks by less than 15 % raw (`SHIP_MIN_RATIO`), mostly BC and ASTC
  textures, on every platform; cooks without `--ship` are byte-identical (ADR-7971)
- The pack stamp is version 2 (manifest hash and pack options): `regia package` takes cooked paks only when
  compressed and repacks for `REGIA_PAK_GPU_MESHES`; chunk-assigned assets with no cooked file fail the pack with
  `chunk_asset_unwritten`. (ADR-7612)
- Packaged games apply `[platforms.<os>] render_profile` from `build_info.json` before Startup (player settings win,
  then the package, then saved or first-launch Auto) and carry `[boot]` movies, also in Android APKs; explicit
  Auto-detect replaces the package profile for one launch. (ADR-7893)
- `regia package` never ships raw ability sources (`.attrs.ron`, `.effect.ron`, `.ability.ron`); the cooked form ships
  in the pak (ADR-7762, ADR-9850)
- A project's `.netprofile.ron` and `.data.ron` files now cook, and a `.netprofile.ron` failing its checks fails the
  cook; `LevelSettings` and `LevelLook` serialize by hand so reflected postcard round-trips (ADR-7232)
- Cooking and level or prefab packaging strip review-note marker subtrees; saved source levels keep them
  (`strip_review_notes`).
- The generic cook no longer offers `.meshlayers.ron`, `.passages.ron` or `.meshlayers` files to an importer (they were
  reported as unsupported on every cook) (ADR-8451)
- `[cook] texture_quality` is the default quality of textures cooked inside the import-rules scope (ADR-7310)
- `TextureImporterSettings::fit` also honors the cook diet's cap for the source being cooked (ADR-8461)
- `AssetServer::open` refuses a torn chunk pak (wrong length or unreadable) into `refused_chunks` and still opens (a
  torn chunk stopped the app at startup); `content.mount` reports a wrong length as `chunk_size_mismatch` (ADR-8714)
- The newest mounted pak that holds an asset decides: an entry there that does not read fails with `pak_entry_corrupt`
  instead of falling back to an older pak; an id a refused chunk lists is served by a newer pak, never an older one
  (`chunk_refused`); a content update that changes a chunk's size reads `NeedsUpdate` (ADR-8810, ADR-8860)
- Agent request paths no longer implicitly allow the working directory. Manifest-listed chunk paks are checked against
  the manifest's size and full-file BLAKE3 before mounting: `AssetServer::open` fails at startup when a listed pak does
  not match (`chunk_integrity_mismatch`), so a damaged install exits with an error code. (ADR-7871)
- Typed loads (`load_mesh`, `load_texture`, ...) pin their id, and memory budgets count CPU plus GPU bytes (ADR-7256)

#### Import and textures

- glTF importer 1.14.2 joins the node-clips and texture-dedupe changes; every glTF/GLB re-cooks once. Converted
  sources apply the `.meta` clip options and the contact-curve and lossless options. (ADR-8574, ADR-7904, ADR-8308)
- `GltfAnimSettings::ranges` apply only to animations that no `clips` entry names.
- A glTF whose triangle meshes are all `_LODn` with `n > 0` (a one-level file, a clip GLB whose bind mesh is `_LOD2`)
  cooks (it failed with `no mesh found in glTF file`); its primary is the lowest level present. (ADR-7959)
- Importer default thresholds for authored `_LODn` chains halve at every level (LOD3 0.125, LOD4 0.0625), so the default
  LOD3 no longer disappears in five-or-more-level chains. (ADR-7869)
- A malformed `.meta` beside an image a glTF references fails that glTF's cook. (ADR-8054)
- A pass-through DDS or KTX 2.0 texture reports the mip that survives `max_size` (ADR-8536)
- The workspace builds its C++ dependencies with `-ffp-contract=off` (per-target `CXXFLAGS` in `.cargo/config.toml`: no
  FP contraction) and astcenc in its invariant build, so cooked bytes do not depend on the cook host's ISA:
  meshoptimizer cluster DAGs and Android ASTC bytes are the same on x86_64 and aarch64 cook hosts. (ADR-7878)

#### Content lint and hygiene

- Source lint has 35 rules with five `texture_*` rules; a robust coincidence profile parses the source on every scan.
  (ADR-8536, ADR-8574)
- Material lint and fix keep authored metallic-roughness products, omitted factors and authored colors by default, so
  default repair no longer replaces a valid authored surface; the older ORM, dielectric and volume-tint repairs run only
  under `compatibility` (ADR-8250)
- `ok` for material lint and fix is true only when `remaining` is empty and `compatible` is true (ADR-8392)
- Source-lint resolves materials as `compatibility` before overlays; a stale hygiene allowance is finding
  `allowance_obsolete` (ADR-8248, ADR-8247)
- Shipping packages reject recognized secret signatures by default, and every profile validates `[package_hygiene]`
  in `Regia.toml`. (ADR-8062, ADR-8247, ADR-8427)
- Unbounded hygiene patterns, among them the default secret signatures, run on the whole input when it fits in the
  memory budget and otherwise fail closed; a file that changes during the audit fails closed (ADR-8244)
- Reordering or inserting explicit hygiene rules does not move a waiver onto another rule body (ADR-8247)
- The built-in `naming_rules` check allows uppercase ASCII, so a project with uppercase shipped paths no longer gets
  `validate_name_invalid`; Windows device names match in any case. (ADR-7961)

#### Formats and upgrades

- `regia upgrade-project --write` is two-phase: any migrator error leaves the project byte-identical; level directories
  swap in by rename. It names the backup it actually wrote (`.bak.2` after an earlier `.bak`), lists and removes staging
  directories an interrupted run left, and skips `build/`, `dist/` and those staging directories. (ADR-7715, ADR-8811,
  ADR-8861)
- `cargo xtask check-formats` fails on a shape change without a bump, also one recorded with `check-formats --update`,
  and on a second bump in one release; spec 25 § 7.1's "an optional, defaulted field keeps the version" is superseded:
  any change of a frozen format's hashed fields bumps it. (ADR-7724)
- `cargo xtask check-formats` reports a version rollback as such and writes `format-hashes.ron` atomically
- Unknown fields are refused in `.track.ron`, `.playlist.ron`, `.contract.ron` and delivery-contract kit rows
  (misspelled keys loaded silently); a newer track or playlist reports its version; an empty playlist `map_id` is
  refused; `.assetids.ron` refuses paths that differ only in case (ADR-8553, ADR-8622)
- `.detutility.ron` is gameplay data, so `regia run --dev` and the live cooker forward its edits (ADR-8609)
- The editor writes `.data.ron` as `DataAsset(format_version, type, value)`; files written with `type_path:` still open
  and are rewritten on save (ADR-7310)
- Engine-owned on-disk names use the new brand: path-tracer EXR keys `regia.*`, bake stamps `regia-bake-stamp/1`,
  crash parts with `X-Regia-Report-Name` (plus the old header for one release), GPU card locks
  `/tmp/regia-gpu-<n>.lock` then the old lock; every reader still accepts the old spelling (ADR-8670)

#### Agent protocol and CLI

- `agent.changes` without `limit` returns at most 200 records; loop `since = next` until a page is empty (ADR-7454)
- The WebSocket and local agent inboxes take turns within the 64-requests-a-frame budget, so a busy WebSocket client no
  longer starves `regia agent call` and other local callers (ADR-7454)
- `agent.run_tests`, `agent.golden_test`, `build_game` and nested cargo runs no longer pass the host's `RUST_TEST_*`
  variables to the child run, so failure output reaches the result; a variable set on purpose in `RunTestsEnv` still
  does (ADR-7611, ADR-7689, ADR-7714)
- `editor.a11y_action` needs the `review` and `build` permissions; `editor.a11y_tree` is read only (its `keep_on`
  moved to `editor.a11y_keep_tree`). (ADR-7795)
- `play.hitch_report` `streaming_waits.render_residency` waits and timeouts are deltas over the measured window, and
  the Streaming row is reported but not judged
- `audio.program_loudness` is a `play` method; `camera.blend_to` `duration` and `logic.reload` `path` of the wrong JSON
  type answer `invalid_params` (ADR-7346)
- `audio.mix` returns both `stall_watchdog` and `output` (ADR-7715)
- `seeing.screenshot` answers `overlay_needs_editor` for `selection` and `labels` and a parameter error for unknown
  overlays; `regia cook --json` sends child stdout to stderr; an invalid `[cook] texture_quality` is refused (ADR-7217)
- `scene.serialize` omits opaque resource backing rows with no reflected values; reflected resources and real empty
  entities stay.
- `regia logs triage` and `logs.triage` read frame timings from `data.frame_ms`, count `summary.json` and
  `summary.partial.json` as session summaries, and charge every visited directory entry against `max_files`.
  (ADR-8442)
- `cargo xtask det-scan` prints `det-scan: N crate(s), N module(s), N file(s), N finding(s), N closure problem(s)`.
- `regia loc export|import` call `regia_asset_pipeline::loc_exchange`, with unchanged output. (ADR-7677)
- `regia loc compile`, `regia package` and the Localization panel share
  `regia_asset_pipeline::loc_exchange::compile_bundles`; PO and XLIFF exports carry every translator-context comment as
  a note.
- `regia bake-lightmaps --gpu --gpu-fallback --checkpoint` reruns a CPU-written checkpoint on the CPU. (ADR-5786)
- `samples/arena/Regia.toml` sets `[package_hygiene] max_memory_bytes = 536870912`, so its ~138 MB shipping binary
  fits the whole-input secret scan (ADR-8244) instead of failing closed with `unbounded_regex` (ADR-8786)
- `photo.capture` confines `directory` with the output-root rule: a relative folder joins the project root (it joined
  the process's working directory), an absolute one must lie under the project, the temp directory, an `output_roots`
  entry or the session pictures folder, and `..` is refused with `path_outside_project` (ADR-8795)
- `detsim.ground_vehicle_simulate` answers a non-string `model` with `invalid_params` naming `model` instead of
  `bad_model`; `terrain.scatter_colliders` `mirror_x` is described as "Answer -x" (ADR-8795)

### Audio, media, UI and accessibility

- Moving focus away from a text field abandons an in-progress IME composition the same frame (preedit, underline,
  IME rect and caret clear) (ADR-7208)
- An idle text field re-syncs its label when the IME composition or composing caret changes (ADR-7310)
- An app with `Draw2dPlugin` draws through the retained target with dirty regions on (ADR-8514)
- `.ui.ron` v11 carries layout `variants` and `back`, `disabled_subtree` and `pivot`; a click under a recursively
  disabled subtree is a disabled hit for the pointer claim too. (ADR-7858, ADR-8252, ADR-8575)
- `.audiopatch.ron` v1 files load through a migrator, `regia upgrade-project` stamps them v2, and
  `AudioPatch::from_def` rejects a layer with no or several sources (ADR-7226)
- `SpeakerActivity` is debounced to 10 Hz per sender (ADR-7237)
- With the `Menu` context enabled its bindings, not raw keys, drive UI navigation (ADR-7070)
- Right arrow in right-to-left text moves the caret right on screen; caption colors come from the theme's `subtitle`
  tokens (ADR-7208)
- Extension discovery refuses a crate whose `[package.metadata.regia] id` differs from the manifest, and reports an
  extension with only other audiences' code as `filtered` (ADR-7237)
- Spawned patches restart on rebuilt output tracks with their ids and state, and live patch updates keep asset volume
  and pitch defaults (ADR-8052)
- A bus with a pre-fader send builds as two mixer tracks; changing a tap rebuilds the bus tree (ADR-8590)
- `editor.ui_designer_edit {op: "set"}` without `node` sets the field on the whole selection. (ADR-9730)
- A ripple's point is the edge's old place, a rippling drag applies from the press, a rippling cut move is bounded by
  the first shot's start only, and the dope sheet refuses only overlaps a move creates; ripples move whole sections
  that start after the edge. (ADR-8640, ADR-7847)
- A previewed video section plays in the bound entity's `VideoPlayerState` and never writes its `VideoPlayer`; reverse
  play is silent in the preview. (ADR-7847)
- The editor's default `accesskit` feature turns on `regia_platform/accesskit-adapters`, so a workspace build no longer
  adds the runtime bridge's system to every `PlatformPlugin` app. The editor window is created hidden and shown once
  the adapter attaches on Windows and macOS. (ADR-7795, ADR-7746)
- Outliner eye and lock buttons, its search field and the viewport quality and pivot combo boxes have spoken names.
  (ADR-7746)
- A `.ui.ron` button, slider or menu bar puts its own widget class before the authored classes, so an authored variation
  such as `classes: ["primary"]` shows `Button.primary` (ADR-8303). The built-in themes are v2 files with the same look.
- An active audio snapshot's mute beats the host mute, and push and pop tween send levels to the snapshot's;
  `BusLayout::validate` refuses newer versions, double bypasses and snapshots naming missing buses or unrouted sends.
- Runtime style inspection keeps the full cascade history, not only the winner; `spawn_ui` inserts the document's back
  policy on the root.
- HRTF at 44.1 and 48 kHz is bit-identical; mixers below 44.1 kHz get their own table; a binaural voice costs more CPU
  at high rates (1.70 ms per second of audio at 48 kHz, 6.34 ms at 96 kHz, 24.4 ms at 192 kHz) (ADR-7461)
- A shortened Catmull-Rom camera path keeps its keys past the new end as control keys, so the shot samples exactly as
  before the cut; `.shots.ron` allows camera path and look-at keys past the shot's duration (ADR-8640)
- A resampled reverb impulse response keeps its gain (taps scale by `from / to`), and the cook refuses an over-long
  IR from its header before decoding. (ADR-7602)
- A video player's PCM ring holds half a second plus its decode-ahead window, at most 4 s and 8 MiB (700 ms for a
  30 fps clip with the default queue); at most 4 `Looped` events are reported per tick. (ADR-7563, ADR-7635)
- A backdrop pool releases its targets after 120 window draws without a blur. (ADR-7869)
- `regia_ui::interaction::sync_pointer_gate`: while `regia_input::PointerClaims` is enabled (the default with
  `UiPlugin`), `UiPointerGate::ui_consumed` is set only while a primary press belongs to a UI node, not while the pointer
  hovers one. With claims disabled the gate keeps ADR-7070's hover-or-press reading. (ADR-8786)

### Core, licence and security

- Recovery report `tail_bytes` is clamped to 8 MiB, and the `regia_core::log::unclean` ring line carries `sanitized`,
  `complete`, `policy_version`, `invalid_utf8` and `partial_line` (ADR-8494)
- The workspace `libm` entry is pinned `=0.2.16`; a future bump is a sim-version change (ADR-8013)
- Console and file log lines and tracing ring messages carry a `t=<tick>` prefix (`log::set_tick`).
- `regia_core::profiling::system_name` returns `Option<&'static str>`.
- `docs/ported-sources.toml` drafts are split around landed requirement lines, and the absorb-plan coverage test
  accepts a landed `requirements` row naming its plan item. (ADR-8497)
- `regia::telemetry::trace` marks on the process recorder are kept in the counter listing's mark list whether or not a
  trace records.
- `FrameProfiler::end_frame` reuses its buffers: a warmed-up frame allocates nothing with the timeline on or off, and
  with flame capture on (ADR-7325, ADR-7493)
- The play flame context covers only the compute task pool: roots on the async-compute and IO pools are their own roots
  (ADR-7427)

### Tests, docs and tooling

- The lockstep `clippy.toml` denies float `max`/`min`/`clamp` and 124 glam scalar-type trig paths; ADR-8012 A-4 names
  these rules and requires `ensure_default_fp_env()` at sim-thread start (ADR-8085)
- The regia_quick `text` golden shows the renamed brand string ("Regia quick draw").
- Soak plans: the gate plan has 61 runs and the nightly 246; the soak kit uses the engine's silence policy and input
  loop, so stored `result.json` numbers from older kits are not comparable run for run. (ADR-8209, ADR-8409)
- `docs/06-testing-and-ci.md`: the net soak and determinism goldens sections moved to § 29.
- Net soak `result.json` is format `regia-net-soak` v2 and the gate plan grows from 14 to 56 runs; bars gate only when
  flipped in `xtask::net_soak::BARS` (ADR-8030)
- `tick_p99` is judged against a tier per client count (8.333/11.111/16.666 ms), `others_unaffected` excuses a client's
  own outages, and `no_hijack` judges every hostile profile (ADR-8454)
- Three soak pin tests now fail on setups that really break their bar (ADR-8424)
- Flaky-test registry rows run `regia_render`'s lib tests (they fill an RTX 5090's 256 MiB BAR1), `emissive_hitch`,
  `arena` `spawn_hitch`, `arena` `repro_check` and `regia_agent` `dispatch_fuzz` alone on the GPU pass
  (`gaterig_gpu_solo`, `cargo xtask flaky --gate-args gpu`). No bar changed. (ADR-8598, ADR-8599, ADR-8635)
- The performance-gate acceptance tests moved to `perf_gate/tests.rs` under `#![cfg(test)]`.
- `agent_parity_scan` fails on a `gap` row its `KNOWN_GAPS` list does not name, and finds `a11y_audit::glyph_button` and
  `bare_checkbox` call sites (ADR-7452)
- `cargo xtask preflight --changed --no-dependents`: a change to `Cargo.lock`, the root `Cargo.toml` or other
  workspace-wide files no longer selects the whole workspace (a scoped preflight test build linked every debug test
  binary when `Cargo.lock` changed) (ADR-8441)
- `regia_render` lib tests keep each device on every hardware adapter, and `regia_agent` test builds share one device
  per process (ADR-7531)
- Nine determinism bit pins follow their crate's determinism feature, and stale pins under `cross-platform-determinism`
  and `physics-cross-platform-determinism` are fixed (ADR-7668)
- `cargo xtask android-pass` falls back to the `boot: first_present at` line for a cold start's first frame when logcat
  drops the marker, and when the resume marker is missing (ADR-7687)
- `regia doctor`'s unit-test smoke is hermetic (fake machine probe, no subprocesses or Vulkan instance)
- `cargo xtask check-formats` checks `docs/formats/proposed-formats.ron`, a register of formats designed before a crate
  defines them (empty now that both world geometry formats are built) (ADR-8019, ADR-8112)
- The `regia_framework` unreliable-mode `net_character` bound is a test-local 1e-3 m (ADR-8763, ADR-8850)
- `cargo xtask release-notes --check` accepts `v<major>.<minor>.<patch>-<lane>.md` fragments from v0.5.1 on, and
  `cargo xtask notices` writes both notices files through staged temporary files. (ADR-7624, ADR-7684)
- The visibility-buffer close and residency tests draw a checked-in cluster DAG of the deterministic sphere.
  (ADR-7878)
- GPU test binaries share one device per process (`RenderDevice::shared_headless()`) instead of destroying a device per
  test (the loader-list race that failed `render_out_shutter` on the Intel Arc), and the agent tests skip with a line on
  a host with no adapter unless `REGIA_REQUIRE_ADAPTER` is set. (ADR-7613)
- `pipeline_cache_instances` opens its second instance explicitly, so `regia_render --test pipeline_cache_instances`
  passes on every adapter again. (ADR-7643)
- `arena --test wc_render_agent` captures use a 480 s answer timeout, so the test no longer fails on a cold, loaded
  card. (ADR-7704)
- `regia-hot-host` (the hot reload acceptance host, `--features hot-dylib`) stops with `RESULT fail` before its next
  script command when the process that started it exits, instead of editing the game crate for minutes after its test
  was killed (ADR-8787)

## Fixed

### Editor

#### Viewport and 2D

- Pressing Enter or Space with a viewport pane focused replaced the selection, and a click in a pane picked nothing
  while the transform gizmo was shown (ADR-8870)
- With the transform gizmo shown, a box select, orbit, pan or wheel zoom that started away from its handles did nothing
  (ADR-7593)
- Show ▸ Navigation drew nothing in a stock editor (ADR-7593)
- Viewport: Enter or Space on a possessed pane no longer captures the mouse; the trigger-volume helper no longer clones
  every collider per frame and no longer hides sensors spawned after a large trimesh; `--headless` auto key records an
  agent's move at the next frame (ADR-7495)
- Viewport gizmo: deselecting clears a temporary pivot offset, `editor.rotate_step` uses the project's saved angle step
  before the toolbar draws, and switching projects no longer carries snap steps over (ADR-7813)
- The gizmo's Local space could never be chosen (it was always world space).
- Viewport camera: Move selection to camera from the toolbar with an orthographic pane active no longer uses that pane's
  hidden eye pose; Move camera to selection stops a running eased move; changing `camera.near_clip` and
  `camera.far_clip` together applies both (ADR-7797).
- A right- or middle-button drag that began on a viewport overlay group did not move the pane camera. (ADR-8532)
- Dragging a selected procedural spline's control point did nothing while its gizmo showed, and a point under a gizmo
  handle took that handle's press. (ADR-7674)
- egui's second UI pass of a frame no longer moves a piloted entity twice, turns measure mode off, restarts a measure
  or toggles a Ctrl+click selection back.
- A terrain hidden in the editor only is no longer drawn (ADR-7255); `editor.capture_viewport` with a size past `u32`
  captures the default size.
- A very thin pane no longer asks egui for an oversized viewport texture and panics, and Tools ▸ Project Health is
  listed under Tools again.
- 2D tab: a frame egui runs twice no longer applies a click, key or guide drag twice, tileset binding edits renumber the
  brush and copied stamp with undo, a filled rectangle past 65,536 cells is refused whole, and a broken
  `.tilepatterns.ron` shows its error (ADR-7813)
- Kit strokes longer than the undo entry cap keep every cell's undo; a repeated egui layout pass no longer applies a
  region or kit stroke twice.

#### Outliner, Inspector and prefabs

- Prefab edit mode leaked a closed prefab into the next one ("duplicate entity guids"); `editor.save_level` did not
  write entities `scene.spawn` / `scene.spawn_primitive` made (ADR-7382)
- Edit in place: the opened prefab can be picked and selected, a level save no longer writes the hidden instance or
  marks the prefab saved, and the Nested apply target is not offered when a variant already overrides the field
  (ADR-7835). A level save during isolated prefab mode no longer writes its scratch level, and opening or closing a
  prefab no longer unloads a level open beside the host (ADR-8128).
- *Break placed level* no longer deletes entities the level put under the instance, and its undo restores children
  under their member; a level save keeps entities authored under an inline placed level (ADR-8593, ADR-8680)
- The Inspector no longer copies a prefab member's component every repaint (ADR-8593)
- The single-entity Inspector shows Revert and Apply again for overrides of list elements, vector lanes and
  extension-drawn or Lua components. (ADR-7794)
- `editor.open_level` answers `prefab_open` while a prefab edit session is open
- An unreadable Outliner view file is kept instead of being replaced by defaults; opening a level with two same-named
  unique entities no longer renames one outside the undo history; the Outliner no longer advances the change tick every
  draw; another project's Outliner view file is read on project switch; undoing Change kind to a directional light
  removes the `InheritedVisibility` it added (ADR-7814).
- Outliner `tag:` follows in-place tag edits and `type:` matches a generic component by its outer name;
  `content::create_material_instance` refuses a `parent` outside the project. (ADR-7662)
- A refused `editor.outliner_rows` call no longer resets the Outliner's open and closed rows.
- A refused `editor.vehicle_tuning_run { tow: true }` no longer leaves tow on; Enter in the Outliner search no longer
  frames the selection; Inspector Ctrl+F works without the pointer over the panel.
- With one entity selected the Inspector could remove only its first removable component (ADR-7472)
- The multi-selection Inspector's `@Button` fields now run, and its link toggle is remembered.
- The instance inspector labels an empty-list slot `empty (hides the default)` with **Clear** enabled, and the Chooser
  tab keeps unparsed text for correction (ADR-7716)
- A newer `inspector_pins.ron` is reported as "written by a newer editor" and no longer overwritten (ADR-7813)

#### Play in editor and launch

- Split-screen play with two to four local players no longer panics on wgpu validation (HDR effect depth, decal targets
  and scratch copies match each player's image), and layer binding undo restores the previous `LevelLayers` (ADR-7956).
- PIE stand-in cameras appeared beside a game's `Startup` camera and overwrote the transforms of controllers the game
  joined; `editor.play_state.play_window.panes` reported rects up to 1 px off (ADR-7466)
- A game panic during play left no backtrace in `editor.play_state` (ADR-7205)
- A game's own component types authored in a level are registered in the play world, not left as unregistered blobs
  (ADR-7251)
- Exiting the editor with the play window open remembers the window's size and position (ADR-7313)
- Preview language **Source** now also resets a running play session to the source locale.
- The Android application id could carry shell syntax to the device through `adb shell`; a standalone launch could run
  another binary when cargo built none for the launcher package (now `launch_build_failed`) (ADR-7548)
- Explicit launch-profile start levels survive launch stop and cleanup. (ADR-7843)

#### Content Browser, import and packaging

- A double click on a level in the Content Browser no longer drops the open level's unsaved edits (Save all dialog
  first; `asset_open_play_running`, `asset_open_prefab_editing` while playing or editing a prefab), and `editor.select`
  with several ids in `"set"` mode selects them all.
- Content Browser typed search and filter chips narrow the shown static or saved-query collection.
- Content Browser search token completion no longer splits a multibyte character.
- The asset tooltip's revision line, the Outliner's Revision column and `editor.status_bar` `revision` now show the git
  state (ADR-8126); the project thumbnail is now saved (ADR-8190).
- Files dropped onto the editor window were ignored. (ADR-7788)
- The Asset Viewer's sound preview decodes Ogg Vorbis, FLAC and MP3 as well as WAV
- `view.content_drawer` with the Content tab shown panicked in debug builds (ADR-8702)
- Import dialog: every control applies once per frame, and failed or canceled copies leave no `.meta` behind.
- Import settings text fields lost characters on every keystroke, and one field's edit no longer clears another's
  refusal
- Import settings ▸ Apply cooked on the UI thread, also under a held cook lock, and never invalidated the content index;
  Show ▸ Build navigation preview voxelized on the UI thread and its size check ignored static colliders. (ADR-7664)
