- Applying import settings to several sources checks them all before writing, cooks once, and restores every written
  `.meta` on failure. (ADR-7794)
- `editor.set_import_settings` and the Import settings Apply no longer refuse with `tools_busy` while the editor spawns
  another process (Linux, Android). (ADR-8366)
- `editor.import { async: true }` canceled after the worker's last cancel check now ends `done`, so state, result and
  files agree; Simulate's entity count subtracts only the entities that went (ADR-7251)
- `editor.spawn_prefab`, `editor.open_prefab`, `editor.edit_material` and the `editor.import` folder took an absolute or
  UNC path as a project path; they refuse it with `path_outside_project`
- A collections file that does not parse is no longer overwritten (`collection_file_unreadable`,
  `favorites_file_unreadable`); `editor.content_favorites` with no params no longer rewrites the file; idle frames no
  longer rebuild the collection library. (ADR-7798)
- A package published into a folder that already had `symbols/` reported failure and left its staging folder; the
  publish now merges `symbols/` file by file and lists what already moved when it fails. The icon picker overwrote an
  icon without asking; Project Settings ▸ Icon ▸ Choose icon… now asks before overwriting an existing icon
- A crash during File ▸ Package left `.regia-package-*` folders for good, and the staging sweep could remove a live
  run's folder or lock file: the sweep now runs on a worker thread, keeps a folder whose package process still runs, and
  removes a lock-less older folder only when its editor process is gone and it is more than 24 hours old. (ADR-7794)
- A canceled package deleted files other programs wrote into the output folder, a canceled re-package left a
  half-built staging folder, Rebuild asset database froze the editor during a cook, and `editor.menu_list` listed items
  no menu drew. **File ▸ Package for platform** now writes into a private staging folder
  (`<out>/.regia-package-<pid>-<n>`) and moves only a finished package into the output folder; a canceled, failed or
  dry run removes the staging folder and nothing else. Rebuild asset database never waits for the cook lock
  (`tools_busy`). (ADR-7496)

#### Levels, saving and autosave

- An editor save no longer drops the level's `LevelLayers` binding (ADR-7203)
- An `AudioEmitter` or `SmartObject` in a level was dropped from the saved file (both now reflect `Clone`). (ADR-7741)
- The Save all dialog no longer lists most asset editors' documents as "save it from its editor", and in prefab edit
  mode Save selected no longer writes the prefab as `assets/unsaved*.level.ron`; inline assets and motion databases
  register a Save all hook.
- Closing the editor with **Save selected** while a document cannot be saved from the dialog no longer exits and drops
  it. (ADR-7822)
- The level autosave holds what a save writes (no `EditorOnly` entities or preview values, layer binding kept); the
  Profiler panel no longer reconnect-loops when a remote build refuses `profiler.set_capture {timeline}` (ADR-7422)
- A panic in an autosave write no longer ends the worker or loses queued writes, the queue no longer grows without bound
  (autosave queues at most one tick at a time), an agent recovery call and a recovery-window button in the same frame
  both stick, and saving one untitled material graph tab no longer renames another's autosave (untitled material graph
  autosaves are labeled by the tab's session number) (ADR-7345)
- An edited `.regia/autosave/docs/index.ron` could delete files outside the docs folder; invalid ids and file names are
  now ignored (security) (ADR-7345)
- A level save during a sequence preview no longer makes the file watcher reload the level or prompt about disk changes
- A table or `.vehicle.ron` opened behind the one its panel shows is now autosaved while dirty (it was lost in a crash)
  (ADR-8365).
- File-only command groups no longer dirty the level; hosted scene spawns keep their names and level ownership through
  redo.
- A broken `recent_projects_meta.ron` no longer loses every project's tags (edits are refused with
  `recent_meta_unreadable`), and a failed File ▸ New level keeps the open level and its entities.
- A compact or hand-written `.data.ron` no longer opens as unsaved, and a theme written from the defaults reads back
  the same.
- Project Settings saves keep debug palette edits saved separately.
- The version history's size cap removed same-millisecond versions out of order. (ADR-7928)
- A large terrain import could clear the undo history with no warning (ADR-8541)
- `regia upgrade-project --write --backup <dir>` keeps the files directly inside `.regia/` (or `.ferrite/`), among them
  editor layouts and `agent.toml`, in the backup (ADR-8435)

#### Graph, procgen and sequence editors

- A material graph collapse whose outputs share nodes compiled each shared node per output; visible node swatches were
  evicted past 256 nodes. (ADR-7730)
- **Apply to selection** in the material graph is all or nothing; fracture bond hover clears when the pointer leaves
  (ADR-7451)
- Material graph editor: undoing an instance reparent points the tab back at the old parent graph.
- Reroutes no longer leave a straight wire under their points (ADR-9970).
- Escape closes the Nodes-list palette of the material and procedural graph panels; accessible names for sequencer
  timeline fields, five procgen combos and the procedural graph's error badge
- The Problems panel draws the logic graph section once, and Find usages walks the chain IK, body aim, stride and
  orientation warp nodes.
- A logic graph's dropped-wire menu no longer pulls focus back every frame, and an unreadable or newer
  `.regia/logic_breakpoints.ron` is never overwritten (`save_failed`).
- A refused procgen data-table filter no longer stays stored and fails every later `editor.procgen_table` call
  (ADR-7239); the preview falls back to the last generator used (ADR-7215)
- `regia procgen bake --prune` deleted bakes of a generator-less anchored entity, of prefab instances in an `Absent`
  data layer, and of instances whose template failed. It now deletes a `.pgout` only when nothing in the level, its
  placed levels or its prefab instances holds its guid, prints each kept file with a reason, and keeps every guid-named
  file while a template, placed level or instance member is missing. (ADR-7743, ADR-7793)
- The Biome editor discarded unsaved edits on open, edited the wrong element of number-named lists, merged two drags
  into one undo step, and refused renames of an element with a pre-existing issue. (ADR-7793)
- Pasting, rippling, moving or deleting sequencer event keys did nothing (ADR-8628)
- The sequence player of `editor.seq_new`, `editor.seq_curve_open` and the Sequence Curves panel is `EditorOnly`: it
  never reaches a level save, autosave, export or live link (ADR-8583)
- A shot list loaded by `render.sequence` after a document was opened kept its path, so Save overwrote the opened file;
  the document now detaches (`shot_panel_detached`) (ADR-8940)
- Timeline cut and fade edits were lost at session end (no save path); auto key never keyed a gizmo move with the
  Sequence Curves panel closed (ADR-8730)
- After `render.sequence` replaces the Shots panel's list, Save, Ctrl+S and autosave no longer write it over the old
  list's file; `editor.data_asset_open { discard: true }` with a bad path keeps unsaved edits (ADR-7495)

#### Panels and tools

- Material Graph first draw blocked the UI for about 10 s on an RTX 3080 Ti (60 s on lavapipe), Anim Graph for up to 43
  s on lavapipe, and Create ▸ Camera's next frame for 10-13 s on cards: the Material Graph preview, the rig preview and
  the viewport's first camera preview now render on a background preview thread, and panels show the last picture or
  "Compiling the preview…" (`editor.panel_timings` `preview_jobs`) (ADR-8584)
- The navigation preview's gather froze the editor beyond a cancel's reach (its geometry gather now runs on the bake
  worker and a cancel reaches it), and the Profiler charged play rendering to the editor (it counts the editor's
  pictures of the play world as play, `pie_render` span) (ADR-8594)
- The Profiler's frozen frame drifted as new frames arrived, a strip click held the newest frame, and a process source
  skipped frames between polls; `profiler.timeline` with no recorded frame panicked. The History freeze now stops intake
  until Unfreeze, and a Timeline click holds the frame under the pointer. (ADR-7849)
- Duplicating a reflection capture gave the copy the original's `LocalCapture.id`, so the next bake refused the level
  with `capture_duplicate_id`. (ADR-7663)
- A finished reflection capture bake did not mark the level unsaved, and a capture moved earlier in the same agent batch
  was baked at its old center (ADR-7592)
- The asset viewer's collision overlay shows the new hulls after an import-settings Apply; the fracture panel's hovered
  bond clears when the gizmo is off (ADR-7495)
- `VehicleTuning::open` drops the previous car's queued Apply, target, runs and telemetry entity, and New no longer
  silently overwrites an existing tuned file (`vehicle_tuning_exists`) (ADR-7343)
- Motion database: the rebuild ends in a headless editor too, and Live rebuild retries a revision whose rebuild could
  not start.
- Montage panel **Set follow** relinked a section to segment 0 (ADR-7630)
- `editor.play_sound` and the Cue editor play `.ogg`, `.flac` and `.mp3`; `abilities.add_attributes` reads an unloaded
  set from the project's `assets/` folder.
- An agent could not answer the level's changed-on-disk prompt, and `editor.anim_skeleton add_socket` reset a socket's
  rotation. (ADR-7747)
- Disabling a UI subtree releases its focused child and cancels its drag; designer samples can be exercised without
  changing the document.
- `editor.ui_designer_edit` `add` and `paste` with `"parent": null` use the default parent; glTF export during a
  sequence preview exported the previewed transforms; restoring the level row during a prefab session spawned the level
  beside the prefab
- An Output Log line naming a `.logic.ron` was not a link; the shell's procgen graph open branch was unreachable.
- A hosted agent's plan printed behind more than eight output lines now closes the mutation gate before the calls
  sent after it; history display tolerates non-UTF-8 project paths. (ADR-7844)
- A collaboration session no longer drops a live peer after a local stall, and a join with a new nonce from a pending or
  rejected address replaces that request (ADR-7842).
- The Version History preview text no longer keeps keyboard focus (ADR-8435)
- The window state is not recorded while the main window is minimized.

#### Menus, look and accessibility

- The UI-scale preference had no effect in the windowed editor, symbols drew as empty boxes, `regia-light` drew dark
  controls on white panels, and tool windows opened stacked over the toolbars. Bridged UI themes (`regia-dark`,
  `regia-light`) now take control fills, outlines, links, selection and status colors from their roles, so a light theme
  draws light controls; the editor installs its own font table with symbol and script fallbacks; tool windows open in a
  cascade inside the dock area (ADR-8585, ADR-8589)
- The default editor theme failed WCAG AA on three pairs, and High contrast's hovered text on panel was 1:1; every
  built-in look now passes (ADR-8585, ADR-8679)
- The six painted canvases had no AccessKit name or role, and fields in the material graph, sequencer and procedural
  graph panels were unnamed (ADR-7383)
- The play toolbar's language picker and viewport overlay grips had empty or "::" accessible names; the grips are now
  spoken by name (ADR-8587)
- Overlay placement and collapse have a keyboard route; the tab-bar add control has the accessible name Add panel and a
  keyboard menu.
- A scoped tool token allowed `editor.console_run` could run methods outside its scope through a console line; console
  calls now keep the caller's scope (ADR-7796). Esc with a drawer open no longer stops play or asks to close the editor;
  the log drawer no longer closes in the frame it opens.
- A keymap file that does not parse is no longer replaced by the next shortcut edit, and unreadable saved-search rows
  are written back instead of lost.
- The command palette no longer lists some window commands twice; equally scored commands keep the palette's order.
- The viewport toolbar and the material graph's file row wrap instead of widening past the panel.
- Windows paths no longer show the `\\?\` prefix in panels (ADR-8586)
- Failed theme batches no longer leave temporary renamed themes behind.
- Toast text "cancelling" is now "canceling".

### Rendering

#### Visibility, culling and streaming

- The sequencer `visible` track, `regia_net`'s hide-until-shown and scripts now hide meshes, lights and static decals
  in a game; `samples/third_person` hides the downed player's rig. (ADR-7200, ADR-7588, ADR-7670)
- Hidden static decals are no longer routed to the forward-decal pass (ADR-7310)
- A character posed off screen or a mesh behind a wall read `ViewVisibility` true; split screen and texture cameras
  switched occlusion off (ADR-7255, ADR-7286)
- Split screen: a mesh behind an occluder that only its own view sees no longer draws or reads `ViewVisibility` true in
  that view, and views far apart no longer discard each other's LOD history (ADR-7286, ADR-7329, ADR-8620)
- Indoor cells: an eye in a doorway sees both rooms, a portal chain at the 32-portal cap counts every cell beyond it,
  a doorway no longer reaches past a thin wall, the walk ignores the near plane for a door nearer than it, the per-view
  report no longer allocates each frame, and building parts inherit the root's cell. (ADR-7639, ADR-7646, ADR-7673)
- A draw the GPU cull missed is drawn per object with a once-per-object warning instead of vanishing (the old
  "object entry" crash on a hand-built pilot). (ADR-8028)
- Cluster residency: a mesh band only a cached shadow map needed could stay in RAM, so a streamed scene settled one band
  coarser; a streamed cluster band that only a cached shadow map's cut needed is uploaded, and residency keeps the band
  demand of the last shadow cull until the next cull replaces it (ADR-7672)

#### Lighting, shadows and upscalers

- Point-light shadows of a moving caster no longer stay where it had been on a warm shadow cache (light `k` redrew
  tile `k`). (ADR-7986)
- A ceiling emissive panel no longer lights the storey below through a 0.3 m slab (leak 1.8 % to 0.55 %). (ADR-7661)
- FSR 3.1 ghosting at 0.67 meets spec 27 (0.0293 to 0.0188 on lavapipe). (ADR-7678)
- TSR at 0.5 passes the orbit thin-geometry row on Metal. (ADR-7876)

#### Counted meshes, textures and LOD loading

- Counted meshes: a same-update `.fmesh`/`.fmlt` hot reload could lose clusters, a stale `.fmlt` mismatch persisted (a
  counted stand-in's `.fmlt` is now checked against the upload's counts whenever the mesh is uploaded again), a vertex
  buffer over the storage-binding limit drew one collapsed frame, an arena range freed the same frame could refuse
  admission for good, and LOD variants double-counted the vertex buffer in `mesh_bytes`. (ADR-7542)
- A morphed counted mesh drew undeformed, and counted meshes were never GI probe proxies. (ADR-7721)
- Counted meshes no longer need CPU vertices on the ECS `MeshRenderer` path, counted textures have a renderer upload,
  counted meshes reach lightmap receivers and graphs reading the second UV set, and evicting an adopted mesh frees its
  memory (ADR-7330).
- A counted texture sampled in one color space (`AssetTextureUploads::set_srgb` now picks the color space the mip chain
  is filtered in), a counted texture's hot reload showed only after its material was rebuilt (a material entry is
  rebuilt when a counted texture it binds is uploaded again), and a skinned `MeshRenderer` on a counted mesh was not
  drawn; releasing a counted `CpuMesh`'s CPU copy also drops its skin influences (`CpuSkin::{joints, weights, joints1,
  weights1}`) (ADR-8610)
- `load_cooked_models_counted_textured` drew a base map that was also a detail, thin-film, parallax or edge-wear map
  with a white stand-in in that slot; a counted mesh respawned on the same asset was released and adopted again
  (ADR-7428)
- A counted mesh drew forward on the visibility-buffer profile even with cooked meshlets (ADR-7462)
- The skin palette and the draw list agree on which meshes the frame draws when a counted mesh and a resident draw are
  both in a scene. (ADR-8385)
- A counted built-in kind switch on hot reload keeps the previous value, its GPU copy and kind when the new kind's
  prepare fails (ADR-7284)
- The all-LOD loader uploads maps at the resident cap (an 8K map no longer uploads at 8K on Desktop High) and shares the
  material maps fitted to that cap as views of a cooked chain's bytes, gives no shared images to a neutral-fallback
  material, and no longer fails a batch for one compact mesh. (ADR-8025)

#### Warm-up, frame pacing and presentation

- A late map spawned after the new-scene warm started, or arriving while a scene-less warm ran, is warmed instead of
  uploaded and compiled on the first play frame. (ADR-7883)
- With a load budget (`Defer`), a late burst of meshes or material graphs no longer lands on one frame, and `Defer` no
  longer compiles every graph set inline when graph workers are unavailable: `LateDraw::Defer` builds an unavailable
  graph set on the frame thread only as the frame's first pipeline work. (ADR-7884, ADR-8122)
- Headless hosts no longer receive `SceneReady` while meshes are pending, and a generated frame's present no longer
  retires a prepare when the real acquire fails. (ADR-8122)
- TSR builds its resolve bind groups for both ping-pong inputs and the stochastic SSR pass's input and output during
  warm-up, so it creates no `tsr resolve` bind group after warm-up on a camera cut followed by a still camera, or when
  stochastic SSR sleeps or wakes; a registered target no longer builds its sets on its first frame. (ADR-7885, ADR-8122)
- Terrain prewarm applies pending ground-look and cost-debug selections, weather prewarm includes the VT bake
  pipelines, and a failed VT allocation fails preparation. (ADR-7964)
- A menu, loading or pause cap is no longer lost when the player changes a setting (the settings apply writes only
  `FramePacing::target_fps`, so a state cap survives every apply), and a held dynamic-resolution controller no longer
  climbs to native in menus. (ADR-7889)
- A paused scene behind a menu no longer renders at full cost when the hold is on. (ADR-7890)
- On Wayland a vsync-off window's frame cost CPU plus GPU time on throttling compositors: a focused window with vsync
  off no longer waits for the compositor's frame callback, so CPU and GPU work overlap there as on X11 and Windows
  (ADR-8170)
- A frame generation present submitted while the present thread was ending could be lost, and a tick that failed after
  the pacer's decision kept it: `FrameGenPacing::fed_this_tick` means the present path accounted for the tick, and a
  frame generation tick that presented nothing leaves the pacer as it was (ADR-8930)
- The frame-generation present path cloned the frame pacer every tick (ADR-7543)

#### Quality, telemetry and performance

- Low profiles with TSR selected lost their ragdoll and vehicle-FX caps because `preset()` returned `None`
- A host that sets its own `RenderProfile::host_memory` on a Low profile no longer turns ambient life on.
- A fullscreen quality probe that drew smaller than the monitor stepped the preset down for good; it is now recorded
  with `size_mismatch: true`, changes nothing and runs again once the window fills the monitor. (ADR-7898)
- 14 ms frames spent in acquire against a 7 ms median are no longer hitches, and 4x4 placeholder textures no longer
  raise `uncompressed_textures` (`TextureMemoryReport::uncompressed` no longer counts textures of at most 4x4 texels).
  (ADR-7898)
- One slow render followed by update-only ticks no longer repeats its timing or labels those ticks CPU-bound; delayed
  hitch records attach GPU timing only from a matching render; a hidden throttle period no longer adds another full
  period; event-loop sleep leaves `outside_update`. (ADR-7965)
- Terrain forward time on a production census map, RTX 5090: TSR 1.90 to 1.80 ms, native 2.31 to 2.18 ms, same
  image. (ADR-7886)

#### Shaders, drivers and resources

- NaN and infinity guards hold on the NVIDIA 620 Linux Vulkan compiler, which folded the masked exponent test (clouds,
  froxel fog, DDGI history, TAAU, TSR, FSR3, SSSR, motion blur, DOF, lens effects, OIT); other drivers are unchanged.
  (ADR-3150, ADR-8366)
- `vkCreateShaderModule` with validation on no longer reports `VUID-StandaloneSpirv-None-10684` for the path tracer,
  the GPU lightmap baker, the baked GI screen and point lookups, the product viewer's family shaders and contact
  shadow, and the looks' shaders. (ADR-7879, ADR-7587)
- The path tracer (`pt.wgsl`) and the GPU lightmap bake (`lightmap_bake.wgsl`) compile again after the extra-light
  layout change. (ADR-7879)
- The lite forward shader composes again in every variant `check-shaders` builds (ADR-7310)
- The `mip_level` debug view read texture sizes as 1024 on the bindless path, and a Mobile tier on a texture-table
  device failed to compose its shader (ADR-7254)
- The FXC assertion rule (`cargo xtask check-shaders`) missed the kill shape when a store to another member of the same
  struct sat between the two tests; it now follows struct members and vector components apart, so it can flag a user
  shader it passed before (no engine shader is affected) (ADR-8930)
- Cluster-capacity growth and reservoir resizes released stale bind groups through resource ownership (ADR-8065)
- A frame that got the forward-decal binding back shaded a stale feed on top of the post pass (ADR-7431)
- Registered runtime materials reuse their emissive-map signature across steady frames. (ADR-7869)
- The glyph atlas keeps both font size buckets and per-frame page pins after the merge (ADR-7555)
- `load_cooked_models_all_lods` no longer applies `COLOR_0` to a mesh's vertex color: a vertex-colored mesh draws
  white, as through `load_cooked_models` and as documented, from compact and GPU-ready paks alike (ADR-8785)

### Lightmaps, GI and lighting

- A baked tunnel keeps its light gradient inside its interior volume on Desktop Low, High, Ultra and Deck (v0.5.0 known
  issue "flat floor"), and a passage past the fourth interior volume no longer glows with sky light (ADR-7975)
- Lightmap cell bakes keep every extra directional light, not only the sun (ADR-7310)
- With `band_bounds_final_exposure` on, a bright snow scene with +1 EV bias and compensation applies EV100 inside the
  band instead of a stop under it (ADR-7969)
- A `DirectionalLight` or `AmbientVolume` that is hidden, or under a hidden group, no longer lights the frame (both
  require `InheritedVisibility`), and hidden lights are not extracted; an entity that drops out of visibility
  propagation is visible again (ADR-7490, ADR-7549)
- Bakes no longer take a hidden sun: the bake panel uses the lowest-entity drawn `DirectionalLight`, `level.export_bake`
  and `regia bake-gi --from-scene` the first drawn one by guid (ADR-7549)
- The radiance-cache planner overflowed on a far eye or a jump across the grid; DDGI max-pyramid bind groups are keyed
  by the (heights key, mip) pair (ADR-7456)
- A `RuntimeRenderer` inserted after a level's lightmap was installed now warms the lightmap variants (ADR-8790)
- A sealed room no longer reads the sunlit ground outside its walls through the denoiser, and buried faces no longer
  pick up light from neighbors: the lightmap denoiser reads across a chart border only where the two texels see each
  other, so denoised bakes can change at chart borders behind walls and on buried faces; re-bake to drop the leak.
  (ADR-7590)
- `BakedGiVolume::from_sections` refuses an exposure `from_bytes` refuses, so a tool cannot write an unreadable
  `.fgiv`.

### World

#### Terrain

- With a matched grid origin, drawn and collided ground no longer sits 0.156 m off the simulation's samples (ADR-8179)
- The scatter height window (`HeightWindow`) copies the map's world depth and grid origin and maps world to grid with
  them, so scatter answers as the map does on rectangular or moved maps (ADR-8459)
- A rectangular heightfield no longer has to be padded to a square (ADR-8369)
- Editing only the datum, scale, cell size or registration in `heightfield.json` now recooks, and count sources no
  longer lose their datum and placement in the cook (ADR-8471)
- A nodata sentinel described by a raster descriptor never becomes an extreme height (ADR-8542)
- `regia terrain source-import` and the editor import refuse a source without a count mapping instead of writing a
  wrong one (ADR-8595)
- Terrain JSON parsing charges each sample before growing storage, and a canceled or failed import publishes nothing
  (ADR-8362)
- `regia cook` refuses a `heightfield.raster.json` beside `heightfield.tiles.json` with `terrain_tiles_ambiguous`;
  before, the descriptor was hashed but never applied. (ADR-8639)
- A saved terrain edit-layer sidecar loads against its cooked terrain's 16-bit heights while keeping the authored layer
  precision (ADR-8202).
- Scatter instances on a runtime crater sit on the edited ground, including tiles placed or streamed in later and edits
  written into the field's source map, and a stale shadow strip at a wide cascade's border after an edit is gone.
  (ADR-7585, ADR-7638, ADR-7646)
- Under a world anchor, a runtime height edit re-sits the rocks of every tile under it, and a new placement origin
  keeps the field's edited heights (ADR-8496)
- A vertical ray exactly on a terrain heightfield's cell line now hits the ground (`PhysicsQuery::raycast`); the terrain
  cook fixtures are fixed too (ADR-8715)

#### Tunnels and mesh layers

- A mesh-layers cook no longer succeeds when a named spec check fails (ADR-8359)
- The over-budget advice names the `Nested` level the cook measured to fit, and `ground_pitch_seam` passes a cooked
  `Nested` ground on the cook's own seam measure (ADR-8436)
- `terrain.bake_report` no longer reports every check failed at `input_missing` on a cook that ran them. (ADR-8627)
- Projects with tunnel maps can cook them with the shipped `regia cook` (ADR-8451)
- The flat collision-tube look of `Passages` layers: the cooked render mesh has 16 times the vertices on the same
  planes (ADR-8175)
- A filled mouth no longer falls through an f32 step off the ring, tunnel fixture openings hop within 5 cm (worst
  3.4 mm), the fixture maps fit the resident budget at full pitch, and no ring edge is the edge of two canonical
  triangles (ADR-8291, ADR-8396)
- A filled mouth's drawn cut no longer sits up to two coverage texels off its ring or slides while a node morphs
  (ADR-8470)
- A body crossing a tunnel mouth no longer hops 0.30 m over the sill (worst step 0.001 m per tick), and a walker no
  longer drops 30 m at the top of a skylight ramp where an open climb met the mouth seal (ADR-8167, ADR-8533)
- The rubble cone at a skylight is a sim floor (a walker moves at most 0.04 m vertically per tick, no 1.4-1.65 m drop),
  and a drawn solid in a skylight patch stops the capsule 0.08-0.21 m from its surface (ADR-8323)
- A body falling into a skylight shaft is no longer clamped under a lid (ADR-8210)
- One far coordinate or a short `covered`/wall list in a baked tunnel file aborted or panicked the mesher; it is refused
  at parse: `parse_tunnels` refuses non-finite or out-of-bounds numbers and wrong list lengths (`MAX_TUNNEL_*` bounds)
  (ADR-8166)
- A sweep with no solids, or a map loaded without its on-foot grid or mouth patches, passed instead of failing
  (ADR-8432)
- A one-frame boom snap at a junction: the interior camera's pull-in follows a 0.06 s time constant. (ADR-8336)
- A drive through a tunnel mouth changes exposure by at most 0.5 EV per sample (ADR-8384)
- The native query core no longer drops a floor edge shared only with a wall, reports column capacity overflow, and
  allocates no candidate list for segments. (ADR-8246)

#### Procedural generation

- A packaged game showed nothing for a sublevel's `Baked` generator, and anchored or prefab-added generators in placed
  levels found no bake (ADR-7262, ADR-7287)
- Saving a level dropped every anchored generator's `ProcGenerator` (`AnchorSettings` now reflects `Clone`); check
  levels saved from the editor while this bug was present (ADR-7287)
- A generator inside a prefab template of an inline-placed sublevel did not find its bake (`procgen_bake_missing`), and
  `regia procgen verify <host level>` called a placed member's stale bake clean (ADR-7331)
- `regia procgen bake`/`verify` skipped generators inside prefab templates. (ADR-7743)
- Procgen: switching a layer at runtime moves prefab instances, colliders and tile cells with the instance sets; nested
  `repeat` patterns, huge biome child counts (now checked against `points_per_item` before allocation) and
  `points.project` over far boxes no longer allocate unbounded; colliders built before the meshes loaded are rebuilt;
  road decals in called graphs are kept; a baked streaming generator picks up a bake that appears later;
  type-conflicting point attributes across cells fail the merge with `attr_type_conflict` instead of dropping points;
  two guid-less generators' roots never share a guid. (ADR-7556, ADR-7595, ADR-7621)
- Procgen: `detmath::pow(1, y)` is 1, `graph.call` refuses unlisted graphs, road pieces follow scaled splines,
  tag-split dependencies hash apart, prefab override array paths compile, and pitched biome boxes no longer exclude an
  infinite slab (ADR-7214)
- A grass class naming a density map failed every biome re-bake (ADR-7262)
- `ClumpSettings::default().scale_bias` is 2.0 again (was silently 2.27) (ADR-7320)
- The biome overlay footprints test measures each ring from its own center (ADR-7346)

#### Navigation

- Navigation: agent parameters are validated (`InvalidInput`); origin shifts drop a waiting tile patch; crowd link
  reservations follow their link across list changes, including duplicate copies; huge vertices no longer overflow
  the rasterizer; lane joins are near-linear; invalid off-mesh links are left out; infinite link durations and huge
  `max_steps_per_advance` are bounded; a portal to a missing polygon is a wall; a streamed goal beside an unloaded tile
  reports `nav_tile_unloaded`; a streamed replan drops the link queue place. (ADR-7558, ADR-7679)
- `NavGrid::sync_stamps` no longer allocates its cluster scratch every tick (ADR-8379)
- `NavGrid::sync_stamps_from` allocates nothing once warm again.

#### Streaming, saves and VFX

- A canceled or failed stream cook left the old world index over new cell blobs; closing one streamed level fired its
  queued teleport moves against another (ADR-8812, ADR-8813, ADR-8860)
- A level switch mid-load holds one map, not two (`switch_cancels_old_load_rss_bounded`). (ADR-8027)
- `stream.pin` with a wait over an area the grid cannot list, and `stream.teleport` outside the grid, are refused with
  `stream_wait_area_refused` instead of timing out. (ADR-7612)
- Loading a save keeps the non-`Saveable` children of `Saveable` entities (re-parented to the respawned entity), and a
  data-layer member respawned under a `Transform`-less group after a rebase lands in local space. (ADR-7601)
- VFX: `.vfx.ron` refuses non-finite numbers in every float field and event links whose worst-case child count
  overflows 24 bits, and `VfxGpu::push_spawn_jobs` bounds-checks `emitter_slot`. (ADR-7560)
- A respawned or teleported hull no longer flings its hover wash. (ADR-8026)

### Animation and characters

- A clip whose soles are not at the rig origin (and blends and retargeted clips) can plant through its contact curve
  (ADR-8308)
- Graph-output foot IK lowers each `TwoBoneIK` target by the bind tip height, so the foot no longer floats a bind
  height above the step (ADR-8388)
- Stopping no longer pops all six gait feet in one frame, and turning in place no longer re-snaps planted feet
  (ADR-8539)
- Planted feet no longer slide and two-handed holds no longer drift on retargets between rigs of different
  proportions, when the setup lists IK goals (ADR-8476)
- Animation graph inspector: "Add joint" on a body aim node appends the first rig joint that does not turn yet (it
  appended an empty name that failed every evaluation with `MissingJoint("")`), and is disabled for a rig with no
  joints. (ADR-8732)
- Foot placement on slopes and stairs reads the plant pin through the shared `PlantLock`. (ADR-8573)
- Pose search no longer sticks at a non-looping clip's end (ADR-7241)
- Socket-targeted IK on a rig away from the origin aimed 100 m off: `SocketTargets` holds object-space positions, and a
  socket entity parented to the rig gets its rig-relative transform
- Malformed skeletons no longer panic the chain IK, body aim, hand placement, eye gaze, ragdoll blend, skeleton profile
  and motion-matching warps
- A negative `UpdateMode::Lod { far_rate }` froze the animator; it counts as 0
- A synced blend space without a shared clock fires notifies from the dominant sample only
- The graph `Slot` node check runs again when the animator's graph or the montage slot names change
- An in-place montage-graph edit warns `animgraph_unknown_slot` again, and `rest_pose_normalization` no longer indexes
  out of bounds for joints outside the bind matrices. (ADR-7596)

### Gameplay systems

#### Match rules and sessions

- A peer can no longer ready, pause, resume, approve a join or override AFK for a seat it does not hold; a seated player
  can leave between matches; a second join request no longer drops the first (join asks queue in order instead of
  replacing each other)
- Score-sheet rows reused a previous seat occupant's id, a move could overwrite a row of nobody seated, damage after a
  team change opened a second row, a team change dropped the pause budget and join ask, and a hill point credited a
  unit twice. (ADR-8657)
- A dropped flag nobody can reach no longer stalls capture the flag when the host sets a return timer or drop check,
  and pickups can be limited to a height band on stacked floors (ADR-8506)
- A guest cannot record a series round or close a vote (`match_not_permitted`, checked before `vote_closed`).
- Undrained board and flow event vectors no longer grow without bound
- A prefab character a join spawned left its identity wrapper and an empty prefab instance behind when despawned
- A server bot whose player the `PlayerManager` forgot still runs `on_player_left` and no longer leaks its
  `PlayerController`.

#### AI, behavior trees and abilities

- After a `self` abort the gated branch restarted while its key was still false; a gated decorator whose key is false on
  the first tick no longer runs its branch; a tree deeper than 256 levels is refused at compile instead of overflowing
  the stack (ADR-7559)
- Utility hot reload reads only the definition whose file changed (ADR-7559)
- NaN lattice spacing and ring radius no longer produce degenerate points, and the smart-object slot index rebuilds when
  an ancestor loses `GlobalTransform`. (ADR-7596)
- Agents sharing one `.statetree.ron` with a `bt:` task all reload (ADR-8593)
- Free-for-all, arena and mode bots picked pickups, repair and ammo goals and hostile focus by slice order, so peers
  could disagree; a broadcast Follow with no entity 0 formed a wedge on the wingman's own position. (ADR-8655)
- A debug and a release peer no longer disagree on out-of-domain avoidance input, and the solver no longer allocates
  per call (ADR-8377). With `OrcaSolver::Lp` a later constraint cannot undo an earlier one and walls are real
  constraints (ADR-8555).
- `WhileTagged` start refusals write `AbilityLifecycle::Refused`, and chance rows for despawned guids are pruned
  (ADR-7573)
- The ability trace pin follows `TargetGone` for a despawned target's effects (ADR-7310)
- `detsim.aim_solve` and `detsim.projectile_simulate` read wrong-typed `arc`, `tolerance_m` and `world.low_g` as
  defaults. (ADR-2622)

#### Physics and vehicles

- The hover step no longer hangs on an infinite `dt` or absurd roll spring, and no longer latches a NaN for the rest of
  a match: hover `step`, `step_world` and `step_tuned` guard non-finite `dt`, inputs, floor, gravity and air, and finite
  valid calls step as before (ADR-8309)
- Vehicle wheel settings above 1e9 are refused (`vehicle_invalid_wheel`), the vehicle private world rebuilds when a
  static collider's shape changes, the impact cooldown list is capped, and `vehicle_track::step_for` refuses more than
  10,000 steps (ADR-7431)
- `closest_point` on a triangle-mesh collider reports the normal of the triangle under the point (ADR-7267)
- Reading a large `.fracture.ron` took minutes (typed parse first) (ADR-7703)
- The ground-shock airblast speed is one `regia_core` constant for server and client builds (ADR-7555)
- The bird trigger scan takes at most its documented 1,000,000 samples.

#### Determinism and sim hosts

- A failing `Serialize` no longer folds a partial desync word silently, and a non-ECS sim behind the sim host no longer
  answers `no_desync_hash` (ADR-8547)
- A manually paced `ThreadedSimHost` no longer loses a granted tick when a hold lands between grant and step (a waiter
  could hang) (ADR-8456, ADR-8547)
- A threaded sim no longer steps under a floating-point environment inherited from the thread that started it, and a
  suspended app no longer steps its threaded sim (ADR-8526)
- A clean determinism scenario whose blessed hash disagrees with its reference now fails, and blessing a differing
  clean non-reference target is refused (ADR-8231)
- `report.same` was true for runs differing only in a zero sign, for partial rows and for windows with no shared tick;
  `sim.desync_rows` `report.same` is now true only when every view agrees. A loaded `SystemRing` attached flags to the
  wrong ticks; a sim host restore restarts the desync ring at the restored tick (ADR-8621)
- Two processes wrote different snapshot bytes for the same `HashMap`, scenario fingerprints missed inputs, radius and
  `dt`, and a `DetRng` saved without its key resumed on a different stream: hash maps and sets in `DetWriter::put` are
  written in sorted entry order, so equal contents give equal snapshot bytes, and engine scenario fingerprints cover
  every stored field (ADR-8634)
- A tiny ground-vehicle length overflowed to NaN with platform-dependent bits and `null` agent answers: lengths under 1
  cm are invalid, a step that would overflow rests the vehicle on the same tick, and `TrackState::forward` matches the
  integrated heading bit for bit (ADR-8638)
- Two hulls differing only in `limits` or `flight` passed the lobby comparison and desynced (ADR-8632)
- `cargo xtask det-golden stage` bundles `regia_detsim` with its `serde`, `tracing` and `serde_json` dependencies again
  (ADR-8525, ADR-8483)
- Lockstep games have an allowed path to the transcendentals `det-scan` bans (ADR-8483)
- A lockstep crate could call float `x.max(y)`, `Mat3::from_rotation_y` or `.map(f32::sin)` and pass both det-scan and
  clippy (ADR-8085)
- The shared deterministic spatial grid coordinate conversion no longer overflows for a negative infinite scaled bound
- `DetIdAllocator` with a cap of `u16::MAX` returns `None` instead of looping forever once every id is issued
  (ADR-8048)
- The ported influence test `flank_bearing_returns_a_finite_bearing` runs (a misplaced `#[test]`) (ADR-8395)

#### Scripting, mods, input and builds

- A game script runs the edited `update` after a hot reload, not the old library's code (ADR-7206)
- A kept-entry `LuaMods::load` whose component declaration clashes rolls back like the other load paths, and
  `lua.globals` no longer lists a key twice.
- A Workshop item claiming another mod's id no longer receives that mod's saved state or entities, held entries over
  the cap fail the save instead of being silently lost, and same-named local mod folders get distinct mount ids.
  (ADR-7881, ADR-7992)
- Save loads refuse a CRC-valid payload without a decodable version varint by name, in `parse_save_file`,
  `SaveGame::load` and `upgrade_save_bytes`.
- Display settings `display.resolution` and `display.vsync` apply, and split-screen rects reach tagged cameras
  (v0.5.0 known limit). (ADR-7880)
- Two `Hold` bindings on one action each reach their threshold independently.
- `validate_curve_refs` recurses into lists, arrays, tuples, tuple structs, maps and enum variants
- `regia_framework` without its `client` feature (the server build) compiles without warnings.
- `cargo check -p regia --no-default-features --features server` compiles again (`regia_core::SPEED_OF_SOUND_M_S`)
  (ADR-7490)

### Networking and Steam

#### Transport and congestion control

- Rate control: congested links stay under the 256 kbit/s bottleneck, random loss no longer collapses the snapshot
  budget, listen servers hold past 32 clients, bulk throughput is no longer collapsed by pacing, and lossy jittery
  links leave the 4 KiB/s minimum (ADR-8301, ADR-8400, ADR-8490)
- `input_mtu::large_samples_survive_ten_percent_loss` passes on x86-64 and aarch64: a client sending large inputs at
  10 % loss no longer has its start target clamped by a half-empty first round. (ADR-8592)
- Honest peers and links no longer fail as `reassembly_stalled` under loss, reordering or outages (ADR-8302, ADR-8521)
- A bulk message sent at connect failed the link with `reassembly_stalled` at 2 s on bloated or deep-queue links, and
  `lossy_jittery_unlimited_links_keep_half_the_uncontrolled_rate` kept 35 % (now 92-95 %). (ADR-8624, ADR-8658)
- A lost fragment of a large transfer no longer freezes every later reliable message (ADR-8302); a socket-refused
  datagram no longer spends a packet number (ADR-8407); a path that drops large datagrams no longer loses every large
  packet with probing on (ADR-8300)
- A 64-packet burst needs one ack (was four), an 8 s outage sends a waiting fragment once (was 5), and 15 %
  reordering counts no loss (ADR-8184)
- Legal bursts no longer become false loss; lost or retransmitted packets no longer pollute RTT samples; long paths
  establish their first RTT; fragments are retransmitted at most once per smoothed RTT; state frames fit the sealed
  endpoint budget. (ADR-7949, ADR-7988)
- A joined peer could hold about 8x the reliable message cap on the server, and a message missing its last fragment
  wedged the reliable stream; the shared port and the Steam backend copied every datagram whatever its size, and
  `SteamSocket::send_to` returned `Ok` for refused sends (ADR-7981)
- Handshake edges: a NAT port change after Challenge no longer invalidates the cookie, raced and forged challenges
  recover, a datagram size refusal keeps the packet sequence, and oversized QUIC receives are skipped. (ADR-7946)
- Transport fixes: joins at 2.4 s RTT, NAT port changes after the key reply, replayed plain join answers, port-bound
  relay cookies, a stuck `NetInterrupted`, half-open links held 30 s, invalid timeouts accepted, and a 200 ms receiver
  hitch entering recovery (ADR-7995)

#### Security and abuse handling

- One flooding peer can no longer lag the others: a peer sending 100,000 valid sealed datagrams a second or about
  190,000 messages a second is removed, and alternating bad and good packets reach the malformed limit. (ADR-8181)
- A spoofer with a sealed peer's token can no longer get it removed with `flood`, and plain-link packets from another
  address no longer score the peer. (ADR-8606)
- A forged handshake flood kept real clients out (`no_wedged_client` and `reconnect_within_grace` on forged-handshake/8
  and /32): pre-proof answers are shared per source prefix (IPv4 /24, IPv6 /56) and
  `ServerConfig::pre_proof_answers_per_s` defaults to 4,096, so under 100,000 forged requests a second from 1,000 /24s
  every real client joins and rejoins in 112-352 ms. (ADR-8718)
- An authenticated record with an unknown kind byte, or a client's authenticated `Accept`/`Deny`, scored 0; a token
  holder could hide a peer's datagrams by filling the failed-open cap (every sealed datagram at a peer's address and
  token is now opened on its own merits); on a plain link an unvalidated address could fail the peer's link with an
  over-long fragment or stream cap. `NetNode::reject` scores `RejectKind::malformed_weight` (16 for `voice_malformed`)
  on a score that leaks 4 points a second, so a sustained malformed voice stream removes its sender in about 1 s.
  (ADR-8666)
- Spectator input, malformed voice and voice control, client reliable decode errors and server-only kinds are no longer
  swallowed: spectator input and malformed voice are refused and scored, a server-only message type disconnects the
  client, and a client reliable decode error ends the link with `protocol_error`; voice floods are rate-limited in the
  log and the rejected list is bounded (ADR-7984, ADR-8438)
- An on-path observer racing a copied record no longer takes over a player's downstream; forged records no longer pin a
  peer whose NAT moved; clients follow a server whose address changes (ADR-8299)
- A forged player proof (zero static key, zero DH) verified as `PlayerKey([0; 32])` and opened a hub session, and a join
  plan with no server key pinned the relay ticket's key: `verify_player_proof` refuses a static or ephemeral key with an
  all-zero X25519 output, `HubServer::connect_player` refuses `PlayerKey([0; 32])` (`hub_identity_mismatch`), and
  `HubReach` fails `hub_key_mismatch` when the relay ticket names a server key and the join plan names none. (ADR-8665)
- A `NetLink` client with other replicated components or content no longer joins when `content_hash` is 0 (ADR-7985)

#### Input, prediction and lag compensation

- One state packet delayed 300 ms no longer moves the interpolation estimate; the input history keeps its redundancy
  across a skipped tick; residual input loss is at most 0.1 % on a 5 % lossy link whatever the setting. (ADR-8312)
- A one-step press made in a frame where dilation ran no tick is no longer lost (or fired twice in a two-tick frame);
  the own pawn no longer hitches at each dilation; a Wi-Fi burst of 5 or more input packets is kept; a late command is
  merged instead of dropped. (ADR-8217)
- Inputs under downstream bufferbloat are no longer stamped for ticks the server already simulated, and the tick
  estimate no longer steps backward on Wi-Fi and 4G. (ADR-8077)
- Shots from players at ordinary to long round trips are judged where they aimed, and a client can no longer claim the
  oldest view tick in the window. (ADR-8315)
- Prediction no longer switches off at about 400 ms RTT (650 ms: 3,501 resets in 2 minutes; now at most one in 5
  minutes), and high-RTT sessions no longer start in a reset loop (ADR-8219)
- A large input no longer wedges the client: a refused send leaves the history unchanged. (ADR-7896)
- Input decode failures are visible to the host and add up: over-cap input disconnects only its sender with
  `ProtocolError`, a packet's malformed credit applies only when every message decoded, and trailing bytes after an
  input bundle are an error. (ADR-7892)

#### Lockstep

- The 40-seat lockstep action mix passes: the essential class no longer starves the reliable stream after a hard cut,
  a capped joiner sends no first burst over its share, and lockstep frames no longer overtake the stream that fills a
  seat's hole. Congested `rate_vs_bottleneck` fails on 0 of 400 seeds. (ADR-8534)
- Lockstep seats behind a NAT that rebinds no longer lose about a second of inputs per rebinding. (ADR-8422)
- Lockstep host upload at 40 seats over 5 minutes: 3.6 Mbit/s in the 95th-percentile second (was 5.07), 0 of 297
  seconds over the 4.0 Mbit/s bar. (ADR-8423)
- One hostile seat cannot end a lockstep match with forged hashes: it is resynced and then removed while the others
  keep stepping. (ADR-8211)
- Lockstep core and session repairs, each with a failing-first test: resynced seats with `hash_every` of 1 or 2, tick 0
  input with zero delay, transfer sources, stalled hole reports, handover of a re-added seat, hole reports after a
  catch-up, more than 64 late inputs between frames, and stale resume tokens. (ADR-8146, ADR-8209, ADR-8212)
- One seat reporting `down_p99_run = 32` or a lead of 8 raised every seat's input delay to `max_delay_ticks`: under
  `DelayPolicy::Shared` a seat's delay need uses only what the host measures (input lateness, inputs loss runs).
  (ADR-8656)

#### Replication and travel

- A server-authoritative or listen-server game no longer has to send hidden enemies to every client (ADR-8524)
- Budget-deferred entities without `NetVelocity` no longer stop and go between updates. (ADR-8418)
- A moving entity no longer costs 23-35 bytes per update (ADR-8467)
- An entity that grew past 16 KiB froze on a client that lost its baseline; above 500 ms RTT every state record counted
  lost; the 256-frame log overflowed at 128 Hz (ADR-8419)
- A late or duplicated snapshot no longer moves `SnapshotDecoder::current()` back, and a budget-limited full packet no
  longer makes entities disappear (ADR-7983)
- The standalone snapshot codec decodes in serial order, merges partial full packets and keeps copy-on-write
  baselines. (ADR-7983)
- A level-placed replicated entity that survives a travel but is despawned before the next gather is named to joining
  peers again, and a join-critical spawn held by `min_interval_ticks` keeps the join window open until it goes
- The old level's copies stayed on a client after `TravelBegin`, or reappeared from in-flight spawn records; mods
  presented empty version text (ADR-7259, ADR-7295)
- `ClientStateChannel::is_tombstoned`: a point tombstone that does not cover a record's tick no longer hides the travel
  floor (ADR-7321)
- The host-set `ClientStateChannel::entity_cap` survives connects and replay resets, and a dead replay mark no longer
  restores a stale mode over a real session (ADR-8850)
- `client_apply_state_frame` refused no tags and applied any frame as a state frame (ADR-8761)
- `ServerReplication::despawn_checks()` counts entities after the `NetId` dedup; engine prefab spawn guids are
  per-world, so replays are bit-deterministic
- Replication warn-once sets (`net.parent_not_replicated`, `net.state_too_large`) are pruned on despawn
- Net vehicles: an ordinary seat exit no longer runs `release_vehicle`, and `VehicleDrivers` is initialized by
  `install_net_vehicle`. The Steam overlay hold test runs the system's own cursor decision, and the `net_game` sample's
  `ShiftHeld` no longer sticks when the overlay opens.

#### Sessions and reconnects

- A phone that loses signal returns to a replication game as the same `PeerId` with its entities, by itself within the
  grace. (ADR-8298, ADR-8352)
- A client whose resume accept was lost retries with its old ticket and resumes the same peer, found by its new
  address. (ADR-8535)
- A reconnect before the server's timeout no longer joins as a second player or gets `server_full`, and a suspended
  Steam lobby client resumes over Steam sockets (ADR-8180)
- Two resumes before a confirmation dropped the first ticket (a resumed peer now keeps up to 8 older resume tickets and
  reconnect tokens until the client confirms); a session peer losing its link while `Loading` or `Joining` after a
  resume lost its slot; a takeover left the old token mapped; a replayed join answer could make a ghost second peer.
  (ADR-8623)
- `NetSession` timeouts reach both datagram endpoints, and authentic keepalives refresh session liveness (ADR-7950)

#### Servers, lobbies, relays and Steam

- A dedicated host running a `GameRules` game ends the match and rotates maps (ADR-8345)
- A `HostGame` that applies `server.toml` `[net]` rates itself without reporting them in `net_rates_in_use` logs one
  warning per key at start and reload (ADR-7213)
- A host behind an address-dependent NAT is reached directly when the client's NAT can be crossed, and a host behind a
  cooperating router is announced with its mapped address. `PcpClient` keeps one nonce per mapping. (ADR-8340,
  ADR-8370)
- A forger who learned a relayed flow can no longer spend its budget (ADR-7982); a host behind a NAT can be joined by
  code, and `ConnectTarget::JoinCode` no longer errors (ADR-8183)
- A late joiner was launched at a departed host's address, two members writing member data could drop the whole lobby,
  one session could force a full directory rebuild per `List`, and a freed handle was a friends-only credential.
  (ADR-8633)
- A lobby created before the manifest follow converges to the session manifest's hashes on the next pumps
- `samples/net_game` names the game when no lobby identity is set, so lobby create and list work. (ADR-7881)
- Builds that never set a lobby identity no longer meet each other's lobbies on a shared app id. (ADR-7881)
- `Lobbies::launch` rolls back a half-published launch; `NetSession::resume` refuses a custom-socket session with
  `transport_error`; a failed in-game travel keeps the host's reported level; the Steam lobby attach follows a
  restarted Steam client; a refused engine-key write fails the lobby create; the quiet-pump cost is asserted.
- Join ticket hook: it runs after the live-peer, per-address and capacity checks, leaving or a kick calls
  `end_ticket`, and a late Steam refusal kicks the peer. (ADR-7989)
- Steam: the Windows build of `regia_net`, ECS-loop kicks, refusals after leave, restarts on a new port, LAN floods and
  the `steamworks` feature build (ADR-8502)
- Steam lobby counts wider than `u32` saturate and lobby data keeps at most `MAX_KEYS * 2` entries (ADR-8790)
- `MockWorkshop` updates on Windows while a file of the installed item is open (ADR-8504)

### Platforms

- Steam cloud saves on Windows skip a taken temp name that is a directory (it failed with `PermissionDenied`), and two
  test-only helpers no longer warn on Windows targets
- Metered networks are detected on Android devices (ADR-7236)
- Windows: import settings were refused with "Access is denied" for up to 1.3 s after a cook; a new `.meta` is published
  with a no-replace move (ADR-8504)
- Intel Arc: `regia_render` lib, `regia_vfx` `ambient_frame` and `regia_ui` `backdrop_targets` tests died with SIGSEGV
  in the Vulkan loader when another instance was created mid-run (ADR-7557)
- Steam Deck display settings: a saved 1280x800 or 1024x600 applied before the OS reported the window size was clamped
  and never retried
- XeSS: the zeroed-heap smoke assertion passes on NVIDIA GPUs without Resizable BAR (243/243 release runs on the RTX
  3080 Ti), and XeSS packaging is staged on request (ADR-7820)
- `regia_platform` power: a sysfs attribute that became a FIFO no longer blocks the caller (ADR-8790)
- An exact full `REGIA_GPU_UUID` selects its card even when a neighbor's information file is malformed, unreadable or
  uninitialized. (ADR-7991)
- macOS: a mesh's cooked cluster DAG no longer depends on the cook host's architecture. (ADR-7878)
- aarch64 test builds no longer warn about the unused `V4_HASH` in `seq_voice_doppler_stretch`.

### Tools, pipeline and agent protocol

#### Content lint and package hygiene

- Geometry and material findings are enforced by the CLI and the cook/package gate through one adapter, and a
  canceled scan returns one JSON object with `content_lint_cancelled` (ADR-8390)
- Wrong target compression, a missing mip, an impossible header and a color/data mismatch are distinct texture findings;
  the glTF import-rule field table lists `clips` for glTF, not FBX (ADR-8536)
- Material checks validate texture indices, nested extension declarations and extension numeric domains (ADR-8392)
- `gltf_lint::evaluate_captured` refuses robust mode instead of replaying legacy checks. Import-rule validation knows
  the glTF `clips`, `meshes` and `materials` settings. (ADR-8376)
- Projected material reports fill `input_limits` and `input_usage`
- Staging hygiene scans are no longer the only evidence: content added after staging and payloads in ZIP, tar and gzip
  containers are inspected (ADR-8348)
- Sensitive filenames no longer bypass redaction through findings, limits, unreadable-file errors or pak diagnostics,
  and hygiene findings and incomplete-scan errors show scrubbed display locations in CLI and agent JSON (ADR-8229); a
  literal across a window edge is still reported (ADR-8244)
- Adjacent binary literals and compressed pak entries can no longer hide forbidden strings from the package audit

#### Cook, paks and packaging

- Unknown required ids and required targets excluded by a profile no longer disappear silently; missing cooked files
  are told apart from unknown registry ids; cook and package errors keep structured content diagnostics
- A torn or truncated chunk pak no longer mounts and lets the base pak serve older bytes (ADR-8810, ADR-8860)
- `regia cook --quality fast` followed by `regia package` no longer ships uncompressed paks; a legacy `Ferrite.toml`
  version bump repacks; a cache without `asset_manifest.bin` fails the pack. (ADR-7612)
- `regia cook`, `package` and `content` read chunk rules and the startup pin from a legacy `Ferrite.toml` too.
- `regia` cook and package validation skipped a non-empty legacy `asset_manifest.bin` with no registry file; a
  malformed registry still fails closed.
- `regia content pack-chunks` prints optional-reference warnings and keeps error reason codes (ADR-7310)
- The content-chunk check order, the glTF sidecar texture scope and the cooked-pack tests. (ADR-8714)
- `regia cook`'s pak step keeps the lz4 `min_ratio` and the structured error chain.
- `regia engine package` refuses tracked source symlinks (`release_file_link`) and non-portable names; relative Steam
  `--dist` and package `--out` produce absolute VDF paths. (ADR-7871)
- A cold driver pipeline cache compiled in play (225 pipelines, 83 s of thread time in one tester run) (ADR-8453)
- `AssetEvent` is sent (ADR-7202)

#### Import and textures

- glTF clip ranges were never applied because the cook always passed empty ranges; the `.meta` `clips` key now reaches
  the cook (ADR-7904).
- Repeated external glTF images produced a private KTX2 copy per glTF, and a glTF-referenced 16-bit PNG silently added a
  standalone heightfield: glTF cooks share external image ids and encodes by source and settings, so repeated images are
  encoded once, and a glTF-referenced 16-bit grayscale PNG cooks only as a texture unless `heightfield: Some(true)`
  (ADR-8055, ADR-8143)
- Project cooks and live reimports write `.meta` files for external glTF images before computing cache keys.
  (ADR-8054)
- The shared-texture scope read image `.meta` files outside the project; sample data tables ship their `.meta`
  sidecars (ADR-8055)
- Texture tiers no longer reduce UI, sky, cube, array, volume, terrain VT, HLOD or voxel textures, and a texture with
  no mip under the cap keeps its bytes with a `texture_tier_missing_mip` warning (ADR-8144)
- Oversized glTF expansion and aggregate external reads are refused before payload allocation (ADR-8226)
- `texture_work` saturates on adversarial blur parameters (now `gen_too_large`), and `curvature_masks` refuses an index
  count that is not a multiple of 3 (`gen_invalid_input`)

#### Formats and upgrades

- The upgrade plan and the cook no longer see an interrupted `regia upgrade-project --write`'s staged level copy as a
  second level with the same GUIDs (ADR-8861)
- `regia upgrade-project` finds plain and mixed-case `terrain.layers.ron` files with the cook's rule (ADR-7978)
- `format_corpus_upgrade` hands the `vfx`, `audiopatch` and `pgout` fixtures to `regia upgrade-project` under their real
  suffixes, and the `logic` v1 format has its corpus fixture (ADR-7346)
- Replays stamped v4 load directly, skipping map-extent and training records (ADR-8191)

#### Agent protocol and CLI

- `agent.cook`, `agent.package`, `agent.build_game` and `agent.run_tests` dry runs answer the real project root as
  `cwd` (was `"."`)
- More wrong-typed fields are refused: `render.set_viscells` `sun_direction`, `terrain.apply_operator` noise `kind` and
  `sharpness`, `geometry.body_probe` `feet`, and `ui.screens` `back` for every action.
- The runtime stub of `editor.viewport_overlays` returns `editor_not_running` with a hint.
- `errors.explain` knows `ui_live_field`, `ui_live_value` and `presentation_physics_unavailable`; `agent.guide`'s
  terrain-editing topic names the edit-layer methods; the `assets.terrain_tiles` schema lists `final_heights`,
  `holes` and `material`.
- The `statetree.control` and `logic.control` `op` descriptions list the ops each handler accepts
- `audio.voice_place` no longer declares a `running` field and `audio.voice` declares `loopback.placement`
- `RESOLVE_REASONS` no longer lists the build caveat `incomplete` (now `BUILD_CAVEAT_INCOMPLETE`)
- `cargo xtask agent-snapshot` renders a repeated error code, limit or param once, so its snapshot parses back
  (ADR-7454)
- `params_hash` streams into FNV-1a with no allocation per request (ADR-7454)
- `cargo xtask det-golden` wrote the bundled crate's `[features]` table twice, so the mac and windows legs could not
  build; it writes it once, and standalone determinism bundles keep crate features. (ADR-8242, ADR-8447)
- `regia logs triage`: slow matches are reported as `frame_rate`, oversized folders return `logs_triage_limit`, and
  timestamps through 1e20 ms saturate instead of allocating. (ADR-8442)
- `regia test` returns "no tests ran" only for the structured `no_tests` reason
- `regia_configurator` refuses a `studio_id` over 64 characters or a tenant slug over 40 as `configurator_bad_options`
  (ADR-8790)
- `assets.texture_coverage` documents its 1 MiB `.meta` read cap.
- Asset, pipeline and CLI audit fixes: MaterialX node overflow and `colorspace` color values, `import-rules check`
  errors and converted glTF keys, converted FBX/OBJ settings warnings, `upgrade-project` backup names, `package` and
  `ship-check` FileVersion without `[project] version`, `ship-check` graph and permutation warnings, `package
  --lightmap-tier` on an unreadable `Regia.toml`, `assets preview` path normalization, temp-file cleanup in texture
  sets, lightmap writes and `loc`, LDtk file limits, Aseprite warning caps, dialogue findings on `#` names,
  `texture-sets` outside the project (`texture_sets_no_folder`), TTML DOCTYPE detection and release checksum lists.
  (ADR-5786, ADR-7666)
- `regia package --profile shipping` of the arena sample no longer refuses with `package_hygiene_limit`
  (`unbounded_regex` on the shipping binary) (ADR-8786)
- The `package-hygiene` agent guide topic no longer quotes a token-shaped test secret, which made every shipping binary
  that links the agent fail its own audit with `secret_api_key`; a `regia_cli` test audits every guide topic with the
  default policy (ADR-8786)
- DCC feed: a `goodbye` or socket error that arrived between a tick's ingest and its flush cut the session without
  detaching its items, which stayed `stalled`; the apply step now sees every close first (ADR-8795)
- The dispatch fuzz test no longer writes photo-mode stills into the source tree through `photo.capture`'s
  unconfined `directory`, which broke the offline `notices --check` on a tree without `.git` (ADR-8795)

### Audio, media, UI and accessibility

#### Audio

- A shipping build no longer aborts when the audio stream restart cannot reopen the output device: `kira` 0.12.5 is
  vendored with a restart that retries every 500 ms (ADR-8830)
- Audio comes back after a fast resume whose output re-open failed; a crafted WAV sample rate can no longer abort a
  cook or `audio.loudness`; the microphone re-opens after a headset switch. (ADR-7602)
- An uncapped game no longer treats a quarter-second audio callback stall as a dead output: the audio stall watchdog
  recovers only after `stall_frames` frames and `min_stall` of engine time (ADR-7543)
- Stopped persistent audio stays stopped across recovery, and rapid headless output cycles reclaim mixer tracks
  (ADR-8052)
- A patch or persistent loop spawned during a render-out take on a suspended output plays in that take, and suspending
  the device mid-take no longer stops the take's patch layers (ADR-8052)
- `spawn_patch` on an unknown bus while the mixer is down returns `AudioError::UnknownBus` instead of an id for a patch
  that never starts
- Voice-stream air absorption no longer low-passes to near silence (cutoff compared with `1/dt`); the loopback resets
  to center when the last `VoiceStreamAnchor` goes; the placement is a seqlock instead of a `Mutex` on the audio
  thread, with acquire and release fences for aarch64 (ADR-7762)
- HRIR tables above 48 kHz keep their DC gain and every resampled tap (ADR-7555)
- 88.2 to 192 kHz devices narrowed the binaural interaural delay by the rate ratio (ADR-7461)
- A routed `SoundCue` with a position plays spatially at it (ADR-7490)
- A caption held for reading speed ends at the same speaker's next caption, voice concealment fades out instead of
  clicking, and a muted account's new voice slot gets its `mute_hint` (ADR-7237)

#### UI and text

- A click on a UI widget no longer fires a gameplay action bound to the same mouse button (ADR-7976)
- IME composition text is visible before commit (ADR-7208); UI events bubble and pointer use is gated (ADR-7070)
- After an IME commit the label's style is restored from the saved style, and a wrapped composition's candidate rect
  stays on the caret's line (ADR-7208)
- The caption `Outline` background drew no outline, and a single-line right-to-left text opening with a number dropped
  the number: a right-to-left paragraph's direction is its base level, and `regia-dark` sets caption outline tokens
  (ADR-9340)
- Glyph atlas pages were wiped under quads drawn earlier in the same frame, in the UI and in `regia_quick` text: the UI
  extract and `QuickRenderer::build` bracket their frame with `GlyphAtlases::begin_frame`/`end_frame`, so a page the
  frame draws from is not recycled until the frame ends; when every page is held, the glyph is skipped that frame and
  counted (ADR-8832, ADR-8930)
- A saved integer or screen UI scale rule now reaches the runtime, not only the designer preview (ADR-8252).
- A window resize that picks another layout variant re-lays out only the stale window roots; a world-space or
  camera-target root from the same `.ui.ron` keeps its variant (ADR-7858).
- An instance's slot content could be set only unchecked through `editor.ui_designer_edit`. (ADR-7677)
- `LayerFile::to_ron` and `InputGlyphTable::to_ron` write `\n` line endings on Windows

#### Video and sequencer

- The sequence preview's sounds keep playing after Pause, Stop or the end, spatial sounds are heard from the editor
  camera without an `Audio` listener, voices no longer restart each tick in reverse or on slow frames, and a voice
  that ended is `past_end`. (ADR-7662, ADR-7847)
- PIE starts from the authored level while a sequence preview runs, and edits, undo and prefab snapshots no longer
  record the preview player; autosave, saves, PIE starts and asset-watch passes no longer re-open a previewed video.
  (ADR-7662, ADR-7847)
- The shot-list autosave writes the current format version, and the close prompt no longer fails on a dirty shot list
  with no file. (ADR-7662)
- Undoing a ripple after another shot list was opened no longer puts the old list into the new document, a plain cut
  edit is an "Edit sequence cuts" entry again, and the sub-sequence Bindings row commits on Enter or focus loss.
  (ADR-8730, ADR-7847)
- `PlayerCore::reposition` during a pending seek lands on its own time, and the sequencer's video driver no longer
  reports `seek_bound` while a seek is in flight
- A tiny declared video duration no longer floods the main thread with `Looped` events; coming back into view seeks
  accurately; low frame rate clips keep their audio; a PCM track without an Audio element plays. (ADR-7563)
- A few-KB file can no longer make each video player allocate about 98 MB of PCM ring. (ADR-7635)

#### Accessibility

- The editor window had no OS accessibility tree, Inspector text fields had no spoken name, and the Preferences search
  field had no accessible name. (ADR-7746, ADR-7795, ADR-7838)
- `render.color_vision`/`editor.color_vision` did not reach the editor viewport. (ADR-7838)
- Keyboard zoom was lost at the next launch. (ADR-7733)
- A world press that moves over UI kept firing only until it reached a widget, and the first world press after the
  pointer left a widget was hidden for a frame (a right click, a chord partner): the hover gate (ADR-7070) hid presses
  that the press-to-release claims (ADR-7976) give to the game. Both work again. (ADR-8786)

### Core, licence and security

- An unclean recovery report no longer copies raw tail lines holding a home path, a token or an email address (report
  fields and tail lines pass through `privacy::scrub_report` before publication); a substituted `session.log` symlink or
  directory exports nothing; an interrupted publication leaves no report (ADR-8494)
- With the async writer on, a stalled log sink no longer holds the producer inside `line` or `hash_trace`
- Peer log checkpoint names cannot escape the peer directory, recreating a session cannot truncate evidence, and a
  failed write cannot become a clean exit.
- The clean exit reports the GPU device as destroyed when its drop ended just past the wait.
- The threat-model row, the `ron_pretty` writers and the `regia_dylib` `logic` feature. (ADR-8716)
- RON writers that wrote `\r\n` on Windows now write LF on every host, as every other Regia RON file does: the
  outbox ledger, `.detutility.ron`, the content-lint policy, logic-graph breakpoints, the install tier file, the net
  desync bundle, asset collections, import presets, asset packages, launch profiles, project settings, inspector pins,
  Outliner columns and recent-project metadata; Linux output is byte-identical (ADR-8716)
- Flame-context guards dropped out of order left a stale context (ADR-7427)
- ECS systems past `MAX_SYSTEMS` vanished from the timeline and flame (ADR-7409)

### Tests, docs and tooling

- Common host-dependent operations no longer pass the determinism scan, and waiver-shaped text in a string or comment
  cannot silence a finding (ADR-8367)
- `agent_parity_scan` passes again on the closure settings UI line (allowlist rows reclassified)
- `RUSTDOCFLAGS="-D warnings" cargo doc --workspace` passes, `regia_net` builds its docs without `ecs`, and
  `regia_world_geom` compiles from its own folder (`check-packaging`).
- The editor-smoke `light:extensions` failure was a stale shader folder from a shared build slot, not an editor bug;
  the `command:view.content_drawer` debug panic and the Sequence Curves smoke fingerprint are fixed. (ADR-8702)
- Merge repairs to `docs/decisions.md`, `docs/progress.md`, `docs/provenance.md`, `docs/ported-sources.toml`,
  `book/SUMMARY.md` and `book/COVERAGE.md` (duplicated or spliced ADRs and rows, dropped sections); the ADR index
  lists 2,478 ADRs with no same-number duplicates.
- Book checks, provenance (60 rows repaired in place), unsafe inventory, feature matrix and soak test pins are green
  again after the merges; the networking guide's manifest snippet puts `default-features = false` on the workspace
  dependency entry.
- `regia_editor` `pie_modes` no longer hangs on NVIDIA cards: its GPU tests share one device (ADR-8411, ADR-9602)
- The soak harness reports a peer whose inputs never reached the server as a `server_no_input` wedge (ADR-8424)
- `agent_parity_scan` classifies 56 unclassified editor UI sites; content-lint, S-14 phase-off, rubble-pile and
  `agent_parity_shell` tests follow the merged behavior
- `net.sealed_endpoint_sequence` fuzz model no longer counts refused replays, `net_body` priority runs at a tight
  3,000 B/s budget, and `regia_agent`'s `terrain_source` test builds for Windows (ADR-8717)
- The PIE start check is counted, not timed, and GPU tests share one device per process (ADR-7703)
- The tunnel exposure-zone test runs green on the cook's band after baked interior GI (ADR-8629)
- `format_corpus_upgrade` places the `.meta` v1 fixture and old fixtures of merged bumps; the `server-toml` index
  listed v4 twice (ADR-8580)
- `cargo xtask book-check`, book coverage (`book/COVERAGE.md`), editor-agent coverage, the manual checks, `regia_editor`
  rustdoc and `cargo xtask doc-examples` pass again on the v0.6 line, and the release-note section reader no longer cuts
  sections short (ADR-8208)
- `cargo fmt --all --check` passes with vendored kira kept in upstream formatting (ADR-7543)
- Test fixes, no bar moved: `clean_exit_renderer_drop` waits for the drop threads; `asset_cooked_model`'s steady-
  allocation tests use their own device and compare paired rounds (trimmed mean at most 0.5); `typed_params_negative`
  runs its passes in sequence; Switchbacks test cooks author the ramp-facet waiver; `quick_draw_agent` and
  `render_graph_agent` hang guards are 90 s per call again. (ADR-8598, ADR-8635, ADR-8436)
- `dispatch_fuzz` skips `seeing.gi_denoise_check`, `viewer.render_still` and `looks.render_board`, and several tests
  match intended changes (`.animgraph.ron` v4, live-opening sessions, `UiPointerGate`, `viewport.show_fog`).
- The counted-mesh steady-allocation and visibility-buffer twin checks count whole frames, readback included.
  (ADR-7542)
- `check-features` row `regia-server-logic` builds a dedicated server that runs logic graphs.
- `cargo xtask editor-smoke` restarts a crashed editor with `--normal-mode` (two crashes used to leave it in safe mode
  with no agent listener), contact sheets keep each screenshot's aspect ratio, and the 200 % and light-theme passes
  start in edit mode and fail a crop identical to the default (ADR-8119).
- `tests/asset_cooked_model.rs` steady-allocation checks no longer flake on a loaded host (ADR-7428)
- `photoreal::metered_exposure_opens_up_a_low_sun` passes on the Intel Arc (averaged codes; the bar is unchanged)
  (ADR-7531)
- `regia_net` prediction and `regia_framework` `net_character` tests in the unreliable state-channel mode, and
  `regia_net` `tests/server_host.rs` on a Windows host with a shared TEMP (ADR-8763, ADR-8504)
- The sequencer timeline's keyboard shortcuts are driven by synthetic input in tests (ADR-8640)
- Tests that could not fail now can: the navmesh ramp, `tq_gaps`, `terrain_height_edits` sample checks,
  `child_decal_free_frame`, the overlay hold test; flaky `a_still_emitter_does_not_retarget_every_tick` and the Opus
  fuzz seed test with codec features are fixed; the lightmap gallery's planted-hole fixture works at LOW. (ADR-7558,
  ADR-7602, ADR-7635, ADR-7618)
- `cargo doc -p regia_render --features test-hooks` passes `-D warnings`; `regia_vfx` tests pass clippy.
- The `regia_editor` `hot_reload` suite recovers from a killed run: it journals the original fixture source in
  `target/hot-reload-m3`, restores it at the start of the next run (keeping the edited copy as
  `hot-fixture-lib.rs.interrupted`) and serializes runs across processes with a file lock; a fixture that is already
  edited with no journal is refused before the build with its cause (ADR-8787)
- `regia_test_utils::det_golden`'s doc example checks that the registry holds every engine scenario
  (`scenarios::engine_scenarios()`, 13 today) instead of a fixed count of 3

## Upgrade: host changes since v0.5.0

v0.6.0 carries the one format bump of the v0.6 line (ADR-7724). Each frozen content format moves at most one version
from v0.5.0, and every change has a migrator, so a v0.5.x project needs no re-authoring and no re-bake from source.
`cargo xtask check-formats` now also fails when a format moved more than one version since the last release or changed
shape without a bump (`--since-tag <tag-or-file>` picks the release; ADR-7724).

**Steps for a v0.5.x project**

1. Commit or back up the project.
2. List what changes (writes nothing):
   `regia upgrade-project --project <dir>` (add `--json` for a machine-readable plan).
3. Rewrite the files in place, each original kept as `<file>.bak`:
   `regia upgrade-project --project <dir> --write`
   To move the project's engine dependency in the same run, add `--engine <source>` (`path:<dir>`,
   `git:<url>#<tag>` or `registry:<name>@0.6.0`).
4. Cook again (`regia cook`). Cooked caches that are new or moved (`motiondb-cooked`, `terrain-final-heights`,
   `terrain-mesh-layers-cooked`) are cooked again from their sources; they are never migrated in place. Cooked terrain
   indexes (`terrain-index`, inside a cooked map's `.fckd`, 1 → 2) still load at v1; step 3 rewrites them as v2
   (header kept, `.bak` written), or a recook writes v2.

**What an old project sees in v0.6.0 without step 3.** Every file below still loads: the loader runs the migrator in
memory, and the next save of that file writes the new version. `regia upgrade-project` without `--write` lists each
such file with its old and new version.

**What an older build sees.** v0.5.x refuses a file written by v0.6.0 in one of the formats below with that format's
version error (the file's version and the newest the build reads), never a silent load. Keep a project on one engine
version; to go back, restore the `.bak` files or the commit from step 1.

### Formats, wire versions and migrators

| Format | Files | v0.5.0 → v0.6.0 | What changes | Migration |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `animgraph` | `.animgraph.ron` | 3 → 4 | the `ChainIK` and `BodyAim` nodes (ADR-8475; they and a `PerJoint` limit entry refuse unknown fields and a `BodyAim` joint needs a name, ADR-8732) and the `StrideWarp` and `OrientationWarp` nodes (ADR-8540) | version number rewritten (additive node kinds) |
| `audiopatch` | `.audiopatch.ron` | 1 → 2 | one-shot, granular and reflected-field-bound layers (ADR-7226) | version number rewritten, rest kept byte for byte |
| `bake` | `.bake.ron` | 2 → 3 | `suns`: up to four baked suns, the first is the scene's sun (ADR-7818) | `sun: Some(s)` becomes `suns: [s]`, `None` becomes `[]`; only that key and the version number are rewritten, and the result must read back as the in-memory migration |
| `cooked-clip` | cooked clips in `.fckd` | 2 → 3 | clips are compressed at cook (ADR-7227); node channels ride in the v3 payload (`node-tracks`, ADR-8157) | old cooked clips are rewritten as lossless v3 (never compressed); a re-cook compresses |
| `matfunc` | `.matfunc.ron` | 3 → 4 | the WGSL Code node in a material function (ADR-7852) | version number rewritten, a v3 function reads unchanged |
| `matgraph` | `.matgraph.ron` | 4 → 5 | the WGSL Code node (`Node::Code`: typed inputs, textures, one output, WGSL body, ADR-7852); `ParamDecl` `category`, `tooltip` and `range` folded into v5 (ADR-8105 (b)) | version number rewritten, a v4 graph reads unchanged |
| `meta` | `<asset>.meta` | 1 → 2 | the importer setting `lossless_clips` cooks a source's animation clips bit-exact (ADR-7819) | a v1 `settings` loses a `lossless_clips` key no v1 importer honored, so the switch is off and the source cooks as before; otherwise only the version number is rewritten |
| `motiondb-cooked` | cooked motion databases | 2 → 3 | optional cooked arc-length and clip-speed arrays (spec 52 § 4, ADR-7817) | a cache: v1 and v2 still load (arc lengths sampled at first use, clip speeds from the raw features, the same database byte for byte); `regia cook` writes v3 |
