| `net-settings` | `config/net.ron` | 2 → 3 | the session silence policy: `interrupt_ms`, `timeout_ms`, `start_grace_ms` (ADR-7950) | `migrate_net_settings` rewrites v1/v2 as v3, an explicit seconds `timeout_s` kept as milliseconds |
| `netprofile` | `.netprofile.ron` | 1 → 2 | `rate_tiers` (ADR-8031) and `extrapolation_gate_m` (ADR-8522), one v2 (ADR-8572) | a v1 file loads with no tiers and the gate off; `regia upgrade-project` rewrites its version number |
| `pgout` | `.pgout` | 3 → 4 | outputs follow data layers, `spawn.mesh` `target_layer` (ADR-7214); an optional prefab-override section, so a bake that uses prefab overrides no longer fails with `pgout_corrupt` (ADR-7750) | binary rewrite, same content |
| `procgen-painted-points` | the `PaintedPoints` component in `.level.ron` | 1 → 2 | painted yaw (ADR-7215) | points read with yaw 0, rotations unchanged |
| `profile-capture` | `.rprof.json` | 1 → 2 | optional per-thread timeline (ADR-7325) | read, written at v2 (no timeline) |
| `retarget` | `.retarget.ron` | 1 → 2 | `ik_goals` solved at bake (ADR-8476) | `ik_goals` added empty, so the setup bakes what it baked before; `regia upgrade-project` rewrites it at v2 |
| `sequence` | `.seq.ron` | 5 → 6 | event channel keys and the track lock (`Track::locked`); solo is not saved (ADR-8105 (c)) | version number rewritten (a v5 file has no event track and nothing locked) |
| `server-toml` | `server.toml` | 3 → 4 | the shared silence policy (ADR-7950); the optional `net.mtu_probing` key (absent means off, ADR-8300); the optional `levels.playlist` key that names a `.playlist.ron` (ADR-8345), folded into the same v4 (ADR-8529) | version line rewritten, comments and every other line kept |
| `shots` | `.shots.ron` | 2 → 3 | camera path and look-at keys may pass the shot's end (ADR-8640); same shape | version number rewritten (`migrate_shots`) |
| `terrain-index` | `terrain/index` in a cooked map | 1 → 2 | optional exact heights, holes and a pak-backed photoreal material (ADR-8024), then an optional count mapping (ADR-8471), one v2 (ADR-8619); v2 files written by v0.5.0 were not registered | `migrate_terrain_index`; header kept, `.bak` written |
| `test-report` | the functional-test report JSON | 1 → 2 | optional per-test `notes` (spec 41 § 2.4, ADR-7980) | `TestReport::from_json` reads a v1 report with empty notes and stamps it v2; reports are run outputs, so `regia upgrade-project` has no row for them |
| `theme` | `.theme.ron` | 1 → 2 | the `focus_visible` layer of a type or variation and structured style `rules` (ADR-8105 (e), ADR-8303) | version number rewritten |
| `ui` | `.ui.ron` | 10 → 11 | text input options and image fit (ADR-9980); theme-token and animation bindings, row height from the template, image `fit: tile` (spec 81 § 10), and `back`, `disabled_subtree` and `pivot` (ADR-8252), all folded into v11 | version number rewritten, rest kept byte for byte |
| `uisettings` | `.uisettings.ron` | 1 → 2 | the optional project `scale_mode` rule (ADR-8252) | version 1 loads and is read as 2; `regia_ui::migrate_ui_settings_ron` changes only the version number |
| `vfx` | `.vfx.ron` | 7 → 8 | mesh-asset particles, CPU-ordered mobile emitters (ADR-7210); authored fixed effect bounds, `SystemModule::bounds`, folded into v8 (ADR-8355) | loaded and saved at v8 |

**New formats, v1 unless stated** (nothing to migrate; a newer file is refused by its version):

- Content: `abilities-cooked`, `ai-det-utility` (`.detutility.ron`, the deterministic utility catalogue, ADR-8609),
  `ai-formation` (`.formation.ron`, formation slots, ADR-8358), `asset-collections` (`config/asset_collections.ron`
  and the personal `.regia/asset_collections.ron`, ADR-7759), `asset-ids` (`.assetids.ron`, the asset id manifest for
  sources not in version control, ADR-8460), `assetpkg` (`.regia_assetpkg.ron`, the asset package manifest,
  ADR-7788), `audio-buses` (`config/audio.ron`: the bus mixer's effect chain, bypass, sends with a pre/post-fader
  `tap`, and snapshots; a versionless file is v0 and gains `version: 1` through `migrate_audio_buses`, ADR-8354,
  ADR-8590), `bike` (`.bike.ron`, a named two-wheel bike the Vehicle Tuning panel edits, ADR-8630), `content-state`,
  `data-asset` (`.data.ron`; a file saved by an earlier v0.6 development build's editor record is detected and
  rewritten as v1, ADR-7257), `delivery-contract` (`.contract.ron`, the per-class delivery contract with its kit
  fields, ADR-8401, ADR-8450), `glyphs` (`.glyphs.ron`), `hoverhull` (`.hoverhull.ron`, hover hull tuning, ADR-8512),
  `import-presets` (`config/import_presets.ron`, ADR-7904), `key-track` (`.track.ron`, a scripted key track for
  `regia play-gate`, ADR-8403), `launch-profiles` (`config/launch_profiles.ron`, ADR-7843), `layer-file`
  (`<level>.<layer>.layer.ron`, ADR-7792), `level-variant` (`.levelvariant.ron`, ADR-7823), `logic` (`.logic.ron`),
  `logic-graph-ref` (the `LogicGraphRef` component a prefab or level names its logic graph with, scene-embedded;
  `scene` stays 13, ADR-8566), `playlist-ron` (`.playlist.ron`, dedicated-server playlist rows, ADR-8345),
  `release-chunks` (`release-chunks.json`, release chunks under a byte cap with their SHA-256 list, ADR-8404),
  `source-lint` (`config/source_lint.ron`, the project source-lint policy, ADR-8248; a missing file is the engine
  default), `terrain-editlayers` (`<map>.terrain.editlayers`, enters at v2 with material weights and a named procgen
  target per layer; v1 was never released, ADR-7825, ADR-8381), `terrain-mesh-layers` (`<map>.meshlayers.ron` and
  `<map>.passages.ron`, ADR-8112), `terrain-raster-descriptor` (`heightfield.raster.json`, ADR-8542),
  `terrain-source-tiles` (`heightfield.tiles.json`, ADR-8595), `tilepatterns` (`.tilepatterns.ron`, ADR-7853) and
  `viscells` (`.viscells.ron`).
- Cooked caches (a mismatch recooks, never migrated): `nav-det-navmesh` (`<map>.detnav`, the deterministic navmesh a
  lockstep authority arms; enters at v2, ADR-8174), `node-tracks` (node channels embedded in the cooked-clip v3
  payload; old clips have no node body, ADR-8157), `terrain-final-heights` (`terrain/heights` in a cooked map,
  ADR-8024) and `terrain-mesh-layers-cooked` (`<map>.meshlayers`, ADR-8112; its section zones store the canonical
  lockstep yaw, ADR-8579).
- Run outputs and wire: `desync` (the bundle a lockstep desync writes, spec 120 § 7.6, ADR-8455), `gpu-breadcrumbs`,
  `regia-det-golden-1` (`.detgold`, the determinism golden fixtures, ADR-8231), `regiatrace` and `version-probe` (not a
  file: the frozen version probe and stamp datagrams on the game socket, ADR-7985).

**Editor files registered as frozen formats (ADR-7902).** The editor's own per-user and per-project files are now
hash-checked and versioned like content, with a corpus fixture and a fuzz target each. They are editor state, not game
content; `regia upgrade-project` walks only the project, so it never rewrites the user-config copies.
`editor-keymap` (`editor_keymap.ron`), `editor-panel` (`.editorpanel.ron`, the utility panel sidecar, ADR-8189; a newer
version is reported and never rewritten), `editor-prefs` (`editor_prefs.ron`), `editor-profile`
(`.editor_profile.ron`), `editor-theme` (`.editor_theme.ron`, ADR-7838), `editor-view-state`
(`.regia/editor_view.ron`), `editor-window-state` (`editor_window_state.ron`), `inspector-pins`
(`inspector_pins.ron`), `saved-searches` (`saved_searches.ron`) and `viewport-snap` (`.regia/viewport_snap.ron`) are
v1. `editor-layout` (`editor_layout.ron`, user, project and named layouts) is v3: v2 added the optional `floating`
list, v3 panel instances (`<panel>#<n>` tab ids and per-instance `instances` state, ADR-8105 (a)); the editor migrator
reads v1 and v2, and `regia upgrade-project` rewrites the version number of a project copy after checking it has a
`root`.

**Not format changes.** The fracture support box is an importer setting inside the `.meta` `settings` string. The
lossless clip cook switch became `meta` 1 → 2 (above).

**Waiting for v0.7** (not in v0.6.0): `nav-cell` 2 → 3 (conservative rasterization, ADR-7558; an owner hold on
re-cooking the lead game's navigation), `decal` 2 → 3 (custom decal textures, ADR-8121), the cell-centered TerrainMap sample
spacing (needs the lead game), and `voxel-field` v1 (ADR-8116).

### Formats: other wire and migrator changes

- **Editor:** The material graph layout sidecar `<graph>.matgraph.layout.ron` is version 2 (`LAYOUT_FORMAT_VERSION`): v1
  loads, and an older editor ignores a v2 sidecar. `.regia/editor_view.ron` (v1, editor-only, safe to delete) gains an
  optional `ui_designer` field. (ADR-9700, ADR-7275)
- **Editor:** More editor files: the hosted-agent history JSONL v1 under `.regia/agent_sessions/` (ADR-7844) and
  `recent_projects_meta.ron` v1 next to `recent_projects.txt`, which is unchanged; `editor-panel` is in
  `EDITOR_FORMATS`. A `.regia/viewport_snap.ron` without `format_version` loads as v1. The keymap file stays v1; a
  scoped binding's key is `"<scope>:<command id>"`. (ADR-7902)
- **Rendering:** Recook desktop paks with `regia cook --ship` to store vertex-colored meshes GPU-ready; older paks load.
  (ADR-7919)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** `.bake.ron`: a `sun` key in a v3 file or `suns` in a v0-v2 file is
  `bake_desc_invalid`; `regia upgrade-project --write` keeps the original as `.bake.ron.bak`; corpus fixture
  `bake/v3.bake.ron`. (ADR-7818)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** `OcclusionProxy::far_band` is `#[reflect(default)]`, so saved scenes load with the
  band off. (ADR-7970)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** Re-bake lightmaps to drop the denoiser leak; older `.flmap` files load unchanged.
  (ADR-7590)
- **World:** `QUERY_BITS_VERSION` is 8 (5 in v0.5.0) and is compared in the lockstep handshake: a peer on another value,
  including the development values 6 and 7, is refused on the same map. Compare content, query and math versions before
  a lockstep match; hosts routing on the layered bake also compare `LAYERED_NAV_BITS_VERSION` and the bake's
  `content_hash`. A lockstep host that recorded hole yaws, `hole_at` or query answers re-records them, and a baked
  tunnel file outside the new bounds no longer arms (re-export it). (ADR-8246, ADR-8323, ADR-8346, ADR-8486, ADR-8531,
  ADR-8166, ADR-8167)
- **World:** A v1 cooked terrain index loads with the four new entries empty; an index cooked by an unshipped
  pre-release v2 layout does not load: recook. (ADR-8619)
- **World:** `<map>.meshlayers.ron` gains optional `waive` and `audit`: old documents parse, the cooked file stays
  version 1 and the source fingerprint moves. `<map>.viscells.ron` stays version 1. (ADR-8359, ADR-8371)
- **World:** The cooked `<map>.meshlayers` stays version 1 while unshipped, but its bytes and `content_hash` change: the
  presentation section is filled, chunks get their own section, section bytes change for maps with sections, and filled
  mouths cook to different triangles; re-cook every map. Its presentation section is version 2 (`PRESENTATION_VERSION`):
  a v1 section, or a pre-release file with chunks in the presentation section, is refused with
  `meshlayers_presentation_stale` (or another `meshlayers_presentation_*` code) and recooks (derived data, no migrator).
  (ADR-8112, ADR-8175, ADR-8210, ADR-8396, ADR-8451, ADR-8533, ADR-8576, ADR-8579, ADR-7724)
- **World:** `nav-det-navmesh` files (`<map>.detnav`) carry the magic `NVM2` and canonical float bits. (ADR-8174)
- **World:** `.pgout` v4 stores per-cell `set_layers`. A `.pgout` whose graph has a `Query` dependency reads as stale
  once and re-bakes; v0.5.x builds refuse a v4 `.pgout` with `pgout_version`; packages no longer carry loose `.pgout`
  files: re-cook. (ADR-7277, ADR-7750, ADR-7214)
- **World:** `regia upgrade-project` lists `terrain-raster-descriptor` and `terrain-source-tiles` files and leaves them
  in place. The overview descriptor v1 is a third authoring artifact; none of the three is a cook-input format, and they
  have JSON schemas under `docs/formats/`. The editor keeps terrain source state in `terrain_sources.json` and
  `terrain_source_backups/*.png`. (ADR-8541, ADR-8542, ADR-8595, ADR-8596, ADR-8639)
- **World:** `regia upgrade-project` re-saves a versionless `.levelvariant.ron` at v1. `edit_layers_migration_sources()`
  is `[1]`: a v1 `<map>.terrain.editlayers` loads with empty weights and no procgen targets, and
  `regia upgrade-project --write` rewrites it. (ADR-7823, ADR-8381)
- **World:** Levels may carry the additive components `UniqueName`, `EditorFolder` and `EditorSelectionRoot` (no version
  step); an older engine loads them with the unknown-component warning and without the marker.
- **World:** `.vfx.ron` loading is stricter: a file with a non-finite number in a newly checked field, a negative
  `drag`, `count_scale`, `alpha_scale`, `softness`, `stretch.velocity_scale`, `heat_haze`, flipbook `Fps` or
  particle-light `intensity`/`radius`, or an event link whose worst case exceeds 16,777,215 children is refused (no
  version step, ADR-7560). A v8 build from before the bounds fold refuses a file that sets `system.bounds` (ADR-8355).
- **World:** A `.biome.ron` child rule with `count.1` above 4,194,304 no longer loads, and a layer whose raw child
  candidates exceed `points_per_item` fails with `limit_exceeded`.
- **Animation and characters:** `ANIMGRAPH_FORMAT_VERSION` is 4; `RETARGET_FORMAT_VERSION` is 2 with
  `retarget_migration_sources()` = `[1]`. (ADR-8475, ADR-8540, ADR-8476)
- **Animation and characters:** Plain-node bodies occupy appended variants of the cooked clip v3 payload; v1-v3 payloads
  stay readable. Recook glTF sources to get node channels that were omitted. (ADR-8157)
- **Animation and characters:** BVH sources recook once after the `meta` v2 step. (ADR-7819)
- **Gameplay systems:** `regia upgrade-project` knows the suffixes of the new `.formation.ron`, `.detutility.ron`,
  `.hoverhull.ron` and `.bike.ron` formats; `.bike.ron` has the fuzz target `bike_ron`. (ADR-8358, ADR-8609, ADR-8512,
  ADR-8630)
- **Gameplay systems:** Lockstep settings handshakes compare `HoverParams::to_le_bytes` (180 bytes) and
  `WorldPhysics::to_le_bytes` (10 bytes); a peer whose bytes differ must be refused at match start (lobbies now compare
  `hull_handshake_bytes`: see Changed). (ADR-8512, ADR-8349)
- **Gameplay systems:** `regia_detsim::state::SnapshotKind::InputLog` is frame kind word 2. Snapshots carry the game's
  own `STATE_VERSION` and have no migrator. (ADR-8479, ADR-8525)
- **Gameplay systems:** `OrcaSolver::Lp`, `Ringed` target rules, new cadence channels or tiers, and a non-default
  `PhaseRule` change simulation results: bump the game's sim version and re-record lockstep pins. (ADR-8555, ADR-8517,
  ADR-8560)
- **Gameplay systems:** Rebuild derived navigation caches and adopt the new sim revision on every peer: zero-bearing
  grid hashes and zero- or NaN-bearing cache signatures can change.
- **Gameplay systems:** The abilities state hash changes where a rolled target's guid leaves the world; rolls for live
  guids are bit-identical. (ADR-7573)
- **Gameplay systems:** `sysring` state bytes written before the partial-flag change do not load (v1 was never
  released). (ADR-8621)
- **Gameplay systems:** A `.bt.ron` `condition` node with an empty `key` no longer compiles, and a `.spatialquery.ron`
  with a non-finite generator or test number fails its first run with `sq_bad_param`. (ADR-7596)
- **Gameplay systems:** `luastate` stays v1, keyed by manifest id; Workshop mounts do not take legacy entries.
  `settings.ron` gains optional probe fields and `DisplaySettings::fullscreen`; older files load. Telemetry JSON schema
  1 and feedback JSON gain optional fields only. (ADR-7992, ADR-7898, ADR-7880, ADR-7965)
- **Networking and Steam:** `REGIA_NET_WIRE_VERSION` is 5 (3, then 4 for input feedback, were earlier v0.6 steps): ship
  clients and servers together. A v0.5.x or earlier build is refused with `protocol_mismatch`, and the version probe
  (`docs/formats/version-probe.md`) names it. (ADR-7896, ADR-8077, ADR-8387, ADR-7985)
- **Networking and Steam:** `NET_PROTOCOL_VERSION` 4 (quantized transforms) and `TRANSFORM_RECORD_REVISION` 3 (the
  steady bit and the predicted residual, after revision 2) change the protocol hash, and session wire revision 2 changes
  every protocol hash (a session with `MontageNetPlugin` also hashes `regia.montage_command`). Deploy clients and
  servers together: older builds are refused at the handshake (`protocol_mismatch` on a session, `content_differs` on a
  `NetLink`), and older `.netdemo` recordings no longer play (`NetDemoError::ProtocolMismatch`), so re-record net demos.
  (ADR-8184, ADR-8300, ADR-8302, ADR-8467, ADR-8522, ADR-7259, ADR-9370)
- **Networking and Steam:** Relay protocol 3 (`RELAY_PROTOCOL_VERSION`; protocol 2 came first, 3 adds introductions and
  the host check). Protocol 1 flows keep their rules, and a protocol 1 relay cannot carry a relay fallback
  (`relay_too_old`). (ADR-7982, ADR-8183, ADR-8340, ADR-8370)
- **Networking and Steam:** The lobby hub wire `regia.lobby_hub` ships at `HUB_WIRE_VERSION` 1; a client or hub built
  with the unreleased version 2 and a release build refuse each other with `protocol_mismatch`. (ADR-8607, ADR-8633)
- **Networking and Steam:** `server.toml` v4 (`SERVER_TOML_FORMAT_VERSION`) also takes optional `[lockstep]` keys; files
  without the new keys load unchanged, and the one v4 has a new hash. A file that sets both `levels.rotation` and
  `levels.playlist` is a config error. When legacy seconds become milliseconds in `server.toml` or `config/net.ron`, a
  non-default `timeout_ms` wins. `formats::SERVER_TOML_KEYS` lists `net.mtu_probing`. Corpus fixture
  `server-toml/v4-playlist.ron`. (ADR-7950, ADR-8300, ADR-8345, ADR-8529, ADR-8553, ADR-8558, ADR-7724)
- **Networking and Steam:** `config/net.ron` v3 also holds `session.rate_control`. (ADR-8301)
- **Networking and Steam:** The `desync` bundle is `*.desync.ron`, with the `net.desync` fuzz target;
  `regia upgrade-project` knows it. (ADR-8455)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** The glTF importer moves from 1.13.0 to 1.14.2 (through 1.14.1): every glTF/GLB
  re-cooks once, so recook and repack every platform. External-image sub-asset ids and their manifest paths change (map
  old `model.gltf#texture/N` ids first), and the first cook writes `<image>.meta` beside each external image a glTF
  references and recooks those glTFs once: commit the new `.meta` files. Importer revisions also invalidate cached glTF
  and converted model cooks (new default `_LODn` thresholds): run `regia cook` after `regia upgrade-project`. (ADR-8054,
  ADR-8055, ADR-8143, ADR-8574, ADR-7869)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** The glTF `clips`, `meshes` and `materials` options live in the `.meta`'s
  opaque `settings` string: no `.meta` migration. (ADR-7904, ADR-8376)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** The `source-lint` policy takes optional geometry overlay fields, and
  `LintFinding` gains an optional `effect`; the policy stays v1 with a new schema hash, the loader accepts both shapes,
  and `Policy::migrated` rewrites a project file. The source-lint rule catalog grows, so `tool_hash` changes. (ADR-8247,
  ADR-8248, ADR-8347)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `delivery-contract` v1: the kit fields are serde defaults and its fingerprint
  hash moved; corpus fixture `delivery-contract/v1-kit.ron`. `regia upgrade-project` recognizes `.contract.ron` and
  `.assetids.ron`. (ADR-8401, ADR-8450, ADR-8460, ADR-8622)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** Package hygiene receipts are schema `regia.package_hygiene.receipt` v1.
  (ADR-8348)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `regia upgrade-project` re-encodes `release-chunks.json`. (ADR-8404)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** Ability files now cook to `abilities-cooked`; a project whose ability files do
  not validate fails `regia cook`. (ADR-9370)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `feedback.json` gains optional `pipeline_cache` metadata and a
  `pipelines/*.fpc` entry (FPCH and feedback stay v1); `HotReloadSnapshot` gains a `scripts` list (older files read
  empty). Projects that set `texture_quality = "draft"` recook once. (ADR-7206, ADR-7217)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `build_info.json` gains an optional `boot` object; repackage to include boot
  settings. (ADR-7893)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** Cooked bytes move: aarch64-cooked meshlet DAGs now equal the x86_64 cook, and
  Android ASTC is built invariant (other content may re-encode). A game project built outside this workspace adds the
  same `[env]` `CXXFLAGS_<target>` lines to its `.cargo/config.toml`. (ADR-7878)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** The pack stamp is version 2; the first `regia package` after the upgrade packs
  once more. (ADR-7612)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** MaterialX color values with `colorspace="srgb_texture"` (or `g22_rec709`) cook
  to linear; recook.
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `PATCH_VERSION` is 2 (`.audiopatch.ron`; v1 loads through the migrator);
  `UI_SETTINGS_VERSION` is 2 (`migrate_ui_settings_ron`; v3 and above are refused); `THEME_FORMAT_VERSION` is 2 with
  `theme_migration_sources()` `[0, 1]`, and a v1 theme that uses v2 fields is refused. (ADR-7226, ADR-8252, ADR-8303)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `.ui.ron`: `UI_RON_VERSION` stays 11 through the folds, earlier v11 files read
  the same, and the `ui` schema hash moves (`cargo xtask check-formats --update`). `regia_ui::upgrade_ui_ron` rewrites a
  v10 file's version number, as `regia upgrade-project --write` does; without `fit`, an image reads `preserve_aspect` as
  before. The v11 folds also cover `split`, `foldable`, `tabs`, `markup`, `nav` and `variants`. A pre-fold v0.6
  development build ignores or refuses the folded fields. (ADR-7751, ADR-7827, ADR-7858, ADR-8252, ADR-9980)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `audio-buses`: `AUDIO_BUSES_FORMAT_VERSION`, migrator
  `migrate_audio_buses_ron`. A v0.5 build ignores `version` and `snapshots` but refuses a layout with a `Bypassed`
  effect. The optional send `tap` moved the format hash at the same version, and older builds read a pre-fader send as
  post-fader. (ADR-8354, ADR-8590)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** Older builds refuse `.seq.ron` v6 with `seq_version` and `.shots.ron` v3 with
  `shot sequence version 3 is newer than supported version 2`. (ADR-8188, ADR-7847)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** A video file declaring a nonzero duration under 1 ms no longer opens
  (`container_invalid`); re-export it. (ADR-7563)
- **Core, licence and security:** `PROFILE_CAPTURE_FORMAT_VERSION` is 2; v0 captures also read as v2 with no timeline.
  The timeline strip is not a format change. (ADR-7325, ADR-7409)
- **Core, licence and security:** The v0.6 format composition records 240 formats in one
  `cargo xtask check-formats --update`, including the new `profile-capture`, `ui` and world geometry hashes; a change to
  a format already bumped in this release folds into that version (`book/changing-a-content-format.md`). (ADR-7724,
  ADR-8659)
- **Core, licence and security:** Unclean-session `privacy.txt` is a diagnostic sidecar (policy version 1, no migrator);
  older unclean directories without it are not selected by `is_published_recovery_report`. (ADR-8494)
- **Core, licence and security:** EXR readers accept `regia.*` keys, bake-stamp readers `regia-bake-stamp/1`, and
  render-out readers `regia.render_out/2` as well as the old names; stamps written before the rename stay current.
  (ADR-8670)
- **Tests, docs and tooling:** The `test-report` JSON is what `regia test --report` writes. (ADR-7980)
- **Tests, docs and tooling:** Net soak `result.json` is `regia-net-soak` v2; `SoakReport::from_json` reads v1.
  (ADR-8030)

  `MaterialFunction`, `MaterialInstance`, `Sequence`, `ShotList`, `AttributeSet`, `Effect`, `Ability`, `ProcgenGraph`,

  gains `cloth` and `soft_body` rows before `viewer`. (ADR-7224, ADR-7234, ADR-7548, ADR-7555, ADR-7774, ADR-7813)
