- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `VoiceChatEvent::DecodedFrame`,
  `regia_ui::BindSource::{ScriptField, Theme, Animation}`, `ImeEvent::PreeditCursor`, `regia_ui::ImageFit::Tile`.
  (ADR-7070, ADR-7208, ADR-7237, ADR-7751)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `regia_ui::authoring::WidgetDef::{Split, Foldable, Tabs}`, and
  `WidgetDef::TextInput` has a third field, `TextInputOptions`, so patterns become `WidgetDef::TextInput(p, m, ..)`; new
  enums `ScreenBackDef` (`ignore`, `pop`) and `theme_file::RuleState`;
  `regia_audio::BusEffect::Bypassed(Box<BusEffect>)`; sequencer `Channel::Event`, `TrackKind::Event` (`TrackKind::ALL`
  has 11), `TrackEdit::{Duplicate, Lock, Mute}`. (ADR-9980)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** New public enum `regia_audio::StallAction` (three variants). (ADR-8830)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `AudioError::SampleRateTooHigh { path, sample_rate, max }` and
  `LoudnessError::SampleRateTooHigh(u32)`. (ADR-7602)
- **Core, licence and security:** `TelemetryLevel::Ship`. (ADR-8417)
- **Tests, docs and tooling:** `det-scan --json` `rule` gains `glam_trig`. (ADR-8085)

### Changed signatures, behavior and defaults

- **Editor:** `editor.content_chunks_set_rule` returns `{ chunk, removed, saved, verified, content, preview, error }`.
  (ADR-8543)
- **Editor:** `build_game::BuildCancel` is an alias of `regia_editor::tasks::CancelToken`; `run_cancellable` puts the
  child in its own process group on Unix (a terminal Ctrl+C no longer reaches it); `regia_editor` sets
  `#![recursion_limit = "256"]`. (ADR-7381, ADR-7422)
- **Editor:** `ChildGroup::spawn` replaces creation flags set on the command on Windows;
  `inspector::defaults::modified_fields` answers every leaf for a component with no baseline. (ADR-7548, ADR-7813)
- **Editor:** Removed from `regia_editor::creators`: `PhysicsLayersEdit`, `PhysicsEdit`, `edit_physics`,
  `PHYSICS_CONFIG_PATH` and `MAX_LAYERS` (use `regia_physics::LAYER_CONFIG_PATH` and `MAX_LAYERS`). (ADR-7267)
- **Editor:** Lists that code may count or index grow: `command_palette::COMMANDS` is a `LazyLock<Vec<CommandDef>>`,
  starts with `search.everything` and gains 37 entries and more (`help.context` among them); `editor_prefs::PREFS` gains
  8 and more, and `OWNER_ONLY_PREFS`, `HOLD_COMMANDS`, `viewport_ops::MENU_PATHS` and `agent_parity::ACTIONS` grow (look
  actions up with `agent_parity::find`); `profiler_model::PIE_SPANS` gains `pie_render`; `menus::MENUS` gains
  `"Sequence"` and the new Window entries; `shapes::ACTOR_ENTRIES` is `[&EditorAction; 15]`, `overlays::OVERLAY_COLORS`
  is `[Color32; 16]` and `ui_designer_component_agent::COMPONENT_ERROR_CODES` is `[&str; 10]`. Code that indexes them by
  position or matches their length must follow. `ProblemsState::default()` collapses duplicates. (ADR-7272, ADR-7283,
  ADR-7307, ADR-7409, ADR-7468, ADR-7592, ADR-7614, ADR-8594, ADR-8640, ADR-9810)
- **Editor:** `workspaces::handle_view_keys(ctx, world, hovered)` takes the hovered tab, and `DockViews::apply` and
  `apply_requests` no longer take it; `content_collections::personal_path(project_root)` (the old form is
  `legacy_personal_path`); `baseline_fuzz::targets()` returns `[FuzzTarget; 11]`; `mode2d::tools::rect_cells` returns
  `Option<Vec<(u32, u32)>>` (`None` past `MAX_TOOL_CELLS`).
- **Editor:** `regia_editor::procgen::presets::all()` returns six entries; `create_asset` refuses `DataAsset` (use
  `create_asset_of_type`). (ADR-7234, ADR-7239)
- **Editor:** `import_settings_ui` takes a third argument `busy`; `LocPanel::exchange_path` is replaced by `export_path`
  and `import_path`; `NavPreviewJob::job` is a `WorkerJob<Result<(NavMesh, usize), BakeFail>>`; `PanelError::draw` no
  longer prints the code in the line. (ADR-7716, ADR-8586, ADR-8594)
- **Editor:** `ThemeTokens::regia_default()` returns the new Graphite look (`regia_classic()` is the old one);
  `ThemeTokens::from_ui_roles` writes widget and accent colors from roles; `theme::apply` installs the editor font table
  on first apply, so a host's own fonts go in after it. Saved `*.editor_theme.ron` files look the same. (ADR-8585,
  ADR-8589)
- **Editor:** A host embedding the editor library inserts `PlayWindowSupport` and drives `PlayWindow::sync`,
  `begin_frame` and `presented` for play windows, and calls `view_state::flush` and `exit_cleanup_with(world)` before it
  exits. (ADR-7272, ADR-7275, ADR-7283, ADR-9770)
- **Editor:** `spawn_placed_levels` no longer tags entities under an inline instance before its fill as members, and
  `level.break_placed` moves non-member children to the instance's parent. (ADR-8593, ADR-8680)
- **Editor:** Sequence Curves players carry `EditorOnly`; `SAVE_ALL_CANCEL` and `SCREENSHOT_DISMISS` are method rows.
  (ADR-8583)
- **Editor:** `BiomePanel::open` and `new_biome` take `discard: bool`; `mm_db_editor::panel::entry_extras` takes
  `&mut PanelState`.
- **Editor:** `content::create_material_instance` returns `Err` for a parent outside the project;
  `log_links::asset_action` answers `AssetAction::Editor` for every `asset_open::OPEN_KINDS` kind;
  `content_collections::ContentLibrary::save` is replaced by `edit` and `refresh`, and `CollectionView` is a struct.
  (ADR-7662, ADR-7725, ADR-7798)
- **Editor:** `sweep_stale_staging(out, now)` takes the current time; `SiteClass::needs_via()` is true for `Confirm`;
  `ToolExtensionContext::register_editor_action` refuses an unparseable shortcut (`manifest_invalid`). (ADR-7747,
  ADR-7791, ADR-7794)
- **Editor:** Hosts that build their own editor app add `regia_editor::editor_jobs::tick_system` to `Last` (it also
  polls terrain creates and blockout work), call `preview_scene::sync` once per frame and
  `asset_viewer::run_helper_requests` after the viewer panel. (ADR-7664, ADR-7771)
- **Editor:** Ctrl+0 no longer resets the interface zoom: use Ctrl+Alt+0 or bind `view.reset_zoom`. (ADR-7733)
- **Editor:** `AudioEmitter` and `SmartObject` reflect `Clone` (`#[reflect(Component, Default, Clone)]`). (ADR-7741)
- **Editor:** `GizmoState.local: bool` is replaced by `GizmoState.space: GizmoSpace` (`local: true` is
  `GizmoSpace::Local`).
- **Editor:** `camera_nav::toolbar` takes a last argument `ease_key: (u64, usize)`.
- **Editor:** `live_link::SessionSink::spawn` takes a fourth parameter `parent: Option<u64>`; `RemoteSink::new` takes
  `(&mut dyn RemoteTransport, &mut HashMap<Guid, u64>)`; `remote_world::refresh` and `select_entities` queue on the
  connection's thread and wait (`refresh_async` does not wait). A host with its own editor loop calls
  `live_link::pump(edit, play)` once per frame and `live_link::flush` to wait for a pass.
- **Editor:** `level_io::save_level_and_terrain` and `save_level_document` return an error (`prefab_in_place`,
  `prefab_open`) while a prefab is open; `regia_scene::level_dir::read_level_as_stored` and `read_level_source` fold
  layer files in and can return `duplicate_entity`; `level_io::save_level_file` writes only differences for a
  `.levelvariant.ron` path.
- **Editor:** A host that runs the shell with its own multi-viewport egui runner and inserts `OsWindowSupport` must run
  `EditorShell::os_window_ui` for every child viewport; `EditorShell` hosts reserve the named tab controls with
  `workspaces::reserve_tab_buttons`.
- **Editor:** A host that draws `material_editor_panel` with its own `MaterialEditorState` calls
  `material_editor::sync_shared_history(world, state)` every frame (hosted `undo()` and `redo()` only queue the step); a
  host registering its own `DocProvider` should also register a save hook (`DocAutosaveRegistry::register_save`).
- **Editor:** `vehicle_tuning_assets::TunedAsset` requires `Send + Sync + 'static`; `EditorCommand` gains the default
  methods `touched()` and `activity_targets`; `pie::catch_in_pie` is `pub(crate)`.
- **Editor:** `regia_ecs::Entity` has a built-in Inspector widget; a game's own `InspectorEguiImpl` for it still wins.
  `pie_process::ProcessRole` derives `Hash`.
- **Editor:** `build_snarl` and `build_snarl_with` leave out a rerouted input's connection: read edges from the
  document. `DockGroups::fingerprint` covers floating split topology and is not interchangeable with
  `editor_layout::dock_fingerprint`. `asset_open::open_from_browser` opens the Save all dialog over unsaved edits.
- **Editor:** `inspector::egui_utils::{add_button, remove_button, up_button, down_button}` take a `spoken: &str`;
  `mode2d::viewport_frame` takes a trailing `pane_focused: bool` (pass `false` for the old gate); a `warning_glyph` is
  read through `a11y_audit::label_names` (role `Label`). (ADR-7396, ADR-8870)
- **Editor:** `SeqTimeline::selection` is `Vec<MarkRef>` and `key_points` is `Vec<(MarkRef, egui::Pos2)>`;
  `RemoteWorker::set_timeline_request` takes the strip length; `menus::play_menu` takes `&mut World`. (ADR-7409,
  ADR-7468, ADR-7592, ADR-7614, ADR-8640)
- **Editor:** `autosave::apply_restore` returns `Err` while prefab edit mode is active; `tracks::new_sequence` refuses a
  blank name; `asset_viewer::kind_of` returns `AssetKind::Sound` for `.ogg`, `.flac` and `.mp3`;
  `RemoteWorker::snapshot` returns `LinkState::Disconnected` for a poisoned lock. (ADR-7427)
- **Editor:** An entity spawned by a runtime method in the editor is level content on the next save; the editor refuses
  to open a `.data.ron` whose type is only `ReflectDefault` (register it with `register_data_asset` or
  `#[reflect(Asset)]`); PIE no longer moves a game's own controllers 4 m apart; `editor.undo` past a variant's creation
  removes the variant file. (ADR-7382, ADR-7466, ADR-7490, ADR-7614)
- **Rendering:** System parameters changed (registration as a system is unchanged; direct callers and wrappers must
  follow): `extract_frame` (its third parameter is the tuple `(mesh query, Option<Res<DrawList>>)`), `select_mesh_lods`
  (a last `Option<Res<DrawList>>`), `regia_render::extract_mesh_occluders`,
  `regia_procgen::draw::extract_proc_instances` (`Without<LayerParked>`, reads `DrawVisibility`),
  `regia_vfx::crash::drive_crash_sequences`, `cull_unreachable_cells`, `extract_glass`,
  `regia_telemetry::telemetry_frame`, `feedback::record_feedback_frame`, `scene_ready::advance_headless_scene_ready` and
  `regia_audio::audio_lifecycle`. `RenderPlugin` inserts an empty `DrawList`, and `ExtractedFrame::meshes` can hold
  `Entity::PLACEHOLDER` entries. `runtime::render_texture_views` takes `GameFrameWarm` mutably; a host running its own
  render schedule adds `take_scene_prepare` before it. (ADR-7395, ADR-7588, ADR-7602, ADR-7634, ADR-7670, ADR-7673,
  ADR-7898, ADR-7965, ADR-8087, ADR-8122, ADR-8457)
- **Rendering:** `regia_render::surface::pixel_viewport` is public and re-exported as `regia_render::pixel_viewport`.
  (ADR-7395, ADR-7634)
- **Rendering:** `CpuMesh::colors` of a GPU-ready entry now holds `COLOR_0` (a host testing `is_empty()` for "no colors"
  sees the stream). (ADR-7919)
- **Rendering:** `RenderSettings::preset()` masks the runtime upscaler, render scale and host memory budget, so it
  returns the matching preset (not `None`) after upscaler or render-scale changes; `wants_frame_callback` is false for a
  focused `Immediate` or `Mailbox` window. (ADR-8170, ADR-8577)
- **Rendering:** `update_view_visibility` takes four more optional system parameters (calling it as a plain function is
  unsupported). A host drawing several window views with one `FrameRenderer` by hand calls `set_occlusion_view(i)`
  before each view and `retain_occlusion_views(views.len())` each frame (a stopped view retires on its own after 16
  rounds). Gameplay that skips work on `ViewVisibility` false now also skips posed-off-screen characters and occluded
  meshes. (ADR-7255, ADR-7286, ADR-7329, ADR-8620)
- **Rendering:** `LightsViewsUniform` is 368 bytes and the frame uniform `LabFrame` 1136 bytes (host WGSL appends
  `ambient_rot` after `ambient_box`); the terrain uniform is 64 bytes; in an app with `UiPlugin`, `DebugText` draws
  atlas glyphs. (ADR-7254, ADR-7275, ADR-8369)
- **Rendering:** `ASSET_MESH_VERTEX_STRIDE` is 64 and `AssetMeshBuffers::vertex` holds `LabVertex`es;
  `AssetLoader::accepts` and `PrepareAsset::unload` have default bodies. (ADR-7256, ADR-7284)
- **Rendering:** Buffer names declared in `render.graph`'s audit identify built-in resources; host passes use other
  names. (ADR-8065)
- **Rendering:** `DebugView::is_supported` is true for every mode; a host driving `FrameRenderer` from its own
  extraction calls `set_lights_views` every frame. Adding `BreadcrumbsPlugin` or `TracePlugin` after
  `regia::DefaultPlugins` now warns and skips it. (ADR-7211, ADR-9070)
- **Rendering:** Adding `CountedMeshPlugin` makes counted `CpuMesh`, `CookedTexture` and `CookedImage` loads drop their
  CPU copy after upload unless they ask for `keep_cpu_data`. (ADR-7330)
- **Rendering:** `AssetTextureUploads::view(id)` is `view(id, srgb)`; a counted skinned mesh has empty `CpuSkin::joints`
  / `weights` once its CPU copy is released (load with `keep_cpu_data` to read them); a host that binds a counted vertex
  buffer sees `STORAGE` in its usage. (ADR-7462, ADR-8610)
- **Rendering:** `regia_matgraph::compile_with` reports a host-shader failure of a graph with Code nodes on those nodes;
  `MatGraphDoc::check_mode_change` accepts an instance whose `parent` changed with its graph and layout; a graph's
  pipeline key changes once when a parameter moves.
- **Rendering:** `render.stats` `mesh_cache.mesh_bytes` is lower for scenes with counted LOD chains. A `DynamicTexture`
  written in place moves the material epoch. (ADR-7542, ADR-9603)
- **Rendering:** `CapturedTarget::checkpoint` and `after_pass` also name a built-in pass for pass-boundary copies;
  `CaptureSession::arm` requests none. Hosts must finish or cancel a capture session on the same renderer. (ADR-7860)
- **Rendering:** `RenderDevice::headless()` and `list_adapters` return the process's shared instance, so an adapter
  hot-plugged after the first call is not listed; use `create_window_instance` for a fresh one. (ADR-7557)
- **Rendering:** `FrameGenPacing::fed_this_tick` is not set on a tick that failed after the pacer's decision; a user
  shader with a member store between two negated tests fails `check-shaders` (write the inner test as `!(a > b)`).
  (ADR-8930)
- **Rendering:** An entity or light that is `Visibility::Hidden` (or under a hidden parent) stops drawing and lighting;
  set `Visibility::Visible` where content relied on it. `MeshRenderer` requires `InheritedVisibility` and
  `ViewVisibility`, and the five light components require `InheritedVisibility`, which changes archetypes (queries using
  `Without<InheritedVisibility>` match differently). A host terrain draw is withheld while every `Terrain` is parked or
  hidden. (ADR-7200, ADR-7588, ADR-7670)
- **Rendering:** `QualityTier::Low.step_down()` is `Some(VeryLow)`; `quality.get_detected` `detected.probe.stepped_down`
  is false for a size-mismatched probe. (ADR-7882)
- **Rendering:** `Fsr3Settings::default()` returns `min_disocclusion_accumulation` -1 and `accumulation_added_per_frame`
  1/4; set -1/3 and 1/3 to keep the SDK tuning. (ADR-7678)
- **Rendering:** `PathTracer::new` needs `max_compute_workgroup_storage_size` of at least 28,672 bytes (else
  `PtError::Gpu`). A custom shader composing `baked_gi.wgsl` defines `fn bgi_u() -> BakedGiUniform` instead of declaring
  `bgi`. (ADR-7587, ADR-7879)
- **Rendering:** Physical-sky cost moves from `atmosphere_ms` to `forward_ms` (airless sky reads about 0 ms there), and
  look comparisons of blended content over sky change on High and Epic. (ADR-7963)
- **Rendering:** A `FrameMesh` with empty slices whose key is resident now draws; `TrackedDevice` pipeline creation is
  `#[track_caller]`; `FrameCpuTimings`/`GpuTimings` readbacks may describe an older frame (compare `sample_id`).
  (ADR-7885, ADR-7891, ADR-7965)
- **Rendering:** `TerrainGpu::set_anisotropy_footprint` with a new value after the first frame pays one shared pipeline
  rebuild; hosts with render-to-texture cameras or second windows call `register_warm_target` before the game-frame
  warm. Load all-LOD models after the tier's resident cap is published. (ADR-7885, ADR-7886, ADR-8025)
- **Rendering:** `Traversal::eye_cell` of an eye in a doorway is the first doorway cell; `VisPortal::doorway_depth` is
  the larger gap plus tolerance. A host that tags a parent with `VisCell` culls its untagged children. A glob import of
  `regia_render::*` can conflict with a host type named like the new `VisCells` exports. (ADR-7586, ADR-7646, ADR-7673)
- **Rendering:** `UiRenderer` backdrop target statistics fall to zero after the idle limit. (ADR-7869)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** `GiBakeDescription::sun` is gone (read `suns.first()`);
  `lights_views::{EXTRA_DIRECTIONAL_CONSUMERS, SUN_ONLY_PATHS}` changed. (ADR-7818)
- **Lightmaps, GI and lighting:** `DdgiSettings::sdf_pool_bytes` above 128 MiB clamps to 128 MiB; a very fine baked-GI
  split returns `BakedGiError::Layout`; an entity with a `DirectionalLight` or `AmbientVolume` also carries
  `InheritedVisibility`. (ADR-7456, ADR-7549)
- **World:** `CookError::GroundOverBudget` is removed: match `CookError::Checks` and read the failed
  `ground_over_budget` finding's `fits`/`tiles`. `ground_over_budget_report` takes
  `(map, mode, bytes, budget, fits, held_fine)`, and `GROUND_BUDGET_SMALL`/`LARGE` are MB, not MiB. (ADR-8436)
- **World:** A project whose `startup_level` names no file fails `regia cook` with `content_root_missing`. (ADR-8477)
- **World:** `TerrainSource::height_m`/`heights_m` return `f32::NAN` for missing samples of a descriptor source (use
  `sample_height_m`), and `TerrainSource::mapping()` reports the X spacing (prefer `count_mapping()`). (ADR-8542)
- **World:** `TerrainIndex::to_postcard` writes a v1 index in the v1 layout and refuses a mapping on it;
  `canonical_extents_bytes` appends the mapping for schema-2 files. (ADR-8471)
- **World:** `NavGrid::label` saturates at 254 on a two-byte grid (use `region_label`); `NavAgentState::set_path` and
  `clear_path` reset the stuck record. (ADR-8295, ADR-8380)
- **World:** `CookedMaterialKtx2` is `CookedMaterialKtx2<B = Vec<u8>>` and `set_material_cooked` is generic over the
  blob type. (ADR-8024)
- **World:** A host moving the grid sets the origin before it builds the collider, a `MinMaxPyramid` consumer or a
  terrain draw, and places the collider at `CollisionHeightfield::center`. (ADR-8179)
- **World:** `CollisionHeightfield::scale` is `[world_width, 1, world_depth]`; set the depth before building a collider
  or draw. (ADR-8369)
- **World:** A host building its own pipelines from `terrain.wgsl` sees `VOut` `@location(6) ref_xz`, new `HoleOut` and
  `VelHoleOut` locations, and a two-argument `in_hole`; hosts that want drawn-pass seam reports insert
  `draw.draw_passes_handle()`. (ADR-8470)
- **World:** The occluder uniform grows from 11 872 to 14 560 bytes. (ADR-7970)
- **World:** `CookedMouth::collar_triangles` also counts the approach strip; a map refused with `ground_over_budget`
  between 34 and 35 MB now cooks. (ADR-8396)
- **World:** A host standing walkers on skylight rubble through `floor_with_skylight_prop` drops that hook on the native
  path, and on-foot set-piece grids use `mover::dressing_grid`. (ADR-8323)
- **World:** A lockstep host uses the same `DetAgentSettings` and `CrowdSettings` on every peer and calls `service`, its
  movers (ascending id) and `commit_escapes` in that order each tick. (ADR-8405, ADR-8448)
- **World:** `WorldGeom::triangles` returns `Vec<GeomTriangle>` (was a slice); the sim path uses `WorldGeom::tri`.
  `regia_terrain::stream::migrate_terrain_index_file` returns `Result<Option<Vec<u8>>, String>`. A document that
  authored `ground: Nested(...)` now cooks, and a `Passages` layer must list the openings it fills in `fills` to get the
  lattice cut. (ADR-8214, ADR-8246, ADR-8619)
- **World:** Linking `regia_agent` turns on `regia_world_geom`'s `stage_b` feature through feature unification.
- **World:** A meshlayers layer with non-zero `look.detail` displacement is refused (`cook_not_built`), `subdivision: 1`
  is refused, and a layer with `blockers` now cooks. `biome.layer` lists two output pins. (ADR-7238, ADR-8175, ADR-8210)
- **World:** A host loading levels without `load_level_source` fills `ProcLevelBakeDirs`; a host opting into a world
  anchor passes the same origin to scatter queries, the collider bake and `StreamScatter`, which moves an existing map's
  rocks once. (ADR-7262, ADR-8029, ADR-8496)
- **World:** `VfxWarmReport::pipelines` counts one more pipeline per warmed format; `lit: true` mesh assets are now
  shaded. (ADR-7261)
- **World:** `apply_terrain_height_patches` takes `Commands` and queries `Option<&mut TerrainEditLayers>`: direct
  callers update the query type. Hosts restoring `LayerStrokeUndo` directly reconcile raw writes, restore the matching
  `StrokeUndo`, then recompute its dirty rectangle. (ADR-8202)
- **World:** Removed `compat::sampler::sample_floor_ext`, `TerrainSampler::{sample_floor_ext, skylight_height_at}` (use
  `shaft_at` or `compat::state::skylight_prop_top`), `SUPPORT_RISE_M`, `PORTAL_HYSTERESIS_M` and `DECK_SNAP_M`.
  `sample_floor_with_tunnels` lost `sticky`, `query_ground`'s fifth argument is the query height
  (`origin_y + BODY_REACH_M`), and `GroundSupport::floor` is the span's floor. (ADR-8167)
- **World:** A host moving its own tunnel modules onto `regia_world_geom::compat` implements `HoverGroundBody`,
  `GroundDelta`, `TunnelNavBake` and `OnFootSolids`, passes `TiltParams` and `OnFootParams`, and gives `load_tunnels`
  its `from_baked` and `for_map` builders; arm `TunnelsState::<NavMesh>::for_layout_with_nav` for the tunnel navmesh.
  (ADR-8050, ADR-8167, ADR-8215, ADR-8306)
- **World:** A live run of an unpinned `Baked` inline-placed member now matches its source level's bake; a host that
  matched `stream_index_corrupt` for a never-cooked level matches `stream_not_cooked`. (ADR-7331, ADR-8812)
- **World:** A project with `heightfield.raster.json` next to a tile set no longer cooks: delete the raster descriptor.
  Hosts matching `procgen_bake_overrides` stop seeing it. (ADR-7750, ADR-8639)
- **World:** `regia_procgen::tiles::{merge_outputs, merge_cells}` return `Result<RunOutput, RunError>`; `pgout::decode`
  and `replace_cell` can fail with `attr_type_conflict`; output roots in a layer carry an `EntityGuid` (find roots by
  `ProcOutputRoot`). (ADR-7556, ADR-7595)
- **World:** Navigation refuses invalid agent parameters (`max_slope` of pi/2 or more is an error) and
  `max_steps_per_advance > 3600` (`InvalidConfig`); `set_off_mesh_links` may keep fewer links; link indices can be
  `NAV_LINK_GONE`; `add_box` with a non-finite corner stores nothing; `find_path_streamed*` can return `Partial`.
  (ADR-7558, ADR-7679)
- **World:** A level replaced through `LevelSwitch` leaves the `LevelLibrary`; an app without the `regia` defaults adds
  `LevelSwitchPlugin`. `WorldStreaming::set_pin_wait` and `prepare_teleport` can return `WaitAreaRefused`. Games that
  relied on a save load clearing runtime children of `Saveable` entities despawn them themselves. (ADR-7601, ADR-7612,
  ADR-8027)
- **Animation and characters:** `FootPlantLeg::contact` is `Option<f32>` (a weight), was `Option<bool>`; write
  `Some(1.0)` for `Some(true)`. (ADR-8308)
- **Animation and characters:** Hip offsets written for `step_gait` (+Z forward) give the same feet in driven mode after
  negating each offset's forward component; hosts that relied on idle feet sitting at neutral read `settled`. (ADR-8474,
  ADR-8539)
- **Animation and characters:** Cooked clip poses differ from source keys within tolerance; pose-search trajectory
  channels on scaled roots read in meters; despawning a montage rig emits interrupted events. (ADR-7216, ADR-7227,
  ADR-7241)
- **Animation and characters:** `cooked_motiondb_migration_sources()` returns `[1, 2]`. (ADR-7817)
- **Animation and characters:** `regia_animation::systems::resolve_sockets` has a new parameter list (ordering against
  it is unaffected); hosts that write `SocketTargets` or `PoseRequest::socket_targets` pass rig object-space positions;
  a socket entity's local `Transform` is rig-relative.
- **Gameplay systems:** `AiCadence` is no longer `Copy`, and `AiCore::cadence()` returns `&AiCadence`. `DecisionPolicy`,
  `AiCore` and `AiDecisionRow` gain defaulted type parameters. `MatchFlow` no longer derives `Default` (the impl stays).
  `SessionSettings` is no longer `Copy`. (ADR-8560, ADR-8609)
- **Gameplay systems:** `regia_detsim::hash::Mix` is a struct with private named fields (was a tuple struct). (ADR-8547)
- **Gameplay systems:** `ArenaScenario` implements both `DetScenario` and `DetInputSim<3>`: write
  `DetScenario::step(&mut arena, tick)` where both traits are in scope. (ADR-8525)
- **Gameplay systems:** `engine_scenarios()` and `DetRegistry::with_engine_scenarios()` return four scenarios, the new
  ones listed under Added; code that asserts the exact names or count must add them. (ADR-8079)
- **Gameplay systems:** `ThreadedSimHost::start` refuses `HostPacing::Lockstep` (use `start_lockstep`); `FixedStepSim`
  gains `det_debug` and `tick_profile` with defaults. (ADR-8498, ADR-8526, ADR-8562)
- **Gameplay systems:** Out-of-domain ORCA input returns the clamped preference in every build profile (was a debug
  panic). (ADR-8377)
- **Gameplay systems:** Deprecated: `ballistic::mortar_*` wrappers (use `ArcLauncher::LEGACY_MORTAR`), the hover
  model-limit constants and `site_physics::TITAN_G` (use `HoverLimits::DEFAULT.<field>`); `-D warnings` builds must
  move. (ADR-8349, ADR-8509)
- **Gameplay systems:** `HullShapes::single` and `with` take a `ColliderShape`, not a `Vec3` half-extent, and
  `PresentationPhysicsStats` renames `hulls`/`terrain_colliders` to `hull_bodies`/`ground_colliders`. (ADR-8356)
- **Gameplay systems:** `HoverParams::REFERENCE_HULL` and the `hover::tuning` constants are gone (use
  `HoverParams::DEFAULT_HULL`, the `ClimbTuning` fields and `FlightRules::lift_accel_max`); `ClimbTuning::MEASURED` is
  `ClimbTuning::AIR_TIME`; a zero from a hover `min`/`max`/`clamp` is now always `+0`. (ADR-8164)
- **Gameplay systems:** `validate()` refuses ground-vehicle lengths under `MIN_LENGTH_M`; `SourceKind::of` returns
  `GameplayData` for `.detutility.ron`; lobbies compare `hull_handshake_bytes`, not `HoverParams::to_le_bytes`.
  (ADR-8609, ADR-8632, ADR-8638)
- **Gameplay systems:** `entity_visible_to_team` hides a stealthed enemy no detector sees (only `stealth` kinds).
  (ADR-8473)
- **Gameplay systems:** A `fog` net profile named by a project should set `linger_ticks: 0`. (ADR-8524)
- **Gameplay systems:** `regia_rules` owns the match core types; `regia_framework::rules` re-exports them, and
  `Resume.host` is deprecated and ignored (authority is the issuer). `RulesEvent::MatchOver.team` is 0 for a draw.
  (ADR-8239, ADR-8485)
- **Gameplay systems:** `from_team_directive(TeamDirective::Follow)` no longer returns `Escort { leader: 0 }`.
  `LedgerBook::rekey`/`rekey_many` skip a move onto a row outside the move; a game calling `MatchSession::assign_team`
  directly calls `retag_moved_seats` in the same step. `MatchRules` derives `PartialEq`, `Serialize`, `Deserialize` and
  `Reflect`. (ADR-8655, ADR-8657)
- **Gameplay systems:** A game moving from its own all-pairs loop uses `FloorRule::UndergroundOnly` to keep its
  checksums; `DetBroadphase::config().max_cells` reports the clamped budget. (ADR-8048)
- **Gameplay systems:** A host running a lockstep sim calls `regia_detsim::fpu::ensure_default_fp_env()` at sim-thread
  start and routes tick-path transcendentals through `regia_detsim::math`. (ADR-8013)
- **Gameplay systems:** `regia_detsim::ai::AiWorld::new(tick, &rosters, &board)` is now `AiTickWorld::new(..)`; the
  scale soak is `regia_detsim::scale_soak` (not `soak`). A lockstep game on `regia_detsim::ai` sees `+0.0` and the
  canonical NaN where it saw `-0.0` or another NaN. (ADR-8085, ADR-8573)
- **Gameplay systems:** Glob imports of `regia_detsim::ai::*`, `regia_ai::det::*`, `regia_framework::rules::*`,
  `regia_animation::foot_ik::*` and `regia_animation::gait` bring new names into scope (`Target`, `plan`, `drive`,
  `Decision`, `Stance`, `bots`, `MatchMode`, `Objective`, `Playlist` and others) that can collide with a crate's own.
  (ADR-8462, ADR-8516, ADR-8554)
- **Gameplay systems:** `InstallTier::graphics_preset` exists only with the `client` feature; server-only code reads
  `preset_max`.
- **Gameplay systems:** Snapshot bytes of states holding hash containers change order; `steer_slalom`, `slope_follow`
  and `disc_collide` fingerprints change (re-record them). (ADR-8634)
- **Gameplay systems:** Remove a left-handed conversion inside `read_snapshot` when using the bridge's handedness
  setting (never both). (ADR-8456)
- **Gameplay systems:** Lua host commands are refused until the game registers their names; the `regia` table gains
  `authority`, `snapshot` and, on the host, `command`. (ADR-8466)
- **Gameplay systems:** A game's `register` runs in the play world before the level spawns; a game that relied on play
  in editor adding `PhysicsPlugin` adds it itself. (ADR-7251)
- **Gameplay systems:** `validate_curve_refs` can newly report `curve_ref_unresolved` for refs inside containers;
  `FractureSites::Clusters` refuses a spread below 0.001. (ADR-7550)
- **Gameplay systems:** `#[derive(Script)]` needs `Reflect`. A project with `[project] startup_level` opens it when the
  game opens none at `Startup`. `Replicated` has an `on_remove` hook. Hosts counting `EffectEnded` get one per live
  effect of a despawned target. (ADR-7203, ADR-7206, ADR-7213, ADR-7216)
- **Gameplay systems:** `LogicGraph::trace()` yields `String`; `BtTask::logic_params`, `ExternalSim::event_place` and
  `ExternalSim::read_pair` are provided methods; `.logic.ron` graphs with `world:` or the new arms compile instead of
  failing with `unsupported_feature`. (ADR-7210, ADR-7268, ADR-7282, ADR-8456)
- **Gameplay systems:** `AiPlugin` adds the `PreUpdate` system `logic_graph_ref_attach` and the `LogicGraphRefLoads`
  resource; `FixedUpdate` is unchanged.
- **Gameplay systems:** A shake on the shared `CameraDirector` no longer moves `PlayerCamera` cameras; a project with
  `[project] loading_screen` gets a loading screen on every waiting `Replace` open. (ADR-7263)
- **Gameplay systems:** `regia_framework::sequencer::audio::world_velocity` is public. `run_proving` and
  `run_proving_retuned` give the same results and digests. (ADR-7745, ADR-8591)
- **Gameplay systems:** A tree that relied on a gated decorator running while its key was false now fails that branch; a
  root-to-leaf path over 256 nodes (summed across state-tree bridges) no longer compiles; `RouteAll` without
  `scan_limit` counts pre-pass units against `max_units` and can end `aborted`. (ADR-7559)
- **Gameplay systems:** `VehicleConfig::validate` refuses wheel settings above `MAX_VEHICLE_SETTING`, `step_for` returns
  `TrackError::BadInput` past `MAX_TRACK_STEPS`, and `DestructionImpact::cooling_pairs` never exceeds
  `MAX_IMPACT_COOLDOWNS`; `WhileTagged` refusals are now reported. (ADR-7431, ADR-7573)
- **Gameplay systems:** A game that creates or lists lobbies without `NetSessionPlugin` and without an identity gets
  `lobby_no_identity`. `ModFiles::from_dir` reports `dir:<canonical-path BLAKE3 hash>`; `LuaMod::save_key()` reports the
  manifest id; `save_state` can return a blob holding held entries while a live mod's line reports `lua_too_large`.
  Scratch `lua.run` spawns no longer persist. (ADR-7881, ADR-7992)
- **Gameplay systems:** Starter projects regenerate to get the sun; `SpawnRequest.prefab` takes a project-relative
  `.prefab.ron` or a registered `asset:<guid>`. (ADR-7868)
- **Networking and Steam:** `Lobbies::identity()` returns `LobbyIdentity` (read `identity_values()` for the values), and
  a lobby on a `Lobbies` with no identity is refused with `lobby_no_identity` unless the game calls
  `Lobbies::allow_unidentified(true)`. `HubServer::set_friends` and `HubLobbyBackend::set_friends` take `&[PlayerKey]`,
  and `HubRequest::SetFriends` carries `Vec<PlayerKey>`. An owner whose ticket names no server key while its relay
  registration names one gets `hub_key_mismatch`: offer the same ticket to hub and relay. (ADR-8385, ADR-8633, ADR-8665)
- **Networking and Steam:** A game that left on `LinkEvent::Ended { reason: "timeout" }` or `Disconnected { timeout }`
  now gets it up to 60 s later, after `ReconnectFailed`; `ReconnectPolicy::off()` keeps the old behavior. A `NetLink`
  server reports a timed-out player as `Dormant` and `Left` only after the grace; do not despawn on `Dormant`, or set
  `LinkOptions::resume_grace_ms: 0`. (ADR-8298, ADR-8352)
- **Networking and Steam:** `NetServer` games get a late input in the next tick's `TickInputs`
  (`server.driver_mut().set_late_merge(None)` turns it off), and client code reading `InputCommand` after
  `NetSet::Predict` sees the delivered value. `NetInput`/`NetCodec` gain provided `merge_late`, `encode_delta` and
  `decode_delta`; a codec that overrides `encode_delta` overrides `decode_delta` to match. (ADR-8217, ADR-8312)
- **Networking and Steam:** `ClientConfig::input_redundancy` defaults to 32 and `InputHistory::push` no longer clears
  the history on a gap; `lag_compensation.max_rewind_ms` defaults to 1,000 (set `250.0` in `config/net.ron` to keep the
  old value), and a host whose transport publishes no endpoint report calls `lagcomp::set_shooter_rtt` per peer.
  (ADR-8312, ADR-8315)
- **Networking and Steam:** `send_input` returns `Ok` for inputs up to 16 KiB and
  `TooLarge { max: MAX_RELIABLE_INPUT_BYTES, .. }` above; a client whose codec writes over-long fields is disconnected
  with `protocol_error`. (ADR-7892, ADR-7896)
- **Networking and Steam:** A peer over four times its per-tick poll allowance for two seconds is removed with `flood`
  (raise `ServerConfig::inbound` or set `inbound.enabled = false`), and a host that sends an undecodable message every
  tick loses that player in about 2 s; tests expecting 1,024 refusals before removal expect about 64. (ADR-8181,
  ADR-8606)
- **Networking and Steam:** A listen host adds its own seat with `add_seat` before `local_input` (now
  `lockstep_unknown_seat`), `LockstepSeat::submit_input` answers `Stamp::Skipped` while catching up, and every
  participant must use the same `missing_input` and `InputRules`. (ADR-8209, ADR-8212)
- **Networking and Steam:** `SteamSocket::send_to` returns `Err` for a refused send; `UdpTransport::send_to` returns
  `WouldBlock`, and a custom `DatagramSocket` must error for unsent datagrams. Both ends of a session must use the same
  `max_packet_bytes` and declare the same `LinkConfig::streams` table. (ADR-7981, ADR-8302, ADR-8407)
- **Networking and Steam:** `regia_net` with `default-features = false` needs `features = ["ecs"]` (on the dependency's
  declaration) to keep replication, prediction, `NetSession`, `NetLink`, `Lobbies` and the dedicated-server host; the
  `voice` feature turns on `ecs`. Without it `ValidateContext` has no `target` or `world` field. (ADR-8032, ADR-8501)
- **Networking and Steam:** `LanSocket::poll` returns `(SocketAddr, ServerAnnounce)` pairs; `ClientEnd::as_str` reports
  `ticket_refused` for a ticket denial, `LiveSteamTickets::new` registers `ValidateAuthTicketResponse` and the server
  calls `end_ticket` on leave. A session client matches `DisconnectReason::ProtocolMismatch(_)` instead of
  `TransportError("protocol_mismatch")`. `LinkShaper` exists only with debug assertions. (ADR-7212, ADR-7954, ADR-7985,
  ADR-7989, ADR-8502)
- **Networking and Steam:** `LinkStats::packets_lost` decreases when a late ACK proves delivery;
  `SendError::Backpressure` can report a full unreliable queue; oversized unreliable frames are no longer promoted to
  the ordered stream; and `NetSettings::from_ron` rejects `replication.mtu_bytes < 208` (`replication_mtu_too_small`).
  (ADR-7949, ADR-7988)
- **Networking and Steam:** `DatagramSocket::max_datagram_size(dest)` is a defaulted method that wrappers should
  forward; QUIC unreliable budgets can be smaller than `LinkConfig::max_packet_bytes`; `CookieMinter` binds the
  canonical IP. (ADR-7946)
- **Networking and Steam:** `rebinds_refused` no longer counts records from a live candidate, and `rate_state` can stay
  `Startup` up to a second longer for games that send under the start rate. (ADR-8422, ADR-8592)
- **Networking and Steam:** A game moving from the bare `SnapshotEncoder` to `ExternalReplication` sends back the
  16-byte `ExternalAck`. To name subsystems in desync reports, implement `DesyncHash` and call `record_state` instead of
  `report_hash`. (ADR-8186, ADR-8211)
- **Networking and Steam:** A relay built from sockets answers probe requests `sent` 0 until `with_probe_socket`; code
  that compared relay answers to protocol 2 sees 3. `PortMapper::name` and `HostGame::lockstep_status` are defaulted.
  (ADR-8340, ADR-8370, ADR-8558)
- **Networking and Steam:** `NetInterrupted` replicates only after `NetPlugin::replicate_interrupted_marker` on every
  process; `connection::MAX_FORWARD_JUMP` is `u64` and `MAX_ACK_RANGES` is 32; a game starting more than 240 reliable
  messages a second on one stream sets `msgs_per_s`. Benchmarks that need bursts set `rate.enabled = false`. (ADR-7995,
  ADR-8184, ADR-8301, ADR-8407)
- **Networking and Steam:** `server.toml` refuses `timeout_ms` under 1000, `interrupt_ms` outside its range and
  `start_grace_ms` over 600000 (exit 2); relay `RegisterChallenge` cookies are port-bound. (ADR-7995)
- **Networking and Steam:** `Peer::addr` changes through `Session::set_peer_addr`; `NetSession::resume` on a
  `connect_on` session (also one without a `SocketOpener`) returns `transport_error`: use `resume_on`; a live token
  takes its slot over. (ADR-7998, ADR-8180, ADR-8299)
- **Networking and Steam:** `UdpTransport::bind` and `from_socket` set buffers and DSCP EF (`from_socket` overwrites
  caller options). (ADR-7998)
- **Networking and Steam:** A late `DecodedSnapshot` keeps its own `seq` with the unchanged `current()`; feed packets as
  they arrive, and call `reset()` on a known reconnect. (ADR-7983)
- **Networking and Steam:** Forced records and join spawns obey the byte budget, entities over 16 KiB despawn on
  clients, and spectators must not call `NetClient::send_input`. (ADR-7984, ADR-8419)
- **Networking and Steam:** `ServerSettings` and `HostNetRates` rates are `Option<u32>`; `HostGame` gains defaulted
  `net_rates_in_use`, `host_game_port_relayed`, `add_game_port_relay_ip` and `publish_relay`. Relayed hosts raise
  `max_peers_per_ip` or mark the relay with `add_relay_ip`. (ADR-7213, ADR-7982, ADR-8183)
- **Networking and Steam:** Tests asserting spec 73 § 13 test 9 ("a 40-tick lead resets") expect one correction.
  (ADR-8219)
- **Networking and Steam:** A custom `SessionServer` driver calls `set_travel_keep` before sending `TravelBegin`;
  `Steam::open_overlay` validates page names and `SteamBackend::open_overlay` has a default. (ADR-7259)
- **Networking and Steam:** `ServerConfig::default().pre_proof_answers_per_s` is 4,096 (was 512), split per source
  prefix; `EndpointTotals::pre_proof_shed` also counts requests over a prefix's share. (ADR-8718)
- **Networking and Steam:** `NetNode::peer_malformed_points` reports weighted points (16 times larger) that leak over
  time. (ADR-8666)
- **Networking and Steam:** Under `DelayPolicy::Shared` a seat on a bursty downlink now gets about one input in a
  hundred late instead of raising every seat's delay; use `DelayPolicy::PerSeat` for such a match. Code that names
  `crates/regia_net/src/lockstep/server.rs` must use `crates/regia_net/src/lockstep_server.rs`. (ADR-8656)
- **Networking and Steam:** Games that send a join snapshot on `Channel::Stream(StreamId::BULK)` should move it to
  `StreamId::REPLICATION`. The stall deadline before the first round-trip sample can be up to 4 s. (ADR-8624, ADR-8658)
- **Networking and Steam:** Server vehicle seats free themselves when the occupant is despawned, and a peer whose
  streaming source has no finite position receives no cell members; routed `SoundCue`s of placed events play spatially
  and are refused past 50 m unless the route sets `max_distance`. (ADR-7549, ADR-8790, ADR-8791)
- **Platforms:** Custom menu-free Windows hosts opt in to bare-Alt suppression with `alt_menu::install`.
- **Platforms:** A game whose `AgentPlugin` sees a `DevLaunch` resource with an agent transport binds that port and
  token instead of a random port and new token. (ADR-8472)
- **Platforms:** When both `REGIA_GPU_PCI` and `REGIA_GPU_UUID` are set, the PCI pin wins (also under
  `REGIA_GPU_STRICT=1`); a short UUID prefix fails with an unreadable neighbor. `headless_bcn_device()` returns a clone
  of one process-wide device. A device from `regia_xess::Runtime::request_device` enables
  `VK_EXT_zero_initialize_device_memory` where offered. (ADR-7613, ADR-7820, ADR-7958, ADR-7991)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `content::project_registry` returns `Result<AssetRegistry>`.
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** Material size refusal uses `content_lint_limit` (JSON default 16 MiB) instead
  of `gltf_material_invalid`; hosts that relied on the old default repair pass `--profile compatibility`. (ADR-8226,
  ADR-8250)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** Hygiene `AuditError::report` is `Option<Box<Report>>`; `0` and `u64::MAX` are
  configuration errors for every byte limit; with secret scanning on, a file or single shipping entry over
  `max_memory_bytes` (32 MiB) fails `unbounded_regex`, and so does a larger shipping AppImage. (ADR-8244, ADR-8348)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** Hygiene `Finding::path` is scrubbed display text; use `location_id` for
  correlation. (ADR-8229)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** Shipping packages reject secret signatures by default and need a valid
  `[package_hygiene]` policy (limits 1 GiB per file, 64 GiB total); `[package] stage_allow` fails unlisted files.
  Symlink pipeline-cache inputs and compressed ZIPs are refused. `collect_pipeline_caches` returns source descriptors.
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `default-features = false` on `regia_asset_pipeline` removes `.fbx`, USD and
  `.svg` import, the Vorbis transcode and `https://` caches, and the functions that name those crates' types. (ADR-7972)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** A bad `[cook.overlay_roots]` table or a malformed `[content.diet]`
  (`diet_invalid`) fails the cook before anything is cooked. (ADR-8155, ADR-8461)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `regia_detsim::math` gains `sin_f64` and `cos_f64`;
  `regia_configurator::resolve::BUILD_CAVEAT_INCOMPLETE` is public. (ADR-8333)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `regia_editor::data_asset::DataAssetFile` serializes
  `DataAsset(... type: ...)`. (ADR-7310)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** Text-log parsers must accept the `t=<tick>` line prefix. Callers that relied
  on log triage ignoring `data.frame_ms` or bare `summary.json` now see those inputs. (ADR-8442)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** Typed loads pin; move to counted lifetime with `load::<CpuMesh>(id)` and
  `keep_cpu_data` where CPU data is read after upload. (ADR-7256)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `AssetServer::open` errors on a damaged content directory, but succeeds with a
  wrong-length or unreadable listed pak in `refused_chunks()`; a same-length changed pak still fails the open. Code that
  relied on an older pak serving an asset whose newer copy fails now gets a load error. (ADR-8714, ADR-8810)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** Shared glTF textures follow chunk selectors by image path; audit chunk rules
  after recooking, and use `shared = "duplicate"` where each claimant needs a copy. (ADR-8143)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** Never mount two texture tier directories together (asset ids match by design).
  (ADR-8059)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `known_fields("gltf")` ends with `clips`, and `known_fields("fbx")` no longer
  lists it.
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `ReplayFile::parse` and `replay.load` accept original v4 replays; malformed v4
  files report their parse error. (ADR-8191)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `regia_agent::call_local` takes a fourth parameter `client: Option<&str>` and
  `console::run_line` a third. (ADR-7796)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** An Android project over the default pack caps fails to package.
  `AssetEvent<T>` is an ECS `Message`. `regia_audio::plugin::audio_sync` takes a `VoiceAnchorQuery` parameter.
  (ADR-7202, ADR-7236)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** A project that relied on the built-in lowercase path check adds a `PathStyle`
  rule to `config/validation.ron`. Replace source symlinks before an engine release and regenerate Steam VDFs after
  moving a distribution tree. A project whose cook leaves chunk-assigned assets uncooked fails with
  `chunk_asset_unwritten` unless `REGIA_ALLOW_UNWRITTEN_ASSETS=1`. (ADR-7612, ADR-7871, ADR-7961)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `BootConfig::load(root)` falls back to `root/build_info.json`. The `regia_cli`
  ship-check helpers take `version: Option<&str>` (binary-internal). `xtask::dx12_shaders` `COMPOSED_LAYOUT_FAILURES`
  and `PLAIN_LAYOUT_FAILURES` are empty arrays. (ADR-7587, ADR-7879, ADR-7893)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** Gameplay no longer sees mouse presses that start on UI when `UiPlugin` is
  present (a mouse press on UI no longer reaches gameplay bindings); opt out with `PointerClaims::set_enabled(false)`.
  `evaluate_actions` and `evaluate_actions_unless_stepped` take `Option<Res<PointerClaims>>`. (ADR-7976)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** Insert `Draw2dSettings { dirty_regions: false, ..Default::default() }` before
  `Draw2dPlugin` for the direct path; `Draw2dGpuStats::bytes_uploaded` counts only dirty tiles. (ADR-8514)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** Hosts that delayed take audio until `resume_output` can spawn it during the
  take; `spawn_patch` of an unknown bus while suspended returns `Err(AudioError::UnknownBus)`. Drop host patch
  recreation on output resume, retry `resume_output` when it returns `false`, and use `start_persistent_loop` for loops
  that must survive suspension; render-out mixdowns end preview patches and loops already playing. (ADR-8052)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `LoudnessMeter::new(6, ..)` returns a 5.1 meter; `VoiceRelay::route` callers
  without `voice_server_system` call `take_rejected` each tick. `regia_ui::inspect::VISUAL_PROPERTIES` is `[&str; 22]`.
  (ADR-7070, ADR-7208, ADR-7227, ADR-8438)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `AudioStallWatchdog::observe` takes a `delta: Duration`; `sync_font_locale`
  takes two more parameters; `TOKEN_SLOTS` is `[TokenSlot; 11]`; theme `height`, `padding_x` and `gap` move layout.
  (ADR-7543, ADR-9340)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** In right-to-left text Ctrl+Right moves toward the start of the text.
  (ADR-9340)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `sequencer::edit::timeline::ripple_span` returns the edge's old place,
  `ripple_shift` refuses keys in the removed span with `seq_ripple_keys_in_span` and moves sections;
  `CutEdit::Move { ripple: true }` accepts a time past the second shot's end; `ClipTrack::keys` may hold section
  channels. `seq_timeline::paste_at` returns `Vec<MarkRef>`, and `seq_tracks::PLAY_STEP_MAX` is removed.
  `ShotPlayer::loads()` does not exist: use `load_count()`. (ADR-7662, ADR-7847, ADR-8640)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** A crate depending on `regia_editor` with `default-features = false` builds
  without the screen-reader bridge; a crate that relied on `drive_accesskit_adapter` through the editor turns on
  `regia_platform/accesskit` itself. (ADR-7746, ADR-7795)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `AudioSnapshot::{bus_volumes, filter_cutoffs}` are `BTreeMap` (were
  `HashMap`); direct callers of `interaction::ui_interaction` and `dragdrop::ui_drag_drop` supply the disabled-subtree
  query; `UiPlugin` installs the event history; a document whose authored class on a button, slider or menu bar relied
  on the widget class being last shows the authored look.
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `AudioPlugin` inserts `AudioStallWatchdog` (insert
  `AudioStallWatchdog { enabled: false, .. }` to turn it off); `GlyphAtlases::get_or_insert` can return `None` inside a
  `begin_frame`/`end_frame` bracket; `PlayerCore::reposition` in `Seeking` pushes one `VideoEvent::Seeked`;
  `SphereSpectra::rates()` lists tables in build order. (ADR-7461, ADR-8830, ADR-8832)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** Code that counted `Looped` events to track wraps reads `count` or
  `PlayerCore::loops_done`. Reverb IRs at a rate other than the mix rate play at the corrected level. (ADR-7563,
  ADR-7602)
- **Core, licence and security:** `SessionOptions::tail_bytes` above 8 MiB publishes at most 8 MiB, and parsers of the
  unclean ring line must accept the new keys. (ADR-8494)
- **Core, licence and security:** A tool parsing `regia::session` accepts the Ship shape (`adapter` as
  `{class, vendor, backend}`, whole-GB `ram_gb` and `vram_gb`). (ADR-8417)
- **Core, licence and security:** Crash and log collectors match `X-Regia-Report-Name` and reassemble outbox parts by
  their `<start>-<end>` range (`X-Ferrite-Report-Name` is still sent in v0.6.0); `DENOISER_KEY`, `EXPORT_FORMAT` and
  `STAMP_FORMAT` changed value, so a host predicting input hashes hashes `INPUT_HASH_TAG`; scripts take
  `/tmp/regia-gpu-<n>.lock` before the old lock. (ADR-8402, ADR-8670)
- **Core, licence and security:** `regia_core::profiling::system_name` returns `Option<&'static str>`, `system_count()`
  counts names past the cap, and `systems_over_cap()` is new; a host that names its own compute-pool threads uses the
  `regia-compute` prefix for play systems to stay under the flame context. (ADR-7409, ADR-7427)
- **Tests, docs and tooling:** A book tutorial page that uses `# until` on a mutating call fails its test;
  `regia_editor::agent_parity::scan_sites` returns `glyph_button` and `bare_checkbox` sites. (ADR-7451, ADR-7452)
- **Rendering:** a host that read `COLOR_0` from `CookedModelLodPart::vertices` gets white now; read the stream with
  `AssetServer::load_mesh` instead (ADR-8785)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `regia_ui::interaction::sync_pointer_gate` takes one more optional system
  parameter (`Option<Res<PointerClaims>>`); hosts that add it as a system are unaffected. Gameplay mouse bindings now
  see a world press that moves over UI (ADR-7976's documented behavior); a host that wants the old hover gate turns
  claims off with `PointerClaims::set_enabled(false)` (ADR-8786)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** projects with secret scanning on (the default) and any shipped file over
  32 MiB must raise `[package_hygiene] max_memory_bytes` (ADR-8244), as the arena sample now does (ADR-8786)

### Agent protocol

- **Editor:** New `editor.content_chunks_preview`, `editor.content_chunks_unshipped`; `editor.content_chunks_set_rule`
  takes `content` and `entry`; `editor.content_chunks_state` adds `unshipped_next_cursor`, `roots`, `roots_total` and
  `root_coverage`. Codes `content_chunks_entry_unmatched`, `content_chunks_no_entry`, `content_chunks_preview_stale`,
  `content_chunks_preview_failed`. (ADR-8477, ADR-8543)
- **Editor:** New `editor.package_hygiene_get`, `_set`, `_audit`, `_audit_status` and `_audit_cancel` with `hygiene_*`
  `data.reason` values; guide topic `package-hygiene`. `editor.project_settings_set` takes `package_hygiene` ceilings.
  (ADR-8244, ADR-8399)
- **Editor:** New `asset.content_lint_start`, `_cancel`, `_status`, `_read`, `_thresholds`, `_fix_preview`, `_apply_fix`
  (returns `content_lint_preview_only`), `asset.content_lint_rules` and `asset.content_lint_policy` (capability
  `content_lint`). (ADR-8248, ADR-8391)
- **Editor:** `editor.set_viewport` takes and reports `ai_bt`, `ai_state_tree`, `ai_goap`, `ai_squad`, `ai_formation`,
  `ai_move_to`, `ai_blackboard`, `lockstep` and `fog`, with matching `viewport.show_*` actions, and takes `navigation`
  and `post_volumes`.
- **Editor:** New `editor.terrain_source_start`, `_status`, `_cancel`, `_apply`, `_reimport` (events kind
  `terrain_source`), `editor.terrain_overview_start` and `_status`; `editor.retarget_goal_set`;
  `editor.anim_add_node`/`anim_set_node` accept the four new node kinds. (ADR-8476, ADR-8541, ADR-8596)
- **Editor:** `editor.launch` adds `pending` and `launch_bad_app_id` and no longer answers `launch_timeout`;
  `editor.open_level` can answer `prefab_open`; `editor.vehicle_tuning_open { create: true }` on an existing file
  answers `vehicle_tuning_exists`; `editor.project_physics_edit` and its `physics_*` codes are gone (use
  `editor.physics_layers_edit`: `collides` becomes `set_collides`, `queries` becomes `set_query`, `on` required).
- **Editor:** `editor.fracture_panel_set` takes one site choice per call; `editor.logic_edit` takes `control`;
  `editor.cancel_task` takes `import`; `editor.import_settings_apply` can answer `asset_settings_invalid` and
  `tools_busy`. (ADR-8587)
- **Editor:** `editor.a11y_tree` names `Label` and `labelled_by` nodes, so `editor.a11y_action { name }` may answer
  `ambiguous_name`; `editor.a11y_contrast` returns 83 rows. (ADR-8585, ADR-8587)
- **Editor:** New `editor.tab_overflow`; `editor.open_asset` opens kinds `motion_database` and `biome`; `profiler.frame`
  takes `since_frame` and `entities` (1-200) and may answer `frame_times`, `newest_frame`, `entities`, `systems`,
  `systems_over_cap` (errors `bad_since_frame`, `bad_entities`); `profiler.timeline` takes `frames` (1-8). (ADR-7409,
  ADR-7849, ADR-7902, ADR-8631, ADR-9740)
- **Editor:** New methods `editor.actions`, `editor.prefab_override`, `editor.prefab_apply_override`,
  `editor.video_boot`, `editor.scatter_class`, `editor.view_state`, `editor.autosave_state`, `editor.autosave_now`,
  `editor.recovery_list`, `editor.recovery_restore`, `editor.recovery_discard`, `editor.cancel_task`,
  `editor.vehicle_tuning_ride`, `editor.extension_ui`, `editor.extension_action`, `editor.extension_asset_tool`,
  `editor.extension_panel`, `editor.fracture_test_hit`, `editor.fracture_test_reset`, `editor.fracture_bonds`,
  `editor.fracture_place`, `editor.panel_timings`, `editor.outliner_rows`, `editor.a11y_contrast`,
  `editor.project_icon_info`. (ADR-7263, ADR-7275, ADR-7306, ADR-7307, ADR-8630, ADR-9750, ADR-9770, ADR-9810, ADR-9850)
- **Editor:** New methods `editor.data_asset_types`, `editor.data_asset_open`, `editor.data_asset_set`,
  `editor.data_asset_save`, `editor.project_localization_edit`, `editor.project_packaging_edit`, `editor.script_wizard`,
  `editor.keep_simulation_changes`, `editor.cancel_job`, `editor.view_preset`, `editor.camera_ease`,
  `editor.set_pivot_mode`, `editor.set_snap`, `editor.gizmo_drag`, `editor.menu_invoke`, `editor.outliner_set`,
  `editor.logic_*` (five), `editor.physics_layers_get`, `editor.physics_layers_edit`, `editor.prefab_break_link`,
  `editor.layer_delete`, `editor.plant_preview`, `editor.procgen_widgets`, `editor.procgen_badges`,
  `editor.procgen_promote`, `editor.procgen_biome_overlay`. (ADR-7203, ADR-7204, ADR-7205, ADR-7215, ADR-7224, ADR-7233,
  ADR-7234, ADR-7238, ADR-7239)
- **Editor:** New methods `editor.profiler_freeze`, `editor.shot_panel_open`, `editor.shot_panel_save`,
  `editor.shot_panel_adopt`, `editor.import_settings_{open, edit, apply, revert, state}`, `editor.launch`,
  `editor.launch_status`, `editor.launch_stop`, `editor.launch_devices`, `editor.menu_list`, `editor.package_start`,
  `editor.package_status`, `editor.clear_caches`, `editor.rebuild_asset_db`, `editor.about`, `editor.help_open`,
  `editor.project_icon`, `editor.open_code_editor`, `editor.create_prefab`, `editor.create_entity`,
  `editor.bake_captures`, `editor.bake_captures_status`, `editor.nav_preview`, `editor.select_by_asset`,
  `editor.matgraph_find`, `editor.asset_field_choices`, `editor.remove_component`, `editor.layer_create`,
  `editor.layer_bind`, `editor.layer_initial` and `editor.break_placed`.
- **Editor:** Every `editor.*` method named under Added is new, with its error codes in the book: across layouts and
  workspaces, panels and OS windows, keymap, preferences and help, search, history and transactions, viewport, Outliner,
  Inspector, levels and prefabs, import, content, revision control, notes, collaboration, remote views, play, graph
  editors, UI designer, theme, localization, profiler, World Map, utility panels and samples. Clients with closed method
  or key lists must allow them; `cargo xtask agent-snapshot --update` and `cargo xtask manual` regenerate the snapshot
  and manual.
- **Editor:** `editor.play` takes `preset` / `size` and answers `window` and `resolution`; `editor.play_state` gains
  `play_window` (with `players` and `panes`) and reports `time_scale`, `muted`, `start` and `device`; errors
  `play_resolution_invalid`, `play_resolution_needs_window`, `play_window_unavailable` ("no window system"). (ADR-7272,
  ADR-7283)
- **Editor:** Additive params and fields: `editor.save_all { cancel }`, `editor.screenshot { close }`,
  `editor.plant_preview { lit }`, `editor.audio_mixer_send { tap }`, `editor.create_prefab` `retargeted`,
  `editor.panel_timings` `preview_jobs`, `editor.import_settings_state` `invalid` / `can_apply`,
  `editor.fracture_panel_get/_set` sites and supports, `editor.procgen_biome_overlay` `draped_cells`. (ADR-7275,
  ADR-8583, ADR-8584, ADR-8590, ADR-8593, ADR-8594, ADR-8628, ADR-9750)
- **Editor:** Answers gain fields: `editor.bake_captures_status` (`changed_capture`, `viewport`),
  `editor.abilities_panel_asset` (`asset.comments_warning`, `comments_dropped`, `undo_depth`, `redo_depth`),
  `editor.autosave_state` (`level_snapshot`), `editor.procgen_badges` (tier fields, `tier.neutral`), `editor.mm_db_*`
  (`live`, `cursor`, `issues[].span`), `editor.seq_preview` (sound, video and `media` fields, `animators`, `sounds`,
  `videos`, `voices`), `editor.ui_designer_state` (`selected`, `shared`, `mixed`), `editor.launch_status` (`agent`, a
  string or null; `pushed`, `push_failed`, `push_error`), `editor.frame_capture` (`pass_copies`, `skipped`, `request`),
  `editor.layout_get` (`panel_regions`, `pixels_per_point`, `focused_widget`), `editor.actions` (`note`), and
  `editor.edit_material`, `editor.xr_settings`, `editor.project_settings_get`, `editor.anim_preview`.
- **Editor:** New result fields: `editor.seq_curve_state` `path`/`dirty`, `editor.prefab_mode` `dirty`,
  `editor.shot_panel_state` `path`/`dirty`/`host`, `editor.seq_timeline_state` event rows, `ripple` and per-row
  `sections`, `editor.data_asset_open` `discarded`, `editor.abilities_panel_get` `effect_assets`,
  `editor.package_status` `staging`/`published`, `editor.trace_timeline` items `count`.
- **Editor:** `editor.open_code_editor` needs `build` in `.regia/agent.toml` and counts against the mutating rate limit.
  `editor.a11y_action` needs `review` and `build` (otherwise `F0012`), and `editor.a11y_tree { keep_on }` is
  `editor.a11y_keep_tree { on }` (`keep_on_moved`). `abilities.add_attributes` reads unloaded sets under `assets/`.
  (ADR-7795)
- **Editor:** `editor.set_import_settings` can answer `tools_busy` and `import_settings_apply_running`; the three
  import-settings and nav-preview methods add `job`, `building`, `applying` and take `async`. (ADR-7664)
- **Editor:** `editor.move_asset` succeeds on referenced assets (no `asset_move_referenced`); File ▸ Save all's
  `editor.menu_list` route is `editor.save_all { dialog: true }`; `editor.menu_invoke "File/Save all"` adds `saved`,
  `failed`, `skipped`; `editor.content_favorites` is mutating with the `assets` permission
  (`editor.content_favorites_get` reads). (ADR-7798, ADR-7822)
- **Editor:** `editor.profiler_timeline { frame }` outside the history fails `profiler_no_timeline` without holding the
  tab; `profiler.timeline` with no recorded frame answers `timeline_off`. (ADR-7849)
- **Editor:** `editor.create_asset` accepts the new kinds (`material_graph`, `material_function`, `material_instance`
  with `parent`, `sequence`, `shot_list`, `attribute_set`, `effect`, `ability`, `procgen_graph`, `vfx_system`,
  `conversation`, `curve_table`, `chooser`, `data_table`, `biome`, `camera`, `montage`, `cloth`, `soft_body`,
  `logic_graph`, `motion_database`, `smart_object`, `tilemap`); `editor.create_entity` accepts eight new kinds;
  `editor.problems_panel`, `editor.reference_graph_panel` and `editor.size_map_panel` take `folder`.
- **Editor:** New hosted-agent entry point `regia_agent::process_hosted_request` and `contract::HOSTED_AGENT_CONTRACT`;
  child adapter protocol 1 uses `REGIA_AGENT_LINE_PROTOCOL=1`, `REGIA_TOOL_ENDPOINT`, `REGIA_TOOL_TOKEN` and
  `REGIA_TOOL_ID`. (ADR-7844)
- **Editor:** Permission changes: `editor.outliner_columns` and `editor.viewport_overlays` need `edit_scene`; read-only
  agents use `editor.outliner_view`.
- **Editor:** Parameter changes: `editor.set_property` takes `expr` and `value` is optional with it;
  `editor.keymap_bind` takes `scope`; `editor.prefs_reset` takes `category`; `editor.prefs_panel` takes
  `tab: "project"`; `editor.project_settings_set` takes `reset_keys` or `reset_category` and saves into the undo stack;
  `editor.content_index` takes `complete`; `editor.hierarchy` refuses an invalid `filter` query with `F0002`
  (`data.query_error`, `data.column`; quote literal text).
- **Editor:** Result changes: `editor.keymap_get` reports `scope`, `scopes`, `active_preset`; `editor.prefs_get` rows
  add `changed`; project settings replies add `settings`; `editor.set_viewport` `layout` reads `side_by_side`, `stacked`
  or `one_large_two`; `editor.capture_viewport` `info` adds `view_mode`, `render_size`, `screen_percentage`,
  `exposure_ev`; `editor.problems_panel` answers groups, `logic_graph` and `graph_errors`; `editor.vfx_state`
  `bounds.kind` is `particle_extents`; `editor.frame_capture` pass rows' `draws` hold draw indices; `editor.save_all`
  answers `queued` and `shell_results`; `editor.recent_projects` adds `entries` and `tags`.
- **Editor:** `editor.import_with` accepts `kind: "mesh"` and `kind: "material"` items on glTF/GLB (FBX and OBJ still
  answer `import_item_unsupported`); `editor.select` in `"set"` mode keeps every id; a duplicate is named "<name> 2", so
  scripts use the ids `editor.duplicate` returns.
- **Editor:** `editor.agent_sessions` takes `action: "staged"` with `approve` and `items` (at most 32) and snapshots
  list `staged`; child protocol v1 gains the stdin line `{"kind":"staged_decision",...}` (children ignoring unknown
  kinds are unaffected).
- **Editor:** New parameters: `editor.profiler_timeline` `frame`/`frames`, `editor.seq_cut_edit` `ripple`,
  `editor.seq_section_edit` op `sub_binding`, `editor.ui_designer_edit` `transform` `delta`, `editor.procgen_param`
  `remove`, `editor.data_asset_open` `discard`, `editor.procgen_table` `cell`, `editor.view_asset` `overlay`,
  `editor.abilities_panel_asset` `field`/`value`, `editor.abilities_panel_call` ops `add_attributes`/`apply_effect`,
  `editor.cue_edit` caption and filter, and the new `editor.tables_panel_chooser_edit` ops.
- **Editor:** `editor.package_start` needs `confirm: true` unless `dry_run`, and its `out` and `editor.project_icon`'s
  `path` are request paths (`path_outside_project`); `editor.help_open`, `editor.about`, `editor.profiler_timeline` and
  `editor.nav_preview` are declared mutating. (ADR-7409, ADR-7496, ADR-7630)
- **Editor:** `editor.save_level` reasons `prefab_in_place`, `prefab_open`, `locked_by_other`;
  `editor.recent_projects_edit` reason `recent_meta_unreadable`; new error code families `remote_*`,
  `live_link_no_session`, `pie_view_*`, `play_start_*`, `play_device_*`, `session_staged_*`, `utility_panel_*`,
  `profiler_bad_*`, `sample_*`, `edit_layer_*`, `ui_designer_bad_variant`, `ui_designer_bad_when`,
  `ui_designer_variant_active`, `ui_designer_variant_locked`, `ui_designer_no_override`, `seq_track_locked`,
  `vfx_editor_empty_bounds`, `view_is_read_only`, `no_sequence`, `unique_name_taken`, `play_time_scale_out_of_range`,
  `not_playing`, `play_frame_no_source`, `ui_copy_value`, `import_material_not_found`, `eyedropper_*`,
  `frame_draw_index`, `capture_no_play_window`, `graph_error_missing`, `loc_panel_compile_failed`, `tables_panel_*`,
  `terrain_region_invalid`, `kit_paint_invalid` and `mode2d_*` (all in `errors.explain` where listed).
- **Editor:** New error codes: `profiler_bad_frame`, `seq_new_name_empty`, `seq_marker_name_empty`,
  `seq_section_overlap`, `seq_ripple_keys_in_span`, `seq_binding_*`, `shot_panel_{unsaved, bad_path, no_project, io,
  bad_file, detached, no_player}`, `import_settings_{unsupported, not_open, unknown_field}`, `overlay_unknown`,
  `overlay_not_mesh`, `uv_set_missing`, `no_collision`, `collision_not_cooked`, `launch_*`, `package_out_unwritable`,
  `tools_busy`, `prefab_create_*`, `create_unknown_kind`, `bake_level_changed`, `nav_preview_*`, `asset_field_unknown`,
  `asset_field_no_filter`, `abilities_panel_{parse, no_field, read_only}`, `data_asset_bad_discard`, `procgen_bad_cell`,
  `procgen_unknown_cell`, `fracture_panel_bad_impact`, `fracture_panel_bad_clusters`, `open_in_editor_no_code_editor`,
  `montage_panel_no_slots`, `bad_layer_name`, `layer_exists`, `bad_defs_path`; `data_asset_no_default` is retired (match

- **Rendering:** New method `render.draw_list`; `render.get_exposure_settings` and `render.set_exposure_settings` carry

- **Rendering:** New methods `render.scene_ready`, `render.scene_hold`, `render.set_scene_hold`, `app.pacing`,
  `app.set_pacing_state`, `render.dynres`, `render.load_budget`, `render.set_load_budget`, `render.set_host_budget`,

  `dynamic_gi_no_renderer` and the `viscells_*` set. (ADR-7200, ADR-7586, ADR-7870, ADR-7883, ADR-7884, ADR-7889,
  ADR-7890, ADR-7891)
- **Rendering:** Results gain fields: `render.pipeline_warm` (`prepare`, `targets`, `recent_pipelines`),

  thresholds), `telemetry.get_last_summary`, `quality.get_detected` (probe display fields,
  `this_launch.reprobe_pending`, `step_down`, `configuration`), `assets.texture_memory`, `render.get_upscalers`

- **World:** `vfx.describe` `render.mesh`, `mesh_status`, `indices`, `lit`, `mesh_draws`; `procgen.list` / `procgen.get`
  `bake` and `bake_levels`; new methods `gen.texture`, `gen.heightfield`, `terrain.apply_operator`,
  `terrain.scatter_colliders`; `terrain.get` `map.grid_origin`, `map.world_depth` and `map.sample_spacing_z`;
  `level.status` `loading_screen`. (ADR-7210, ADR-7261, ADR-7262, ADR-7263, ADR-7280, ADR-7287, ADR-8029, ADR-8063,
  ADR-8369, ADR-8465)
- **World:** `terrain.mesh_layers` result arrays are renamed (`layers` → `layer_reports`, `mouths` → `mouth_reports`,
  `sections` → `section_rows`, `portals` → `portal_rows`, `blockers` → `blocker_rows`), `chunks` is a count, the blocker
  generation is `geom_generation`, and an unknown parameter is `F0002`. `terrain.bake_report` rows use `passed` (was
  `pass`). `PROTOCOL_VERSION` stays "0.2". (ADR-8627)
- **World:** `procgen.verify` returns `findings`; `render.get_exposure` returns `zones` (`null` without
  `BakedZoneList`). (ADR-7331, ADR-8384)
- **World:** New methods `level.switch` and `level.switch_status` (`level_switch_bad_params`); `terrain.patch_heights`
  takes `heights_mm` and replies `edits_this_frame` (`edit_too_large`, `edit_frame_full`, `edit_out_of_range`);
  `stream.pin` can return `stream_wait_area_refused` (`F0002`); `nav.link_traversals` reports a gone link as `null`;
  `pattern_too_large` and `attr_type_conflict` are run codes; `vfx.set_param` refuses loader-refused `count_scale`,
  `drag` and `alpha_scale` with `value_out_of_range`. (ADR-7560, ADR-7585, ADR-7595, ADR-7612, ADR-7679, ADR-8027)
- **Animation and characters:** `anim.clip_report` clips gain `contact_curves` and the report adds `node_animation`;

  `editor.retarget_preview` takes `goals`.

- **Gameplay systems:** New `sim.tick_profile`, `sim.det_debug_draw`, `sim.host_snapshot` and `sim.host_restore` (debug

  adds `source`, `raw`, `partial_rows` and raw and partial lines. (ADR-8498, ADR-8526, ADR-8547, ADR-8562)

- **Gameplay systems:** `fog.status` adds `view`, `net`, detector, query and ghost counters, and answers on a server

- **Networking and Steam:** New `server.rotation_get` and `server.rotation_skip` (code `server_no_rotation`).

- **Networking and Steam:** New `net.lockstep`, `net.rollback`, `net.resume`, `net.relay_status` (`relay_bad_params`,

- **Networking and Steam:** `net.state_channel` adds five client keys; `fuzz.targets` lists `net.snapshot_delta`,

- **Networking and Steam:** New method `net.socket_buffers` (`socket_buffers_bad_params`, `socket_buffers_bind`);

- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** New error codes include `package_hygiene_*`, `parity_*`, `logs_perf_gate_*`,

  `patch_unknown`, `patch_not_one_shot`; `play.inject_input` takes `scancode`; `seeing.screenshot` takes `camera` and

  ADR-7819, ADR-8553)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** Wrong-typed `arc`, `tolerance_m` and `world.low_g` on the `detsim.*`

- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** New method `diagnostics.report_sources`. Agent request paths no longer allow
