  adds `configuration`. (ADR-7461, ADR-7543, ADR-7602, ADR-7893, ADR-8052, ADR-8830, ADR-8832, ADR-9340)
- **Core, licence and security:** `telemetry.get_last_summary` can report `level: "ship"`; `agent.cook`,
  `agent.package`, `agent.build_game` and `agent.run_tests` dry runs answer the project root as `cwd`.
- **Core, licence and security:** New method `report.outbox_status` (error `outbox_unavailable`). (ADR-8402)
- **Core, licence and security:** `profiler.timeline` (new, with `frames`), `profiler.set_capture { timeline }` (`flame`
  optional), `profiler.capture { timeline? }` with `timeline_spans` and `timeline_truncated`, `profiler.frame`
  `timeline_enabled`, `systems` and `systems_over_cap`. (ADR-7325, ADR-7409)
- **Tests, docs and tooling:** Regenerate `cargo xtask manual`, `cargo xtask agent-snapshot --update`,
  `cargo xtask api-snapshot --update` and the editor agent-methods snapshot (`REGIA_UPDATE_EDITOR_METHODS=1`) after
  these changes.
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `photo.capture` adds error `path_outside_project`: a `directory` outside the
  project, the temp directory, `output_roots` and the session pictures folder is refused, and a relative one lands
  under the project root (add the folder to `output_roots` in `.regia/agent.toml`, or omit `directory`). A DCC
  session that closes after a tick's ingest shows `closing` in `dcc_feed.status` for one more tick. (ADR-8795)

### CLI, features and dependencies

- **Editor:** `regia_editor` depends on `regia_detsim` (`serde` feature) and `regia_nav`, and ships the brand fonts
  under SIL OFL 1.1; `regia_asset_pipeline` depends on `regia_platform` on Windows. (ADR-7468, ADR-8504, ADR-8585,
  ADR-8630)
- **Editor:** The editor binary takes `--safe-mode` and `--normal-mode`; xtask's `LaunchSpec` has a `normal_mode` field.
- **Rendering:** `regia_matgraph` depends on the workspace's `naga` with `wgsl-in`. No other new dependency.
- **World:** `regia terrain check-layers`, `source-inspect`, `source-import` and `source-overview`.
- **World:** `regia_nav` depends on `regia_world_geom` and gains the `stage_b` feature; `regia_terrain` and
  `regia_asset_pipeline` depend on `regia_world_geom`; `regia_agent` enables `regia_world_geom/stage_b`. (ADR-8291,
  ADR-8323, ADR-8398, ADR-8486)
- **World:** `regia_world_geom` is a new crate depending on `regia_detsim`, `ron`, `postcard`, `blake3` and `thiserror`,
  on the `cargo xtask det-scan` lockstep closure, with the features `fixtures` and `stage_b` (both off), `stats` and
  `allocation-tests`. `regia terrain passages import` (takes `--meshlayers`, `--heightfield`, `--world-width-m`,
  `--height-scale-m` and `--height-offset-m`) and `export`; `regia procgen verify` prints `anchored_generator_missing:`
  lines. (ADR-8050, ADR-8112, ADR-8214, ADR-8432)
- **Gameplay systems:** New crates `regia_detsim` and `regia_rules`. `regia_detsim` depends on `libm =0.2.16`, `serde`,
  `ron`, `tracing`, `libc` and `windows-sys` (`serde`, `ron`, `libc` and `windows-sys` are existing workspace
  dependencies), has an optional `serde` feature (hover derives), the dev-only feature `fp-test-hooks` and a `glam`
  dev-dependency for tests, and gains the `ai`, `avoid`, `broadphase`, `hash` and `ids` modules. `regia_framework`,
  `regia_physics`, `regia_ai`, `regia_nav` and `regia_test_utils` depend on it; `regia_ai` re-exports the core as
  `regia_ai::det` and has the feature `debug_draw`. (ADR-8012, ADR-8013)
- **Gameplay systems:** New `regia` feature `logic` (`regia::logic_bridge`), forwarded by `regia_dylib`. (ADR-7268,
  ADR-8716)
- **Gameplay systems:** `regia_framework` opt-in feature `visual-log`.
- **Networking and Steam:** `regia package --server` output adds three systemd unit files and `tier.ron`.
- **Networking and Steam:** `regia_net` has a default-on `ecs` feature and depends on `regia_detsim`; it uses the
  existing workspace `libc` on Unix and enables the `windows-sys` feature `Win32_Networking_WinSock` on Windows (no new
  crate). `regia_net_soak` has a default `ecs` feature (a dependent with `default-features = false` loses `timed`). New
  `regia net desync-replay <bundle> [--json]` (exit 0 replayed, 1 refused). (ADR-7998, ADR-8032, ADR-8455, ADR-8558)
- **Networking and Steam:** `--relay local` opens 64 allocation ports; dedicated-server console command `relay`.
  (ADR-7982)
- **Platforms:** Windows builds enable the windows-sys feature `Win32_System_Diagnostics_ToolHelp` (no new crate).
  (ADR-7548)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `regia assets lint-geometry`;
  `regia assets validate --target --report --cancel-file`; `regia assets lint-materials --profile
  standard|compatibility`; `regia package --audit-artifact` and `--verify-receipt`; `regia contact-sheet`;
  `[package_hygiene]` keys for rules, memory ceilings, `max_container_depth` and `strict_containers`.
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `regia_agent` depends on `regia_detsim`; `regia_editor` and `regia_terrain`
  use the workspace `blake3`; `regia_cli` adds a direct `flate2` edge. No new crates.io packages.
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `REGIA_ASSET_TRACE` environment variable (set before launch; restart to change
  it).
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** New `regia assets ids export|import`, `regia cook --ids FILE`,
  `regia assets contract-check` and `regia terrain overview-bake`; `regia cook --json` adds `overlay_fill`;
  `regia assets chunks --json` adds `content` and `unshipped`; `REGIA_COOK_OVERLAY_ROOTS` replaces the overlay list on
  one machine.
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `regia_asset_pipeline` features `http_cache`, `fbx_import`, `usd_import`,
  `svg_import` and `audio_transcode` (default on); `rcgen` and `rustls` become dev-dependencies. (ADR-7972)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** New `regia logs triage` (ZIP64, multi-disk and encrypted archives are
  refused), `cargo xtask check-net-core`, `[[module]]` rows for `cargo xtask det-scan`, and `cargo xtask net-soak`
  `--two-host`, `--two-host-seats`, `--two-host-ticks`, `--two-host-port`, `--plan scale`, `--plan scale-gate` and
  `--wire udp`; `lockstep_bot` takes `--bind`, `--mix` and `--seed`. `regia_cli::package_hygiene` re-exports
  `PathScrub`, `StageRules`, `EffectivePolicy`, `Rule` and `definition_digest`. (ADR-8427, ADR-8442, ADR-8501)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** New commands `regia play-gate` (`check`, `replay`, `run`),
  `regia logs perf-gate`, `regia package parity` and `regia terrain cook`; new flags `regia package --audit-dir`,
  `--pipeline-cache-reference`, `--json` on `regia package`, `regia run` and `regia cook`, and `--map-id` on
  `regia terrain passages import`. (ADR-7217, ADR-8175, ADR-8403, ADR-8452)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** New `regia engine chunk` subcommand (payload `Box<ChunkCommand>`); chunk files
  need the `zstd` program. (ADR-8404)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `regia prepare --scene`, `--project`, `--target`;
  `regia package --prepare-scene`; `regia cook --texture-tier NAME=MAX_EDGE_PX`. (ADR-8059, ADR-8453)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `regia upgrade-project --backup <dir>`; `regia procgen bake --dry-run`
  (`prune_orphans` is replaced by `plan_prune` and `PrunePlan`); `regia terrain cook --json` adds `viscells`,
  `vis_cells`, `vis_portals`. (ADR-7743, ADR-7928, ADR-8371)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** New xtask commands and flags: `check-formats --since-tag`, `editor-smoke`,
  `editor-monkey`; `xtask::do27_ports` gains `HEADERLESS_DATA`, `NOTICE_SIDECAR`, `headerless_data`. (ADR-7724,
  ADR-7748, ADR-7865, ADR-8497)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** New `regia assets validate --staged`, `regia hooks install|uninstall|status`
  (`--force`), `regia table to-json` and `regia table from-json`.
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `regia test --timeout-s`; `regia upgrade-project` no longer walks directories
  named `build` or `dist` (rename any that hold frozen-format content). (ADR-7878)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `.cargo/config.toml` `[env]` sets per-target `CXXFLAGS_<target>`
  (`-ffp-contract=off`, astcenc invariant); projects built outside this workspace copy that `[env]` block to cook the
  same bytes. (ADR-7878)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** New environment switches `REGIA_REQUIRE_ADAPTER` (also
  `FERRITE_REQUIRE_ADAPTER`), `REGIA_ALLOW_UNWRITTEN_ASSETS`, `REGIA_PACKAGE_XESS` with `XESS_SDK` and
  `REGIA_XESS_HEAP_ZEROING`; `cargo xtask demo-package --xess-sdk <SDK>` (`demo_package::Args::xess_sdk`). (ADR-7612,
  ADR-7613, ADR-7820)
- **Tools, pipeline and agent protocol:** `regia bake-lightmaps --gpu --gpu-fallback` accepts a CPU checkpoint.
  (ADR-5786)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `regia_quick` depends on `bytemuck` and `cosmic-text` (workspace). (ADR-8458)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `regia_editor` default feature `accesskit`; new `regia_platform` feature
  `accesskit-adapters` (`accesskit` = adapters plus the runner's primary-window adapter). No new dependency. (ADR-7746,
  ADR-7795)
- **Audio, media, UI and accessibility:** `kira` is a path dependency on `vendor/kira` (0.12.5 plus the restart patch);
  `regia_agent` gains `cosmic-text` as a dev-dependency. (ADR-8830)
- **Core, licence and security:** `REGIA_TELEMETRY=ship`. (ADR-8417)
- **Core, licence and security:** `regia_core` has an opt-in `devtools` feature (keep it off for shipping builds);
  `regia_dylib` has a `logic` feature.
- **Core, licence and security:** New crate `regia_gen` (`regia_agent` depends on it). New in-workspace edges:
  `regia_audio` → `regia_world_geom`, `regia_asset` → `serde_json`, `regia_editor` → `regia_physics`,
  `regia_asset_pipeline` → `regia_net`. No new external crate. (ADR-7232, ADR-7233, ADR-7240, ADR-8416)
- **Core, licence and security:** `regia_agent` depends directly on `regia_crash`, and `regia_crash` on `regia_core`
  (both already did transitively). (ADR-8402, ADR-8716)
- **Tests, docs and tooling:** New `cargo xtask det-scan` (with `--external`, `--list-rules`) and
  `cargo xtask det-golden`; a crate on `docs/ci/lockstep-closure.toml` copies `crates/regia_detsim/clippy.toml`; code
  copied from the lead game needs a `[[do27]]` row, a `do27-owner-code` provenance row and the file header. (ADR-8012,
  ADR-8081, ADR-8085, ADR-8192)
- **Tests, docs and tooling:** `cargo xtask net-soak` (`--plan gate|nightly|scale|scale-gate`, `--wire virtual|udp`,
  `--list`, `--bars`; `REGIA_NET_SOAK_GATE`, `REGIA_NET_SOAK_WIRE`); `regia_net_soak` is a new dev-only crate in the
  test-only layer and `regia_test_utils` dev-depends on it. (ADR-7899, ADR-8030, ADR-8454)
- **Tests, docs and tooling:** `cargo xtask rename-brand --check` and `cargo xtask editor-agent-coverage [--update]`.
  (ADR-7307, ADR-8670)
- **Tests, docs and tooling:** Gate operators pass the registry's GPU solos to the merge gate's GPU branch (`--solo
  regia_render:emissive_hitch:test --solo arena:spawn_hitch:test`, plus `regia_render:regia_render:lib` and
  `arena:repro_check:test`). (ADR-8598, ADR-8599)
- **Tests, docs and tooling:** A workspace test build (`cargo test --workspace --no-run`) is skipped and marked FAILED
  with under 500 GB free on the target filesystem; set `REGIA_PREFLIGHT_ALLOW_WORKSPACE_TEST_BUILD=1` to run it anyway.
  (ADR-8441)
- **Tests, docs and tooling:** The showcase sample adds `wgpu` and the `net_game` sample adds `regia_input` as
  dev-dependencies (already in their graphs). (ADR-8122)

## Platform evidence

### Passes of the v0.6.0 gate candidate

The platform passes ran on the freeze candidate (`a03439ea2`, or `691dd2b9c`, which it contains; both report the engine
string 0.5.0 because the version bump is this release commit). Open failures are under **Known issues**.

- **Steam Deck** (`a03439ea2`, ADR-8782): every row passes. Showcase at the `Deck` preset holds its 30, 40, 45, 60 and
  90 fps caps for 5 minutes each (p99 33.6 / 25.4 / 22.5 / 16.9 / 12.5 ms, no hitches, GPU time within 0.2 ms of
  v0.5.0-rc6); render-out frame hashes are byte-identical to rc6; the arena smoke exits 0; `vfx_lab --deck-check` holds;
  the forest demo holds 30/40/45/60 on every view from cold caches (the 90 cap reaches 67-75 fps, known since v0.4.7).
  Window screenshots raise the process's resident memory by about 10 MB each until it levels off (glibc keeps the freed
  frame copy; not a leak; buffer reuse is the planned fix).
- **Android, TEST Pixel 9 Pro** (`a03439ea2`): `cargo xtask android-pass` 3 of 3 PASS with 27 of 27 budget rows (50
  lifecycle cycles and 10 rotations, a 10-minute Mobile High soak at 16.66 ms p50 with no thermal throttling, cold
  first frame median 2.06-2.08 s against 5 s), pacing Auto/Off, the arena sample, the native crash record and
  `cargo ndk -t arm64-v8a check -p regia` pass. Against v0.5.0 on the same phone, PSS is 19 MB higher (native heap +14 MB,
  code +5 MB; GPU memory identical) and the first start after install is 0.36 s slower at the median.
- **Windows** (`691dd2b9c`): release builds pass on the Intel N150 min-spec laptop and the RTX a test machine with no Code
  Integrity blocks. N150: adapters, the Deck check on Vulkan and DX12, `vfx_lab` quits 5 of 5, showcase Mobile and
  Desktop Low and the editor row 3 of 3 pass; one showcase quit in five did not answer. RTX 3080 Ti: editor quit 3 of 3
  with exit code 0, but about 10 s each (see Known issues); `cargo xtask windows-pass` `test platform` passes.
- **Linux GPU sweep** (`691dd2b9c`, ADR-8768): player sweep 9 of 9 and the crash row pass on the RTX 5090, Intel Arc Pro
  B65, RTX 5080 and RX 7900 XTX. The gate's GPU subset (15 packages, goldens skipped) passes in full on the RTX 5090
  (15,395 tests) and the RTX 5080 (12,492); the Arc has its known sampler-change red (1 of 15,404) and the RX 7900 the
  RADV visibility family (5 tests) plus two editor hot-reload tests the sweep's no-build guard refused. Lead-game plates
  against the RTX 5090 reference: RTX 5080 55.0 / 57.5 / 61.4 dB, a second RTX 5090 54.6 / 59.9 / 56.7 dB; the `intel_arc` and
  `amd_rdna3` plate floors pass. The GTX 1080 host was not reachable.
- **macOS (MacBook Air M5)** (`a03439ea2`, testing only, never gating): the release build passes (one new rustc
  warning, in the vendored audio backend on macOS only); live rows pass (agent hello, render-out, editor start in 2.2 s
  cold and 0.3 s warm, quit in 0.2-0.3 s); `editor-smoke --headless` matches the Linux RTX 5090 run failure for failure
  (the two editor-smoke known issues, no new failure). With the display asleep, window rows hang (see Known issues).

  the Windows min-spec laptop (N150) and the Mac (M5); no gain at 300 units; the Steam Deck was not reachable
  (ADR-8518)

  Pro cost numbers are owed (ADR-8517)
- Roster tick-chain pins match natively on Windows (MSVC, debug and release) (ADR-8484)

- The test Pixel 9 Pro reproduced `match_rules_ctf_koth_3600` as `b41b84ff4f19669b`, equal to x86-64 Linux; the Mac,
  Windows and Deck legs were not reached
- The clustered flow-field rebuild takes 1.7 s at 4096 x 4096 on the Windows min-spec laptop (ADR-8294)

  were not measured (ADR-8523)

  target; device timings are unavailable

- `input_mtu::large_samples_survive_ten_percent_loss` passes on x86-64 and aarch64 Linux (both failed with an identical

- Headless editor split-screen screenshot failure reproduces on lavapipe and an RTX 5090; editor-smoke level hashes are

## Known issues

Source: the v0.6.0 gate candidate's platform passes and gate-red reports (Deck, Windows, Linux GPU sweep, net-soak gate
plan), the integration lines' own Known issues, and the fragments' Known issues that no later fragment fixes. Each item
names its ADR; "v0.6.1" or "v0.7" says where the fix is planned.

### Release blockers carried as known issues

- **Net-soak gate plan: five gating bars stay red** (`cargo xtask net-soak --plan gate --release`; ADR-8718). Each has
  a measured cause and none is a regression of v0.6.0's own merges except the last; no bar, scenario or threshold was
  changed. Fixes are planned for v0.6.1 or v0.7:
  - `cgnat/10` and `cgnat/32` `reconnect_within_grace`: nine clients share one IPv4 address, and the per-address cap
    (`max_peers_per_ip` 8, ADR-3283) refuses a rejoining client once another client of the same address took its slot.
    This conflicts with spec 120 § 1 ("nine players behind one CGNAT address keep playing") and needs an owner
    decision on the cap policy.
  - `asym-bufferbloat/8` and `/32` `reconnect_within_grace`: a fresh join is three round trips, and over the profile's
    1 s downstream queue that is more than the 3,000 ms grace (3,104-3,376 ms measured). The planned fix is a shorter
    rejoin handshake (an address validation token), a wire change.
  - `congested/8/listen` `no_wedged_client`: the listen server's shared uplink reads the host queue as the median of its
    peers' queue delays, so congested clients' own downlinks hold the aggregate rate down and the 16 KiB join snapshot
    takes 3-7 s (a regression since the streams line, ADR-8490, ADR-8624). A measured fix (the minimum over peers)
    passes the bar but breaks the 64-client listen-uplink test, so it was not shipped.
  The forged-handshake rows of the same plan are fixed in this release (pre-proof answers shared per source prefix,
  ADR-8718).
- **Windows editor: first frame about 10 s and quit about 10 s** (RTX a test machine, release `regia-editor.exe`; v0.5.0
  quit in 0.2-0.5 s). The exit thumbnail (ADR-8190) renders a 256x144 game-look frame through a separate screenshot
  renderer, which compiles every pipeline again at exit, and the editor's own wgpu device does not request
  `Features::PIPELINE_CACHE` or install the pipeline cache, so neither the viewport nor the thumbnail reuses compiled
  pipelines within a run or across launches. The thumbnail is also written for a folder that is not a Regia project.
  Quit still exits with code 0 (3/3). Fix planned for v0.6.1.
- **Windows N150: one showcase quit in five did not answer** (no agent answer in 30 s, not exited after 150 s; runs 2 to
  5 passed). Not root-caused.
- **Android, TEST Pixel 9 Pro: the first start after install takes over 5 s** (5.6-5.8 s in 3 of 3; 0.36 s slower at the
  median than v0.5.0, which takes about 5 s on the same phone and Android build). Later cold starts meet the 5 s budget (2.06-2.08 s median).
- **macOS: a window can deadlock at present while it is unfocused or the display is asleep** (MacBook Air M5, 3 of 6
  window launches with the console locked): the render worker's per-frame `Window::pre_present_notify` call waits on the
  main thread, which waits on the render schedule. A fix that skips the call on macOS and iOS is verified on the Mac
  (9 of 9 launches answer and quit) and is planned for v0.6.1 (ADR-8170 path). macOS is a testing-only platform.
- **`cargo xtask perf-budgets --counts-only --enforce` fails two rows on Linux** (the 128-thread build host): `net_relevance_n214_allocs`
  measures 38,677 allocations per send tick against a 37,000 budget, and `net_relevance_n214_allocs_p99` measures 46,387
  against 38,000. The integration base gives the same counts exactly, so the v0.6.0 merges did not cause this. The
  budgets come from a MacBook M5 run (ADR-8031: 35,392 / 36,180); they need a Linux measurement or per-host budgets
  from the net-relevance line.
- **GPU sweep: two card-only reds** (not gate cards; ADR-8768):
  - RX 7900 XTX (RADV): the visibility-vs-forward test family (5 tests: 4 in the `regia_render` lib, 1 in
    `visbuffer_masked`). The RADV mip-bias offset (0.25, ADR-5670) reaches the forward path as `textureSampleBias` and
    the visibility resolve as `textureSampleGrad` with scaled gradients, and the driver's anisotropic filter treats the
    two differently. The proposed fix (the forward path also uses scaled gradients when the frame bias is not 0) moves
    every RADV and TSR frame and needs a look check on the 7900 and the Deck.
  - Arc Pro B65 (Mesa ANV): the sampler-change one-level red (the temporal clip box's residual on a rounding boundary,
    ADR-7689; options in ADR-7689 and ADR-8768) and the `planet_earth_noon` plate floor (35.47 against 35.6), both
    known since v0.5.0.
- **Chain IK: two review findings are proven and deferred to v0.6.1** (ADR-8732; the proof tests are `#[ignore]`d in
  `crates/regia_animation/tests/chain_ik_nodes_review_c.rs` and lose the ignore with the fix):
  - `finding_sixty_five_chain_nodes_stay_allocation_free_when_warm`: the rig's IK-node cache holds 64 chains and is
    cleared when full, so a rig with 65 or more chain IK nodes (or 64 plus stale entries after graph reloads) never
    warms and allocates every frame.
  - `finding_retargeted_body_aim_axis_follows_the_target_rigs_bone_roll`: `retarget_graph` keeps a `BodyAim` axis and a
    hinge limit's axis in the source joint's local frame, so a target rig with another bone roll aims a different
    anatomical direction (needs a decision).
  The third deferred finding (a work bound for the CCD solver) changes no format either.
- **Tunnels (W-11): the native query core misses its Deck and host performance bars** (open W-11 work; no
  Deck-specific cause). On the Steam Deck: the 1,000 mixed query calls tick p50 1.65 ms against 0.5 ms; the `column`
  support median 1,090-2,160 ns against 150 ns, and the native/compat ratio 1.85-3.07 against 0.25 on all four
  fixtures; `query_index_build_switchbacks` p50 78.7 ms against 50 ms; `ground_edit_crater_6m` p50 0.48 ms against
  0.2 ms. Allocations per mixed tick (0) and resident sim geometry (34.0 MB of 35 MB) hold. Other hosts miss the same
  bars (Pixel 0.94 ms, the Linux build host 1.65-2.13 ms, N150 3.75 ms mixed tick).

### Editor

#### Play in editor and launch

- The separate play window's real present path and window glue (window creation, Wayland app id, cursor grab and lock,
  sRGB blit, present, surface loss) are exercised only by hand; raw mouse motion is not scaled to the play resolution
  (ADR-7283, ADR-7466)
- A fully covered editor window still steps play at about 10 fps through the play window (the 100 ms `EDITOR_STALL`
  fallback) (ADR-7762)
- A runtime-joined player's controller is never stood apart in PIE, and PIE-joined controllers stay put when a game
  camera appears later (ADR-7466)
- After a split-screen play session in the headless editor, every later `seeing.screenshot` of the offscreen viewport is
  one flat color (lavapipe and RTX 5090); the windowed editor is not affected.
- Create ▸ Decal and the first Play / Simulate frame still build renderers on the UI thread; `editor.matgraph_preview`
  renders inside its request (ADR-8584)
- Play: a Game tab forwards no gamepad input and plays no sound, and a process with a renderer pays a window read-back
  per picture; log lines carry no network-role prefix; the virtual pad holds one button at a time; view switch, speed,
  mute and device settings are not saved.
- Remote views: an enum is a choice only for types the editor registers (a data-carrying variant needs
  `editor.remote_set`), asset paths have no picker, and the live link does not mirror reparenting.
- Play in editor with a project generated by `regia new --template third_person` and its built dylib was not checked by
  hand
- Launch: a Stop during the cook lets the cook finish in the background; the Windows kill path, Windows package cancel
  and an on-device Android launch have not been run on hardware (ADR-8980, ADR-7496)
- Package and Clear caches need a `regia` binary (`package_spawn_failed`) (ADR-7496)
- Unused-feature suggestions for Android launches are deferred. (ADR-7843)
- Samples: an installed editor with no engine checkout lists only the templates, a copied repo sample builds only inside
  the engine checkout, the `quick` template is not offered (use `regia new --template quick`), and `editor.save_all`
  from an agent saves no thumbnail.

#### Viewport, Outliner and Inspector

- Viewport: Move camera to selection ignores a selected camera's field of view; Move selection to camera moves the
  primary selection only; orthographic panes keep their fixed depth slab; quality and show flags are per viewport, not
  per pane; splines have no icon filter.
- Gizmo: a scale in Parent or View space runs along the entity's own axes, a multi-selection with different parents
  moves by one delta in the active entity's parent space, and the pivot offset ignores individual origins.
- Selection: the hover highlight shows only while the pane reads its id buffer (grid or selection outline on); a
  selection root without its own mesh has an empty selection outline; a prefab's drop ghost is a 1 m box.
- With a pane focused in the four-pane layout, keys stay with the focused pane while the pointer is over another;
  collider handles, procedural path and area tools and 2D mode were not checked for the gizmo-widget swallow (ADR-8870,
  ADR-7593)
- Screenshots past the device's texture limit are refused (`screenshot_needs_tiles`): the still-tile renderer API is not
  yet wired to `editor.screenshot` or `editor.capture_viewport`. A 16384 x 16384 capture needs several GB of GPU memory.
  Pilot, look-through and the camera preview act on the main viewport only.
- Only the main perspective camera is kept per level; a skeleton-only plant node has no lit thumbnail; a biome cell over
  a terrain hole drapes on the cut surface (ADR-7275)
- Show ▸ Lockstep skips text labels, and contacts and queries appear from the tick after the switch turns on (ADR-8562)
- No Create entry for a water plane or fog volume (`create_unknown_kind`). (ADR-7741)
- Reflection capture handles have no image test, a capture moved after a bake shows no reflection in the editor until
  the next bake, and `editor.capture_handles { enabled }` switches the handle tool although declared read-only.
  (ADR-7663, ADR-7699)
- The reflection capture bake's byte equality with `regia bake-captures` is tested with a deterministic stand-in, not
  GPU-rendered maps (ADR-7592)
- Outliner: property search terms re-run only after undo-stack edits; folders hold top-level entities only and an empty
  folder lasts for the session; the row context menu opens with a right click only; the creation-order sort is not kept
  across a save and reload; a script from a mod outside `mods/` has no known file; a prefab instance's Revision cell
  shows the open level's state.
- An Outliner copy showing the play world keeps its columns in the play world's view state; a second Inspector shares
  the first's property search; Content Browser copies share the view mode and type filters.
- Inspector context menus open from a pointer only (keyboard: the focused label's shortcuts and agent methods);
  `@EditCondition` has no `||` or parentheses; fields of Lua script and extension-drawn components are not filtered or
  pinnable; pinned Inspector windows float inside the editor window.
- No engine curve or gradient type is reflected yet; Make unique always writes a file (no inline asset value); a
  component action's undo covers only the component it ran on; drag-down across stacked number fields is not built.
- `StaticDecal.receiver_mask` stays a number (its bits are decal receiver classes), and no light component has a layer
  mask yet.
- The color eyedropper reads the main editor window only; a pick over a floated OS window reads the main window's pixel.
- An inline-edited document is re-read from disk only when expanded again with no unsaved edits, and a data asset's
  autosave is not restored inline.
- The inspector's runtime text fields are not locked while a translation previews; a JSON number beyond 64 bits reads
  back through a 64-bit float.
- A variable-height list's undrawn rows sit at their estimated height, so the scroll bar can shift; a component written
  with change detection bypassed shows its old value until the selection changes.

#### Prefabs and levels

- Create prefab does not rewrite guid references held in other files, and scene files store entity fields as raw ids
  (ADR-8593)
- An Apply on an override of a prefab nested inside a variant instance is refused (ADR-7263)
- Prefab Overrides lists added and removed components and children but cannot apply or revert them; there is no separate
  Prefab menu.
- A capture in a prefab nesting the same prefab twice keeps a shared id, and a copied variant instance renumbers only
  captures an override sets (ADR-7716)
- Undoing a Break re-roots non-member entities whose parent guid is not refilled (ADR-7614)
- Levels: a level opened beside the host opens at its own coordinates; a redo of a create after the active level changed
  lands in the new active level; `level.convert` does not fold layer files in (merge first); the context terrain is
  drawn at full brightness during Edit in place.
- A base level edited on disk does not hot-reload its variants; variant marks refresh at most every 15 frames; an
  autosave recovery copy of a variant is a plain resolved level.
- A project template that places other levels does not load them; the New level menu label has no ellipsis.
- `editor.placed_create` still calls the record-only `check_placement`, so its count can be lower than what the fill
  refuses.

#### Content Browser and import

- The Content Browser's first plain-word search reads every `.meta` on the UI thread, and engine content folders are not
  listed. (ADR-7798, ADR-7759)
- An audio patch has no panel (`asset_kind_unknown`); `.smartobject.ron` does not open on double click; a retarget or
  move does not rewrite the open level. (ADR-7725, ADR-7822)
- `.camera.ron` and `.smartobject.ron` have no `asset_open` row (use `editor.camera_panel_load` and
  `editor.smart_object_def_edit`); the Extensions panel labels its license row "Licence"
- A level switch from a Content Browser click that the Save all dialog confirms later does not re-check a running game
  or prefab edit (File ▸ Open behaves the same).
- Packages are folders (no zip), and winit 0.30 reports no drop position and delivers no file drops on Wayland.
  (ADR-7788)
- The status bar does not show the Asset Jobs count. (ADR-7788)
- The import dialog shows FBX and OBJ meshes and materials read-only (their settings refuse unknown keys), previews an
  FBX or OBJ without textures whose paths are relative to the source, and runs one extra import for Extract; the
  generated read-only prefab of an imported model is not built (ADR-7904, ADR-8376).
- Import settings have no per-field reset; the collision overlay reads only the host platform's dev cache (ADR-7469)
- Un-skipping a mesh that kept an older id through a rename gives it its derived id again.
- A created terrain has no asset-database row until the next project cook, and an untitled level builds it only once
  saved. (ADR-7727)
- The closure preview fingerprint walks `assets/` on the editor thread twice per preview, and the UI preview uses the
  host platform's cooked cache (ADR-8543)
- The asset preview scene's `level` lighting reads only the sun, and the material graph preview does not use the shared
  scene yet. (ADR-7771)

#### Shell, saving and undo

- Sequences, prefab edit mode and the UI animation editor have no document autosave; a VFX autosave from before this
  release restores without the disabled-module stash (ADR-9770)
- Trailer and aircraft documents of the Vehicle Tuning panel have no autosave provider, and the incremental level
  autosave still walks every entity on the main thread.
- With `panic = "abort"` a panic in an autosave write still ends the editor
- Undo: older commands may omit touched details; transaction abort does not roll back external effects such as saves; a
  Material slider drag is one entry per changed frame; undoing an entry for a theme draft, UI animation library or UI
  document that is no longer open changes nothing; an entry joins the history at the next request boundary or frame; a
  file opened with `discard: true` over unsaved edits gets them back when an older entry is undone.
- Save all: shell rows saved through `editor.save_all` are written on the next frame and only while the editor window
  runs.
- The Version History window shows a version written by another process only after it is reopened (ADR-8435)
- Version history backups are offered on the command line only, and a document whose editor keeps no dirty flag keeps no
  versions. (ADR-7928)
- Graph diffs draw animation state machine sub-canvases on the root plane, binary assets answer `unsupported_asset`, and
  a file missing from the revision is refused (`not_in_revision`). (ADR-7799)
- Each asset editor's own Save button does not check LFS locks; the save guard uses the lock list read at the first edit
  or last Refresh; locking talks to the LFS server on the editor's main thread.
- Revision control: a commit refused by validation has no link to the Problems panel; push, pull and fetch are not in
  the editor.
- Universal search: palette entries for menu items that open a file dialog or ask for confirmation say to use the menu;
  committing an asset opens levels, prefabs and data assets and reveals other types in the Content Browser; the preview
  shows no thumbnail; the selection history is a palette window, not an Inspector-header dropdown.
- The Laptop keymap preset moves no numpad view keys; project identity, platform targets and extension manifest keys
  have no documented defaults and are not reset; a Markdown-only manual opens the page without the anchor.
- A workspace's tab overflow list is the top of the `+` popup; user workspaces get no palette command; a hidden panel's
  tab returns beside a neighbor tab; workspace mode overrides and layout edits are session-only and outside the undo
  history.
- OS windows: no hover highlight in the target window during a drag; on Wayland saved positions are not restored and the
  lost-monitor re-dock does not apply; a content drop onto a 3D viewport floating in its own window does not place the
  asset; a tab cannot be dragged from one OS window onto another's dock.
- Quick settings has no default play mode entry; project console variables are an editor-side registry that game code
  cannot register into, and any caller allowed `editor.console_run` can set them.
- Project Settings does not show `max_container_depth` or `strict_containers`, and the editor hygiene audit reports a
  `receipt` of `absent` (a staging audit is not a final-artifact receipt) (ADR-8348, ADR-8399)
- Project Health toolchain rows need the `regia` CLI on `PATH`, next to the editor or in `REGIA_CLI`.
- Windows uses the current UTC offset for every activity row; Bake GI and Lightmaps failure lines keep their code
  (ADR-8586)
- The egui key paths have no automated input test (ADR-7204)

#### Graph editors

- Material graph: an instance's parent must be a graph (no instance-of-instance chains); graph static switches do not
  depend on the tier; animation graph compile errors are not in Problems; a parked function tab is checked against the
  graph it was opened from; Open does not pan the canvas to the node.
- The material graph editor draws reroute wires straight, node swatches are not drawn in a function tab, an error inside
  a shared expansion shows on the first call only, and a function tab with a never-saved calling graph previews its
  first output. (ADR-9700, ADR-7730)
- A rerouted wire cannot be right-clicked or dragged off its input pin, and a reroute point cannot be started from a
  plain wire on the canvas (the graph widget has no wire hook).
- A Code node has one output, and a WGSL `loop` that never ends is stopped only by the GPU driver's watchdog.
- Logic Graph: entity variables take no type or default; breakpoints apply in development builds only; F9 acts without
  the panel holding focus; usages miss events emitted from code and keys built at run time; Problems lists only the open
  logic graph's issues.
- The Logic Graph canvas edits conditions as RON text, warns rather than errs on unknown tasks without a registry, and
  was not drawn on a GPU in its tests (ADR-7224)
- The logic graph's focused row draws no focus ring on the canvas node (ADR-8628)
- The procedural graph's dropped-wire menu and palette are not on the toolkit's fuzzy search.
- The panel budget of the procedural graph canvas is an allocation count (8 000 per steady frame), not a measured time
  (ADR-3480)
- Real mouse use of the new procgen widgets is untested (ADR-7215, ADR-7239)

#### Asset and tool panels

- Trailers and aircraft have no live Apply, and a bike or hover hull has no Apply to a play session; the hover form
  edits `params` only; rides are on flat ground only, and the proving ground is flat apart from its tracks. (ADR-8630,
  ADR-9760, ADR-7745)
- Only the last physics layer can be removed (middle layers can only be renamed; there is no layer index remap), and
  colliders keep old groups until rebuilt after a new table (ADR-7267, ADR-7233)
- RON comments are lost on save in biome, camera, motion database, smart object and ability forms (each warns first; the
  ability form drops them at its first typed edit) (ADR-7472)
- The ability form edits existing values only (list entries and data-carrying enum variants go through **Advanced**)
  (ADR-7472)
- The ability asset editor's empty list of a type with no zero value cannot be grown from the form; montage slot edits
  made with `anim.montage_slot_edit` directly are not in the panel's journal; the warp `tint` has no control. (ADR-7731)
- The chooser add-column box offers parameterless kinds only; the montage slot table edits groups and masks by add only
  (ADR-7605)
- Animation recording and live writes need the Anim Graph panel open, a replayed pose drives the preview only with the
  preview rig's joint count, and onion skins and the root-motion floor are drawn as lines. (ADR-7800)
- The retarget map cannot yet be written into a BVH file's import settings, baked clips go to a chosen folder rather
  than the cook cache, and the panel previews with 2D stick figures.
- The camera rig editor has no node canvas, and a duplicated rig's own transitions still name the original. (ADR-7734)
- A host's `SequencePlayer::set_shots` with another list while that list is adopted counts as an edit; the edit-world
  preview plays pitch-preserving sections by varispeed without Doppler or occlusion and skips fullscreen, mesh and UI
  video targets (ADR-8940)
- A Smooth-eased scalar camera track can bend locally past a trim; the timeline has been tested with headless input only
  (ADR-8640)
- UI designer: Shift-to-pivot is not built (pivot is numeric rows); box-model bands omit percentage and auto spacing;
  group, paste and make-component edit the base while a variant is selected; a variant's `when` bounds are edited
  through `editor.ui_designer_variant add` or the file; variants of a component document do not apply to its instances;
  `nav` names do not resolve inside the preview's headless app; Copy to document has no destination for generated repeat
  children or a text input's current value (`ui_copy_value`), and live bindings may replace a transient edit.
- Box support rules are not stored in the `.meta`, and the test break shows no debris motion (ADR-9750)
- The Terrain Overview panel shows state and mapping as text, not the image; the terrain reimport record lives in the
  editor state directory, not version control (ADR-8596, ADR-8541)
- Tile map binding edits renumber tilesets from GID 1, and a tile object whose tileset is removed keeps GID 0.
  (ADR-7742)
- World Map: a cell's members are those of the last cook, cells are shaded by recorded height, and unloading cells
  clears the undo history.
- Bulk Edit grid edits of prefab instances do not record overrides. (ADR-7787)

#### Profiler and debugging

- Profiler: spans of the render, audio and loader threads are not attributed to play; the first poll of a process source
  reads its whole retained history; Entities rows are per component type, not per archetype. (ADR-9740, ADR-7849)
- Profiler: buffers show as plain allocation rows; process and remote sources have no archetype rows, modules, monitors
  or marks; a GPU preview reads the whole mip back; audio has no mix-time metric; timeline marks sit at frame start;
  typed asset loads report 0 ms upload.
- GPU pass timings of play-world pictures still show under the `edit` profiler source; the Content Browser shows an icon
  for `.pgraph.ron` (ADR-8594)
- The Timeline tab's held frame from a process source leaves the remote history after about 300 frames (`bad_frame`
  until Live); a full timeline history holds about 118 MB; a strip boundary can pass through a merged bar at low zoom
  (ADR-7325, ADR-7409)
- The Frame Debugger captures the main viewport only, records no draw identities, copies no pass-internal resources, and
  passes that share a record site have one image. (ADR-7860)
- Frame Debugger: draws are not replayed one at a time (`draw_replay_unsupported`); shadow, visibility-buffer,
  subsurface, transparency, terrain, sky and extension draws are not recorded one by one; out-of-process launches and
  viewport instances are not capture sources.
- Visual Log recordings are in memory, and the captured-overlay rasterizer omits text labels.
- `editor-smoke --headless` still reports the File/New level first-screenshot timeout and the Create/Shape/Cuboid and
  Create/Player start undo fingerprint pair.

#### Accessibility and themes

- Still mouse-only in the editor: the shot curve, the 2D tile palette, box select, tangent handles and section trims.
  Spoken list indexes are 0-based. (ADR-7383, ADR-7396)
- egui_dock tab title and close areas reach UI Automation as about 146 unnamed elements, and the Inspector's Transform
  drag values as unnamed spinners (ADR-8587, ADR-9810, ADR-7746, ADR-7795)
- Menus and popups do not fade (egui's global animation time), overlays are opaque, and Geist is not yet routed to help
  text; the bridged `regia-dark` and `regia-light` themes fail 10 and 14 pairs of the wider list (ADR-8585, ADR-8679)
- 63 `colored_label` calls, graph pin colors, play tints and viewport overlay colors keep fixed colors (ADR-8589)
- Theme panel preview documents do not run game bindings, editor palette imports cover runtime color roles and button
  states only, and editor font and layout imports are deferred; theme rules are edited in the file (`ui.theme_set` has
  no rule edits); the built-in themes have no keyboard-only focus look of their own.

#### Agents, extensions and collaboration

- Agent Propose: a staged call cannot name an entity an earlier staged call creates, validation runs at approval, and
  the queue is discarded when the agent stops.
- Hosted agents: modes constrain the agent connection, not the executable's OS permissions. (ADR-7844)
- The value-editor parity scan matches source text, so a custom `Widget` impl is not seen. (ADR-7747)
- Extension commands are rebound in the Keymap tab only, and extension handles have no keyboard nudge. (ADR-7791)
- A game's development run discovers no engine extensions; asset-tool context-menu entries are not clicked by a test
  (ADR-9850)
- Utility panels are not restored with a saved layout, lay out at the sidecar's fixed size, use the editor's active
  theme, cannot read a call's result, and the starter sample is compiled into the editor.
- Collaboration is presence only on a trusted LAN (no edit or asset replication, encryption, account identity,
  discovery, relay or reconnect); only the main viewport's active pane is shared, selections carry at most 64 entities,
  and a stall over five seconds ends the session.
- Review notes: historical audit rows carry no entity or asset targets, and the Activity timeline is bounded and resets
  per process.

### Rendering

#### Lights, shadows and culling

- Extra directional lights cast no shadows; terrain scatter impostors and water see only the sun, the path tracer treats
  them as delta directions, atlas labels skip color glyphs, and the lite forward's `light_complexity` counts lights past
  its cap; `AmbientVolume` takes a color, not SH; the Mobile forward has no shader debug views (ADR-7211, ADR-7254)
- Moving casters still redraw sun pages with every static caster there. (ADR-7986)
- When the shadow pass stops while cluster shadows stay on, the last shadow cut's bands stay requested (memory held)
- Sun-sealing needs a host-filled `SunCover`; the heightfield does not fill it yet (ADR-8414)
- A transform-less light (the editor's default sun) taken out of a hidden group stays dark until it gets its own
  `Visibility`. (ADR-7549)
- Occlusion: persistent instance sets and blended draws stay frustum-only; the two-phase cull runs only with at least
  2048 opaque depth draws, a GPU-driven prepass and Hi-Z; an uncovered mesh can read false for more than one frame under
  a deep queue (ADR-7255, ADR-7286)
- Indoor cells do not cull shadow views (VisCell shadow-view culling is not built), fog's local lights, emissive fitted
  lights of culled casters, procedural instance sets, quick-draw meshes, terrain scatter, water, VFX particles or
  decals; the reached set is the union over views; a portal far from its boxes gets a deep doorway; past the depth cap
  the flood ignores the frustum. (ADR-7673, ADR-8371)
- Grass, scatter impostors and water are not culled through interior portals yet, and only the first window view is
  culled (a render-to-texture camera inside a cell draws as before). (ADR-8413)
- Each extra split-screen view costs a pyramid (about 5.3 bytes per pixel) and LOD boundary words; a view drawn less
  often than every 16 rounds starts each draw without history; multiview was not run on Android or macOS devices
  (ADR-7329, ADR-8620)
- Keyed draws are not entities: `Visibility`, layer parking, render overrides, glass, morph targets and `ViewVisibility`
  do not apply, and their LOD selection needs an extracted camera (ADR-8087, ADR-8457)
- Vertex-cluster far LOD refuses skinned and morphing meshes and lives in memory only. (ADR-8464)

#### Counted meshes and loading

- Counted meshes: morph targets, the GI proxy brick, the compute-skinning twin and a vertex-splitting lightmap unwrap
  (`lightmap_receiver_counted`) still need `keep_cpu_data`; counted meshes draw forward on the visibility-buffer profile
  with cluster residency on, for LOD variants, without cooked meshlets or past the storage-binding limit; a counted
  mesh's second UV set comes from the draw; graph textures and the foliage-sway twin have no counted form; a
  `VoxelFarSource` takes plain meshlets (ADR-8610, ADR-7428, ADR-7462)
- A counted texture's mip chain is filtered once in one color space, and GLES/WebGL2 or BC4/BC5 keep one view;
  `load_cooked_models_counted_textured` counts only the four base maps and still decodes detail, thin-film, parallax and
  edge-wear maps on the CPU (ADR-8610)
- A counted LOD or cross-fade variant drawn with joints keeps the vertex-shader path, and a counted emissive mesh adds
  no probe emitter clusters. (ADR-7721)
- Skinned and morphing meshes stay compact on desktop and pay the tangent pass at load (GPU mesh layout v3 proposed),
  and `CpuMesh::from_cooked` always rebuilds MikkTSpace tangents (ADR-7919)
- The all-LOD loader keeps an extra CPU copy of RGBA8-only images, pays MikkTSpace per load for compact meshes, draws
  skinned meshes rigid in bind pose and does not apply `COLOR_0`. (ADR-8025)
- Borrowed `LabMaterial` maps keep full emissive hashing, and default LOD thresholds reach zero after level 149.
  (ADR-7869)

#### Warm-up, frame pacing and memory

- Frame-level forward sets (a new target format, sample count, sun trace, many-light or double-sided key) are not
  bounded by `Defer` and build on the frame that needs them; cover them with the warm. The budget is per `render_into`
  call (each view budgets separately), and `Defer` makes a late object pop in a few frames late.
- A target created after the game-frame warm is covered only after `GameFrameWarm::request()`; DDGI compute pipelines
  are built per probe-volume instance and the warm covers the current settings only; `regia prepare` does not warm a
  game's maps, so a cold driver cache for a map's materials compiles during play.
- `regia prepare --scene` does not warm skinned, morphed or code-spawned content, or terrain and effects that are not
  level components (ADR-8453)
- `SceneReady`: engine-owned audio is not muted until it arrives (the host gates its own audio), and a minimized window
  keeps a prepare `resident` until restored.
- Paused scene hold: a swapchain without `COPY_SRC` renders full frames, and frame generation's first frame after a hold
  may blend across the pause.
- A frame that loses the forward-decal binding draws its forward-only decals in neither pass for one frame; frame
  generation's present-race fixes are tested without a window (ADR-7431, ADR-8930)
- The vsync-off Wayland change is not confirmed on a Wayland desktop; a focused vsync-off window with no frame cap uses
  the whole GPU (ADR-8170)
- The engine does not feed `DynResGovernor` itself, and every app state stays uncapped by default. (ADR-7889)
- `play.hitch_report` still reads renderer timings raw and can repeat a stale sample, and a renderer replaced while a
  hitch is pending can mis-pair its GPU timing.
- `RenderTimingAbsence` and `RunnerState` are not `#[non_exhaustive]`; `window_events` has no "moved" count, and the
  engine starts unfocused until an OS focus event.
- Host memory budget: texture payloads are counted, not dropped; `MeshRenderer` keeps its CPU mesh; frames with the
  visibility buffer, DDGI, traced sun, ReSTIR GI or lightmap receivers keep every copy (Desktop High and up drop nothing
  yet); per-tier values await measurements. (ADR-7891)
- The ReSTIR GI buffer report is an audited subset, not a VRAM total (ADR-8065)
- Renderer buffers and transient aliasing are still outside the frame graph. (ADR-7877)
- Editor tiled capture, background rendering and cancellation wait for a capture-snapshot contract for project renderer
  hooks; the still renderer disables temporal and neighbor-dependent effects.
- Template and sample menus do not list `VeryLow`. (ADR-7882)

#### Upscalers, exposure and shaders

- FSR 3.1 at 0.67 still misses spec 27 § 2.3 thin geometry (pan 0.66, orbit 0.57), smear (3 px) and held stability
  (0.0157), and at 0.5 every row; the 0.67 ghosting margin (0.0188 against 0.02) is 0.0012 and measured on lavapipe (the
  software rasterizer) only. (ADR-7875, ADR-7933)
- TSR 0.5 orbit thin geometry has one bar of margin on Metal, and some run lengths read under 0.90 on every backend.
  (ADR-7876)
- A level file cannot set `band_bounds_final_exposure` until the scene format carries it (ADR-7969)
- The ray-query shader modules `hwrt_shadow_ray_query.wgsl` and `sun_trace_ray_query.wgsl` still fail the shader
  matrix's layout scan (naga's ray-query helper), workgroup arrays are flagged by SPIRV-Tools 2026.4, and on DX12 with
  FXC 22 of 47 path-tracer readbacks differ within tolerance from the previous release. (ADR-7879, ADR-7587)
- The young-budget DDGI pipelines are built while every tier keeps the budget off (ADR-8785)

### Lightmaps, GI and lighting

- The Mobile lite forward shader reads no baked GI volume, so a baked tunnel there keeps the flat floor; with more than
  four interior volumes a far box seen through a mouth can gain or lose its air in one frame; the glass and canopy pass
  reads no probe or baked term (ADR-7975)
- The far-band blob is a contact shade only (no sun shadow or bounce), more than 160 opted-in proxies drop the farthest,
  and GI capsules have no distance band (ADR-7970)
- `NearOccluders` lifts nothing with the sun under about 3 degrees, and its weight is approximate on slopes (ADR-8412)
- A baked volume records one sun, so `follow_sun` rescales the other suns' bounce with the first; the level export and
  the editor's bake panel still write one sun (ADR-7818)
- The bake panel and `level.export_bake` pick their sun by different orders (lowest entity vs. lowest guid) (ADR-7549)
- No tier turns `young_budget` on, and with a tight budget a moving camera's far new probes can stay unlit longer than
  sixteen frames. (ADR-7870)
- A large unbaked ceiling emitter leaks about 0.5 % of its light under a slab where it meets the wall, and
  `stacked_floors` does not cover the lightmap path. (ADR-7661)

### World

#### Terrain

- Terrain imports never resample (`terrain_source_grid_mismatch`); a rectangular or off-center count grid cooks but a
  level refuses it (`level_terrain_mapping`); the cook refuses unequal-spacing and masked rasters until terrain index
  v3; `source-import` does not take a tile set yet (ADR-8541, ADR-8471, ADR-8542, ADR-8595)
- The cooked terrain index has no `check-formats` row or fuzz target yet; overview slope and passability layers are not
  drawn (ADR-8471, ADR-8595, ADR-8596)
- The grid origin is not saved with the map, and grass placement, scatter density maps, forest gaps, `SimGround` and
  `terrain_plane_body` do not follow it (ADR-8179)
- The terrain depth is not saved yet, and grass, scatter, GI heightfield, water plane, `SimGround` and stream tiles do
  not follow a rectangular extent (ADR-8369)
- Terrain height edits: a re-sat scatter instance keeps its old tilt on a crater slope; visible instances' seat cache is
  recomputed once per edit; without `prepare_height_edits()` the first edit copies the whole map once; an edit that
  moves the map's extreme sample triggers a full height-range scan. (ADR-7585, ADR-7638)
- Each terrain edit layer holds a full-grid `f32` array (64 MB on a 4097 x 4097 map); procedural cleanup of base-target
  generators compares composite samples, so a Set layer or later layer change can keep an absorbed base edit.
- Terrain preparation: a driver compile is not preemptible, the prepared configuration must match the first frame, water
  bodies need their own warm path, and releasing an old terrain can cost driver time. (ADR-7964)
- The terrain virtual texture is not at quality parity with the per-pixel surface (30.8 to 31.4 dB for the only mode
  that saves time); it stays opt-in. (ADR-7886)
- The far backdrop ring has no blessed plate, and an 8192² ring holds 256 MiB of CPU heights (ADR-8459)
- A kit's grid spacing is session state (the kit manifest has no grid field); region interchange files store 16-bit
  heights only.
- `regia_gen::Heightfield` and `regia_asset::Heightfield` share a name; the terrain export is square only (ADR-7280)
- Terrain operator bits differ from the old external tools for the same settings (ADR-8465)
- Overview images are not byte-compatible with earlier preview tools (ADR-8063)
- `<name>.overview.json` stays unregistered and its reader accepts an extra top-level field. (ADR-8639)

#### Tunnels and mesh layers

- The pinned quad-split mesher still emits steep floor facets: an unwaived cook of some tunnel maps fails on a floor
  facet over the limit until the next sim-bit bump; ship those maps with an authored `waive` list. Stage B mesher waiver
  K1 stays open: the Switchbacks, Atacama Rail and Cinder Steps cooks need an authored waiver. (ADR-8359, ADR-8447)
- The mesh-layer cook builds `Passages` sources only (blocker plugs and `Gltf`, `Collision` and `Volume` sources fail
  with `cook_not_built`); part of a skylight climb past the authored mouth stays under uncut sim ground (ADR-8112,
  ADR-8533)
- Mesh layers: a layer material with a non-opaque or double-sided render state can build its pipeline on first draw,
  without an `AssetServer` a material draws scalars only, LOD switches are discrete, and the terrain does not draw the
  band (strip, skirt, throat) yet, so a lattice-cut mouth shows an empty cut (ADR-8451, ADR-8398, ADR-8470)
- `regia terrain cook` takes flat ground only, shafts that are not ramps need authored shapes, portals do not join
  layers, and plugs have no render mesh (ADR-8175, ADR-8210)
- The mesh-layer cook builds its sim ground from width, scale and offset, not from a count source's mapping; the
  mesh-layer chunks still cut the canonical triangles rather than the subdivided render mesh. (ADR-8451, ADR-8576)
- The over-budget refusal comes after the layers are meshed; a `Nested` map over budget lists its held-fine tiles but
  does not name a larger level; a seam failure stays `CookError::PitchSeam` (ADR-8436)
- Seam rule 5 keeps the ground at full pitch along a whole underground tube (conservative). (ADR-8214)
- The approach rise is a steep face on the flat acceptance datum; the relief fade near a cut is a hard zero, not a ramp
  (ADR-8396, ADR-8470)
- A level-loaded map inserts only the `regia_terrain::TerrainMeshLayers` component, so `terrain.surface_at`,
  `terrain.bake_report` and `terrain.blockers` answer `no_world_geom` until a host inserts the agent resource.
  (ADR-8627)
- The CLI overview bake does not hide covered ground (no cooked mesh); `geometry.body_probe` loads the cooked file on
  every call, and a capsule move reads up to 18 columns per substep (ADR-8333, ADR-8323)
- The ground-edit state hash is FNV-1a-64 with a SplitMix64 finish; there are no `terrain.ground_edit` agent methods yet
  (ADR-8313)
- The interior camera's agent method does not run the sphere sweep; a host with a cooked world calls
  `InteriorProfile::place`. (ADR-8336)
- The aerial composite packs four interior slots (ADR-8384)
- Stage A keeps the source tunnel code's documented behaviors by design (Stage B fixes them); `tunnel_mouth_issues`
  orders `unique_id` issues by `HashMap` iteration; the export's `coordinates` text changed wording; the tunnel navmesh
  is baked at load (no cooked `.detnav` yet) (ADR-8050, ADR-8167, ADR-8215)
- The lead game's tunnel bake mouth portal `yaw_mdeg` keeps the source baker's signed-zero `atan2`. (ADR-8579)

#### Navigation

- Deterministic navigation: tunnel mouths and junctions are not registered as chokes from the grid, pilot-only crawlways
  are not searched, a passage narrower than two lanes has no one-way reservation, dirty tiles are reported but not
  re-baked, there is no cooked layered-bake file, `from_bytes` refuses a mesh past about 16 km square, and linking
  `regia_nav::det` compiles the ECS crates (ADR-8448, ADR-8195, ADR-8486, ADR-8174, ADR-8049)
- Det grid: one cost overlay per grid; a search heuristic must scale by `GridSettings::min_area_pct()` when an area is
  cheaper than 100 %; `set_overlay` relabels every cluster; the path-request queue plans surface routes only (ADR-8379,
  ADR-8295)
- `regia_nav::det::flow::ClusterFlow` assumes the default 8-cell clusters and one-byte region labels.
- A clustered flow field rebuilds its abstract route whole on each changed sync (1.7 s at 4096 x 4096 on the Windows
  min-spec laptop), and neither blends nor sets line-of-sight flags (ADR-8294)
- Stuck detection uses distance to the next corner, so an agent orbiting its corner is reported after three windows; the
  navmesh raycast ignores off-mesh links and `end.y` (ADR-8380)
- `NavTileCache`, the region rebuild and the streamed nav cook still build the top layer only; space enclosed inside a
  solid thicker than the agent height becomes an unreachable layer of its own; on the four-deck fixture a top-to-bottom
  pilot path is not guaranteed. (ADR-8076)
- Navmesh rasterization samples cell centers, so geometry thinner than a cell can be missed; bit-identical duplicate
  links cannot be told apart. (ADR-7558, ADR-7679)

#### Procedural generation, scatter and effects

- Procgen outputs are world space: re-baking a member placed away from the origin writes host-space output into the
  source level's file (ADR-7331)
- A level with a prefab instance in an `Absent` data layer, or instancing a prefab variant from the CLI, prunes no
  guid-named file. (ADR-7793)
- Scatter placement still measures the map as a square centered on the world origin (ADR-8496)
- `regia cook` places HLOD scatter proxies from the map edge; painted instances never become colliders (ADR-8496,
  ADR-8029)
- `biome.resolve` is still serial (ADR-7252, ADR-7214)
- `regia_gen` has no overlapping union or difference, bevels cannot merge outlines, and ear clipping limits rings to
  4096 points (1024 per ring in `gen.mesh`) (ADR-7240)
- `mesh_hash` does not cover vertex colors from `sweep_section` wear. (ADR-8044)
- Lit mesh particles use flat face normals and front faces only, the built-in cube stays unlit, and the cube draw still
  walks the whole mesh list (ADR-7261)
- Measured and GPU VFX bounds leave out the screen-size floor and treat a registered render mesh as a unit cube; depth
  collision in measured bounds uses a flat-ground stand-in.
- The VFX packed-count check uses the largest LOD rate scale, so it refuses some links that only overflow in a scaled
  band. (ADR-7560)
- No golden image covers the new hover-wash look. (ADR-8026)

#### Levels, streaming and 2D

- Level switch moves only the read and parse off the frame; entity spawns and asset loads run as before, and a load that
  never checks its token runs to its end. (ADR-8027)
- A same-length corruption inside an uncompressed pak entry is caught by the background hash or the decode, not at
  mount; two overlapping open levels bind a teleport move to the lower level id; two concurrent `regia upgrade-project
  --write` runs are not supported (ADR-8810, ADR-8813, ADR-8861)
- A pin wait set before any level is open is not checked. (ADR-7612)
- An attachment of a runtime-spawned `Saveable` parent stays at the root after a load, and a prefab added after a fill
  is not counted against the placement cap. (ADR-7601)
- 2D: tile and object layers are a tree in the 2D tab, not Outliner rows; object boxes ignore rotation and drag on
  orthogonal maps only; pattern saves are not undo steps; rotatable scene tiles are not built.

### Animation and characters

- Multi-effector full-body IK and hand grips are not graph nodes; no agent method reads `EvalReuse::ik_node_residuals`
  (ADR-8475)
- Warp nodes: pose snapshots do not keep the smoothed ratio, `Rate` mode lags one frame and scales the clock of
  everything under it (keep a whole sync group under the node) (ADR-8540)
- BVH sources do not read `contact_curves`, and a blended contact weight below 1 never starts a stance (ADR-8308)
- `FootPlacement` allocates a constant amount per frame (not zero), pose-mode probes can race an unordered `PostUpdate`
  physics mutation, and the toe joint is not solved; graph output gets no foot tilt or toe bend (ADR-8388, ADR-8505)
- Gait: a foot whose neutral spot is too steep stays and counts as settled, and a body yaw updated on only some frames
  steps feet more often (ADR-8539)
- Gait math is presentation only: no cross-slope body tilt or collision response, and a refused footstep does not block
  body travel
- Retarget `Contacts` goals on clips that walk in place act as `ScaledWorld` goals, and a goal chain with a twist joint
  between links is skipped as `goal_not_a_chain` (ADR-8476)
- Joint compression stays separate from the lossless node-track body; no automatic node-track graph blending (ADR-8157)
- The spec 34 montage frame budget is not measured, and `Animator::graph` does not apply `.animgraph.ron` parameter
  defaults (ADR-7216)
- Montage commands made in `Update` play one tick later on clients (ADR-9370)
- `lossless_clips` is per source, not per clip, and does not turn off import-time key reduction (ADR-7819)
- Clips marked distance matched at runtime have no stored arrays (ADR-7817)
- Node drives do not write joints or blend through an animation graph (ADR-8410)

### Gameplay systems

#### Determinism and lockstep

- Several new lockstep pins and goldens are recorded on x86-64 Linux only, with aarch64 (Mac, Android, qemu) legs owed:
  `input_log_arena`, `math_rng_fixed_vectors` (not yet in `CLEAN_SCENARIOS`), cadence, roster tick chain, blocker pins,
  terrain raster and tile fixtures, and the `hover_bad_input` qemu and Android legs
- The determinism scan is lexical; a clean scan is not a determinism proof. The det-scan text scan cannot see types (an
  unhinted float `a.max(b)` is caught only by clippy) and does not expand macros or follow generated modules;
  `regia_detsim::math::sqrt` of a negative number returns the host's NaN (ADR-8367, ADR-8085, ADR-8192)
- The ECS desync hash's default order depends on entity ids (peers spawn lockstep entities in the same order, or use
  `order_by`), and there is no agent-protocol divergence injection yet (ADR-8080)
- The desync mixer cannot see unit fields or skipped option slots and folds hash containers in iteration order; a
  partial flag names the tick, not the system (ADR-8621, ADR-8634)
- `LockstepSim` and `DetInputSim` are separate traits with no blanket impl, and `sim.host_snapshot` has no form for a
  future tick (ADR-8526)
- Windows thread CPU time advances in about 15.6 ms quanta, so per-tick profile rows read 0 or 15.6 ms there (use
  `--clock wall`); `TickProfiler::report` allocates (ADR-8498, ADR-8561)
- `ground_vehicle_mix` has no Windows hash line yet, and yaw is not wrapped (ADR-8638)
- No engine lobby calls `hull_handshake_mismatch` yet (ADR-8632)
- No golden covers a mirrored mesh (ADR-8456)
- Enhanced-determinism physics digests are not pinned on any target (ADR-7668)

#### AI, crowds and fog

- `PerceptionIndex`, `TeamRosters`, `TargetPickReport`, `CadenceReport` and `ContactStats` counters are host-side only:
  no agent method or tick-profile row yet; flow fields and the path-request queue also have no agent method
- `OrcaSolver::Lp` costs several times Stage A per body, and dense counterflow can still overlap briefly (ADR-8555)
- Integer avoidance keeps the source's unchecked bounded arithmetic and one-pass projection (ADR-8150)
- `Ringed` takes the lowest slots of a crowded ring, not the best-scoring; a unit missing from a `due_units` call loses
  its queue place (ADR-8517, ADR-8560)
- Formations: the squad's `column` flag is not written by the crowd, followers need a higher max speed than the leader,
  and assignment above 64 is greedy (ADR-8358)
- The AI line-of-sight adapter over the native world query is a follow-up; wingman plans and mode orders have no agent
  method; the broadphase emits no span (wrap it in the tick's span) (ADR-8395, ADR-8554, ADR-8048)
- `ai.bt_validate` cannot see across bridge files. (ADR-7596)
- Fog: the elevation rule does not apply to fog grid cells, explored ground accumulates only for the current viewer,
  ghosts are drawn by the game, and lockstep fog is presentation only (ADR-8473, ADR-8523, ADR-8524)
- Fog of war has no sight-table file format and `fog.status` reports counts, never hidden ids (ADR-8154)

#### Vehicles, physics and destruction

- Ground vehicles are kinematic (no tires, drivetrain, suspension or vehicle contact), projectiles have no penetration
  or ricochet rules, and `HoverFleet` does not collide hulls (ADR-8551, ADR-8509, ADR-8508)
- A static collider replaced by a same-type shape with the same bounds does not rebuild a vehicle's private world
  (ADR-7431)
- `PresentationGround` must be inserted before the plugin's `Startup`, and the plugin expects `PhysicsPlugin` (ADR-8356)
- Presentation physics bodies are not sim state, so clients may scatter debris differently
- A hit far outside the solid at bias 1 or 2 still fails with `fracture_site_sampling` (ADR-7550)
- The mesh `.meta` stores only fracture `chunks` and `seed`; a biased or clustered cut is kept by **Save** (ADR-9870)
- `.soft.ron` has no plasticity field (ADR-9070)

#### Rules, logic and game framework

- Logic graphs: phase 3 (sequencer, audio, Lua, net, input integrations, world blackboards) and the allocation-free
  measurement are not built; a raw `Blackboard::values` write is seen only when the value changes (ADR-7201, ADR-7224)
- The logic net route is not tested with a dedicated server plus client; untargeted `regia.emit` events reach only
  `LogicModHost` entities; some allocations remain by design (ADR-7268, ADR-7282)
- Saving a logic graph during play reloads a prefab's running copies only when its `LogicGraphRef` has no overrides, a
  `.logic.ron` changed outside the editor does not hot-reload, and the Inspector shows `LogicGraphRef` without an asset
  picker.
- Rules: the agent's `drop: "return"` hint covers only a flag dropped where that unit died; there is no `WinCondition`
  adapter yet (use `MatchSession::report_condition`) (ADR-8506, ADR-8485)
- Objective events do not record a kill or a structure loss: the game calls `note_kill` and `note_structure`.
- `MatchFlow::leave` keeps the leaver's `pause_used` row, and a party split across teams is not moved by rebalance.
  (ADR-8657)
- Leaving a game does not despawn the player's character (the game's rules own it)
- A `TagGained` trigger does not fire for a tag already held at load until it is lost and regained (ADR-7573)
- Lua host commands are not saved: a save restore clears waiting commands.
- `luastate` v1 has no mount-ownership field: a Workshop item cannot restore its own legacy entry, a local folder
  mounted as `workshop.<digits>` is held, and held entries alone over the cap fail every save. (ADR-7992)
- The hot-reload facade split (`regia_internal`) is not built; a hand-written `ReflectScript` rebuilds with default
  fields (ADR-7206)
- `LevelManager::loading_screen` is unread (ADR-7203)
- No exclusive fullscreen and no monitor or video-mode list; the game still spawns and tags its own player cameras.
  (ADR-7880)
- `play.step` runs on wall-clock deltas, so the third_person stair-flight check can fail on loaded hosts (ADR-8716)

### Networking and Steam

#### Replication and prediction

- The fog lead ring puts an enemy on the client a few ticks before it is seen, and a listen-server host holds the full
  state (ADR-8524)
- An entity gaining or losing `NetProfileRef` between join-snapshot re-captures shows with the next replication change
- The prediction path keeps its own input redundancy and margin report and does not take the loss-aware copy count; the
  first input lead before any feedback starts about twice as high as needed on a bloated downlink. (ADR-8312, ADR-8077)
- `merge_late` defaults are coarse (`NetCodec` keeps the next input whole), the default `max_dilation` stays 5 %, and
  the client does not learn which tick a late press was merged into. (ADR-8217)
- Lag compensation: a forging client gains up to 250 ms extra rewind in its first seconds, a client can lengthen its
  window by holding acknowledgements, and the 1,000 ms default is an open owner question. (ADR-8315)
- The extrapolation gate is off by default (the project default is an owner decision), the tick stream ignores it, and
  it trusts `NetVelocity`: keep velocity zero when an entity is blocked. (ADR-8522)
- The extrapolation gate for transform records and the reduced far-tier record are not built; packing still allocates
  per connection (ADR-8467, ADR-8031)
- A high-RTT correction re-runs up to RTT + lead ticks in one frame (about 45 at 650 ms) (ADR-8219)
- The client keeps 64 versions per entity, so a round trip over 64 ticks sends full records; at 4,096 B/s a 16 KiB
  record holds the connection's state for about 4 s (ADR-8419)
- While rebasing, an entity removed meanwhile stays shown, and a full packet delayed over `capacity()` replaces the
  world (ADR-7983)
- Near the client entity cap, churn can trip a false refusal, and above about 55 despawns per tick sustained a reordered
  late spawn can bring back a despawned ghost (ADR-8850)
- External replication has no engine agent method (the host returns `ExternalReplication::stats()`), and
  `ExternalInterpolator::current()` can show an older state for a deferred entity. (ADR-8186)
- After a dormant resume, copies that only left a client's relevance stay on that client; a session client's reconnect
  goes through the whole join and is slower than a `NetLink` client's. (ADR-8298, ADR-8352)
- Replication and the session must share peer ids; a dev game started outside `regia run`/`regia server` presents an
  empty manifest (ADR-7213, ADR-7212)
- Travel tombstones are not verified on a real two-machine LAN; macOS not checked (ADR-7295)
- Passenger seats held by a pawn handed to the server stay taken (ADR-8790)
- An owner change by session hand-off between live peers updates the vehicle driver record without releasing.

#### Transport and security

- On a plain link a packet from an unvalidated address is still applied, a spoof from the peer's address can score it,
  and a copied token makes the server do one AEAD open per datagram. Sealed links are not affected. (ADR-8666)
- A relay attacker carrying both directions can become the path, as a NAT can (ADR-8299)
- A flood larger than 16,384 datagrams per receive still delays other peers in the shared kernel queue. (ADR-8181)
- A peer that skips packet numbers to claim loss holds a gap up to `max(16 x RTO, 8 s)`; a receiver over 1,024 packets
  behind resynchronizes after 8 such packets (ADR-8521, ADR-8184)
- After the client confirms, a stale join carrying an older ticket is a fresh join. (ADR-8623)
- A reliable input that loses a fragment arrives one resend timeout later (100 ms floor) and holds later reliable
  messages back. (ADR-7896)
- Runtime refusals are not `ServerEvent::Rejected` events: bridge hosts read both (ADR-7984)
- No agent method reports rejections per peer. (ADR-7892)
- Clients older than stamps send no content hash, and the 1.5 s probe wait applies against older servers (ADR-7985)
- QUIC remains loopback-only. (ADR-7946)
- Sockets do not set don't-fragment, so IPv4 probes can arrive fragmented; the QUIC transport ignores `mtu_probing`;
  Windows applies DSCP only under a QoS policy (ADR-8300, ADR-8407)
- A 2 MiB receive buffer request is often granted under half on Linux (`rmem_max`), DSCP EF is best-effort, and
  `net.socket_buffers` probes a new socket rather than the live one. (ADR-7998)
- `regia_net`'s integration tests, examples and benches need `ecs`. (ADR-8032)

#### Rate control and soak results

- The listen-uplink rejoin second can sit up to 0.8 % over the cap on 9 of 40 seeds; bufferbloat+bulk seed 54 reaches
  100.2 % and lossy-fat-pipe seed 69 fails `random_loss_kept`. (ADR-8534, ADR-8592, ADR-8407)
- Rate control: the controller counts body bytes, not wire bytes. The receive-timestamp trendline runs only with
  `receive_timestamps` on. `PeerQuality` is not built. (ADR-8490, ADR-8301)
- On `congested` missed input is 0.3-0.7 points higher, and a 1 MiB upload beside inputs runs at about 12 KB/s; ECS
  `ReliableUnordered` messages are unordered only on `Channel::Stream(StreamId::UNORDERED_RPC)` (ADR-8407, ADR-8302)
- `rpc_p95_with_bulk` on congested+bulk fails 5 of 24 seeds; `LinkStats::reassembly_stall_deadline_ms` omits the
  unanswered-packet bound. (ADR-8658)
- Soak: the server tick grows faster than the client count (128 clients over their tier), and `staleness_p99` fails on
  `mixed` (ADR-8454)

#### Lockstep

- Lockstep: an outvoted observer stops voting instead of taking the majority's state; `server.lockstep_status` is not
  answered on a headless `LockstepHostGame` (use `/health` and the console); a mid-match joiner captures from the tick
  after its join frame; a catch-up for a seat whose leave is pending is refused (retry a tick later); a `Relay` host
  must get catch-up state from a seat. (ADR-8558, ADR-8212)
- Lockstep: interleaved redundancy repairs some loss runs a tick later than `Adaptive`; late-stamp detection cannot tell
  a client that delays every packet from a slow uplink; `desync_zero` does not gate. (ADR-8209, ADR-8409)
- Lockstep: a seat whose round trip exceeds delay plus window stalls the others (ADR-8361, ADR-7953)
- Desync: a forged reference seat can make a probe name the wrong subsystem (diagnosis only); the verdict rule is a
  strict majority of votes cast; the `regia` binary replays only the engine's sample game; a `Relay` host's bundle has
  no host rows. (ADR-8211, ADR-8455)
- The lockstep soak models the reliable stream rather than running `NetServer`/`NetClient`. (ADR-8656)

#### Lobbies, relay, NAT and Steam

- NAT and hosting: two symmetric NATs always play through the relay (expected); host check sessions end 10 s after the
  introduction; the port-forward self-check cannot tell a forward from address-only filtering with one relay IP; mapping
  is automatic with no off switch; gateway discovery is Linux-only (`port_map_unavailable` elsewhere). (ADR-8340,
  ADR-8370)
- Relay: no full spec 120 § 10.7 candidate checks, relayed clients lose a host after a host-side NAT rebind until they
  rejoin, the `net_game` template CLI client connects directly only, and `RelayServer::update` is not measured at 4,096
  allocations (ADR-8183, ADR-7982)
- The relay and hub unit files written by `regia package --server` do not start a shipped binary: point `ExecStart` at
  an installed one
- Lobby hub: a fresh `PlayerIdentity` per connection is the default (no identity file format yet), and there is no
  standalone hub binary yet. (ADR-8559, ADR-8607)
- Chat lines dropped at the outbox cap are not replayed by a resync, and a dropped join plan must be asked for again.
  (ADR-8633)
- `join` returns distinct codes for a missing, private and friends-only lobby. (ADR-8665)
- The join ticket hook can be installed on `NetServer` only (not `NetSession`, `NetLink` or the dedicated host);
  `MAX_JOIN_DATA_BYTES` stays 512; address-code nonce and expiry are advisory; `LiveSteamTickets` is not exercised by
  `cargo test`. (ADR-7989)
- A Steam refusal for a session a restart replaced can kick the new session; `cargo test` does not run the live Steam
  callback (ADR-8502)
- Real Steam lobbies are verified only by a two-account session. (ADR-7881)
- The launch rollback does not clear the `SetLobbyGameServer` field on the real Steam backend; the real-backend create
  path builds only with the `steamworks` feature.
- `MockSteamNet` does not model `flappy`'s 12 s outage or `mobile-4g`'s MTU and NAT rebinding in a single link.
  (ADR-8351)
- GOG has no linked Galaxy SDK, cloud notifications are hooks only, and native Steam account and overlay checks need a
  real Steam session

### Platforms

- The very-low integrated-GPU floor's OS and driver memory share on an 8 GB machine is not measured (no such farm host)
  (ADR-7977)
- The optional base-reference mark is per asset, not per field; Windows and macOS read as unmetered (ADR-7236)
- `xr_over_budget` measures CPU frame time only (ADR-7237)
- The Android trace marker and folder were checked by unit tests and an `aarch64-linux-android` build, not on a device
  (ADR-9070)
- Steam Deck: spatial index query p99 2.171 ms at 900 rows (over the 1.0 ms Deck bar), a `[4000, 3000]` window clamp
  failure on the rotated panel, and a null host memory budget for `render.memory_budget {"tier":"deck"}`
- The Android editor launch's agent attach was verified against a fake `adb` only (ADR-8594)
- All-thread stack sampling is Linux GNU only; other targets return `Unsupported`
- Package-off Android launches stay refused; if the device port (47800 + launch id % 100) is taken, the launch runs
  without push and remote panels.
- Android first start after install compiles every pipeline: 4.7 to 5.7 s to the first frame on a Pixel 9 Pro; no
  reference-class, low-end, Adreno or Mali-G78 phone was tested (ADR-7687)
- Intel Arc (not a gate card): a device created mid-run can still race the Vulkan loader until the distribution's loader
  carries the upstream fix (ADR-2113)
- Intel Arc: `planet_mars_noon` (35.93 dB) sits at its plate floor because of inside-out test tower faces (fix needs a
  re-bless). (ADR-7704)
- XeSS on AMD under Windows has not been run. (ADR-7820)
