- The Windows native-fault exit and signal paths of the server host are compile-checked only
- Windows cook hosts are not measured for astcenc invariance (UCRT `sinf`/`cosf`/`logf`, MSVC flags). (ADR-7878)
- macOS: `net_game --test hosted` `two_clients_join_by_code_play_and_leave_under_the_host` fails about 1 run in 20.
- Adapter enumeration and device creation still depend on a healthy driver; the UUID lookup deadline does not bound
  them. (ADR-7991)

### Tools, pipeline and agent protocol

#### Cook, package and assets

- One fatal cook error still makes `regia cook` return before `pack_paks`, and a glTF with a skin and animations but no
  triangle mesh still fails. (ADR-7959)
- Closure cooks follow cooked dependency lists only: a file opened by a run-time path must be an `entry`, and a DLC rule
  in a closure project needs its own `entry` (ADR-8092)
- Levels have no field-typed root extractor, so every decoded string that names project content is a reference
  (ADR-8477)
- The cook diet's triangle ceiling counts `CookedMesh` assets only, a profile's own texture keys do not recook, and DDS,
  SVG and channel-pack sources recook on a cap change only when their import runs (ADR-8461)
- Texture tiers reduce only `CookedTexture` entries; tier rebuild caching and packaging selection are follow-ups, and
  old tier directories stay on disk (ADR-8059, ADR-8144)
- Overlay roots fill holes only in `cook_project` (not the live cooker), the runtime does not search them, and a cook
  that fails on a bad table leaves an old `overlay_fill.json` (ADR-8155)
- Identical packed ORM payloads with different ids still occupy separate FPAK v1 blobs (ADR-8055)
- A release-chunks plan can count more chunks than `cut` writes (ADR-8404)
- Chunk pak verification adds a synchronous full read at mount and is relative to the supplied manifest. (ADR-7871)
- `regia package parity` compares file access only (no stills or pixels)
- `cargo xtask package-smoke` does not call `regia play-gate run` yet, and the installer refuses entries over 256 MiB
  (ADR-8452)
- The slim `regia_asset_pipeline` build still links the cook database and the render, UI and audio crates the cook
  reaches (ADR-7972)
- Asset reference cycles stay loaded (ADR-7202)
- The asset access trace covers only the asset hooks, one log per process, with no rotation
- Unsaved pipelines wait for a cache checkpoint, crash bundles omit caches, and only stored ZIPs are accepted
- Deferred audit findings: MaterialX `tiledimage` `uvoffset` sign, zero-scale rest bones failing a cook, `regia
  dcc-feed` texture URIs leaving the glTF folder, MaterialX `noise2d` and `constant_input` defaults,
  `SettingsObject::parse` with unsupported RON features, double file reads on image hot reload, `regia procgen` envelope
  meshes from disk only, and stale incompatible Windows build-dependency pins. (ADR-7666)

#### Content lint and package hygiene

- Material warnings (including `factor_missing`) do not stop `cook: Fail` or package; strict `regia assets validate`
  does (ADR-8390)
- glTF lint does not read `KHR_texture_transform` or most `KHR_materials_*` extensions, robust rows are not restored by
  `gltf_report_from_findings`, and lint cancellation is cooperative between units (ADR-8347, ADR-8226)
- glTF lints: coincident detection is per primitive in source-local space, strips and fans are skipped, files over 64
  MiB are refused, and source fixes are outside editor undo (ADR-8068, ADR-8061)
- Unsupported containers are recognized by magic bytes only, gzip header fields are not scanned, the AppImage payload is
  always uninspected (strict projects cannot ship AppImage), and nested containers over the memory budget spool beside
  the artifact (ADR-8348)
- Package hygiene signature scans cannot prove secrets absent; the matcher reservation is an estimate; a legacy hygiene
  file without definition digests binds by index until migrated; the read-only audit does not run the dev probe, and an
  inconclusive probe does not fail the package (ADR-8244, ADR-8247)
- Hygiene location ids are traversal ordinals, stable only for an unchanged tree (ADR-8229)

#### Formats, tools and agent protocol

- `peek_ron_version` still parses whole files, so `regia upgrade-project` waits on large `.fracture.ron` files in its
  plan phase (ADR-7703)
- The shared option-graph schema's `reason.code` enum still lacks `incomplete` (ADR-7703)
- Replays: only bounded extensions can be skipped; versions above 4 return `unsupported_version` (ADR-8191)
- The asset-id case fold applies no Unicode normalization (ADR-8553)
- Runtime screenshot overlays have no depth test or labels (convex hulls draw their box), and the `ui` target has no
  safe-area parameter (ADR-7217)
- Log triage refuses partial or conflicting source-range overlaps, and percentiles of periodic frame summaries describe
  the summaries, not individual frames. Peer log file IO is synchronous. (ADR-8442)
- `diagnostics.report_sources` cache keys do not validate payloads or authorize upload. (ADR-8058)
- The shipped `perf-gate.toml` holds class and tier defaults only, trace-mode budgets are fixed, and summary and trace
  percentiles use different definitions
- The default `[package_hygiene]` refuses every shipping binary over 32 MiB (`unbounded_regex`), so a new project's
  first shipping package fails until it raises `max_memory_bytes`; a larger default or bounded streaming forms of the
  secret signatures are planned (ADR-8786)

### Audio, media, UI and accessibility

- Pointer claims gate mouse buttons only (not touch, the wheel, motion or direct `regia_input::Mouse` reads; touch
  reaches the gate only through mouse emulation), the UI pointer gate follows the previous frame's hover, and
  script-field sources cannot be written in `.ui.ron` (ADR-7976, ADR-7070)
- Draw2d has no filled-triangle or radial-gradient instance kinds, draws the same list into every window, and its
  retained target composites every frame even when nothing changed (ADR-8458, ADR-8514)
- Complex-script caret edge cases at bidirectional run boundaries remain; visual word moves and Home/End in
  right-to-left text stay logical; real IME candidate placement on Windows and macOS is unchecked; theme `height`,
  `padding_x` and `gap` do not reach layout, and captions have no font or size tokens (ADR-7208)
- Delete and Backspace stay logical in right-to-left text, ellipsized single-line text still drops a leading number, and
  nothing was checked with a real input method or on Android (ADR-9340)
- Keyboard layout presets cover only the 30-key letter block; Colemak-DH, Belgian AZERTY and revised French AZERTY need
  custom bindings.
- UI designer: no text-input maximum length or password flag, list selection or image fit rows; repeat-template align
  uses the first sample row; dragging moves only the grabbed node. (ADR-9730)
- An image's `fit: tile` is refused; a group drag never reorders a node in the flow; a text input's custom filter is
  code-only (ADR-9980)
- A theme binding reads the active theme, not a `ThemeScope`, and template row height is one fixed height. (ADR-7751)
- Markup applies to literal spans only; a root whose scale mode is set in code to exactly the engine default takes the
  saved rule.
- A Localization import key defined again in a later file of the locale does not change what the runtime shows.
  (ADR-7677)
- Real-window screen reader checks (Orca, NVDA, VoiceOver) are not run, and a reader cannot `set_value` a text field.
  (ADR-7746, ADR-7795)
- Still mouse-only: the montage strip, the procedural curve parameter, the sequencer timeline rows, the curve table plot
  and the UI designer canvas. (ADR-7733)
- The high-contrast theme needs the owner's look sign-off; Color Vision does not filter textured images in the chrome
  and is not saved. (ADR-7838)
- The sequence preview does not pose rigs or show UI image video sections; the occlusion mirror rebuilds on any collider
  change. (ADR-9710, ADR-8591)
- A shortening ripple over a key, marker or section start is refused, not resolved; a transport Stop inside a sound
  section blips its voice; scaling a whole section keeps its speed. (ADR-8640, ADR-7847)
- Render movie renders the saved file, and a loaded shot list keeps driving its camera during it; scalar Shots panel
  curve edits make no undo entry.
- Sub-sequence event tracks do not fire, event keys are not drawn in the timeline's dope sheet, and `.uianim.ron` has no
  frame events.
- The sequencer authoring rows, timeline, Render movie dialog, preview overlay and new UI were driven by tests, not yet
  judged on a screen.
- Mixer: a chain change, send add or remove, bypass toggle, or toggling a send's **Pre** rebuilds the bus tracks and
  stops playing voices; effect parameters are edited in `config/audio.ron`, not the dock. (ADR-8590)
- A `kira` upgrade must re-apply the `vendor/kira` patch; a watchdog recovery ends every playing voice; the
  device-unplug race is not verified on hardware (ADR-8830)
- Live voice-chat streams and music scheduling keep their old teardown on output suspension (ADR-8052)
- The engine never drains `VoiceChatSession::take_events` (ADR-7237)
- Patch bindings read reflected fields only, and patch smoothing stays on the game thread (ADR-7226)
- During a render-out mixdown `Audio::program_loudness` reads the mixdown's own meter (off at start), and the live
  relative gate runs at 0.01 LU resolution (ADR-7227)
- Portal sound takes one path per source with no diffraction, blocker plugs stay at their cooked state, and the air
  between two shells counts as rock (ADR-8416)
- HRTF at 96 kHz is not verified on a real device, and CPU cost grows faster than the rate (ADR-7461)
- A capture re-open after a device change runs on the game thread, and FLAC or Ogg files with no stated length are
  decoded whole before the IR tier check. (ADR-7602)
- A `Lockstep` player outside render-out takes audio at the device's pace; the resync holds the old picture up to 120
  ticks; a low-fps clip without `DefaultDuration` is sized for 30 fps and can overflow (one warning). (ADR-7563)
- The hitch report's Streaming row has no numeric target
- `streaming_waits.render_residency` is `null` without the render plugin

### Core, licence and security

- With the async log writer, `flush`, `finish` and drop wait for the sink; recovery file IO stays synchronous and the
  host owns retention (ADR-8494)
- At Ship telemetry, warnings and errors are still free text that can name a project-relative asset path, and
  `REGIA_TELEMETRY=normal` does not filter the `REGIA_JSON_LOG` file (ADR-8417)
- The outbox does not read the sanitized-payload manifest yet; only start-time-named logs are crash-resent (ADR-8402)
- `demo-notices --verify` fails until the final EULA replaces the draft, and the sample-license text is missing
- Raw ELF names in hang reports may be mangled; file and line resolution is offline
- Other compute-pool work started during a play frame is still filed under the play flame context (ADR-7427)

### Tests, docs and tooling

- Pre-existing test reds: the unreliable state-channel runs of `net_replay` (three tests), `dcc_feed` joint-cycle
  folding, `thin_film_500`, `frame_work_ratchet`, the tire transient pin, `startup_idle_ratchet` and `studio_frame_pin`
  (ADR-7346)
- `regia_editor` `crash_reports` tests `agent_lists_and_reads_and_refuses_other_paths` and
  `editor_panic_report_matches_the_game_layout` fail in a full parallel run and pass alone
- `player_data::save_loads_sweep_damage_versions_and_interrupted_writes` expects a v1 save to need a migration that save
  v2 performs built in (ADR-7310)
- `editor_screenshot`'s window capture test is red (ADR-8680)
- `arena` `wc_systems` and `lattice_windows` stayed red at the format freeze.
- `third_person` `agent_expect` stays load-dependent, and wall-clock hitch tests (`emissive_hitch` at 1 ms, `arena`
  `spawn_hitch` at 50 ms, `two_phase_occlusion`) can fail on a heavily loaded host; the pre-gate reports
  `emissive_hitch`, `spawn_hitch` and `dispatch_fuzz` as NOT JUDGED there. (ADR-8716, ADR-5908, ADR-8635)
- `view_asset_overlays_through_the_dispatcher` skips without a headless adapter.
- The first run of a fresh `pie_modes` binary on an NVIDIA card takes about 90 s (cold shader cache) (ADR-8411)
- `cargo xtask det-scan` reports two findings in `crates/regia_net/src/lockstep/server.rs` (`Instant::now`,
  `std::thread::Builder`).
- The 40-seat, 600 s wander-fire lockstep test is `#[ignore]`; run it with `--ignored --exact`. Stateful fuzz targets
  for stream windows, path validation, lockstep frames, desync bundles and QUIC are not written yet. (ADR-8409,
  ADR-8468)
- The net-soak gate row is off (`xtask::net_soak::GATE_ROW_ENABLED`); Steam's relay network is not soaked; the
  `relay-path` and `lockstep-mix` profiles are declared and skipped (ADR-7899, ADR-8030)
- `missed_input` does not see lateness the late-input merge absorbs (ADR-8424, ADR-8301)
- The time harness runs one fixed tick per update at a 16,667 µs period (ADR-8185)
- The full `DefaultPlugins` postcard registry round trip (needs a device) is not run (ADR-7232)
- GPU coverage of mesh particles, pipeline warm and multi-view occlusion is lavapipe only (ADR-7261, ADR-7286)
- The terrain prewarm test reads the process-wide pipeline counter.
- `REGIA_REQUIRE_ADAPTER` does not cover window devices or `regia_viewer`'s own device, and does not catch a lavapipe
  fallback with a card present. (ADR-7613)
- The world geometry `mesh_bits` and tunnel-plan golden legs on Windows, Android and qemu-aarch64 are not recorded; the
  source game's own pins for a Stage A swap are listed on the book page "Proving a swap" and not run here (ADR-8166,
  ADR-8215, ADR-8432)
- Five older panels have no editor-guide page of their own; the editor guide pages for the Cameras, Smart Objects,
  Motion Matching, 2D mode and Terrain panels may still sit in the no-page table. (ADR-8208)
- Duplicate listings remain in `book/COVERAGE.md` and `book/SUMMARY.md` (`asset.export_glb`, `agents/crash-fx.md`,
  `agents/net-prediction.md` and others)
- `docs/provenance.md` "Render (motion-look)" still names a Catmull-Rom history fetch for TAAU; the code uses Keys'
  cubic at a = -1 (ADR-7456)
- `test-report` 1 → 2 has no `regia upgrade-project` row yet, and the v0.5.0 format baseline was taken from a release
  candidate's table. (ADR-8588, ADR-7724)
- `release-notes --check` keeps a stale-version finding for root fragments named for a release before v0.5.1. (ADR-7684)
- A cold `target/hot-reload-m3` beside a full lavapipe pass can still exceed the gate's 1,200 s per-binary limit; that
  run is lost, and the next one passes (ADR-8787)
- `crates/regia_test_utils/goldens/sponza.png` and `skinned_fox.png` keep `licence = "unknown"` in
  `docs/asset-licences.toml` (warnings): the models' own licence files are not in the fetched asset cache (ADR-8795)

## Golden changes

- Image goldens: one re-blessed on the lavapipe reference, `regia_quick` `quick_text.png`, for the engine-name change of
  its drawn string ("Regia quick draw"; ADR-8670). No other image golden was added or re-blessed.
- Editor screenshots change with the new default editor look (the Graphite theme, owner decision 2026-11-18, ADR-8585);
  no editor screenshot is a checked-in golden image.
- Frames these changes can move, with no golden re-blessed for them: frames inside an interior box with a covering baked
  GI volume (the bake's gradient instead of the 40 lux floor, ADR-7975); a material graph with repeated identical
  function calls compiles to less code and another pipeline key (ADR-7730). No plate is blessed yet for cooked photoreal
  terrain (existing image bar), the Draw2d HUD tile (CPU reference tolerance), the far backdrop ring, the fog overlay,
  the Lockstep overlay (pixel-class checks) or the hover-wash look. The Intel Arc planet plates need a re-bless once the
  test tower's winding is fixed (proposal in ADR-7704).
- New determinism goldens (`.detgold`, one hash per target): the engine scenarios in `crates/regia_detsim/goldens/`
  (`steer_slalom`, `slope_follow`, `disc_collide`, `hover_libm_1000`, `hover_bad_input`, `math_rng_fixed_vectors`,
  `hover_mixed`, `lowg_ballistic_mixed`, `hover_fleet_terrain`, `projectile_mix`, `input_log_arena` (`.detlog`) and
  `contact_pile`; ADR-8531, ADR-8573), `flow_fields`, `spatial_queries`, and in `crates/regia_nav/goldens/`
  `search_plans`, `navmesh_bake`, `layered_plans` and `tunnel_plans` (64 grid and 64 navmesh plans; ADR-8215). Target
  lines: x86-64 Linux; `aarch64-macos` and qemu `aarch64-android` lines added where they equal x86-64 (ADR-8447); other
  targets report `unrecorded_target` until rerun. The AI soak's 600-tick fingerprint is pinned.
- Determinism fingerprints re-recorded on purpose, each with its ADR: `steer_slalom` `32317dd9a9adab0f`, `slope_follow`
  `849a12ce8edf1c88`, `disc_collide` `21bccc1737941271` (x86_64-linux, ADR-8634); `hover_libm_1000` and
  `tests/fixtures/hover/libm-1000.txt` on the engine-authored default hull (ADR-8164); the `abilities_equivalence`
  `BASE_TRACE` (despawned guids' chance rows fold into the abilities hash, ADR-7573; despawned units end effects with
  `target_gone`, ADR-7216); `plant_lock_identity`'s `PLANTER_DIGEST` (ADR-8388); `all_phase_settings_off_keeps_s14_bits`
  `0x1cd8db9c792e0949` (events and clocks byte-identical; the board gained five off-feature fields);
  `presentation_trace_pin`, a presentation trace, not a simulation pin (ADR-8539). A pinned `TrackState::digest` or
  `physics.track_step` value from an older build re-records; no engine pin exists (ADR-8551). Nine determinism-feature
  test pins take the aarch64 value or are unpinned under their feature; default pins are unchanged (ADR-7668).
- World geometry and lattice pins: `regia_world_geom` `stage_a_bits` at `QUERY_BITS_VERSION` 8 (every pinned mesh, query
  and on-foot row unchanged; the x86-64 native query transcript pins moved at version 6; ADR-8531, ADR-8246);
  `tests/native_table.rs` query-table pins for the `push_capsule` rows, with capsule vectors in
  `tests/fixtures/regia/capsule-vectors.tsv` (ADR-8323); `lattice_cook_hashes_are_pinned` (sections, portals, zones,
  plugs, the skylight-opened mesh and filled mouths; ADR-8346, ADR-8533) and, with
  `section_bytes_are_pinned_across_hosts` and `portal_sound_bits_are_pinned`, the canonical-yaw values (ADR-8579). New
  pins: `clump_member_placement_bits_are_pinned_for_every_platform` and
  `painted_placement_bytes_are_pinned_for_every_platform`.
- Net pins: `regia_net` `pmtud::OFF_WIRE_HASH` retaken for the new startup pacing (same datagrams, ADR-8592) and for the
  streams line, with proof that path MTU code leaves the default wire alone (ADR-8624).
- Format pins: the frozen corpus gains `animgraph/v4.animgraph.ron`, `logic-graph-ref/v1.ron` and `retarget/v2.ron`;
  `terrain-index/v2.bin` gains one trailing byte and its `FROZEN.txt` line is re-recorded; `netprofile/v2.ron` and the
  `.animgraph.ron` v4 row of `format-hashes.ron` are re-recorded (ADR-8619, ADR-8659, ADR-8732). Hashes that move with
  no version change, because the version is unreleased: `pgout` v4 (ADR-7750), `ui` v11 (ADR-7751), the `test-report`
  and `server-toml` v4 schema fingerprints (ADR-7320, ADR-8300).
- Budget tables: the startup/idle ratchet table is re-recorded (ADR-8716); `pipeline_count_pin` pins DesktopHigh and
  DesktopUltra at 164 pipelines (the young-budget DDGI pipelines, ADR-7870); `feature_cost_pins` pins `thin_film_500` at
  `0xa1ae_0124_6ebb_4491` (the sky enters the forward image before SSR and SSGI, ADR-7963); `tests/data/frame_work.ron`
  warm heap allocations are re-recorded for the larger forward shaders, with the cells that went down tightened
  (ADR-8785).

- New format pages: `docs/formats/level-variant-v1.md`, `layer-file.md`, `terrain-editlayers.md`, `audio-buses-ron.md`,

- Spec 121 (`docs/specs/121-world-geometry.md`): the world geometry core's design, its amendment for lockstep peers on

  handshake), and the applied sign-off changes (span rule, O(1) `state_hash`, settings in the canonical section); spec
  26 § 2a follows it (ADR-8019, ADR-8084, ADR-8072)
